However, there is limited understanding of how non-toy LMs address ET problems of realistic difficulties expressed in natural language. To this end, we investigate the mechanisms underlying ET in more complex scenarios featuring multiple state-changing operations. We find that LMs do not incrementally track world states across tokens or query-relevant states across layers, but simply aggregate relevant information in parallel at the last token when the query becomes evident. We further investigate mechanisms of individual operations ($\texttt{PUT}$, $\texttt{REMOVE}$, $\texttt{MOVE}$) to characterize this non-incremental ET mechanism. Surprisingly, LMs implement the $\texttt{REMOVE}$ operation with a fragile global suppression tag; this global removal mechanism predicts various failure modes that we confirm behaviorally. We provide a mechanistic solution of nullifying this tag to partially address this issue. Overall, our findings reveal that LMs solve a fundamentally sequential task using a non-sequential strategy. More broadly, our work illustrates how behavioral and mechanistic analyses can fruitfully interact. Behavioral results inform mechanistic hypotheses, and insights from mechanistic analyses help build stronger behavioral evaluations by predicting failure modes missing from existing evaluations.

</details>


### [112] [Knowing What to Solve Before How: Preplan Empowered LLM Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30245)
*Shaojie Wang,Liang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 PPC 框架，通过增加显式的“预规划”阶段，让大模型在规划前更深入地理解问题，显著提升了数学推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有“问题→规划→思维链(CoT)”范式中，模型对“解决什么问题”的理解往往是隐式的，缺乏对问题类型、适用工具及潜在陷阱的显式识别，这导致了推理过程中的范式级性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出 PPC (Preplan-Plan-CoT) 框架，包含三个阶段：1. 预规划（Preplan）：在规划前显式理解问题类型、工具及陷阱；2. 三阶段综合管道：利用 spoiler-score 检测器过滤噪声，构建高质量的预规划监督数据；3. 复合 GRPO 奖励机制：确保后续的规划步骤在逻辑上严格遵循预规划结果。

Result: 在四个骨干模型及五个数学推理基准的 40 个评估指标中，PPC 在 39 个指标上取得最优结果，相比最强基线在 maj@16 和 pass@16 上分别提升了 2.23 和 3.06 个百分点，且未增加推理 token 开销。

Conclusion: PPC 框架通过引入显式“预规划”阶段，成功弥合了现有 LLM 推理范式中对问题理解不足的缺口。该方法无需增加推理阶段的 Token 开销，在多个数学推理基准确立了新的性能基准，证明了显式问题理解对提升复杂推理任务表现的关键作用。

Abstract: Current plan-based reasoning methods improve large language models (LLMs) by inserting a planning stage before execution, giving rise to the question $\rightarrow$ plan $\rightarrow$ cot paradigm. While effective, a closer examination reveals an inherent paradigm-level gap: both the planning and its execution stages decide how to solve a problem, while the prior question of what to solve; recognizing the problem type, the applicable tools, and the foreseeable pitfalls; remains entirely implicit. To bridge this gap, we propose PPC (Preplan-Plan-CoT), a framework that introduces an explicit problem-understanding stage, the preplan, yielding a new question $\rightarrow$ preplan $\rightarrow$ plan $\rightarrow$ cot paradigm. Realizing this paradigm requires safeguarding the conceptual integrity of preplan at both ends. Specifically, we design a three-stage synthesis pipeline with a spoiler-score detector that filters out leakage and spoiler failures to build clean preplan supervision, and a composite GRPO reward enforces that the generated plan genuinely follows from the preplan. Experiments across four backbones and five mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that PPC achieves the best results on 39 of 40 metrics, improving maj@16 and pass@16 by +2.23 and +3.06 over the strongest baseline without introducing additional inference token overhead.

</details>


### [113] [Same Evidence, Different Answers: Canonical-Context On-Policy Distillation for Multi-Turn Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30251)
*Zizhuo Lin,Quanling Liu,Jinsheng Quan,Chao Zhang,Yifan Zhu,Xing Shi,Jingtao Xu,Zhihui Li,Yawei Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对LLM在多轮交互中因信息断点导致“自锚定漂移”的问题，提出CCOPD蒸馏方法，利用全知情境下的教师模型引导分步学习，显著提高了模型在多轮对话中的信息处理能力与答案准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLMs）在获取完整指令时表现良好，但在分步交互中因“自锚定漂移”（self-anchored drift）导致性能大幅下降。即模型在早期对话中产生的无根据假设会在后续步骤中产生偏差，从而扭曲最终答案。

Method: 提出“规范上下文在线策略蒸馏”（Canonical-Context On-Policy Distillation, CCOPD）。在训练期间，使用同一个基座模型分别扮演两个角色：以完整FULL prompt为条件的冻结教师模型，以及接收分步证据的训练中学生模型；通过对齐学生模型在分步轨迹上的输出与教师模型在完整语境下的规范行为，引导学生模型进行学习。

Result: 在数学问题对话数据集上训练后，CCOPD在RAW-SHARDED场景下取得了32%的平均相对性能提升；同时在五项零样本域外任务中表现稳健，且基本保持了FULL prompt下的原始性能。分析表明，该方法增强了模型对用户证据的锚定能力，减少了对早期模型错误回复的污染。

Conclusion: CCOPD有效减轻了自锚定漂移（Self-anchored drift）现象，通过强化证据归因和弱化对早期错误假设的依赖，在保持全知情境（FULL prompt）性能的同时，显著提升了模型在分步对话（RAW-SHARDED）场景下的表现。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often solve a task when all instructions are given in a single prompt, but fail when the same information is revealed gradually across turns. When a clean FULL prompt and a RAW-SHARDED conversation contain the same complete user evidence, the model should still arrive at the same answer. We argue that a key reason for this gap is self-anchored drift: responses produced under partial information introduce unsupported assumptions, and those assumptions later distort the final answer. To reduce this effect, we propose Canonical-Context On-Policy Distillation (CCOPD). During training, the same base model is used in two roles: a frozen teacher conditioned on the clean FULL prompt and a trainable student that receives the same evidence incrementally through a multi-turn conversation; CCOPD aligns the student's behavior on its own trajectories with the teacher's canonical full-context behavior. Trained only on math problem conversations, CCOPD yields a 32\% average relative improvement in RAW-SHARDED performance over the original base model across math and five zero-shot out-of-domain task families, while largely preserving full-context performance. Further analyses suggest that CCOPD strengthens grounding in user evidence and reduces sensitivity to contamination from earlier assistant turns.

</details>


### [114] [How LoRA Remembers? A Parametric Memory Law for LLM Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30260)
*Ziwen Xu,Haiwen Hong,Linsong Yu,Benglei Cui,Longtao Huang,Hui Xue,Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过将 LoRA 作为探针，量化了 LLM 的参数记忆能力，提出了遵循幂律的“参数记忆定律”，并基于“p > 0.5 逐字回忆阈值”提出了高效的增量训练算法 MemFT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多依赖下游定性评估，缺乏对 LLM 参数记忆容量极限及底层动态机制的定量研究。为了填补这一空白，需对精确参数记忆进行系统性量化。

Method: 使用 LoRA 作为潜在空间中的受控记忆容量探针，通过量化损失减少量（ΔL）与有效参数及序列长度的关系，推导出“参数记忆定律”（Parametric Memory Law）；提出基于阈值的优化策略 MemFT，通过动态分配训练预算优化处于回忆阈值边缘的 Token。

Result: 发现了连接损失下降、有效参数量与序列长度的幂律关系；在 Token 级别确定了“确定性相变”，指出 p > 0.5 是贪婪解码下实现逐字回忆的充分条件。

Conclusion: 建立了参数记忆的量化定律与动态优化框架。证明了预测概率 p > 0.5 是逐字回忆的充分条件，并验证了 MemFT 在提升参数记忆保真度与效率方面的有效性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) must continuously learn and update knowledge to remain effective in dynamic real-world environments. While Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is widely used for such memory updates, existing studies mainly rely on qualitative downstream evaluations, leaving the quantitative capacity limits and underlying dynamics of exact parametric memory largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we employ LoRA as a controlled memory capacity probe within the latent space to systematically quantify exact parametric memory. We introduce the Parametric Memory Law, a robust power law linking loss reduction Delta L to effective parameters and sequence length. At the token level, fine-grained analysis reveals a deterministic phase transition, demonstrating that a prediction probability of p > 0.5 constitutes a sufficient condition for verbatim recall under greedy decoding. Driven by these insights, we introduce MemFT, a threshold-guided optimization strategy that dynamically redistributes the training budget toward sub-threshold tokens. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that MemFT can enhance memory fidelity and efficiency. Code will be released at https://github.com/zjunlp/ParametricMemoryLaw.

</details>


### [115] [MedCase-Structured: A Text-to-FHIR Dataset for Benchmarking Diagnostic Reasoning in Clinically Realistic EHR Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30295)
*Valentina Bui Muti,Eugénie Dulout,Ziquan Fu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对当前 LLM 临床评估脱离真实电子病历数据的问题，研究者开发了一个将非结构化文本转化为 HL7 FHIR 格式的流水线，并发现 LLM 在处理结构化临床数据时准确率下降，证明了现有基准测试可能高估了 LLM 的真实临床能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 临床推理基准测试多依赖静态数据集或非结构化输入，未能反映临床系统中实际使用的结构化、可互操作数据（如 HL7 FHIR），限制了其在真实临床决策支持系统中的应用评估。

Method: 提出了一个将非结构化医学文本转化为临床真实 HL7 FHIR R4 标准格式的自动化流水线。该方法采用“分阶段 LLM 生成 + 基于术语基础的验证与修复”机制，以减少幻觉代码并确保结构与语义一致性。基于此生成了数据集 MedCase-Structured。

Result: 所提出的流水线成功为 82.5% 的案例生成了有效的 FHIR 数据；实验表明，LLM 在处理结构化 FHIR 输入时的性能表现弱于处理纯文本。

Conclusion: 在结构化数据（临床电子病历环境）下，大语言模型的诊断准确率显著低于处理纯文本，强调了基准测试必须与实际部署场景对齐的重要性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) show promise for clinical reasoning and decision support, but evaluation in realistic, electronic health record-congruent settings remains limited. Existing benchmarks often rely on static datasets or unstructured inputs that do not reflect the structured, interoperable data formats used in clinical systems. We introduce a pipeline for generating clinically realistic HL7 FHIR R4 bundles from unstructured text, enabling controllable evaluation of clinical decision support systems. The pipeline combines staged LLM generation with terminology-grounded validation and repair to reduce hallucinated codes and enforce structural and semantic consistency. Applying this approach to MedCaseReasoning, we construct MedCase-Structured, a synthetic dataset aligned with clinician-authored diagnostic cases, achieving valid FHIR generation for 82.5% of cases. Evaluation on MedCase-Structured reveals consistently lower diagnostic accuracy for LLMs on structured FHIR inputs than with plain text, highlighting the importance of deployment-aligned benchmarking.

</details>


### [116] [Resolution Diagnostics for Paired LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30315)
*Anany Kotawala*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 当前大模型排行榜中，大量模型排名的“胜负”差异缺乏统计显著性。由于现有样本量计算方法存在两倍量级的系统性偏差，许多排名是不稳固的。研究建议采用解析比（resolution ratio）进行评估设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出当前大模型排行榜（如Open LLM Leaderboard和MMLU-Pro）中，模型排名间的微小差异往往并未达到统计显著性水平，现有评估设计在样本量规划上存在科学缺陷。

Method: 将配对LLM评估框架化为假设检验问题，提出通过计算“解析比”（resolution ratio, q = N/N*）作为衡量样本量充足性的核心诊断指标，并证明了现有工具中关于样本量计算（基于Cohen-h及无关相关系数的近似）存在约两倍的偏差。

Result: 在Open LLM Leaderboard v1和MMLU-Pro中发现大量配对（甚至在处理聚类效应后）无法在常规显著性水平（α=0.05，1-β=0.8）下获得解析；证实通用计算工具普遍低估了准确评估所需的样本量。

Conclusion: 当前主流大语言模型（LLM）排行榜中的配对评估方案往往存在样本量不足的问题，导致模型间的排名差异在统计学上无法区分；现有的效应量计算公式及评估工具存在系统性偏差。

Abstract: Across two public LLM leaderboards, many displayed pairwise rankings do not meet a conventional paired-test resolution target under the actual paired evaluation design: 11 of 40 Open LLM Leaderboard v1 pairwise comparisons and 4 of 9 MMLU-Pro top-10 adjacent-rank pairs are unresolved at (alpha, 1-beta) = (0.05, 0.8). The MMLU-Pro count rises to 6/9 under real subject-level clustering and stays at 5-6 out of 9 in 99.9% of category-bootstrap resamples. We frame paired LLM evaluation as a hypothesis-testing problem, invert level-alpha, power-(1-beta) tests, and report a per-pair resolution ratio q = N/N* as the primary diagnostic. A sharp small-effect expansion with an explicit second-order constant shows that the widely-used unpaired Cohen-h-plus-(1-rho) shortcut deviates from the correct N* by approximately a factor of two in the close-comparison regime, a deficit that three of five off-the-shelf calculators(Cohen 1988, G*Power, R pwr) silently inherit when the user post-multiplies their per-arm output by (1-rho). The unresolved-pair pattern remains under multiplicity correction and anytime-valid sequential testing.

</details>


### [117] [COMPOSE: Composing Future Theorems from Citations and Formal Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30333)
*David Busbib,Michael Werman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 为解决单一模型无法同时捕捉数学猜想的“研究方向性”与“形式化逻辑依赖”问题，研究提出了COMPOSE框架，通过融合科学引用与定理依赖图，实现了更具科学基础和数学严谨性的未来数学猜想生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的未来数学猜想生成方法通常仅考虑一种来源（引用关系或形式化逻辑），导致生成的结论要么论据不足（weakly grounded），要么缺乏数学动机（insufficiently motivated）。研究旨在通过结合两种来源来实现更具合理性的生成。

Method: 提出了名为COMPOSE的双图框架。该模型将大型语言模型（LLM）与科学引用图（关联上下文）及形式化定理依赖图（关联逻辑结构）相结合。此外，构建了一个包含108K对样本的数据集和47K篇未来论文的基准测试集。

Result: COMPOSE在检索真实未来论文的基准测试中优于强基线模型，并在LLM评估中表现出更强的关联性和数学深度，证明了混合建模策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 将科学引用图与形式化定理依赖图相结合，对于生成具有高度可信度和数学严谨性的未来数学猜想至关重要。该方法有效地克服了仅依赖单一上下文来源产生的局限性。

Abstract: A plausible future mathematical claim must satisfy two constraints: it should follow the direction of prior work and respect the formal dependencies that constrain what can validly follow. Existing approaches typically model only one of these sources, producing claims that are either weakly grounded or insufficiently motivated. We introduce grounded future mathematical generation, where the goal is to generate a plausible future theorem-like claim for an anchor paper using two complementary sources of context: its scientific citation graph and aligned formal theorem dependency graph. To address this setting, we propose COMPOSE, a dual-graph framework that conditions a language model on both scientific citation context and formal theorem structure. To support this setting, we construct a dataset of 108K paired scientific-formal graph examples from arXiv and Mathlib, together with a benchmark of 47K future papers from 2024--2025. Experiments show that COMPOSE outperforms strong baselines on retrieval to real future papers and achieves the best overall performance under LLM-judge evaluation, producing more grounded and mathematically richer outputs. These results show that future mathematical generation benefits from combining scientific context with formal structure. Project page is available at https://david-busbib.github.io/COMPOSE-page/.

</details>


### [118] [Unlocking the Working Memory of Large Language Models for Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30343)
*Lukas Aichberger,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RiM 提出了一种无需自回归生成中间推理步骤的潜空间推理方法，通过固定序列的“记忆块”充当工作记忆，实现了更高效的推理计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通过生成中间推理 token 增加推理计算量的方法存在耦合性问题，即推理过程（内部计算）与输出过程（外部交流）被迫绑定，导致推理冗余且低效。旨在模仿人类“内心思考”机制，实现在无需外部化中间思维的情况下进行推理。

Method: 提出 Reasoning in Memory (RiM) 方法：引入固定的“记忆块”（Memory Blocks）作为特殊 token 序列来扩展模型的工作记忆；采用两阶段课程学习（Curriculum Learning），先通过显式推理步骤进行监督训练，后通过迭代优化最终答案来移除步骤级监督。

Result: 在多个推理基准测试中，RiM 在不同规模和架构的语言模型上表现优于现有的潜空间推理方法，同时避免了自回归生成思维链带来的计算开销。

Conclusion: RiM证明了大型语言模型可以通过训练利用工作记忆作为潜空间推理的有效机制，实现了在不生成中间推理 tokens 的情况下提升推理能力，并在效率上优于传统的思维链方法。

Abstract: To improve the reasoning capabilities of large language models, test-time compute is typically scaled by generating intermediate tokens before the final answer. However, this couples reasoning to autoregressive generation and thereby conflates internal computation with external communication. In contrast, human cognition can use working memory to hold and manipulate information internally without the need to externalize intermediate thoughts. Drawing on this principle, we introduce Reasoning in Memory (RiM), a latent reasoning method that replaces the autoregressive generation of reasoning steps with memory blocks. These memory blocks are fixed sequences of special tokens that unlock the working-memory capacity of large language models. Since they are fixed rather than generated, they can be processed in a single forward pass, enabling compute-efficient latent reasoning. To operationalize these memory blocks, we employ a two-stage curriculum. First, we ground them by predicting explicit reasoning steps after each memory block. Second, we discard this step-level supervision and iteratively refine the final answer after each memory block. Our experiments on reasoning benchmarks show that, across language models of different families and sizes, RiM matches or exceeds existing latent reasoning methods while avoiding the autoregressive generation of thoughts. These results demonstrate that large language models can be trained to use working memory as an effective mechanism for latent reasoning.

</details>


### [119] [LLMSurgeon: Diagnosing Data Mixture of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30348)
*Yaxin Luo,Jiacheng Cui,Xiaohan Zhao,Xinyi Shang,Jiacheng Liu,Xinyue Bi,Zhaoyi Li,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为 LLMSurgeon 的框架，仅通过分析模型生成的文本，即可反向推断出其预训练数据的领域分布，解决了大模型训练数据配比不透明的审计难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型的预训练数据配比通常未公开，导致无法对模型的训练数据来源和组成进行有效的后验审计（post-hoc auditing）。

Method: 提出 LLMSurgeon 框架，将数据混合比推断（DMS）建模为标签偏移假设下的逆问题。该方法通过构建经过校准的软混淆矩阵（soft confusion matrix），并求解带约束的逆问题，以纠正模型在分类过程中的系统性偏差，从而恢复潜在的领域分布。

Result: 研究团队引入了 LLMScan 作为验证基准。实验结果表明，LLMSurgeon 能够在已知配比的开源模型上高保真度地恢复出领域的混合比例。

Conclusion: LLMSurgeon 提供了一种实用的、事后审计大模型预训练数据配比的方法，在无需访问原始训练数据的情况下，能够有效推断出底层的模型“数字 DNA”。

Abstract: The pretraining data mixture of Large Language Models (LLMs) constitutes their "digital DNA", shaping model behaviors, capabilities, and failure modes. Yet this composition is rarely disclosed, making post-hoc auditing of data combination or provenance difficult. In this work, we formalize $\textbf{Data Mixture Surgery (DMS)}$: given only generated text from a target LLM, estimate the domain-level distribution of its pretraining corpus under a predefined taxonomy. We propose $\textbf{LLMSurgeon}$, a strong framework that casts DMS as an inverse problem under the label-shift assumption. Rather than directly aggregating classifier outputs, LLMSurgeon estimates a calibrated $\textit{soft}$ confusion matrix and solves a constrained inverse problem to correct systematic domain confusion and recover the latent mixture prior. To evaluate, we introduce $\textbf{LLMScan}$, a recipe-verifiable evaluation suite built from open-source LLMs with transparent pretraining mixtures. Across LLMScan, LLMSurgeon recovers domain mixtures with high fidelity under fixed protocols. Our work presents a practical, post-hoc approach for auditing the digital DNA of foundation models without access to their training data.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [IORM: Hierarchical I/O Governance for Thousands of Consolidated Databases on Oracle Exadata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29006)
*Rajarshi Chowdhury,Akshay Shah,Zakaria Alrmaih,Chenhao Guo,Anubhav Singh,Sue Lee*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了用于Oracle Exadata的I/O资源管理器（IORM），通过引入数据库语义感知和分层资源治理，解决了大规模租户共享存储时的性能干扰（吵闹邻居）问题，实现了高效的资源隔离与分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Oracle Exadata在大规模多租户部署中，现有的通用块层调度器无法识别数据库内部的语义和租户边界，导致多级资源竞争，无法满足高效的性能隔离需求。

Method: 提出I/O资源管理器（IORM），包含三个核心机制：1. I/O标记（I/O Tagging）：在数据库内核与存储调度器间传递语义上下文；2. 分层资源配置文件（Hierarchical Resource Profiles）：支持跨多层级（容器、租户、负载）的份额与限制策略；3. 统一存储治理（Unified Storage Governance）：跨持久内存、闪存和磁盘全存储介质实施一致性策略与缓存管理。

Result: 在生产环境评估中，IORM显著提升了延迟一致性，几乎消除了长尾延迟异常，在混合工作负载下大幅改善了平均读取延迟，并成功实现了对分层资源限制的精确控制。

Conclusion: IORM通过在存储层引入对数据库语义的感知，有效解决了大规模多租户环境下的资源争抢问题，为Oracle Exadata实现极端高密度部署提供了核心的技术保障。

Abstract: Oracle Exadata consolidates thousands of tenant databases onto shared storage infrastructure deployed at hundreds of customer sites worldwide. Oracle Multitenant architecture enables this extreme density, with thousands of tenant databases sharing a single Exadata storage system -- but this creates a multi-level resource hierarchy (container databases, tenant databases, and workloads within tenants) that commodity block-layer schedulers cannot govern, as they lack visibility into database semantics and tenant boundaries. This paper presents the I/O Resource Manager (IORM), a storage-side scheduler built on three mechanisms: I/O Tagging, which propagates semantic context from the database kernel to the storage scheduler; Hierarchical Resource Profiles, which express compositional allocation policies across consolidation tiers using shares and limits; and Unified Storage Governance, which applies these policies consistently across all tiers of the storage hierarchy -- persistent memory, flash, and hard disk -- including cache placement decisions. IORM enables successful cloud deployments where thousands of tenants coexist on shared storage: production OLTP workloads run alongside concurrent analytical workloads from the same or different databases without noisy-neighbor interference. Evaluation on production Exadata systems demonstrates that IORM dramatically improves latency consistency, virtually eliminating tail latency outliers and delivering several-fold improvements in average read latency under mixed workloads. Hierarchical limits compose correctly across all three levels, and proportional share allocation tracks configured ratios closely even under highly skewed demand.

</details>


### [121] [ScanTwin: Simulating Performance Regressions Without Access to Tenant Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29093)
*Donghyun Sohn,Jennie Rogers*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: ScanTwin 是一种通过提取 Parquet 元数据并应用差分隐私，在保护租户数据隐私的同时，辅助开发人员在本地准确重现云数据平台中查询性能回归的轻量级框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云数据平台中，开发人员因数据隐私限制无法访问原始数据，导致难以在本地重现特定租数据集中的性能回归问题。现有的合成数据方法主要关注统计属性，忽略了影响数据库扫描性能（尤其是行组剪枝）的物理特性。

Method: 提出了一种名为 ScanTwin 的框架：(1) 从 Parquet 文件页脚中提取行组（row-group）级别的草图信息，包括边界值和压缩大小；(2) 对数据应用 ε-差分隐私，采用边界参数化方案；(3) 利用这些草图在本地模拟数据库引擎（如 DuckDB）的行为。

Result: 在 TPC-H 和 SSB 数据集上，该方法在不加隐私保护时（ε=∞）实现了 0% 的剪枝误差和小于 1% 的字节数误差；在 ε=5 的强隐私约束下，针对高选择性查询（>30%）的剪枝误差控制在 8.5% 以下，且测试环境下的查询扫描耗时与生产环境高度趋同。

Conclusion: ScanTwin 是一个轻量级且具有隐私保护特性的框架，能够通过 Parquet 足迹（footer）提取的元数据草图，在不泄露原始数据的前提下，高保真地模拟云数据库引擎的扫描性能，有效解决了因隐私约束导致的性能回归复现难题。

Abstract: In cloud data platforms, developers often encounter performance regressions that occur in specific tenant datasets. However, due to confidentiality constraints, they cannot access the original data, which makes it difficult to reproduce these regressions locally. Current methods for synthetic data usually focus on statistical properties, such as matching data distributions or improving query accuracy. However, they overlook the physical properties that control how the engine behaves during scans, including row-group pruning.
  We propose ScanTwin, a lightweight framework that extracts a per-row-group sketch from the Parquet footer, including boundary values and compressed sizes, and releases them under $\varepsilon$-differential privacy using a boundary parameterization. On TPC-H and SSB (6M rows), ScanTwin achieves 0% pruning error and less than 1% byte error at $\varepsilon{=}\infty$. Under $\varepsilon{=}5$, high-selectivity queries ($>$30%) incur below 8.5% pruning error on both datasets, and per-query scan timing on DuckDB closely tracks the original.

</details>


### [122] [One Ring to Shuffle Them All: Scalable Intra-Process Data Redistribution with Ring-Buffer Shuffle in Redpanda Oxla](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29099)
*Adam Szymański,Tyler Akidau*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对大规模多核 CPU 下并行查询引擎 Shuffle 性能扩展受阻的问题，提出了一种环形缓冲区流式设计，通过无锁原子操作显著降低同步开销，在超高核心数环境下实现了大幅性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着服务器 CPU 核心数增加至数百个，现有的数据重分区（Shuffle）机制存在扩展性瓶颈：批量分区存在内存开销大且缺乏缓存局部性的问题；通道流式设计则因过度的跨线程同步而限制了大规模并行下的性能。

Method: 提出了一种基于环形缓冲区的流式 Shuffle 设计，通过固定大小的批量组实现无锁原子槽位获取（lock-free atomic slot acquisition），从而实现了摊销 O(1) 的同步成本和与输入大小无关的 O(M) 内存开销。并与现有的批量分区（batch partitioning）和通道流式传输（channel-based streaming）进行了对比评估。

Result: 在 72 核至 192 核的高性能服务器平台上，环形缓冲区 Shuffle 表现出显著的加速效果。在 192 核系统上，该方法性能超越传统通道流式设计超过 100%，超越批量分区方案超过 300%。但在特定芯片架构下，该方案表现受架构特征影响。

Conclusion: 在多核 CPU 环境下，提出的环形缓冲区（Ring-buffer）流式 Shuffle 设计能显著提升并发性能。然而，在具有跨芯片（chiplet）及分区 L3 缓存的架构中，全局原子计数器可能成为性能瓶颈，此时通道（channel）流式传输仍具竞争力。该方法的实际性能增益取决于工作负载的具体特征。

Abstract: As server CPUs scale to dozens and now hundreds of cores per socket, parallel query engines must rethink how they redistribute data between threads. Partitioned operators such as hash joins and aggregations require frequent data redistribution across threads, yet existing intra-process shuffle designs fundamentally fail to scale with core count: batch partitioning avoids cross-thread synchronization in the hot path but materializes all intermediate data, introduces a global producer/consumer barrier, and requires a consumption approach with low cache locality, while channel-based streaming avoids materialization but incurs per-channel synchronization that scales poorly with core count. As core counts rise, these architectural tradeoffs increasingly prevent engines from fully utilizing modern hardware.
  We present a ring-buffer streaming shuffle design that addresses these shortcomings through lock-free atomic slot acquisition into fixed-size batch groups, achieving amortized O(1) synchronization cost per batch and O(M) memory independent of input size. Ring-buffer shuffle has been implemented in Redpanda's Oxla query engine for two years, where it currently powers production queries for Redpanda SQL users.
  We evaluate all three approaches on a 72-core NVIDIA GraceHopper, a 192-core dual-socket AWS Graviton4, and a 96-core (192-thread) AMD EPYC. On a 72-core single-socket system the ring buffer outperforms channel streaming by up to 44% and batch partitioning by up to 79%; at 192 cores the advantage over channel grows to over 100% and over 300% versus batch partitioning. Even so, on chiplet architectures with many partitioned L3 caches, the shared atomic counter becomes a cross-die bottleneck and channel-based streaming remains competitive. End-to-end Graviton4 evaluation on TPC-H (21 queries) and ClickBench (43 queries) shows the advantage is workload-shape-dependent.

</details>


### [123] [Towards Reliable Agentic Progressive Text-to-Visualization with Verification Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29692)
*Wenxin Xu,Chen Jason Zhang,Xiaoyong Wei,Haoyang Li,Hwanhee Kim,Yuanfeng Song,Raymond Chi-Wing Wong*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对现有 Text-to-Vis 模式导致的认知过载和错误率高的问题，本文提出了渐进式多轮交互框架 PMVis，通过构建专属数据集 PMVisBench 和多智能体处理框架 PMVisAgent，实现了交互过程中的意图修正与差错验证，显著提升了可视化生成的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Text-to-Vis 方法多采用“单次生成（one-shot）”模式，要求用户一次性定义所有细节，极易引起认知过载并导致生成错误。

Method: 1. 提出 PMVis 渐进式多轮交互范式；2. 构建包含渐进式真实查询轨迹的 PMVisBench 数据集；3. 开发基于多智能体（用户/系统/验证）的 PMVisAgent 框架，并通过 ReAct 循环进行差错验证与修复。

Result: 在 PMVisBench 上进行实验，对比 SOTA 基线具有显著优势：单表任务执行准确率提升 17.57%，多表任务提升 23.21%。消融实验证实了渐进式交互结合澄清机制的重要性。

Conclusion: PMVis 框架及其对应的 Bench 与 Agent 提供了一种更符合人类交互逻辑的渐进式文本生成可视化方法，有效提升了多轮交互中的可视化生成准确性，减少了认知负担。

Abstract: Text-to-Visualization (Text-to-Vis) translates natural language queries into visualization query languages, enabling non-expert users to perform data analysis. However, most existing methods follow a one-shot paradigm that requires users to specify all visualization details in a single round, often leading to cognitive overload and incorrect visualizations. In this paper, we propose PMVis, a progressive multi-turn paradigm for text-to-vis, where users' intents are refined through multi-turn interactions. To support research in this paradigm, we construct PMVisBench, the first dataset designed to capture the progressive and iterative nature of real-world user queries. It is built through VQL simplification and NLQ reconstruction, with explicit rule constraints to ensure each intermediate VQL remains valid and meaningful. Building upon PMVis, we further introduce PMVisAgent, an agent-based framework that simulates realistic user-system dialogues. PMVisAgent consists of a User, a System, and a Validation Agent that performs verification and repair via a ReAct-style tool-use loop to mitigate error accumulation across rounds, with explicit interaction and verification rules to ensure reliability of the multi-agent system. Extensive experiments on PMVisBench demonstrate that PMVisAgent significantly outperforms state-of-the-art text-to-vis baselines. It achieves up to 17.57\% and 23.21\% improvements in execution accuracy in single-table and multi-table settings, respectively, while ablation studies confirm the importance of combining progressive interaction with clarification. The code is available at https://github.com/wxxv/PMVis.

</details>


### [124] [Zero-Scan Data Quality: Leveraging Table Format Metadata for Continuous Observability at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30308)
*Mohit Verma,Shantanu Rawat,Christian Bush,Sumedh Sakdeo,Lokesh Amarnath Ravindranathan,Dwarak Bakshi*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出利用Apache Iceberg的写入时元数据及其扩展存储的轻量级草图，在无需全表扫描的情况下，实现高效、低成本的持续数据质量可观测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据质量监控通常需要全表扫描，成本高昂；而现代数据表格式（如Iceberg）在写入时已生成的元数据与数据质量监控需求存在高度重叠。

Method: 提出一种“元数据优先”的方法，利用Apache Iceberg写入时生成的元数据（如统计信息）进行数据质量监控，并扩展引入了轻量级计数器和可增量合并的草图（Theta sketches、KLL sketches）。

Result: 在LinkedIn 20万+张表（800+ PB）上的实践证明，该方法在零边际计算成本下满足了约60%的数据质量规则，并将性能分析资源消耗降低了约50%。

Conclusion: 通过在Puffin sidecar文件中存储文件级草图（sketches），可以以近乎零的边际计算成本覆盖近90%的生产数据质量规则，是实现高效持续数据质量可观测性的最佳实践。

Abstract: Modern table formats such as Apache Iceberg compute and store metadata-commit timestamps, record counts, and column-level statistics such as null counts and value bounds at write time as part of file writing. These statistics serve query planning, yet they overlap substantially with data quality (DQ) monitoring needs. We describe a metadata-first approach that repurposes write-time statistics for continuous DQ observability: anomaly detection, drift monitoring, null-rate tracking; without scanning any data. Deployed at LinkedIn across 200,000+ Iceberg tables (800+ PB), this approach satisfies approximately 60% of user-defined DQ rules at zero marginal compute cost and reduces profiling resource consumption by around 50%. Extending manifest statistics with lightweight counters (sum, zero-value counts, boolean counts) and incrementally mergeable sketches; Theta sketches for distinct counts, KLL sketches for quantiles; can further raise metadata-satisfiable coverage to close to 90% of production DQ rules. We validate sketch accuracy, mergeability, and storage overhead on production data and propose that table formats should store per-file sketches in Puffin sidecar files, following the same store-then-aggregate pattern used for existing manifest statistics.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [Residual-Entropy Accounting for Routed Atom-Budgeted Learned Indexes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29061)
*Faruk Alpay,Levent Sarioglu*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文为具有原子预算和认证功能的学习索引架构建立了一套查询性能分析理论，采用条件残差答案熵来量化查询时间，并通过影子价格策略和基准测试揭示了该类系统的实际运行开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前学习索引设计缺乏针对特定受限架构（路由、原子预算、认证修复）的查询时间查询时间复杂性分析与理论保障。现有的性能评估往往缺乏对残差熵和认证开销的系统性量化。

Method: 提出了针对有序路由、计数预测器、认证修复的学习索引系统架构，并引入“条件残差答案熵”作为核心参数。证明了双向会计定理，推导了查询时间规模；设计了非预言机（non-oracular）的分段线性模型，并提出影子价格分配规则对资源进行优化，在真实数据集（SOSD/Zenodo）上进行评估。

Result: 证明了条件残差答案熵可以作为该特定架构下查询时间规模的度量指标；对比了 PGM-index、RadixSpline 和二分查找，量化了原型系统中存在的额外计算开销与性能瓶颈。

Conclusion: 本文建立了学习索引架构下查询时间的性能分析理论，通过条件残差答案熵量化了基于原子预算的学习索引的查询性能边界。通过影子价格分配规则和基准测试，揭示了该架构在实际应用中的性能特征、开销及瓶颈。

Abstract: We study exact predecessor and rank search in a routed, atom-budgeted, certified-repair learned-index architecture. An ordered directory routes each query to a contiguous interval, a counted local predictor returns a certified rank window, and exact repair resolves the remaining uncertainty by comparisons. The result is scoped to this architecture and does not claim guarantees for arbitrary learned-index designs such as unconstrained RMI dispatch, hash routing, neural routing, or exact-payload indexes without additional accounting.
  The main parameter is conditional residual answer entropy: the entropy of the exact answer after the leaf, predictor output, certificate, and charged pre-repair information are observed. We prove a two-sided accounting theorem showing that this functional gives the query-time scale under the stated architecture and local predictor-atom budget. Directory space, sorted-array storage, and transcript-indexed repair-program space are treated as separate system costs, so the theorem is not a byte-level space lower bound or a compact implementation recipe.
  We also give a rank-spread specialization in which the radius term log(1 + Delta) is valid only when many residual ranks remain likely after the predictor transcript is known. For counted piecewise-linear segments, we make the profile term non-oracular, derive a shadow-price allocation rule, compute finite-instance RGapM and GapM values on real SOSD and Zenodo samples, and report benchmarks against PGM-index, RadixSpline, and binary search. The benchmarks expose overheads and bottlenecks rather than claiming speed for the shadow prototype.

</details>


### [126] [An Improved Greedy Approximation for (Metric) $k$-Means](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29165)
*Moses Charikar,Vincent Cohen-Addad,Ruiquan Gao,Fabrizio Grandoni,Euiwoong Lee,Ernest van Wijland*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文通过克服平方距离度量下的算法适配技术难点，为k-Means问题提出了一种新的近似算法，在一般度量空间下实现了小于5.83的近似比，创下了该问题的最佳性能纪录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: k-Means是数据分析中的核心问题，尽管已有研究，但之前在一般度量空间下的近似比（9+ε）仍有较大优化空间，且针对平方距离度量下的算法适配存在理论瓶颈。

Method: 1. 对设施选址问题中的LMP（Lagrangian Multiplier Preserving）贪心近似算法进行了适配，解决了其在平方距离度量下违反三角不等式的技术障碍；2. 将改进后的LMP算法与近期针对k-Median问题提出的近似框架相结合。

Result: 实现了针对一般度量空间k-Means问题的多项式时间3+2√2+ε（<5.83）近似算法，显著优于先前的(9+ε)近似，甚至优于针对欧几里得空间特例的5.92近似。

Conclusion: 本文通过适配更先进的LMP算法并结合新的理论框架，成功打破了k-Means问题在一般度量空间中的近似比限制，为该领域提供了当前最优的算法保障。

Abstract: Clustering is a basic task in data analysis and machine learning, and the optimization of clustering objectives are well-studied optimization problems; amongst these, the $k$-Means objective is arguably the most well known. Given a collection of points in a metric space, the goal is to partition them into $k$ clusters, each with an associated center, so as to minimize the sum of squared distances of points to their cluster centers. In this paper, we present a polynomial-time $3+2\sqrt{2}+ε<5.83$-approximation algorithm for $k$-Means in general metrics. This substantially improves on the current-best $(9+ε)$-approximation in [Ahmadian, Norouzi-Fard, Svensson, Ward - FOCS'17, SICOMP'20], and even slightly improves on the $5.92$-approximation in [Cohen-Addad, Esfandiari, Mirrokni, Narayanan - STOC'22] for the Euclidean special case.
  A natural approach for $k$-Means is to leverage Lagrangian Multiplier Preserving (LMP) approximations for the facility location problem. The previous best results for $k$-Means build upon an adaptation of an LMP $3$-approximation for facility location with metric connection costs in [Jain, Vazirani - J.ACM'01] based on a primal-dual method, rather than on the improved LMP greedy $2$-approximation for the same problem in [Jain, Mahdian, Markakis, Saberi, Vazirani - J.ACM'03]. The barrier to using the improved LMP algorithm was that no adaptation of this algorithm and its analysis to the case of squared metric connection costs was known (since squared distances violate triangle inequality). Our main contribution is overcoming this barrier by providing such an adaptation. This new LMP approximation algorithm is then combined with the framework recently introduced in [Cohen-Addad, Grandoni, Lee, Schwiegelshohn, Svensson - STOC'25] for the related (metric) $k$-Median problem.

</details>


### [127] [Distributed Gaussian Mean Testing under Communication Constraints: messages, samples, and coins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29426)
*Clément L. Canonne,Nimitt*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了分布式通信受限环境下的高斯均值检验问题，通过引入共享随机数限制、用户间不等量的样本分布及不等量的通信预算，推导了该复杂设定下的理论极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式高斯均值检验研究多假设用户样本量相等、通信预算一致且拥有充足共享随机性；为适应更真实的分布式计算环境，需要研究在资源受限和异构配置下的通信高效统计推断问题。

Method: 采用统计学习理论和信息论方法，通过极小极大估计（minimax estimation）框架，分析在共享随机位受限（$s$）、样本量异构（$m_k$）及通信预算异构（$\ell_k$）等复杂分布式约束下的假设检验误差。

Result: 论文推导出了在共享随机位受限、异构样本大小和异构通信预算下的样本复杂度下界，证明了上述因素对分布式高斯均值检验极小极大风险的具体影响。

Conclusion: 该研究通过推广分布式高斯均值检验模型，明确了在有限通信、共享随机数受限以及异构样本量（$m_k$）和异构比特预算（$\ell_k$）约束下的统计极小极大风险界限，增强了该领域理论模型的鲁棒性和适用范围。

Abstract: We revisit the problem of Gaussian mean testing in a distributed, communication constrained setting, where each of $n$ users independently observes samples from an unknown $d$-dimensional spherical Gaussian distribution $\mathcal{G}(μ,\mathbb{I}_d)$, and can communicate up to $\ell$ bits to a central referee. The referee's goal is then to distinguish between cases (i) $\|μ\|_2 = 0$ versus (ii) $\|μ\|_2\ge \varepsilon$. This problem has been considered in the private- and public-coin settings, when each user holds exactly one sample, or more generally when each holds exactly $m$ samples. In this work, we significantly generalize the question in three directions: when the users only share a small number $s$ of random bits, when each user holds a different number of samples $m_k$, and when each user can send a different number of bits $\ell_k$ to the referee.

</details>


### [128] [Sampling Directed Eulerian Tours in $\widetilde O(m^{3/2})$ Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29566)
*Nima Anari*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种能在 $\widetilde O(m^{3/2})$ 时间内对任意有向欧拉多重图进行近似均匀采样的随机算法，打破了传统算法在稀疏图上的复杂性限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的欧拉回路采样算法在稀疏图上受限于 O(mn) 复杂度（基于树形图/arborescence 的方法），作者旨在开发一种更快、更通用的算法以克服这一瓶颈。

Method: 核心方法是针对 2-in/2-out 图提出一种新的局部马尔可夫链（flip-repair walk），利用动态弦数据结构（dynamic chord data structure）进行高效实现，并结合逐点度数缩减（pointwise degree-reduction）包装器将其扩展至任意度数的有向欧拉多重图。

Result: 对于具有 m 条弧的任意有向欧拉多重图，该随机算法能够在 $\widetilde O(m^{3/2})$ 时间内近似均匀地采样欧拉回路。

Conclusion: 本研究通过创新的算法设计与马尔可夫链分析，成功打破了稀疏图上近似均匀采样欧拉回路的复杂度壁垒，实现了接近线性复杂度的多项式时间采样。

Abstract: We give a randomized algorithm that samples a nearly uniform Eulerian tour of a directed Eulerian multigraph with $m$ arcs in $\widetilde O(m^{3/2})$ time. The guarantee is worst-case, applies to arbitrary directed Eulerian multigraphs, and breaks the $mn$-type arborescence-sampling barrier on sparse graphs.
  The core case is a $2$-in/$2$-out graph. We introduce a new local Markov chain, the flip--repair walk: one step locally splits a tour into two circuits and then chooses uniformly among the local flips that repair the state to one tour. We prove that this walk mixes in nearly linear many steps and implement the walk using a dynamic chord data structure. A pointwise degree-reduction wrapper extends the sampler from this degree-two core to arbitrary degrees while preserving the $\widetilde O(m^{3/2})$ total running time.
  The high-level algorithmic plan, the switching-network reduction, and the dynamic data-structure argument were devised by the author. The author conjectured the mixing theorem underlying the analysis, and GPT 5.5 Pro Extended produced its linear-algebra proof. Codex assisted with manuscript assembly and typesetting.

</details>


### [129] [On Language Generation in the Limit with Bounded Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30324)
*Jon Kleinberg,Anay Mehrotra,Amin Saberi,Grigoris Velegkas*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限内存限制下语言生成与识别的理论边界，发现生成任务比识别任务更具容忍度，且证明了自适应内存存储优于固定窗口存储。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的算法通常只能保留有限的内存，但现有的研究（如语言生成）大多假设能够访问全部历史记录。本文旨在探索在有限内存（特别是无内存或受限内存）限制下，语言生成与识别任务的“可学习性”边界。

Method: 结合计算学习理论与统计学方法进行分析：在生成任务中通过枚举限制与组合分析（Sperner定理、对称链分解）；在识别任务中研究增量式学习（仅记录上一次预测）；并对比分析了无内存、滑动窗口与自适应存储三种内存模型。

Result: 1. 无内存生成器在特定枚举限制下可生成任意可数语言。
2. 确定了无内存生成器在有限集合下的最小极大密度上限。
3. 证明了滑动窗口对最坏情况下的密度改善无效，而自适应存储能显著改进性能。
4. 增量式识别（仅记前次预测）在处理超过三个语言的集合时失效，但放宽条件至“近似识别”后可实现。

Conclusion: 有限内存对“语言生成”与“识别”任务的影响不同：生成任务对于任何可数集合都是可实现的，而密度和识别任务仅限于有限集合，且随着集合规模扩大，其性能保证会减弱。此外，自适应内存比固定窗口内存更具优势。

Abstract: We study language generation in the limit under bounded memory. In this task, a learner observes examples from an unknown target language one at a time and must eventually output only new valid examples. Prior work assumes access to the entire history, a strong assumption since realistic algorithms retain limited past information. Classical work in learning theory shows memory constraints dramatically alter learnability; we extend this to language generation.
  First, we study memoryless generators. Under a mild enumeration restriction, every countable collection of infinite languages remains generable without memory. Without this restriction, we exactly characterize when memoryless generation is possible. For finite collections, we characterize the optimal minimax density achievable by memoryless generators -- the best density guaranteed against any collection of a given size. This combinatorial bound relies on Sperner's theorem and symmetric chain decompositions.
  We further show that a sliding window of the last $W$ examples does not improve this worst-case density, whereas allowing it to store $b$ adaptively chosen past examples improves the achievable density for every $b \geq 1$.
  Finally, we revisit identification in the limit, where the learner must converge to a single correct hypothesis for the target language. We focus on its incremental variant, where the learner remembers only its previous guess. Here, although exact identification fails on a collection of just three languages, a mild relaxation requiring convergence to an ``approximate'' version of the target is achievable for every finite collection.
  These results show bounded memory affects these tasks differently: generation remains achievable for every countable collection, while density and identification are confined to finite collections, with guarantees weakening as the collection grows.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [130] [Behavior-Induced Mirror-Prox Temporal-Difference Learning for Faster Off-Policy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28849)
*Xingguo Chen,Yuchen Shen,Shangdong Yang,Chao Li,Guang Yang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为STHTD-MP的离策略时序差分学习算法，通过利用行为策略贝尔曼矩阵构建新的度量几何，有效提升了线性函数近似下的收敛性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Mirror-Prox TD方法过度依赖特征协方差度量，未能充分利用行为策略的转移信息，导致在某些任务中几何结构不佳，影响收敛性能。

Method: 提出STHTD-MP算法，将Mirror-Prox TD方法中的特征协方差度量替换为行为策略贝尔曼矩阵的对称部分；采用单学习率策略，并利用Mirror-Prox预测-校正步骤求解鞍点方程。

Result: 理论分析证明了算法在标准随机近似假设下收敛；误差界分析和数值实验表明，在适当的几何条件下，STHTD-MP相较于GTD2-MP具有更好的收缩特性。

Conclusion: STHTD-MP通过引入基于行为策略的贝尔曼矩阵作为度量，优化了鞍点问题的几何结构，在特定条件下能实现比传统GTD2-MP更小的平均收缩因子，从而提高收敛效率。

Abstract: Gradient temporal-difference methods provide stable off-policy prediction with linear function approximation, but their practical performance is strongly affected by the geometry induced by the auxiliary-variable metric. Existing Mirror-Prox TD methods typically use the feature covariance metric, whereas hybrid TD methods suggest that behavior-policy transition information can provide a more informative update geometry. This paper proposes a behavior-induced Mirror-Prox temporal-difference method, called STHTD-MP, which replaces the covariance metric in the primal-dual saddle-point formulation with the symmetric part of the behavior-policy Bellman matrix. The method keeps a single learning rate for the primal and auxiliary variables and applies a Mirror-Prox prediction-correction step to the resulting hybrid saddle-point operator. We provide a formal convergence analysis for fixed-policy linear prediction under standard stochastic approximation assumptions: the behavior-induced metric is positive definite, the joint mean system is Hurwitz, boundedness follows from a Lyapunov argument, and the stochastic recursion converges by the ODE method. We further derive projected-oracle ergodic gap bounds and an exact mean-operator comparison with GTD2-MP based on the spectral radius of the deterministic Mirror-Prox error matrix. The analysis shows that STHTD-MP can have a smaller mean contraction factor than GTD2-MP when the behavior-induced metric improves the saddle-point geometry. Exact numerical mean-operator analysis on two-state, Random Walk, and Boyan Chain benchmarks supports this condition, while Baird's counterexample is identified as a singular boundary case where the strict assumptions fail.

</details>


### [131] [Behavior-Aware Auxiliary Corrections for Off-Policy Temporal-Difference Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28855)
*Xingguo Chen,Zhiang He,Yuchen Shen,Shangdong Yang,Chao Li,Guang Yang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了BA-TDC和BA-TDRC算法，通过将行为感知矩阵引入离策略TD学习的辅助校正中，提升了价值函数逼近的稳定性与收敛性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决离策略时间差分（TD）学习中，基于函数逼近的不稳定性问题，并探讨如何通过改进辅助几何结构（如对比协方差与行为感知矩阵）来优化价值函数逼近的动态性能。

Method: 引入“行为贝尔曼矩阵”（A_μ）替代TDC中的辅助协方差矩阵（C），构建BA-TDC，并在此基础上通过正则化得到BA-TDRC；通过有限状态平均系统进行线性分析与收敛性证明。

Result: 构建了BA-TDC和BA-TDRC算法，明确了行为感知几何对学习动态的影响，并在Baird反例、随机游走等经典任务中验证了其算法的有效性和稳健性。

Conclusion: 通过实验验证表明，“行为感知”替换在某些任务中能显著提升性能，但正则化对于在困难环境中保持稳健表现至关重要。研究证明了该方法具有固定点保持特性与几乎处处收敛性。

Abstract: Temporal-difference learning with function approximation can be unstable under off-policy sampling. TDC stabilizes off-policy TD through an auxiliary covariance correction, and TDRC further regularizes this correction in a single-timescale recursion. This paper studies a behavior-aware replacement of the auxiliary covariance geometry in the linear prediction setting, which is the standard local model for understanding the feature-space dynamics of value-function approximation. We first replace the TDC auxiliary matrix (C) by the behavior Bellman matrix (A_μ), yielding BA-TDC, and then regularize the same behavior-aware equation to obtain BA-TDRC. This two-step construction separates the contribution of behavior-aware geometry from the contribution of regularization. The linear analysis also provides a tractable model for an auxiliary-geometry design question that arises in neural-network value approximation, where feature covariances and temporal transition matrices jointly shape the last-layer correction dynamics. We give a finite-state mean-system formulation, prove fixed-point preservation and almost-sure convergence under a Hurwitz stability condition on the instantiated mean system, and compare deterministic mean rates through the spectral radius of the exact linear error recursion. Experiments on the two-state counterexample, Baird's counterexample, Random Walk, and Boyan Chain show that the behavior-aware replacement can be highly beneficial by itself on some tasks, but that regularization is necessary for robust performance across harder settings.

</details>


### [132] [The Cognitive Categorical Transformer: Category-Theoretic Inductive Biases for Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28864)
*Al Kari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CCT架构，通过将范畴论中的单纯形消息传递（Simplicial Message Passing）引入GPT-2，在大规模语言模型任务中显著降低了困惑度，并揭示了模型拓扑结构的增益规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索将范畴论与认知科学原理整合进深度学习架构，旨在验证基于范畴论的结构拓扑方法能否在大规模语言模型（306M参数）中超越传统Transformer架构。

Method: 提出认知范畴Transformer（CCT），以GPT-2 Small为背板，引入范畴论与认知科学启发的组件（如Simplicial Message Passing）。采用严格匹配步骤（215,000步）与基线对比，并进行消融实验以定位性能增益来源。

Result: CCT（306M）在WikiText-103上取得21.27的验证困惑度（PPL），优于基线GPT-2 Small（24.19），实现了12%的相对提升；其中84%的性能增益归功于Simplicial Message Passing机制。

Conclusion: 研究确立了“结构/一致性区分”（structure/consistency distinction）经验规律：增加新拓扑结构的范畴先验（如Simplicial Message Passing）能提升语言模型性能，而强制执行一致性恒等式的先验则无效。该研究为拓扑方法在LLM中的应用提供了实证支持。

Abstract: The Cognitive Categorical Transformer (CCT) is a 306M-parameter architecture that augments a pretrained GPT-2 Small backbone with cognitively grounded components derived from category theory and several inspirations from cognitive science. Under a matched-step protocol (215,000 optimizer steps, matched data, matched optimizer and schedule) on WikiText-103, CCT reaches 21.27 validation perplexity, compared with 24.19 for an identically fine-tuned GPT-2 Small baseline. The architecture therefore contributes a 2.92 PPL (12% relative) reduction beyond what in-domain fine-tuning alone provides. A retrain-from-scratch ablation that holds GT-Full simplicial message passing bypassed across the entire seven-phase activation schedule reaches 23.72 PPL, localizing 84% of the architectural improvement (2.45 of 2.92 PPL) to GT-Full. We present the first ablation-validated evidence that simplicial message passing improves language-model perplexity at the 306M-parameter scale on WikiText-103. Published GPT-2 Large reaches 22.05 zero-shot PPL on WikiText-103 with 6.2x more parameters than GPT-2 Small; this paper treats that number as an external published reference, not as the architectural benchmark. Three negative results on consistency-style categorical priors (sheaf smoothing, adjunction round-trip, curvature regularization) and the joint structural-prior result for GT-Full and PrecisionWeightedPP together support an empirical pattern termed the *structure/consistency distinction*, in which categorical priors that add new topology improve language modeling and those that enforce a consistency identity do not.

</details>


### [133] [Review Arcade: On the Human Alignment and Gameability of LLM Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28897)
*Hans Ole Hatzel,Sebastian Steindl,Jan Strich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究分析了AI在论文评审与修订中的角色，发现LLM评审与人工评审的一致性较低且不稳定；同时揭示了利用LLM进行论文迭代修改能显著提高评分，“操纵”评审过程的现象在AI时代趋势明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鉴于LLM在学术写作及评审中被广泛使用，作者旨在探究LLM生成的审稿意见与人类审稿的一致性，以及作者利用LLM改进论文以“博取”更高评分的可行性。

Method: 基于2025年ACL Rolling Review (ARR)的论文数据集，对比人类审稿与LLM审稿的一致性；设计并实验了一种“作者利用LLM评审意见进行迭代式修订（draft-revise workflow）”的策略，以评估其对论文评分的影响。

Result: 1. LLM评审与人类评审的对齐度有限且随模型及提示词（prompt）的变化表现出极大不稳定性；2. 作者通过LLM改进论文的策略是有效的，高达35%的论文在经过此类迭代修订后，获得了统计学意义上的评分提升。

Conclusion: AI辅助审稿与写作在学术生态中已不可避免。研究表明，利用LLM进行迭代修改（"gaming"）能够显著提升论文评分，这可能对同行评审的公正性构成挑战，提示我们需要重新审视AI时代的评审机制。

Abstract: LLM-generated reviews for scientific papers are gaining considerable traction and are even being officially piloted by major conferences. We have to assume that not only reviewers are using LLM-assistance, but also that authors use LLMs to revise their papers before submitting. In this work, we perform empirical experiments on papers from the 2025 ACL Rolling Review (ARR) to evaluate LLM reviews from both the author and the reviewer perspective. First, we identify a limited alignment of LLM reviews with human ones. In the best-case scenario, the alignment is reasonable. However, we also find that LLM-human alignment varies substantially across prompts and models. Finally, we investigate the scenario in which the author uses an iterative draft-revise workflow to improve the submission according to the LLM review. We find that this "gaming" of LLM reviews can be effective in specific scenarios, leading to a statistically significant increase of overall scores for up to 35\% of papers. We publish our code: https://github.com/uhh-hcds/reviewarcade.

</details>


### [134] [Orthogonal Concept Erasure for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28902)
*Yuhao Sun,Lingyun Yu,Haoxiang Xu,Fengyuan Miao,Zhuoer Xu,Hongtao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对扩散模型概念擦除，本文提出正交概念擦除（OCE）方法，通过乘法更新替代传统的加法更新，在保持模型生成能力的同时实现了高效、精确的单/多概念擦除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于编辑的扩散模型概念擦除方法依赖于加法更新，这种方式会同时扰乱神经元方向、幅度和角度几何，导致擦除目标概念时损害模型的整体生成能力。

Method: 提出正交概念擦除（OCE）方法：1. 将参数更新从加法改为基于几何视角的乘法更新；2. 使用闭式解推导出的逐层正交变换，在擦除概念的同时保留神经元幅度和角度几何；3. 引入子空间目标函数以处理多概念擦除的冲突约束。

Result: OCE 在单概念和多概念擦除任务上均优于现有方法，能以极高的效率（4.3秒内擦除多达100个概念）实现精确擦除并保留非目标生成内容。

Conclusion: OCE 通过将概念擦除重新定义为乘法参数更新，成功解决了加法更新带来的语义干扰问题，实现了在保持生成能力的前提下精确且高效地擦除多个概念。

Abstract: Concept erasure has emerged as a promising approach to mitigate undesired or unsafe content in diffusion models, yet existing methods still face significant limitations. While training-based methods are effective, their high computational cost limits scalability. Editing-based methods are more efficient and deployment-friendly, yet they struggle to simultaneously achieve precise concept erasure and preserve overall generative capacity. We identify this core limitation of the editing-based methods as reliance on additive parameter updates. Our empirical analysis reveals that concept semantics primarily depend on neuron direction rather than neuron magnitude, while overall generative capacity relies on the angular geometry of neurons. As additive updates inherently entangle direction, magnitude, and angular geometry, they inevitably introduce unintended interference between concept erasure and overall generation performance. To address this, we propose Orthogonal Concept Erasure (OCE), which reformulates editing-based erasure as multiplicative parameter updates from a geometric perspective. Specifically, OCE applies layer-wise orthogonal transformations derived from a closed-form solution to the parameters, enabling precise concept erasure while preserving the neuron magnitude and angular geometry. Furthermore, to address conflicting constraints in multi-concept erasure, OCE introduces a subspace-level objective with structured subspace manipulation, yielding a more effective and scalable erasure. Extensive experiments on single- and multi-concept erasure demonstrate that OCE outperforms existing methods in concept erasure and non-target preservation, erasing up to 100 concepts in 4.3 s. Code: https://github.com/HansSunY/OCE.

</details>


### [135] [VFEAgent: A Multimodal Agent Framework for End-to-End Automated Finite Element Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28978)
*Jiachen Zhang,Junyi Lao,Chenghao Liu,Siyuan Liu,Shixin Wu,Linsen Zhang,Boyu Wang,Songfang Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VFEAgent 是一款能够从图像和文本输入自动完成 FEA 仿真建模的多智能体系统，通过融合视觉推理与验证优先的代码生成技术，实现了高效且可靠的工程分析自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有限元分析（FEA）流程复杂且依赖专家经验；现有结合大语言模型（LLM）的方法在处理多模态输入及复杂任务执行方面存在局限，难以实现全流程自动化。

Method: 提出 VFEAgent 多智能体系统，包含两大核心模块：1. 基于 ReAct 推理机制的多模态视听语言管道，负责从图像和描述中提取结构化 FEA 参数；2. 验证优先的代码合成框架，集成自调试与故障恢复机制，确保仿真代码的物理有效性和可执行性。

Result: 在多种工程力学场景的测试中，VFEAgent 在仿真建模的成功率、物理有效性及结果可靠性方面均优于现有 LLM 基准方法。

Conclusion: VFEAgent成功验证了自动化有限元分析（FEA）工作流的可行性，通过多智能体协作与代码自校验机制，显著提升了工程仿真的可靠性与正确性，有效减轻了工程师的繁重工作。

Abstract: Finite Element Analysis (FEA) serves as the cornerstone of modern engineering design. However, its workflow is inherently complex and relies heavily on domain expertise. Although recent efforts have integrated Large Language Models (LLMs) into FEA, existing approaches face limitations in handling multimodal inputs and executing complex tasks. To address these limitations, we propose VFEAgent, an end-to-end multi-agent system designed to automate FEA modeling and simulation directly from input images and problem descriptions. Our methodology integrates two core components: (1) a multimodal vision-language multi-agent pipeline that employs ReAct-driven reasoning to extract structured FEA specifications from heterogeneous inputs and (2) a verification-first code synthesis framework, incorporating robust self-debugging and fallback mechanisms to ensure executability and physical validity. We systematically evaluated the system across various engineering mechanics scenarios. The results demonstrate that VFEAgent achieves a high success rate in generating complete and physically valid simulations, outperforming LLM-based baseline methods in reliability and correctness. These findings validate the feasibility of automating the complete FEA workflow, highlighting the framework's potential to liberate engineers from tedious manual analysis.

</details>


### [136] [BEAMS: Benchmarking and Evaluating AI for Modeling and Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28994)
*Sara Metcalf,William Schoenberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BEAMS倡议旨在定义AI辅助建模与仿真的评估基准，通过“sd ai”开源架构对各类模型进行测试，研究发现目前的AI在定性辅助与讨论上已具备一定能力，但在严谨的因果推理与定量纠错方面仍有提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI工具若要支持现实世界的决策，必须具备构建解释性仿真模型的能力，且应作为人类专家的辅助而非替代品。当前缺乏针对“以人为本”的建模与仿真实践的评估基准，迫切需要建立相应的标准以确保AI工具的负责任和伦理性。

Method: 建立BEAMS倡议，构建开放的数字化与组织基础设施，通过由指导组（优先级设定）和技术组（自动化测试实现）组成的架构，对AI建模与仿真工具进行评估。评估类别涵盖定性与定量建模及模型讨论，通过将sd ai项目引擎与不同大语言模型（LLM）结合，进行多维度的基准测试。

Result: 通过对不同LLM组合的评估发现，AI辅助建模工具在定性任务及模型讨论上表现较好，但在因果推理、定量错误修复等核心任务上仍面临挑战；不同基准工具之间表现出明显的性能差异，不存在单一的“最佳LLM”。

Conclusion: 当前的AI建模工具在辅助讨论和基础定性任务上表现良好，但在因果推理和定量纠错方面仍存在不足。没有单一的LLM在所有任务中均占优势，强调了针对特定任务进行评估及权衡速度与准确性的必要性。BEAMS倡议将持续通过引入考虑偏见与多样化视角的人机中心基准，推动建模工具的负责任发展。

Abstract: AI tools to support real world decision making must be able to build simulation models that inform their recommendations and render them interpretable. Tools that can automate aspects of modeling practice must complement human expertise, not replace it. The BEAMS Initiative aims to guide the development of AI tools for modeling and simulation toward forms that are responsible and ethical by establishing benchmarks for human centered modeling and simulation practices. The initiative uses open digital and organizational infrastructure to collaboratively evaluate AI tools for modeling and simulation. The open source sd ai project hosted by the initiative establishes transparency and enables contributions to be shared broadly. A steering group focuses on prioritizing potential benchmarks, while a technical group focuses on implementing the benchmarks in the form of automated tests. Tests for several distinct categories of evaluation have been implemented and applied to AI tools that support qualitative model building, quantitative model building, and model discussion. These include tests for causal translation, model iteration, causal reasoning, conformance, model behavior explanation, suggested model building steps, and suggested model fixes. When engines from the sd ai project are coupled with different LLMs, their performance on these evaluations reveals variability across different AI tools. The evaluations implemented by the initiative demonstrate that AI enabled modeling tools perform better at discussion and basic qualitative tasks than with causal reasoning and quantitative error fixing. No single LLM dominates across engine types, highlighting the importance of specific tasks and tradeoffs between speed and accuracy. Ongoing efforts of the initiative aim to incorporate benchmarks that address concerns about bias by considering alternative perspectives and human centered use cases.

</details>


### [137] [Adopt $\neq$ Adapt: Longitudinal Analyses of LLM Conversations in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29018)
*Rebecca M. M. Hicke,Kiran Tomlinson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文通过分析1.2万名Bing Copilot用户的长期交互数据，发现用户使用LLM的习惯高度稳定且难以改变；此外，研究指出WildChat等常用数据集存在高级用户比例过高的问题，无法代表典型用户的人机交互行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于用户与大语言模型（LLM）交互的研究大多是静态的，缺乏关于个体用户随时间推移如何演变其行为的纵向研究，且现有的大规模公开数据集（如WildChat）的代表性有待商榷。

Method: 对约1.2万名微软Bing Copilot用户的对话轨迹进行纵向分析，并将其与WildChat-4.8M数据集进行对比研究，通过分析不同活跃度用户的行为模式和演变轨迹评估用户习性的稳定性。

Result: 发现个体用户行为轨迹的演变趋势较弱（用户习惯粘性强）；高活跃度用户在任务复杂度和任务成功率上表现更优；WildChat数据集存在明显的“高级/专业用户”偏差。

Conclusion: 用户行为具有极高的惯性，难以改变，且用户群体表现出显著的异质性。WildChat数据集由于偏向高级用户，不能代表典型的人机交互场景，研究者在使用此类公开数据时需谨慎。

Abstract: Although a growing body of research has begun to describe user--LLM interactions, the picture it paints is largely static; little is known about how individual users change their behavior over time. To address this gap, we analyze the conversational trajectories of $\sim$12,000 randomly sampled Microsoft Bing Copilot users and compare these with data from WildChat-4.8M. While the Copilot data contains significant population-level trends, we find that trends in individual user trajectories are much weaker; user habits prove to be overwhelmingly sticky. We also find stark differences between users of different activity levels: more active users have more successful conversations and use the LLM for more complex and professionally oriented tasks. Some user trends also appear in WildChat-4.8M, but we find evidence that this dataset is significantly skewed towards highly proficient "power" users. Ultimately, our results suggest that existing user behavior is difficult to change and demonstrate the extent of user heterogeneity. Our comparison between datasets highlights that WildChat does not represent typical user-AI interactions, an important caveat for downstream uses of the data.

</details>


### [138] [When Models Disagree: Rethinking LLM Evaluation for Public Comment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29025)
*Aisha Najera,Alvin Moon,Vedant Srinivasan,Rajesh Veeraraghavan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM辅助政策分析中的“解释性偏差”问题，提出了一种利用多模型分歧来筛选复杂文本并辅助人类审核的审计管线，强调分歧分析比单纯准确率更能反映模型在语义理解上的本质差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦机构在使用LLM处理公众评论时，模型分类方式直接影响政策制定者对民意的感知。目前的评价体系过度依赖针对小样本的“立场准确性”，无法检测不同模型在处理相同输入时可能产生的实质性解释差异，进而导致潜在的决策偏差。

Method: 提出了一种“解释性审计管线”（Interpretive Audit Pipeline），将模型间的不一致视为解释复杂性的诊断指标，并据此引导人类进行针对性的复核。研究对比了4种大型语言模型在处理USDA 1,260条公众评论时的差异，并通过两阶段标注分析研究了模型与人类在修订过程中的行为差异。

Result: 研究发现模型的专题分类分歧（inter-model thematic divergence）大于单一模型的提示词变异；专家准则虽抑制了深层分歧，但并未真正解决问题；在复核阶段，人类标注者引入了模型集合中不存在的独特框架，证明了模型分类的局限性。

Conclusion: 基于模型间的分歧进行评估，是准确性指标在LLM辅助解释性编码任务中的必要补充。单纯的定量准确性评价无法捕捉模型在处理复杂文本时的解释性偏差，需引入分歧检测以辅助人类决策。

Abstract: Federal agencies are deploying large language models (LLMs) to categorize public comment corpora, where the model's organization of the record shapes what policymakers see and which arguments register. Standard evaluation, anchored on stance accuracy against a small validated set, cannot detect when different models produce materially different categorizations of the same public input. We propose an Interpretive Audit Pipeline that treats multi-model disagreement as diagnostic of interpretive complexity and directs human review toward genuinely ambiguous public input. Analyzing 1,260 public comments on a federal USDA docket across four LLMs, we find that inter-model thematic divergence exceeds within-model prompt variation, and that an expert rubric suppresses deep interpretive disagreement without resolving it. In a two-stage labeling study on a stratified 40-comment subsample, four LLMs and a human annotator labeled independently and then revised after seeing the others' labels. Revision behavior varied across labelers, and the human annotator's revisions frequently introduced framings absent from the ensemble's collective output. We argue disagreement-based evaluation is a necessary complement to accuracy metrics for LLM-assisted interpretive coding.

</details>


### [139] [Mind Your Tone: Does Tone Alter LLM Performance?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29027)
*Om Dobariya,Akhil Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究发现大模型对客观题的回答准确率会随提示词语气的改变而产生显著且系统性的差异，这种敏感性因模型和学科而异。结论提醒用户在实际应用中，不能默认模型对语气具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型使用广泛，但其性能受提示词风格和语气的影响很大，目前缺乏关于提示词语气如何导致客观多选题准确率差异的系统性研究。

Method: 使用两个数据集（50题自定义数据集和570题MMLU子集），评估了四种LLM（ChatGPT-4o, ChatGPT-5-nano, Gemini 2.5 Flash, Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite）在不同提示词语气下的表现，并引入了路由框架来解释语气如何影响内部推理模式。

Result: 不同模型受语气影响的程度差异巨大，部分模型表现稳定，部分则出现显著的准确率波动；此外，模型对语气的敏感性存在学科间差异。

Conclusion: 提示词的语气对大语言模型的性能具有系统性且高度依赖模型的影响。用户在部署LLM应用时，不应假设模型对语气变化具有鲁棒性，需警惕语气带来的可靠性风险。

Abstract: The use of Large Language Models (LLMs) is proliferating, yet their performance is observed to vary based on prompting styles and tones. In this study, we investigate both whether and how tonal variations in prompts lead to disparate LLM accuracy for objective multiple-choice questions. We use two datasets: a 50-base question dataset with five tone variants and a 570-base question MMLU subset spanning 57 subjects with seven tone variants. Experiments were conducted to evaluate the performance of four cost-efficient, popular LLMs: ChatGPT-4o, ChatGPT-5-nano, Gemini 2.5 Flash, and Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite. Across models, tonal effects are systematic but highly model-dependent. Some models show small, yet statistically significant, shifts, while others exhibit large accuracy swings across tones. Further, we identify subject-level differences in tone sensitivity and present a routing framework to explain how tones may attune internal reasoning modes. Our findings caution users against assuming tone-robust reliability in LLM deployments.

</details>


### [140] [Practitioner Beliefs and Behaviors in AI-Enhanced Education: DOT Framework Survey Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29041)
*David Gibson,M. Elizabeth Azukas,Gerald Knezek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过对72名高校教师的调查，发现尽管教师认可AI的教学辅助作用并坚持人工监管，但目前AI应用多局限于生成内容，缺乏深层的设计思维闭环，且受到体制性资源匮乏的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在深入探究高等教育从业者在AI整合教学与学习过程中的信念、行为及机构支持情况，解决从理论框架到实际应用转化的认知偏差。

Method: 采用横断面调查法（n=72），基于DOT框架（整合设计思维与开放系统理论）；利用探索性因子分析（EFA）分析19项信念条目，识别出三个因子结构（AI功能能力、监管与治理、教师协作与规划）。

Result: 从业者对AI作为教学支持工具持积极态度，但强调必须保留人工监管；实际操作多集中于提示工程（prompting）与内容生成，缺乏系统性的需求评估与反馈闭环；普遍面临政策、培训及基础设施等体制性障碍。

Conclusion: DOT框架为描述高等教育领域AI应用者的信念与实践提供了初步经验支持，但当前实践与设计导向的理论之间仍存在明显差距，未来需加强验证性研究及AI应用对教学质量影响的实证分析。

Abstract: This study reports findings from a cross-sectional survey (n = 72) of higher education practitioners examining beliefs, behaviors, and institutional conditions related to artificial intelligence (AI) integration in teaching and learning. Grounded in the DOT Framework, which integrates design thinking and open systems theory, the study investigates AI familiarity, usage patterns, design-oriented practices, and pedagogical beliefs. Exploratory factor analysis of 19 belief items identified a three-factor structure: AI Functional Capabilities, Oversight and Governance, and Instructor Collaboration and Planning (α = .90). Results indicate that practitioners hold favorable views of AI as a pedagogical support while maintaining strong commitments to human oversight and critical evaluation. Reported practices emphasize iterative prompting and content generation, with less consistent use of needs assessment and feedback loops. Institutional barriers including limited policy, training, and infrastructure were widely reported. These findings provide preliminary empirical support for the DOT Framework as a descriptive model of practitioner beliefs and practices, while also highlighting gaps between design-oriented theory and current implementation. The study contributes an initial measurement structure and identifies directions for confirmatory validation and outcome-based research linking AI-supported design practices to instructional quality.

</details>


### [141] [Differentiable Belief-based Opponent Shaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29042)
*Aarav G Sane,Karthik Sivachandran,Rohan Paleja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了D-BOS（可微信念对手塑形）算法，通过直接对对手的信念更新过程进行微分来引导对手行为，而非简单的欺骗，实现了在复杂多智能体博弈中更有效的策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对手塑形方法主要局限于对手的参数、策略或价值空间，而隐秘角色博弈中的信念操纵技术往往依赖于硬编码（如欺骗或信念饱和）的目标，缺乏灵活性且难以适应各种环境。因此，需要一种更通用的能够自然产生协调行为的方法。

Method: 提出Differentiable Belief-based Opponent Shaping (D-BOS)方法，这是一种一阶方法，将观察者的信念视为被塑形的对手状态，并推导通过k步softmax-Bayes信念动态的梯度。该方法不显式奖励欺骗或合作，而是通过对对手信念更新过程进行微分，从而提供对手塑形信号，并支持通过聚合多个观察者的梯度来处理多观察者场景。

Result: 在多个隐秘角色博弈实验中，D-BOS表现优于传统的PPO和BBM算法，特别是在混合动机设置中取得了最显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: D-BOS是一种通过将信念状态作为塑形目标的创新型对手塑形方法，在隐秘角色博弈中展现出优越的性能，并能在混合动机环境中自然产生复杂的策略。

Abstract: Human coordination often relies on the ability to influence the beliefs of others through strategic action. In multi-agent reinforcement learning, opponent shaping attempts to replicate this influence, though existing methods typically operate within an opponent's parameter, policy, or value space. Meanwhile, belief-manipulation techniques in hidden-role games often rely on hard-coded objectives, such as deception or belief saturation. We propose Differentiable Belief-based Opponent Shaping (D-BOS), a first-order method that treats each observer's belief as the shaped opponent state and differentiates through $k$-step softmax-Bayes belief dynamics. Rather than explicitly rewarding deceptive or cooperative behavior, our method treats the belief state as the target for shaping. This allows the optimal strategy to emerge naturally from the environment's reward structure. This belief-space formulation provides an opponent-shaping signal by differentiating through opponent belief updates, and naturally extends to multiple observers by aggregating gradients over their individual inferred belief trajectories. Empirically, D-BOS outperforms PPO and BBM in hidden-role games, with the largest gains in mixed-motive settings.

</details>


### [142] [Hallucination Mitigation with Agentic AI, Nested Learning, and AI Sustainability via Semantic Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29055)
*Diego Gosmar,Deborah A. Dahl*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合嵌套学习、连续记忆与多智能体审核的架构，通过引入新的幻觉评估指标体系（THS），在不重训模型的前提下，显著降低了生产级LLM的幻觉，并同时实现了运行效率优化与高可观测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 幻觉是生产级LLM系统的主要可靠性障碍，特别是在多智能体流水线中容易发生错误传播，需要一种能够在不进行模型重训练的前提下，同时平衡幻觉缓解、运行效率与可观测性的方案。

Method: 采用HOPE架构的嵌套学习，结合连续记忆系统（CMS）与语义相似度缓存；设计三阶段智能体流水线（通过开放楼层协议OFP协调），利用高随机性生成器（FrontEndAgent）与两个审核智能体进行逐步修正；使用五个KPI（FCD, FGR, FDF, ECS, OSR）聚合为总幻觉分数（THS）进行评估。

Result: 端到端THS降低了31.3%至35.9%；语义缓存实现了47.3%的命中率（减少了LLM调用次数），显著降低了能源消耗；ExtremeObservability配置表现最优（THS为-0.0709），证明可观测性与幻觉缓解呈正相关。

Conclusion: 基于记忆增强的嵌套学习与多智能体审核流水线（Nested Learning with multi-agent pipeline）能有效缓解生产级LLM的幻觉问题，同时提升事实可靠性、运行效率与可审计性，且无需模型重新训练。

Abstract: Hallucination remains a major reliability barrier for production LLM systems, particularly in multi-agent pipelines where unsupported claims can propagate unchecked across stages. This paper adapts a HOPE-inspired Nested Learning architecture with Continuum Memory Systems (CMS) and semantic similarity caching to a hybrid benchmark of 310 prompts combining 217 epistemic-uncertainty prompts and 93 fabrication-induction stress-test prompts. A three-stage agentic pipeline orchestrated via the Open Floor Protocol (OFP) is evaluated with five KPIs -- FCD (Factual Claim Density), FGR (Factual Grounding References), FDF (Fictional Disclaimer Frequency), ECS (Explicit Contextualization Score), and OSR (Observability Score Ratio) -- aggregated into THS (Total Hallucination Score) across five weighting configurations to study mitigation-observability trade-offs. FDF, ECS, OSR, and FGR are subtracted as mitigation signals, so that a more negative THS indicates stronger mitigation. The FrontEndAgent is configured as a high-stochasticity generator (temperature = 1.0) to produce a realistic hallucination baseline, while the SecondLevelReviewer and ThirdLevelReviewer operate as progressive correctors. This asymmetric design yields end-to-end THS reductions of -31.3% to -35.9% across five weighting configurations. Semantic caching achieves 440 cache hits over 930 potential calls (47.3% hit rate), reducing LLM invocations to 490, lowering energy and CO2e footprint, and making multi-stage review pipelines operationally viable at production scale. ExtremeObservability attains the most negative final THS (-0.0709), confirming that observability-heavy configurations reinforce rather than compromise mitigation. These findings suggest that memory-augmented multi-agent designs can jointly improve factual reliability, operational efficiency, and auditability without model retraining.

</details>


### [143] [Robust and Efficient Guardrails with Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29068)
*Siddharth Sai,Xiaofei Wen,Muhao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: COLAGUARD 通过将多步安全推理映射到潜在空间，在保持高性能安全审核的同时，极大地提高了推理速度并降低了计算成本，解决了现有推理护栏在生产环境中的实用性瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于推理的安全护栏虽然效果优于单纯的分类器，但因需要生成推理过程导致查询延迟高、令牌开销大，无法满足高吞吐场景的需求。

Method: 提出 COLAGUARD，一种通过分阶段训练课程将多步安全推理转换到连续潜在空间（continuous latent space）的护栏模型，在推理过程中直接传播隐状态（hidden-state propagation），免去了显式文本生成的耗时过程。

Result: 在十个安全审核设置（八个基准测试）中，COLAGUARD 相比 Llama Guard 3 的 Macro-F1 提升了 8.24 点，与显式推理基线 GuardReasoner 效果相当，同时实现了 12.9 倍的速度提升和 22.4 倍的令牌开销降低。

Conclusion: 潜在空间推理（Latent reasoning）是开发可部署安全护栏的有效替代方案。它成功克服了显式推理在延迟和令牌开销上的弊端，证明了安全鲁棒性与推理效率可以实现双赢，而非此消彼长。

Abstract: Maintaining the safety of large language models (LLMs) is crucial as they are increasingly deployed in real-world applications. Existing safety guardrails typically rely on single-pass classification or, more recently, distilled reasoning. Reasoning-based guardrails significantly outperform classification-only baselines, but they incur substantial query latency and token overhead that make them impractical for highthroughput deployment. To address this challenge, we propose COLAGUARD, a guardrail model that transfers multi-step safety reasoning into a continuous latent space through a stage-wise training curriculum, enabling direct hidden-state propagation at inference. Evaluated on ten prompt- and response-moderation settings spanning eight safety benchmarks, COLAGUARD improves macro-F1 by 8.24 points over Llama Guard 3 and matches our explicit reasoning baseline, GuardReasoner, in macroF1 while delivering a 12.9X speedup and 22.4X reduction in token usage. Our results suggest that latent reasoning offers a practical alternative to explicit rationale generation for deployable guardrails, jointly improving safety robustness and inference efficiency rather than treating them as competing objectives.

</details>


### [144] [Rethinking Literature Search Evaluation: Deep Research Helps, and Human Citation Lists Are Not a Ground Truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29234)
*Gaurav Sahu,Laurent Charlin,Christopher Pal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高性能的Deep Research检索流水线，并揭示了传统文献引用基准中人类偏见（倾向于引用合作者）的问题，主张通过多维度指标共同评估文献搜索质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文献搜索评估标准（特别是依赖人类参考列表作为基准）存在局限性，且学术检索效果仍有大幅提升空间。

Method: 1. 构建Deep Research流水线：通过处理完整查询论文并沿着参考书目进行广度优先搜索来扩展检索结果；2. 评估基准：使用RollingEval-Jun25基准测试；3. LLM评估与分析：利用中立的LLM-as-a-judge评估人工参考文献的合理性，并结合OpenAlex共作者图表分析人类引用偏见。

Result: 1. 基于Deep Research的流水线将召回率从20%提升至80%以上；2. 人工引用存在高度偏见，仅51%被判定为中度相关以上，而AI排名系统达到86-88%；3. 人类引用其直接合作者的概率是AI排名系统的2.5倍。

Conclusion: 文献搜索的评估不应仅依赖单一指标（如召回率），应将召回率、主题相关性、排序列表多样性及共作者距离诊断等指标联合使用，以更全面地衡量文献引用的质量。

Abstract: We study large-scale literature search from two complementary angles: improving the retrieval pipeline, and stress-testing the human reference list as an evaluation target. First, we implement a Deep Research pipeline that processes the full query paper and expands the retrieved results breadth-first along their bibliographies, and show that it substantially outperforms vanilla API-only search, raising recall on RollingEval-Jun25 (a 250-paper literature-search benchmark) from below 20% to above 80%. Second, we use a neutral LLM-as-a-judge to determine if human references are sound ground truth for the task. We find significant limitations: only 51% of human citations are judged moderately relevant or higher, against 86--88% for the strongest AI-based re-rankers. We study this gap on the OpenAlex co-authorship graph, finding that humans are 2.5x more likely than the best AI re-rankers to cite a direct collaborator. Together, our results argue against single-axis literature-search evaluation: recall, topical-relevance scoring, ranked-list diversity, and a co-authorship-distance diagnostic each measure complementary properties of citation quality and should be reported jointly.

</details>


### [145] [Bridging the Sim-to-Real Gap in Reinforcement Learning-Based Industrial Dispatching through Execution Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29078)
*Jonathan Hoss,Noah Klarmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对工业事件驱动调度中的执行不确定性问题，提出了一种中间执行与测量层，通过标准化契约和错误归因机制，实现了策略意图与执行结果的解耦，显著提升了系统的可观测性与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业环境中的事件驱动调度面临异步观测、状态非时间一致性、动作容许度不明以及执行错误来源模糊等问题，限制了系统的稳定性和可解释性。

Method: 提出一个策略中立（policy-neutral）的执行与测量层，通过构建决策有效快照、定义标准化执行契约、记录意图与实际执行之间的差异（divergences）来处理异步事件流，实现决策语义与执行行为的分离。

Result: 在离散事件模拟中，该框架将原本难以区分的执行失败转化为结构化、可归因的类型化结果，并在低观测延迟环境下有效降低了提交前的执行错误。

Conclusion: 该中间层成功将执行层面的不确定性转化为监督数据，不仅提升了策略的可靠性与可解释性，还为后续的策略优化提供了闭环反馈机制。

Abstract: Event-driven scheduling policies are increasingly deployed in industrial environments, where decisions are made under asynchronous and partially observed system states. As a result, decision states are not temporally consistent, action admissibility is not explicitly defined, and the origin of execution errors remains ambiguous. These issues limit both reliability and interpretability.
  To address this gap, a policy-neutral execution and measurement layer is proposed to mediate between scheduling policies and the industrial execution environment. The layer constructs decision-valid snapshots from asynchronous event streams, defines a standardized execution contract with explicit action admissibility, and records outcomes as divergences between policy intent, transactional outcomes, physical execution, and human intervention. This enables a separation between decision semantics and execution behavior and makes deployment mismatch observable and structurally attributable.
  The proposed framework is evaluated using a discrete-event simulation. The results show analytical benefits across all observation lag regimes, as undifferentiated execution failures are transformed into structured, typed outcomes with full attribution coverage. Operational benefits are strongest under low observation lag, where avoidable execution errors can be prevented before commitment. Overall, the layer turns execution uncertainty into supervisory data for evaluation and policy refinement.

</details>


### [146] [Surfacing Isolated Learners with Outcome-Independent Mediation of Feedback between Teachers and Students Using AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29240)
*Junsoo Park,Youssef Medhat,Htet Phyo Wai,Ploy Thajchayapong,Ashok K. Goel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需依赖成绩标签的可解释AI决策层，通过综合学生行为反馈和教师关切，准确识别并优先处理课程教学中的难点，促进师生与AI的协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助课堂产生了大量的过程性反馈（在成绩公布前），但教师难以将这些信号转化为及时的教学决策，缺乏有效的工具来处理这些未完成的反馈信息。

Method: 提出了一种可解释的AI决策层机制。该机制通过整合三个信号对课程主题进行优先级排序：(1) 学生学习困难的普遍性；(2) 学生自我报告与观察到的困难之间的分歧；(3) 未解决的教师疑虑。系统输出包含优先级排序及解释性决策记录，无需依赖成绩或事后结果标签。

Result: 在一门计算机科学研究生课程的验证中：(1) 排序结果与教师关切高度一致（Top-5重合度3/5，Spearman相关系数ρ=0.80）；(2) 与学生报告的难度相关（ρ=0.46）；(3) 多信号整合显著提升了对学习困难学生的识别能力（AUC从0.91提升至0.96）。此外，研究证实学生的行为信号（反思、求助、自我效能感）与学习构建高度相关。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，在反馈不完整的情况下，透明的协调机制（如提出的决策层）有助于支持人类与AI的协作（co-agency），通过整合多种信号可有效弥补单一信号源的不足。

Abstract: AI-augmented classrooms generate rich teacher and student feedback before graded outcomes become available, yet these signals can be difficult to translate into timely instructional decisions. We propose an interpretable decision layer: a transparent mechanism that ranks course topics requiring attention without using grades or post-hoc outcome labels. The approach combines three signals: student learning difficulty prevalence, disagreement between learner self-reports and observed difficulties, and unresolved teacher concerns. The output is a ranked set of topic priorities with per-topic decision records explaining each ranking. In one graduate CS course offering ($n=5$ instructor interviews; $n=279$ survey responses), prioritized topics aligned with instructor concerns (top-5 overlap 3/5; Spearman $ρ=0.80$) and student-reported topic difficulty ($ρ=0.46$, $p=.048$). Multi-signal integration also surfaced learners not identified through individual signal sources alone (AUC $=0.96$ vs. $0.91$ for gap prevalence alone). Reflective thinking, help-seeking, and self-efficacy provided additional evidence that student behavioral signals align with learning-related constructs. While preliminary, these findings suggest that transparent coordination mechanisms may help support human-AI co-agency when feedback is incomplete.

</details>


### [147] [The Importance of Out-of-Band Metadata for Safe Autonomous Agents: The Redpanda Agentic Data Plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29082)
*Tyler Akidau,Tyler Rockwood,Johannes Brüderl,Marc Millstone*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对 AI 智能体可能绕过安全协议或出现不可控行为的问题，本文提出了 Redpanda ADP 架构，通过创建一个独立的“带外数据平面”通道，在不依赖智能体本身可靠性的前提下，对其数据访问和动作执行进行强制性监控与治理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 智能体在企业环境中执行任务时具有不可预测性（幻觉、误解、对抗性操纵）且具备高权限，直接让智能体处理安全关键元数据极其危险，因此需要一种独立于智能体逻辑的外部强制安全机制。

Method: 提出了一种“带外智能体数据平面”（Agentic Data Plane, ADP）架构，通过独立于智能体读写路径的带外通道传输安全上下文、策略信号和审计跟踪，从而在智能体生命周期的各个阶段强制执行治理约束。

Result: 通过多智能体投资组合再平衡系统的实验证明，ADP 能够成功在物理隔离的客户端账户上实施数据范围限制、交易审批阈值及防篡改审计，且这些控制手段智能体无法观测或绕过。

Conclusion: Redpanda ADP 提供了一种在不依赖智能体本身诚信的情况下，通过带外（out-of-band）机制强制执行安全治理和合规控制的有效架构，增强了智能体在企业环境中的可控性与安全性。
