It features event-centric memory extraction to selectively handle complex information streams, while entities are dynamically updated by events to achieve stateful evolution. Using temporal compression via a topic-wise timeline and time-weighted recall, the system progressively produces high-level summary memories, prioritizes recent items, and compresses and fades older ones. Extensive evaluations on long-term memory benchmarks demonstrate that VikingMem outperformes baselines by up to 30% in memory retrieval effectiveness while maintaining the low latency essential for interactive applications.

</details>


### [183] [LLM-Evolved Domain-Independent Heuristics for Symbolic AI Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29649)
*Elliot Gestrin,Jendrik Seipp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种结合LLM与进化搜索的方法，成功生成了首个在通用规划任务中超越手工设计基准的“领域无关启发式函数”，并实现了直接在现有规划器中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的规划领域启发式算法多依赖人工设计，且尽管LLM已能为特定领域设计启发式函数，但尚未出现能处理通用规划任务的LLM生成启发式算法，研究旨在探索能否自动化生成超越人类专家设计水平的领域无关启发式算法。

Method: 1. 利用LLM对C++启发式函数代码进行变异生成新候选方案；2. 使用MAP-Elites算法维护和筛选启发式函数，以“信息量（informedness）”和“搜索速度”作为指标；3. 通过计算覆盖率（coverage）和求解时间的加权得分作为适应度函数（fitness）；4. 对比进化算法生成的启发式函数与手工设计的基准算法，并分析其帕累托前沿表现。

Result: 进化生成的最佳启发式函数在未见过的测试领域中解决了比最强基准算法更多的任务；研究还发现，从零（盲启发式算法）开始进行进化优于基于FF启发式算法进行微调，并指出LLM的推理努力程度主要影响候选代码的编译成功率，而非其执行质量。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了利用进化搜索引导大语言模型（LLM）生成领域无关启发式函数的有效性。生成的启发式函数在未见过的规划任务中优于现有的手工启发式算法，且由于其生成的C++代码结构，可直接集成到现有规划器中，兼具性能与实用性。

Abstract: Heuristic search is the dominant paradigm in symbolic AI planning, and the strongest heuristics are the result of decades of work by planning researchers. Recent work has shown that large language models (LLMs) can design heuristics for individual planning domains, but no LLM-generated heuristic has so far worked on arbitrary planning tasks. In this paper, we use evolutionary search to produce the first LLM-generated domain-independent heuristics that exceed the hand-engineered state of the art. We let an LLM mutate parent heuristics written in C++, store candidates in a MAP-Elites archive keyed on informedness and speed and calculate fitness scores by blending coverage with solving time. To place the evolved programs in context, we additionally benchmark a broad set of hand-engineered heuristics on their informedness-speed tradeoff, which to our knowledge has not been done before. On unseen testing domains, our best evolved heuristic solves more tasks than even the strongest baseline, with our full heuristic suite spanning the Pareto frontier of said tradeoff. We also find that seeding evolution from the trivial blind heuristic outperforms seeding from the strong FF heuristic, even when the resulting program is itself an FF variant, and that LLM reasoning effort affects how often candidates compile much more than the quality of those that do. Because the evolved programs are plain C++, they slot into existing planners as drop-in replacements and inherit the soundness and completeness guarantees of the underlying search.

</details>


### [184] [Think Fast, Talk Smart: Partitioning Deterministic and Neural Computation for Structured Health Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29652)
*Kai-Chen Cheng,Haejun Han,David Q. Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对健康文本生成，提出“代码分析+LLM生成”的流水线架构，证明了确定性计算在数值精度和合规性上优于单纯的提示工程（Prompting）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在生成健康类文本时，仅依赖提示词往往无法解决数据保真度、证据归因、策略合规、机器可读性及高频应用成本等问题。

Method: 提出“Think Fast, Talk Smart”流水线：将定期分析交给确定性代码处理，仅在最终步骤调用一次受限的LLM进行文本生成；通过层级替换实验剖析了LLM在数值计算、排序、因果归因等任务中的失败点。

Result: 在280个用户夜的测试中，该方法相比零样本和少样本基线，显著降低了数值错误率、指令违规率及计算成本。明确了将核心业务逻辑交由代码处理的性能优势。

Conclusion: 在开发结构化健康数据生成系统时，应采取“代码处理重复分析、LLM仅负责在有界接口内表述经验证事实”的系统设计原则。将确定性计算与生成任务解耦，能有效降低成本并提高准确性与合规性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly being used to generate health text from structured records such as wearable time series, biomarkers, vitals, and care-management logs. For recurring health outputs, fluency is not enough: systems must remain faithful to source data, ground explanatory claims in available evidence, follow stated policies, emit machine-readable outputs, and run cheaply enough for repeated use. We ask which responsibilities in structured health generation should be deterministic computation rather than runtime LLM prompting.
  We introduce Think Fast, Talk Smart, a sleep-health insight pipeline in which deterministic code performs recurring analysis before one bounded LLM writer call. Across 280 user-nights and six models, achieves lower numeric error, lower instruction-compliance error, and lower end-to-end cost than structured zero-shot and few-shot one-call baselines.
  Layer replacement reveals contract-specific failures: LLM comparison raises numeric error, LLM ranking degrades policy selection, LLM attribution increases unsupported causal language, and an LLM-generated writer interface reintroduces errors even after upstream facts are deterministic. The results support a broader design rule: let code own recurring analysis, and let LLMs express verified facts within bounded interfaces.

</details>


### [185] [TRACE: Toulmin-based Reasoning Assessment through Constructive Elements for LLM CoT Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29656)
*Yundong Kim,Heyoung Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了 TRACE 指标，通过结合论证理论与元认知框架评估 LLM 的思维链（CoT）逻辑结构，解决了现有评估方法忽视推理过程的局限性，并证实了该指标在提升模型推理质量方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估 LLM 的方法多依赖最终答案准确率或表面统计特征，缺乏对推理过程本身的评估。

Method: 结合图尔敏论证理论（Toulmin's argumentation theory）与弗拉维尔元认知框架（Flavell's metacognitive framework），构建了 TRACE 指标，用于分析思维链（CoT）推理过程的结构。

Result: 在 26.3K 个问答样本上的实验表明，TRACE 与基准准确率有强相关性（r=0.74），且作为强化学习奖励信号时，表现优于仅依据准确率的基准方法。

Conclusion: TRACE 提供了一种评估 LLM 推理过程的新视角，证明了评估逻辑构建过程不仅能与最终结果高度相关，还能作为有效的强化学习奖励机制来提升模型质量。

Abstract: Evaluating open-ended outputs from large language models (LLMs) remains challenging due to the absence of ground truth. Existing metrics rely on final-answer accuracy or surface-level statistics, leaving the reasoning process itself unexamined. We introduce TRACE (Toulmin-based Reasoning Assessment through Constructive Elements), a metric that analyzes Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning processes. Rather than judging outcomes, TRACE inspects how arguments are constructed by integrating Toulmin's argumentation theory with Flavell's metacognitive framework to assess reasoning structure. Experiments on 26.3K QA samples across 7 reasoning models show strong correlation with benchmark accuracy (r=0.74). Furthermore, TRACE is effective as a reinforcement learning reward signal, outperforming accuracy-only baselines. Together, these results indicate that logically sound reasoning leads to higher-quality answers. TRACE thus serves as a complementary metric for evaluating open-ended outputs. Code is available at https://github.com/hyyangkisti/trace.

</details>


### [186] [GRASP: Gated Regression-Aware Skill Proposer for Self-Improving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29668)
*Johannes Moll,Jean-Philippe Corbeil,Jiazhen Pan,Martin Hadamitzky,Daniel Rueckert,Lisa Adams,Keno Bressem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对 LLM 智能体在结构化任务中自我改进导致的性能倒退问题，GRASP 通过引入“门控回归感知”机制，在严格的验证与约束下更新技能库，从而显著提升了智能体的执行鲁棒性和任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型智能体在结构化环境中常因处理程序化任务时的错误（操作非对话层面的失误）而失败；现有的自我改进方法缺乏对“回归”的控制，即引入新知识往往会破坏已有的正确行为。

Method: 提出 GRASP（门控回归感知技能提议者）框架。该方法将智能体改进视为对受限技能库的一系列编辑，只有在通过平衡的保留测试集且不违反严格回归预算的前提下，才接纳新候选技能。

Result: 在 MedAgentBench 上，GRASP 使 gpt-oss-120b 的成功率从 40.6% 提升至 88.8%，领先现有基准 21.0 个百分点；在多个基座模型上（包括 GPT-4.1, GPT-5.4 等）均实现 17.2-40.3 个百分点的显著提升；并验证了技能库跨模型的有效迁移性。

Conclusion: GRASP 有效地解决了 LLM 智能体在结构化环境中自我改进时存在的“回归”问题。通过严格的准入机制和回归预算，GRASP 能稳定地构建性能优越且可迁移的技能库。同时，研究揭示了技能迁移中存在的不对称性，即更强模型生成的技能对较弱模型具有显著的提升作用。

Abstract: LLM agents acting in structured environments fail in operational rather than conversational ways, and reliability depends on procedural knowledge of the environment. Prior self-improvement methods accumulate natural-language guidance without checking that each new item preserves previously correct behavior, so a note that fixes one trajectory can silently regress another. We introduce GRASP (Gated Regression-Aware Skill Proposer), which treats agent improvement as a sequence of edits to a bounded skill library, admitting each candidate only if it produces a net improvement on a balanced held-out probe under a hard regression budget. We evaluate GRASP across five base models (gpt-oss-120b, DeepSeek V4 Flash, Gemini 3.1 Flash Lite, GPT-4.1, GPT-5.4) on two FHIR-based clinical benchmarks. On MedAgentBench, GRASP lifts gpt-oss-120b from 40.6% to 88.8%, exceeds the strongest of five self-improvement baselines by 21.0 points, and improves every other base model by 17.2 to 40.3 points. Ablations attribute the gain to comparative proposal generation, the acceptance gate, and the hard regression budget rather than to skill writing itself, which without validation is no better than using no skills. The mechanism generalizes beyond the clinical domain, improving agents on three of four non-clinical environments and remaining flat only where the action space is open-ended. Frozen libraries transfer across models, where skills from a stronger model improve weaker executors beyond what they learn for themselves while the reverse does not, an asymmetry that no ungated baseline reproduces.

</details>


### [187] [NICE: A Theory-Grounded Diagnostic Benchmark for Social Intelligence of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29685)
*Yunjin Qi,Zhaojun Jiang,Xuan Wu,Hanxi Pan,Yixuan Wang,Yanfang Liu,Xiang Ji,Churu Yu,Chunyuan Zheng,Yingze Chen,Jie He,Liuqing Chen,Zaifeng Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个基于社会理论的 LLM 社会智能诊断框架 NICE，通过细粒度的维度划分，精准识别了大模型在处理复杂社交动态时存在的沟通短板，为提升 AI 的人机交互质量提供理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的社会智能评估基准缺乏统一的理论框架，难以对大语言模型（LLM）的社会表现进行精细化诊断，导致其在情感陪伴和客户服务等社会化场景中的表现力和安全性难以保障。

Method: 1. 构建框架：通过文献综述和心理测量学专家多阶段验证，确立包含 4 大类（规范、交互、认知、体验）及 11 个维度的社会智能框架。
2. 构造基准：基于框架开发 NICE 基准测试，包含 137 个具有中国社会文化背景的测试项。
3. 对比评估：在该基准上对 5 个前沿 LLM 与人类对照组进行测试与性能诊断。

Result: 实验表明，尽管 LLM 在宏观指标上得分较高，但在“沟通（Communication）”能力维度存在系统性缺陷，具体体现为多轮交互处理、非语言信息理解及社会同步性上的不足。

Conclusion: NICE 框架成功地将心理测量学原则与社会理论结合，为评估 LLM 的社会智能提供了一种细粒度、诊断性的方法。研究表明，虽然大模型在综合准确率上表现尚可，但在多轮交互、非语言沟通及同步性等特定维度上仍存在明显的“社会缺陷”。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied in social contexts such as emotional companionship and customer service, measuring their social intelligence has become critical to the quality and safety of human-AI interaction. However, existing social intelligence benchmarks lack a unified framework that organizes social abilities into a unified structure, and therefore cannot enable fine-grained diagnosis. To build the first holistic diagnostic evaluation grounded in social theory, we first construct a social intelligence framework through a literature review and multi-stage expert validation guided by psychometric principles. The resulting framework includes 4 categories and 11 dimensions, each further specified by fine-grained capability facets. Building on this framework, we introduce NICE (Norm, Interaction, Cognition, Experience), a diagnostic benchmark of 137 items operationalized through representative Chinese contexts. Across 5 frontier LLMs and a human reference group, models score higher in aggregate accuracy yet show a consistent weakness in Communication, which the framework localizes to 3 specific capability facets: multi-turn communication, nonverbal communication, and synchrony. NICE thus reframes social intelligence evaluation toward theory-grounded diagnosis of socially consequential weaknesses in LLMs.

</details>


### [188] [Reliable Reasoning with Large Language Models via Preference-Based Maximum Satisfiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29687)
*Pedro Orvalho,Marta Kwiatkowska,Guillem Alenyà,Felip Manyà*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对LLM在复杂约束优化任务中表现不佳的问题，研究者提出了一种将LLM代码生成与MaxSAT优化求解相结合的框架，通过外部验证确保了结果的可行性与最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）擅长语言理解，但在处理包含复杂约束和用户偏好的优化任务（如机器人领域）时表现较差，难以保证解的正确性。

Method: 提出一种混合推理方法：LLM负责将自然语言问题转化为Python代码，通过构建偏好驱动的MaxSAT（Maximum Satisfiability）问题进行求解，并引入独立的验证机制来确保生成解的可行性与最优性。

Result: 实验表明，相比直接回答、Chain-of-Thought和Program-of-Thought等基线方法，该方法在三类任务中显著提高了可行解的生成率，部分场景下成功率超过80%。

Conclusion: LLM生成的代码结合MaxSAT求解器是一种有效的混合推理方法，能够显著提升LLM在受限优化任务中的准确性和可行性，克服了传统LLM推理方法在复杂约束问题上的局限。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) excel at understanding natural language but struggle with optimisation tasks involving multiple constraints and user-defined preferences, which commonly arise in domains such as robotics. We propose a hybrid reasoning approach in which LLMs externalise reasoning through code generation. Given a natural language problem description, an LLM generates Python code that encodes user-defined constraints and preferences as a preference-based Maximum Satisfiability (MaxSAT) problem, which is then solved by an exact MaxSAT solver. To ensure correctness, solutions returned by the model-generated code are independently verified for feasibility and optimality against a canonical MaxSAT encoding, allowing for different encodings and multiple optimal solutions. We evaluate our approach using both open-source and closed-access LLMs on three families of preference-based reasoning tasks, and compare it against direct-answer, chain-of-thought, and program-of-thought baselines using the same models. While these baselines rarely produce feasible solutions, the MaxSAT-based pipeline achieves substantially higher acceptance rates, in some cases exceeding 80%. Our results demonstrate that LLM-driven code generation combined with preference-based MaxSAT enables solver-verifiable optimisation with respect to generated encodings, and substantially improves correctness under independently verified reference semantics.

</details>


### [189] [Beyond Trajectory Rewards: Step-level Credit Assignment for Agentic Search via Graph Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29697)
*Yuchen Liu,Yingjie Feng,Lixiong Qin,Jiasi Chen,Jianing Yu,Sheng Gao,Sheng Yang,Weiran Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需昂贵树搜索的Agentic Search新方法：GDCR利用实体关系图中的路径距离评估搜索步骤的有效性，并结合SAPO进行强化学习优化，有效提升了搜索准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agentic Search方法中，轨迹级奖励无法细化到步骤贡献，而步骤级奖励通常依赖昂贵的树搜索采样，效率低下。

Method: 1. 提出Graph-Distance Contribution Reward (GDCR)：通过在训练集实体关系图（ER Graph）中计算新检索/引用实体与答案节点的距离，量化步骤贡献。2. 提出Step Advantage Policy Optimization (SAPO)：将GDCR转化为步骤级优势函数，并结合轨迹级结果优势进行策略优化。

Result: 在四个具有挑战性的基准测试中，GDCR与SAPO的结合显著优于现有基准方法，验证了其在引导智能体进行有效信息搜寻中的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将世界知识建模为潜在图并利用实体距离来量化步骤贡献，GDCR和SAPO有效解决了Agentic Search中步骤评估的难题，显著提升了智能体搜索性能。

Abstract: In Agentic Search, trajectory-level outcome rewards fail to quantify the behavioral contributions of individual steps, while existing step-level reward methods typically rely on costly tree sampling. We view world knowledge as a latent world graph and each IS task as search within a latent task graph, where effective steps should make graph progress toward the answer node. Based on this prior, we propose Graph-Distance Contribution Reward (GDCR), a step-level process reward that scores newly-retrieved and newly-cited entities by their distance to the answer node in a training-time Entity-Relation (ER) graph. We further propose Step Advantage Policy Optimization (SAPO), which converts GDCR into step-level advantages and combines them with trajectory-level outcome advantages. Experiments on four challenging benchmarks validate the effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [190] [BitTP: The Lightweight Trajectory Prediction Model with BitLLM for Edge-Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29705)
*Mincheol Kang,Hyunjin Lim,Bomin Kang,Daehee Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 BitTP，通过 1.58-bit 权重仅量化技术，在显著降低计算功耗的同时，提升了 LLM 在自主系统轨迹预测任务中的性能，实现了高性能模型在边缘设备上的高效部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的轨迹预测模型虽然推理能力强，但计算和内存开销巨大，难以部署在资源受限的自动驾驶边缘设备上。

Method: 提出 BitTP 框架，将LLM轨迹预测器转换为轻量级 bitlinear 架构，通过实证分析确定了权重仅量化至 1.58-bit (BitTP-Weight) 的最优策略，并保持激活值（activations）为全精度。

Result: 相比 BF16 全精度基准，BitTP-Weight 将平均位移误差 (ADE) 降低了 14.29%，最终位移误差 (FDE) 降低了 20.97%，同时显著降低了内存占用和推理延迟。

Conclusion: 通过精细设计的权重仅量化（1.58-bit），可以在资源受限的边缘设备上部署基于LLM的轨迹预测模型，且性能优于全精度基准。量化在此处充当了有效的正则化器，提升了模型的泛化能力。

Abstract: Trajectory prediction is a fundamental task for autonomous systems, requiring complex reasoning about multi-agent interactions and intents. Large language models (LLMs) have recently been adopted for this task, as they provide strong contextual reasoning and interpretable, language-based trajectory representations. However, these LLM-based predictors are extremely memory- and compute-intensive, making them difficult to deploy on resource-constrained edge devices such as on-board computers in autonomous robots. To bridge this gap, we propose BitTP, which converts an LLM-based trajectory predictor into a lightweight bitlinear architecture. We demonstrate that weight-only quantization to 1.58-bit (BitTP-Weight) is optimal. Crucially, activations must remain in full precision, as quantizing them leads to severe degradation and instability in spatio-temporal reasoning. Empirically, BitTP-Weight not only preserves but improves prediction quality over the full-precision (BF16) LLM baseline, reducing ADE by 14.29% and FDE by 20.97% on average, while simultaneously reducing memory usage and inference latency relative to other quantization methods. These results demonstrate that carefully designed quantization acts as an effective regularizer, enabling the practical deployment of sophisticated LLM-based reasoning on edge devices. Code is available at: https://github.com/MintCat98/BitTP.

</details>


### [191] [Uncertainty-Aware Transfer Learning for Cross-Building Energy Forecasting: Toward Robust and Scalable District-Level Energy Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29733)
*Shadmehr Zaregarizi,Khashayar Yavari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究提出了一种基于TFT的迁移学习框架，通过极少参数的微调实现了跨建筑的高效能源预测，并提供了高质量的不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多建筑区域能源预测中，现有模型难以在目标建筑数据稀少的情况下实现高效复用，且大多数模型缺乏高质量的不确定性评估。

Method: 提出了一种基于Temporal Fusion Transformer (TFT) 的不确定性感知迁移学习框架；引入了衡量跨域泛化质量的指标（Transfer Robustness Index, TRI）；通过四种层冻结策略进行消融实验；利用蒙特卡洛Dropout进行不确定性量化。

Result: Probe-Only微调策略（仅更新约0.06%的参数）达到了最优的迁移性能（TRI = 3,097）；蒙特卡洛Dropout实现了93.2%的预测区间覆盖率，非常接近95%的理论值。

Conclusion: TFT的编码器能够学习到具有高度泛用性的时间特征表示；仅微调输出层（Probe-Only）即可实现最优的迁移效果；该方法在数据稀缺场景下表现稳健，并能通过蒙特卡洛Dropout提供可靠的预测不确定性估计。

Abstract: Scaling data-driven energy forecasting to district level requires models that can be re-used across buildings with minimal target-domain data and honest uncertainty estimates. We present an uncertainty-aware transfer learning (TL) framework for cross-building energy forecasting based on the Temporal Fusion Transformer (TFT), evaluated on a newly released high-resolution real sub-meter dataset: an educational building at Aalborg University, Denmark (source) and the multi-typology NEST building at EMPA, Switzerland (target). We introduce the Transfer Robustness Index (TRI), an architecture-agnostic metric for quantifying generalization quality across domain gaps. A four-strategy layer-freezing ablation shows that Probe-Only fine-tuning, updating only 455 output-layer parameters out of 806K, achieves the best transfer quality (TRI = 3,097), outperforming full fine-tuning and suggesting that TFT encoders learn transferable temporal representations. Monte Carlo Dropout yields a prediction interval coverage probability of 93.2%, close to the nominal 95% target. A data-scarcity analysis further shows monotonic improvement with increasing target-domain data, providing practical guidance for district energy deployment.

</details>


### [192] [Citation-Closure Retrieval and Per-Rule Attribution for Real-World Regulatory Compliance Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29742)
*Yeong-Joon Ju,Seong-Whan Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对监管合规领域，论文提出了RegOps-Bench基准与RefWalk框架。通过图驱动的跨文档引用遍历和严格的归因机制，解决了传统RAG系统在处理多层级监管结构时的证据溯源和准确性痛点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型在监管合规任务中面临挑战，现有RAG系统无法处理多层级权威结构，存在引用边扁平化、检索碎片化及引用溯源脆弱等问题。现有的法律问答基准未能覆盖结构化程序查询和证据集闭合需求。

Method: 提出了RegOps-Bench基准测试（包含运营知识图谱），并设计了RefWalk框架。RefWalk采用“共享主题锚点”（shared topic anchor）驱动，核心机制包括跨文档引用遍历、基于最大池化的多视图候选融合，以及强制性的按规则引用归因（enforced per-rule attribution）。

Result: 在RegOps-Bench上表现出显著的检索召回率和引用准确性提升；在HIPAA对比评估中，揭示了传统平坦结构检索系统在处理复杂监管规则时已达到性能饱和。

Conclusion: RefWalk框架通过处理跨文档引用和强制执行引用归因，在合规性问答中显著优于传统RAG系统；通过RegOps-Bench评估显示，现有扁平化检索方法已触及性能瓶颈，证实了针对多层级监管规则进行专门化架构设计的必要性。

Abstract: Deploying Large Language Models (LLMs) for regulatory compliance demands rigorous traceability via comprehensive citations across multi-tiered authority structures. Unlike traditional multi-hop or legal QA, this task requires structured procedural lookups and evidence-set closure rather than entity resolution or case-law reasoning. Existing RAG systems struggle here due to flattened citation edges, fragmented retrieval expansions, and fragile post-hoc attribution. We formalize Regulatory Compliance QA with RegOps-Bench, a novel benchmark featuring an Operational Knowledge Graph derived from complex national R\&D regulations. To address these bottlenecks, we propose RefWalk, a unified framework driven by a shared topic anchor. RefWalk traverses cross-document citations, fuses multi-view candidates via max-based aggregation, and enforces per-rule attribution to explicitly map claims to sources. We establish a strong baseline with substantial improvements in retrieval recall and citation accuracy. Finally, a contrastive evaluation on a U.S. health compliance dataset (HIPAA) reveals that existing systems exhibit saturation on flat-structure rules, underscoring the need for RegOps-Bench. Our code is available at https://github.com/yeongjoonJu/RefWalk.

</details>


### [193] [Why Specialist Models Still Matter: A Heterogeneous Multi-Agent Paradigm for Medical Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29744)
*Yanan Wang,Shuaicong Hu,Jian Liu,Guohui Zhou,Aiguo Wang,Cuiwei Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 为应对医疗AI发展中通用大模型与专精模型的角色争议，本文提出了HetMedAgent多智能体协作框架，通过整合通用LLM的推理和专精模型的精度，并引入人机交互机制，实现了比单一模型更优的临床决策表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对“通用大语言模型（LLM）是否将取代医疗领域专精模型”的争议，旨在探究如何有效整合通用大模型的推理能力与专精模型的领域深度，以实现更优的临床决策效果。

Method: 提出HetMedAgent框架，这是一种异构医疗多智能体架构，核心机制包括：冲突感知证据融合（conflict-aware evidence fusion）、基于不确定性的临床干预触发（uncertainty-based clinician intervention triggering）以及自适应阈值校准（adaptive threshold calibration）。

Result: 在三个真实临床决策任务中的实验结果表明，通用LLM与专精模型的协同效应显著优于单一来源模型，证实了专精模型在模态特定分析中具有不可替代的价值。

Conclusion: 医疗AI的未来在于构建通用大模型、领域专精模型与临床医生三者协同的AI生态，而非单一的大一统模型。HetMedAgent证明了多智能体协作机制能在保证通用推理能力的同时，显著提升特定领域的决策精度。

Abstract: The impressive performance of generalist large language models (LLMs) such as GPT and Claude in healthcare raises a critical question: will domain-specific medical specialist models become obsolete? We argue that the future of medical artificial intelligence (AI) lies not in building monolithic medical foundation models, nor in replacing human expertise, but in orchestrating collaboration among generalist LLMs, domain-specific specialist models, and clinicians. We propose HetMedAgent, a heterogeneous medical multi-agent framework that enables conflict-aware evidence fusion, uncertainty-based clinician intervention triggering, and adaptive threshold calibration. Experiments on three real-world clinical decision-making tasks demonstrate that the synergy between generalist LLMs and domain-specific specialist models significantly outperforms using either type of model alone, validating the irreplaceable value of specialist models in modality-specific analysis. HetMedAgent represents a shift from building medical LLMs or foundation models to multi-agent collaboration, achieving a balance between general reasoning capabilities and domain-specific precision.

</details>


### [194] [Benchmarking Positional Encoding Strategies for Transformer-Based EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29754)
*Ayse Betul Yuce,Sebastian Stober*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究评估了Transformer模型在EEG解码中的五种位置编码策略，发现不存在统一的最优方案，位置编码的选择需根据具体解码任务进行调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型在处理EEG数据时具有置换不变性，需要显式的位置信息。然而，EEG电极具有特殊的非欧几里得拓扑结构，目前尚不清楚何种位置编码策略最适合此类模型。

Method: 以CBraMod为基础模型，在运动想象分类和情绪识别任务上，对比了五种位置编码策略，并采用了线性探测（Linear Probing）和微调（Fine-tuning）两种评估协议。

Result: 实验发现五种策略中没有单一最优解；球面位置编码（SPE）在运动想象任务中表现出色但在情绪识别中不佳，而非对称条件位置编码（ACPE）在不同任务间表现更为均衡一致。

Conclusion: 不存在一种适用于所有EEG解码任务的“通用”位置编码策略；位置编码的选择具有显著的任务相关性（Task-dependent）。在具体应用中，ACPE表现出更好的稳健性，而SPE在特定任务（如运动想象）中更具优势。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) is a widely used non-invasive technique for measuring brain activity in brain-computer interface (BCI) applications. Supervised EEG decoding models often struggle to generalize across tasks, subjects, and datasets, motivating transformer-based EEG foundation models trained with self-supervised learning. Since transformers are permutation-invariant, they require explicit positional information. Unlike textual tokens, EEG electrodes are spatially distributed across the scalp, raising the question of how electrode positions should be encoded in transformer-based EEG models. In this study, we benchmark five positional encoding strategies within the CBraMod backbone and evaluate them under linear probing and fine-tuning protocols on motor imagery classification and emotion recognition. Our results show that no single strategy consistently outperforms across tasks. Spherical Positional Encoding (SPE) yields strong representations for motor imagery but underperforms on emotion recognition, while Asymmetric Conditional Positional Encoding (ACPE) demonstrates more consistent performance across tasks. These findings suggest that the optimal positional encoding strategy is task-dependent, with no universal solution across EEG decoding scenarios.

</details>


### [195] [LFQ: Logit-aware Final-block Quantization for Boosting the Generation Quality of Low-Bit Quantized LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29756)
*Jung Hyun Lee,June Yong Yang,Jungwook Choi,Eunho Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对大模型低比特量化导致的生成质量下降问题，本文提出了LFQ方法，通过对齐最终块的Logit而非仅优化MSE，显著提升了长文本生成任务的准确度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的低比特权重仅（weight-only）PTQ方法在基础语言建模上表现良好，但在长生成任务（尤其是长思维链）中性能严重退化，主要原因是忽视了输出层（LM head）量化和MSE损失函数导致模型生成分布偏离。

Method: 提出Logit-aware Final-block Quantization (LFQ)技术：针对传统块级PTQ忽略LM Head和使用MSE准则的问题，在最终Transformer块中通过最小化全精度模型与量化模型Logit之间的交叉熵损失，优化量化参数。

Result: LFQ在多个大模型系列上实验表明，其在复杂的生成任务中表现优于现有的SOTA块级PTQ方法，同时保证了在基础语言模型和理解任务上能够达到与全精度基线相当的性能。

Conclusion: LFQ方法通过在最终Transformer块中对齐Logit，有效解决了低比特量化大模型在长文本生成任务中的性能退化问题，在保持语言建模能力的同等水平下显著提升了生成任务的准确性。

Abstract: As large language models continue to scale, low-bit weight-only post-training quantization (PTQ) offers a practical solution to their memory-efficient deployment. Although block-wise PTQ is capable of matching the full-precision (FP) baseline on basic language modeling and understanding, its quality is degraded for generative tasks -- especially at longer responses and extended chains of thought, which is critical in boosting task accuracy. We attribute this shortfall to two factors: (i) the omission of the unembedding layer (the LM head) in block-wise optimization and (ii) the reliance on the mean squared error (MSE) objective. Both factors cause the token probability distribution of the quantized model to misalign with that of the FP model, yielding notable accuracy drops on text generation benchmarks. To rectify the discrepancy, we introduce Logit-aware Final-block Quantization (LFQ), a simple yet effective enhancement to block-wise PTQ that quantizes the final Transformer block by minimizing the cross-entropy between the logits of the FP model and those of its quantized counterpart. By aligning token probabilities at the logit level in the final block, LFQ consistently improves the accuracy of complex generation tasks over state-of-the-art block-wise PTQ across diverse model families, while maintaining parity with FP baselines on language modeling and understanding.

</details>


### [196] [Croissant Tasks: A Metadata Format for Reproducible Machine Learning Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29786)
*Omar Benjelloun,Leonardo Martins Bianco,Isabelle Guyon,Thanh Gia Hieu Khuong,Jonathan Lebensold,Sebastian Lobentanzer,Luis Oala,Benedictus Kent Rachmat,Ihsan Ullah,Peyman Vahidi,Joaquin Vanschoren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Croissant Tasks 格式，通过将任务与实现解耦来支持机器学习的“概念性可复现性”，并证明了自主智能体能够利用该格式自动构建复现流水线，从而解决了传统可复现性方法难以扩展的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习领域存在严峻的可复现性危机，现有的手动检查和代码重现方法过于依赖具体环境且难以扩展。

Method: 1. 设计 Croissant Tasks 规范：将任务问题与具体实现细节解耦。2. 构建自动化 LLM 流水线：用于将现有的机器学习基准测试转化为该格式。3. 经验性验证：测试自主智能体根据规范从零构建功能性复现流水线的能力。

Result: 验证了自主智能体能够成功解析 Croissant Tasks 规范，并能从零开始生成准确、具备功能性的复现流水线。

Conclusion: Croissant Tasks 提供了一种支持“概念性可复现性”的声明式元数据格式，能够利用自主智能体实现机器学习基准测试的自动化复现，从而有望解决传统代码重现的脆弱性和可扩展性问题。

Abstract: Reproducibility is fundamental to the scientific method, yet remains a critical challenge in machine learning. Contributing factors include underspecified execution details and brittle software environments. Human-centric remedies, such as checklists and manual verification, help but require intensive effort and fail to scale. To address this, we introduce Croissant Tasks: a declarative, machine-actionable metadata format that abstracts low-level implementation details into high-level specifications. This format enables conceptual reproducibility: verifying claims via independent, agent-generated implementations rather than brittle source code replication. We contribute: (1) the Croissant Tasks specification, formally decoupling task problem from solution; (2) an automated LLM pipeline that retrofits existing benchmarks into this format; and (3) empirical validation showing autonomous agents can ingest these specifications to generate functional, accurate reproduction pipelines from scratch. We envision this format as a new foundation for automated and conceptual reproducibility in machine learning.

</details>


### [197] [Certified Policy Optimisation for Nested Causal Bandits via PAC-Bayes Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29788)
*Tim Woydt,Paul-David Zuercher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了嵌套上下文因果赌博机（NCCBs）框架及其推断算法NCTS，通过因果PAC-Bayesian界实现离线风险认证，并在分层决策系统中实现了安全可靠的渐进式控制器替换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的标准赌博机和强化学习理论无法捕捉关键顺序决策中高层战略对底层战术的因果耦合影响，导致多时间尺度决策失效。

Method: 1. 形式化定义“嵌套上下文因果赌博机”（NCCBs）：一种结构因果模型（SCM），高层动作决定底层上下文分布；
2. 提出“嵌套因果汤普森采样”（NCTS）：在每轮通过机制因子分解的信念进行递归决策；
3. 导出“因果PAC-Bayesian超额风险界”：仅基于历史数据即可离线、随时地评估部署策略的风险。

Result: 1. 在分层SCM实验中，NCTS相比联合回归方法在外部偏移下具有更好的零样本迁移能力；
2. 递归元到内的提交策略表现优于联合提交；
3. 随着离线数据的积累，证明的风险边界显著收缩，为部署决策提供了有效信任指标。

Conclusion: 提出了一种名为“渐进式认证移交”（Progressive Certified Handover）的安全部署方法，允许在不同时间尺度下，当增益可认证时，独立地将遗留控制器切换为NCTS，从而实现系统级的安全决策进化。

Abstract: Critical sequential decisions are rarely single-timescale: a strategic decision causally shapes the context in which every subsequent tactical choice is made; standard bandit and reinforcement-learning theory does not capture this causal coupling between timescales. We formalise the problem class as Nested Contextual Causal Bandits (NCCBs), a hierarchical SCM where each level's action sets the next level's context distribution, and propose Nested Causal Thompson Sampling (NCTS), which draws one mechanism-factorised belief per episode and acts recursively under it. Our main theoretical result is a causal PAC-Bayesian excess-risk bound that certifies any candidate deployment policy from historic data alone, off-policy and anytime, answering the deployment question: can we trust this agent here, and at what risk? Experiments on a hierarchical SCM show that, against a matched RFF-GP joint regression on the same function class, the factorised SCM-mechanism posterior transfers significantly better zero-shot under exogenous distribution shifts, the recursive meta-to-inner commit significantly dominates the joint-commit alternative in distribution, and the certificate significantly contracts as offline data accumulates. Combining these results, we establish progressive certified handover, a safe-deployment method: each timescale flips from a legacy controller to NCTS when gains can be certified, independently of the others.

</details>


### [198] [SkillsInjector: Dynamic Skill Context Construction for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29794)
*Yanchao Li,Wanhao Liu,Ben Gao,Jiaqing Xie,Zhehong Ai,Na Zou,Yuqiang Li,Tianfan Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 SkillsInjector，通过两阶段自适应机制动态规划和渲染 LLM Agent 的注入技能，解决了静态注入导致的性能衰减问题，显著提升了 Agent 执行复杂任务的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM Agent 技能注入大多采用静态处理方式（固定的选择标准、预算和描述），这不仅无法保证性能，反而可能因为干扰或冗余导致任务完成率下降。

Method: 提出了两阶段自适应方法 SkillsInjector：1. 上下文规划器（Context Planner）：基于执行结果学习技能偏好并确定任务所需的自适应技能数量；2. 集合感知渲染器（Set-aware Renderer）：根据共存技能组合优化技能描述的呈现方式。

Result: 在 tau2-bench、SkillsBench 和 ALFWorld 三个基准测试中表现最优，分别比最强基线提升了 3.9、6.1 和 7.3 个百分点。消融实验证实了技能选择、自适应预算和集合感知渲染模块的必要性。

Conclusion: SkillsInjector 通过对注入技能的动态优化（选择、数量控制及描述渲染），有效克服了静态技能注入带来的性能退化问题，证明了优化注入上下文对于提升 LLM Agent 任务完成能力的重要性。

Abstract: LLM agents now draw on growing skill libraries to handle complex tasks. However, injecting more skills does not always improve task completion and can even degrade it. Existing methods still treat skill injection as a static step, selecting skills with fixed criteria, fixing the budget in advance, and leaving descriptions unchanged. We argue that this static treatment can undermine the utility of skills, because which skills are exposed, how many are included, and how they are presented all affect downstream performance. We propose SkillsInjector, a two-stage adaptive method that jointly addresses these decisions. First, a context planner learns execution-grounded skill preferences and admits an adaptive number of skills for each task. A set-aware renderer then tailors how selected descriptions are presented relative to their co-injected neighbors. Across tau2-bench, SkillsBench, and ALFWorld, SkillsInjector achieves the highest score, improving over the strongest baseline by 3.9, 6.1, and 7.3 percentage points, respectively. Ablation studies show that skill selection, adaptive budgeting, and set-aware rendering each contribute to the gain. These results show that skill-augmented agents benefit from optimizing the injected context itself. Code will be released upon publication

</details>


### [199] [MEMENTO: Leveraging Web as a Learning Signal for Low-Data Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29795)
*Ashutosh Ojha,Vinay Aggarwal,Ashutosh Srivastava,Siddharth Yedlapati,Yaman K Singla,Jitendra Ajmera*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MEMENTO框架通过模拟人类从网上进行自主学习的过程，利用“自适应探索与双通道记忆”机制，无需额外训练即可从交互轨迹中自动获取领域专长与搜索策略，在小样本复杂任务中大幅超越了传统基于检索的AI能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有小样本学习方法过度依赖有限的标注或伪标注数据。作者受人类专家通过互联网自主学习的启发，旨在探索如何利用动态的互联网交互作为学习来源，解决数据匮乏场景下的专业任务处理问题。

Method: 提出MEMENTO框架，核心包含两个模块：1. 自适应探索树（AET）：在单次会话内通过任务分解、动态提问和中间结果反思进行迭代式探索；2. 双通道记忆系统：在跨会话中分别存储陈述性知识（事实）和过程性知识（搜索策略），实现知识与策略的积累与复用。

Result: 在销售自动化和法律研究两个专业领域进行评估，MEMENTO相比ReAct基准模型性能分别提升了25.6%和36.5%，证明了其在低数据场景下的高效能与通用性。

Conclusion: MEMENTO证明了通过将互联网作为交互式学习信号而非静态检索库，智能体无需额外训练即可在低数据环境下获取任务专业知识。其通过双通道记忆机制积累的科研策略和领域知识，显著提升了复杂任务的执行效果。

Abstract: Real-world tasks often lack large labeled datasets, motivating extensive work on learning in low-data regimes. However, existing approaches such as few-shot prompting, instruction tuning, and synthetic data generation, continue to treat labeled or pseudo-labeled data as the primary learning signal. In contrast, human practitioners acquire expertise through repeated, self-directed interaction with the open web, progressively refining both domain knowledge and search strategies. We propose MEMENTO, a framework that treats the web as a learning signal rather than a stateless retrieval interface. MEMENTO operates at two levels: within each session, it conducts iterative web exploration via an Adaptive Exploration Tree (AET) that decomposes tasks into evolving questions and reflects on intermediate findings; across sessions, it accumulates experience through dual-channel memory, separating declarative knowledge (facts) from procedural knowledge (search strategies). This design enables agents to learn reusable research strategies and domain expertise from trajectories of web interaction without additional model training. We evaluate MEMENTO on two low-data professional domains: sales automation and legal research. Our empirical results show consistent improvements in performance over ReAct based baselines (+25.6% on sales automation and 36.5% on legal research), demonstrating that the web can serve as a scalable learning source for acquiring task-specific expertise in data-scarce settings.

</details>


### [200] [SAAS: Self-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Over-Search Mitigation in Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29796)
*Yunbo Tang,Chengyi Yang,Shiyu Liu,Zhishang Xiang,Zerui Chen,Qinggang Zhang,Jinsong Su*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对智能体搜索中存在的“过度搜索”问题，本文提出了 SAAS 框架，通过强化学习赋予智能体动态搜索感知能力，在保障任务准确性的同时大幅节省计算成本和时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体搜索系统缺乏对自身知识边界的认知，往往在无需搜索时盲目触发搜索或在获取足够信息后未能及时终止，导致严重的过度搜索和高昂的计算成本。

Method: 提出了一个名为 SAAS 的强化学习框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 搜索边界建模机制，通过对比开启与关闭搜索的运行情况来界定搜索边界；(2) 边界感知奖励模块，通过轨迹级惩罚抑制冗余搜索；(3) 分阶段优化策略，利用序列化课程学习防止奖励欺骗。

Result: 大量实验证明，SAAS 在维持模型回答准确性的同时，显著减少了过度搜索行为，提高了推理效率。

Conclusion: SAAS 通过强化学习训练智能体具备动态搜索感知能力，能够有效减少“过度搜索”问题，在不牺牲准确率的前提下降低了推理延迟和计算成本。

Abstract: Agentic search enables LLMs to solve complex multi-hop questions through iterative reasoning and external search. Despite the effectiveness, these systems often suffer from a critical limitation in practice: agents fail to recognize their own knowledge boundaries, blindly triggering searches when internal knowledge suffices and failing to terminate search even when adequate evidence has been collected. The lack of self-awareness leads to severe \textbf{over-search}, incurring substantial inference latency and prohibitive computational cost. To this end, we propose SAAS, a novel RL framework designed to cultivate dynamic self-awareness that precisely regulates search behavior without compromising accuracy. SAAS introduces three key components: (i) a search boundary modeling mechanism, which identifies the search boundary under the evolving policy by contrasting search-disabled and search-enabled rollouts; (ii) a boundary-aware reward module, which translates this boundary awareness into trajectory-level penalties, suppressing unnecessary and redundant searches; and (iii) a stage-wise optimization strategy, which leverages a sequential curriculum to prioritize reasoning over search regularization, thereby avoiding reward hacking. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SAAS substantially reduces over-search, while maintaining accuracy. Our code is anonymously released at https://github.com/XMUDeepLIT/SAAS.

</details>


### [201] [AgentDoG 1.5: A Lightweight and Scalable Alignment Framework for AI Agent Safety and Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29801)
*Dongrui Liu,Yu Li,Zhonghao Yang,Peng Wang,Guanxu Chen,Yuejin Xie,Qinghua Mao,Wanying Qu,Yanxu Zhu,Tianyi Zhou,Leitao Yuan,Zhijie Zheng,Qihao Lin,Yimin Wang,Haoyu Luo,Shuai Shao,Chen Qian,Qingyu Liu,Ling Tang,Ruiyang Qin,Qihan Ren,Junxiao Yang,Kun Wang,Zhiheng Xi,Linfeng Zhang,Ranjie Duan,Bo Zhang,Wenjie Wang,Wen Shen,Qiaosheng Zhang,Yan Teng,Chaochao Lu,Rui Mei,Man Li,Jialing Tao,Xi Lin,Tianhang Zheng,Yong Liu,Quanshi Zhang,Lei Zhu,Xingjun Ma,Junhua Liu,Hui Xue,Xiaoxiang Zuo,Xiangnan He,Chao Shen,Xianglong Liu,Minlie Huang,Jing Shao,Xia Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AgentDoG 1.5，这是一个针对开放世界智能体的高效安全对齐框架，通过精简数据工程与低开销训练环境，实现了轻量级模型的SOTA安全防御能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代开放世界智能体（如OpenClaw）引入了广泛的新型安全风险，而现有智能体对齐框架在面对 frontier AI 模型带来的降低攻击门槛时已显不足。

Method: 1. 更新智能体安全分类法（taxonomy）以覆盖 Codex 和 OpenClaw 等新兴风险；2. 构建基于影响函数（influence-function）清洗的分类引导数据引擎；3. 开发高效的智能体安全SFT和RL训练环境，降低Docker环境部署开销；4. 部署为无需进一步训练的在线安全护栏。

Result: 1. 仅需约1k样本即可训练出轻量级模型（0.8B-8B），性能堪比 GPT-5.4；2. 部署开销降低了两个数量级；3. 在多种复杂交互式智能体场景中达到SOTA安全水平。

Conclusion: AgentDoG 1.5提出了一种轻量级且可扩展的智能体安全对齐框架，通过税则引导的数据引擎和高效的训练环境，显著降低了部署开销，并在复杂交互场景中实现了SOTA的安全表现。

Abstract: Modern open-world agents such as OpenClaw exhibit powerful cross-environment execution capabilities yet introduce broad new safety risk sources. Meanwhile, advanced frontier AI models drastically lower attack barriers, rendering current agent alignment frameworks inadequate for real-world deployment. To tackle these emerging threats, we propose a lightweight and scalable agent safety alignment framework. Specifically, we update the agent safety taxonomy to accommodate emergent risks from Codex and OpenClaw execution scenarios. We further build a taxonomy-guided data engine with influence-function purification to train lightweight AgentDoG 1.5 variants (0.8B, 2B, 4B, and 8B parameters) using only around 1k samples, achieving comparable performance with leading closed-source models (e.g., GPT-5.4). Based on AgentDoG 1.5, we construct a highly efficient agentic safety SFT and RL training environment, which reduces deployment overhead in Docker-level environments by two orders of magnitude. Finally, we deploy AgentDoG 1.5 as a training-free online guardrail for real-time safety moderation. Extensive experimental results indicate that AgentDoG 1.5 achieves state-of-the-art performance in diverse and complex interactive agentic scenarios. All models and datasets are openly released.

</details>


### [202] [PRAIB: Peer Review AI Benchmark of Behaviour of LLM-Assisted Reviewing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29815)
*Krzysztof Żurawicki,Julia Farganus,Arkadiusz Gaweł,Mateusz Bystroński,Tomasz Jan Kajdanowicz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了PRAIB评估框架，通过对比分析发现LLM在学术评审中表现出与人类显著不同的行为模式（如忽视关键漏洞、存在系统性偏见），指出了目前LLM在全自动审稿中的局限性与潜在风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着投稿量激增，寻求使用LLM辅助同行评审以提高速度和扩展性成为研究热点，但目前尚不清楚LLM是真正理解并参与了审稿，还是仅仅在生成仿真文本，因此需要评估LLM与人类审稿人在行为模式上的差异。

Method: 构建了Peer Review AI Benchmark (PRAIB) 评估框架，并基于1,000篇ICLR/NeurIPS论文，利用5种不同的LLM生成了11,000份模拟审稿意见，最后通过该框架将这些模拟意见与人类原始审稿意见进行对比分析。

Result: LLM生成的评审意见存在评分变异性低、正向偏倚、过度自信等问题；与人类相比，虽然其生成的文本更长更复杂，但经常忽略人类专家指出的具体原子性漏洞；其交叉引用模式也明显区别于人类专家习惯。

Conclusion: LLM目前的审稿行为与人类审稿人存在显著差异（如评分偏倚、过度自信及忽视核心问题），PRAIB框架可作为评估LLM在学术审稿中适用性的诊断工具，为模型在同行评审中的辅助作用提供明确的边界指引。

Abstract: The growing number of submitted papers has motivated the exploration of Large Language Models (LLMs) as a means to support and augment the peer review process, particularly in terms of improving its speed and scalability. Yet, it remains unknown whether LLMs engage with scientific manuscripts in the same manner as human reviewers, or whether they merely produce review-looking text. To address this, we introduce the Peer Review AI Benchmark (PRAIB), a novel framework comprising thoroughly defined metrics that measure review specificity, style, and behavior of engagement. To complement the PRAIB framework, we conduct a large-scale empirical study leveraging a dataset of 11,000 reviews generated by five proprietary and open-source models for 1,000 ICLR and NeurIPS papers. Spanning the 2021--2025 period, these machine-generated reviews are compared against original human feedback across diverse prompting strategies to identify systematic behavioral divergences. Our analysis reveals that the generated reviews diverge significantly from feedback provided by human reviewers: LLM ratings are less variable, positively biased, and overconfident, and their cross-reference patterns are model-dependent and distinct from human norms. Furthermore, when evaluated through PRAIB, we observe that LLMs tend to generate longer, more complex reviews, yet frequently overlook the atomic weaknesses noted by human reviewers. By characterizing where and how LLMs reviewing behavior departs from human norms, PRAIB provides the community with a diagnostic tool for identifying which aspects of the review process LLMs can reliably support today and which require further development before deployment.

</details>


### [203] [OptSkills: Learning Generalizable Optimization Skills from Problem Archetypes via Cluster-Based Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29829)
*Haochen Yang,Ke Zhao,Mengyuan Ma,Xingyu Lu,Xiangfeng Wang,Hong Qian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OptSkills 是一种以问题原型为核心的优化建模与求解系统，通过将求解经验提炼为可复用的“技能”，显著提升了 LLM 在处理复杂、跨领域优化问题时的鲁棒性与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型在自动优化领域存在泛化能力弱的问题，主要表现为对文本表述敏感、经验重用局限于案例层面以及难以适应分布外或新兴问题类型。

Method: 提出 OptSkills 系统：1. 通过问题原型（Archetype）而非表面描述进行聚类；2. 在同类问题中蒸馏成功的建模与求解路径为“工作流级技能”；3. 通过扩展技能库实现对分布外（OOD）问题的持续学习与适应。

Result: 在多场景数据集上达到 68.27% 的准确率；在 MIPLIB-NL 基准上以 26.91% 的表现优于 DeepSeek-V3.2；在 OOD NLCO 基准上通过技能学习达到 72.79% 的准确率。

Conclusion: OptSkills 通过以原型为中心的技能学习和推理，有效提升了大模型在处理优化问题时的泛化能力，在多个基准测试中达到了 SOTA 水平。

Abstract: Leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) to automatically formulate and solve optimization problems from natural language has emerged as an efficient paradigm for automated optimization. However, existing methods still exhibit limited generalization: they are sensitive to superficial narrative variations, reuse experience mainly at the case level, and struggle to adapt to shifted or emerging problem types. We propose OptSkills, an archetype-centric skill learning and reasoning agent system for optimization modeling and solving. To improve robust generalization, our system clusters problems by their underlying archetypes rather than surface narratives. To improve in-distribution generalization, it explores diverse modeling paradigms and solver configurations within each cluster, then distills successful trajectories into reusable workflow-level skills. To improve out-of-distribution generalization, it refines existing skills or expands the skill library using newly obtained trajectories. Our system achieves a state-of-the-art micro-averaged accuracy of 68.27% on datasets encompassing diverse problem types and scenarios. In addition, on MIPLIB-NL, a highly challenging large-scale and high-dimensional benchmark, it achieves 26.91% accuracy, outperforming DeepSeek-V3.2-Thinking by 4.53%. After skill learning on Nano-CO, it reaches 72.79% on the OOD NLCO benchmark. Code and skills are available at https://github.com/fujiwaranoM0kou/OptSkills.

</details>


### [204] [OmniMatBench: A Human-Calibrated Multimodal Reasoning Benchmark Across 19 Materials Science Subfields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29833)
*Wanhao Liu,Jiaqing Xie,Qian Tan,Weida Wang,Jue Wang,Ran Sun,Zhuo Yang,Wanli Ouyang,Lei Bai,Tianfan Fu,Lu Chen,Xin Chen,Yuqiang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 OmniMatBench，一个人工校准的材料科学多模态推理基准，并揭示了现有大模型在该领域处理复杂知识应用和跨学科推理时的明显能力缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有材料科学基准多局限于属性预测或基础知识问答，缺乏对材料知识到应用转化过程中的复杂多模态推理能力的评估。

Method: 构建了包含 3,171 个专家精选 QA 和计算问题的多模态推理基准 OmniMatBench，涵盖材料科学 19 个子领域；对 13 个主流闭源和开源多模态大模型（MLLMs）进行了系统评测，并分析了模型在不同辅助设置（公式、检索、代码）下的表现。

Result: 评测发现模型表现普遍不佳，最高得分仅为 0.372；模型在子领域表现存在显著差异，且在面对需要高级知识应用和复杂推理的任务时表现出局限性。

Conclusion: 现有的多模态大模型在材料科学领域的推理能力仍存显著差距，OmniMatBench 为评估和提升 AI 辅助材料科学研究提供了重要的基准和洞察。

Abstract: As multimodal language models play an increasingly important role in scientific research, materials science offers a critical testbed due to its interdisciplinary, multimodal, and application-driven nature. However, existing materials benchmarks mainly focus on property prediction, knowledge QA, or characterization understanding, leaving the broader reasoning process from materials knowledge to application underexplored. To fill this gap, we present OmniMatBench, a human-calibrated multimodal reasoning benchmark for materials science. OmniMatBench contains 3,171 expert-curated QA and calculation problems across 19 materials-science subfields, spanning fundamental materials knowledge, structural and engineering materials, materials processing and manufacturing, and functional and applied materials. We evaluate 13 open-source and closed-source MLLMs and find that the best model achieves only a 0.372 overall score, revealing a substantial gap in current materials-science reasoning. Further analysis shows strong variation across subfields, fixed reasoning heuristics, uneven materials knowledge, and limited high-level knowledge application under formula-, retrieval-, and code-assisted settings. OmniMatBench provides crucial insights into the capabilities and limitations of current MLLMs and establishes a foundation for reliable AI assistants in materials-science research.

</details>


### [205] [Moment-KV: Momentum-Based Decode-Time KV Cache Compression for Long Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29873)
*Soumyadeep Jana,Sagar Nishad,Sanasam Ranbir Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Moment-KV 是一种用于 LLM 解码阶段的 KV Cache 压缩方法，它利用动量驱动的注意力聚合策略，动态识别并保留关键 Token，解决了静态压缩算法无法捕捉长期注意力模式的问题，提升了长文本生成的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 KV Cache 压缩方法对预填充和解码阶段采用统一处理，导致预填充阶段关键信息丢失；且现有解码阶段的压缩方法多依赖静态启发式规则（如近端窗口），无法捕捉注意力在大时间跨度上的持续性与局部推理中的突发性。

Method: 提出 Moment-KV 方法，在解码阶段通过动量驱动的时间注意力聚合来动态建模 Token 重要性，将 Token 的长期影响力与近期相关性结合起来进行缓存压缩。

Result: 在长文本生成任务中，相较于传统方法，Moment-KV 显著提高了生成忠实度（2.3-3.2%），并有效地维持了原有的解码延迟性能。

Conclusion: Moment-KV 通过引入基于动量的动态重要性评估机制，在维持解码延迟的同时，显著提升了长文本生成任务中的模型忠实度（2.3-3.2%）。

Abstract: Key-Value (KV) cache remains a major bottleneck for deploying Large Language Models (LLMs) in long-generation tasks. Prior work often applies uniform compression across both prefill and decoding caches, but compressing the prefill cache degrades performance by corrupting critical context. While preserving the prefill cache is essential, decoding-phase compression remains underexplored, with existing methods relying on rigid recency windows or instantaneous attention. Our analysis of attention dynamics reveals strong temporal patterns: critical tokens receive sustained attention over long horizons, while local reasoning involves short-lived bursts. Static heuristics fail to capture this behavior, leading to premature eviction of important tokens or retention of stale ones. We propose Moment-KV, a decoding-time KV cache compression method based on momentum-driven temporal attention aggregation. Our method models token importance as a continuously evolving state, where attention is aggregated with decay, capturing both long-term influence and recent relevance. Experiments show that Moment-KV significantly improves generation fidelity in long-generation tasks (2.3-3.2 %) while maintaining decoding latency.

</details>


### [206] [Redundant or Necessary? A Benchmark for Detecting Redundant Steps in Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29893)
*Minyang Hu,Bo Yang,Zhinuo Zhou,Jiachen Liang,Guo Jiahao,Yiyang Yin,Xiongwei Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出了大模型智能体执行任务时存在效率低下问题，定义了“冗余步骤检测”任务，并提出了相应的RedundancyBench数据集，实验证明当前模型处理此类问题的能力仍处于早期且匮乏阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评测主要关注任务成功率，忽略了执行效率。智能体在执行过程中往往包含大量低贡献、高资源消耗的冗余步骤，缺乏对这类低效行为的量化评估与检测手段。

Method: 1. 提出了“冗余步骤检测”的新研究范式；2. 构建了RedundancyBench基准数据集，对多任务场景下的智能体轨迹步骤进行详细标注；3. 开发并评估了3种基准检测方法，用于判断步骤的必要性。

Result: RedundancyBench基准测试显示，现有代表性方法在检测冗余步骤方面的性能极其有限，最高得分仅为24.88%，部分方法的效果甚至低于随机猜测。

Conclusion: 当前针对智能体轨迹中冗余步骤的检测能力极弱（最佳方法得分仅24.88%），甚至部分方法表现不如随机猜测，表明该领域极具挑战性，需要学术界进一步研究。

Abstract: LLM-based agents have demonstrated strong capabilities in solving complex tasks through multi-step reasoning and tool use. However, existing evaluation protocols primarily focus on task success, overlooking a critical aspect of agent behavior: execution efficiency. In practice, agent trajectories often contain redundant steps that consume substantial resources while contributing little to task completion. In this work, we propose and formulate a new research area: \textbf{redundant step detection} for agent trajectories. To support this initiative, we introduce \textbf{RedundancyBench}, a new benchmark that contains diverse tasks with carefully annotated trajectories, where each step is labeled according to its contribution to task completion. Using RedundancyBench, we develop and evaluate 3 representative methods to answer whether a step within trajectory is redundant or necessary. Our results show that even the best-performing method achieves only 24.88\% score in detecting redundant steps, while some methods perform worse than random guessing. These results highlight the task's complexity and the need for further research in this area. \footnote{Code and dataset in this paper are both available in \href{https://anonymous.4open.science/r/RedundancyBench}{https://anonymous.4open.science/r/RedundancyBench}.}

</details>


### [207] [Toward AI Systems That Understand Self and Others: A Multi-Phase Inference Framework for Human Cognitive Diversity and World-Model Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29930)
*Toru Takahashi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了“多阶段推理机制（MIM）”，通过形式化主体间异质的世界模型构建过程，重新定义了对齐问题：即非消除差异，而是通过可视化和显性化个体对世界的差异化理解，提升主体间的“相互可处理性”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当代社会互不理解的根源不仅在于观点差异，还在于不同主体对观察结果存在异质的推理目标、状态表征、预测错误及更新优先级。现有对齐目标过于片面。

Method: 提出多阶段推理框架（MIM）。核心包括：相形成空间（phase-formation space）、前景场（foregrounding field）、主体特定侧写状态（subject-specific profile states），以及状态表征间的对齐映射（alignment maps）来形式化异质世界模型的形成过程。

Result: 构建了一套形式化的逻辑词汇表，将世界模型的异质性拆解为可操作的认知结构，从而为处理哲学分歧、认知类型学与AI对齐挑战提供了一种技术与认知上的新路径。

Conclusion: 世界模型对齐不应旨在强制达成一致或收敛，而应致力于使异质表征具有“相互可处理性”（mutual processability）。通过让预测错误、意义和价值观差异变得可见、可比且可转化，MIM能辅助人类理解自我与他人，并为AI对齐提供新范式。

Abstract: Mutual misunderstanding in contemporary society does not arise merely because people hold different opinions or values. Even under the same observations, different subjects may form different inferential targets, state representations, prediction errors, and update priorities. This paper proposes a multi-phase inference framework and defines its core internal mechanism as the Multi-Phase Inference Mechanism (MIM). MIM formalizes how heterogeneous world models arise through a phase-formation space, a foregrounding field, subject-specific profile states, and alignment maps between state representations. On this basis, the paper reframes world-model alignment as the problem of making heterogeneous representations mutually processable, rather than forcing agreement or convergence to a single value system. It further connects this formalism to philosophical disagreements, cognitive typology, social fragmentation, and AI alignment. The aim is to provide a constructive vocabulary for AI systems that can help humans understand self and others by making differences in meaning, value, and prediction error visible, comparable, and transformable.

</details>


### [208] [It`s All About Speed: AI`s Impact on Workflow in Music Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29931)
*Finn McClellan,Fabio Morreale*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过民族志方法深入分析了AI对专业音乐制作的影响，指出尽管AI提升了效率，但也引发了关于技术干预与创作者自主权之间的张力，并为后续工具设计提出了平衡建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨AI和自动化工具在专业音乐制作工作流中的影响，特别是职业人士如何处理技术进步与传统创作实践之间的关系。

Method: 定性民族志研究（Ethnographic study），通过对专业录音师、混音师和制作人的访谈与观察，分析其对AI和自动化工具的实际应用及态度。

Result: 研究识别了用户与自动化工具之间的核心冲突点：即对速度/效率的渴求与对创作过程的控制力（Controllability）及创作主体性（Creative agency）的需求之间的矛盾。

Conclusion: 在音乐制作中，AI工具与人类创作者之间存在关于效率、可控性与创作主体性的张力。未来的工具设计应致力于平衡自动化与创作者的掌控权，以缓解这些冲突。

Abstract: In this paper, we present the results of an ethnographic study into the impact of AI and automated tools on music production workflow. Focusing specifically on professional participants who identified as recording engineers, mixers, and producers, we discuss their usage of common AI and automated software, as well as their sentiments on the proliferation of these tools. We discuss tensions that may be created between users and automated tools in key areas such as the need for speed and efficiency, controllability, and maintaining creative agency, and how these tensions may be alleviated through tool design.

</details>


### [209] [Make LLM Learn to Synthesize from Streaming Experiences through Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29940)
*Zhenlin Hu,Yan Wang,Zhen Bi,Zihao Xue,Bingyu Zhu,Longtao Huang,Xiongtao Zhang,Zeyu Yang,Zhixuan Chu,Jungang Lou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对合成数据生成任务，提出SynLearner框架，实现模型从序贯任务流中积累与迁移合成经验，从而提升生成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多将合成数据生成视为独立的孤立任务，忽略了模型可以通过积累过往任务经验并迁移至未来任务的能力。

Method: 提出了SynLearner框架，该框架旨在通过任务流积累合成经验，通过探索多元生成模式、从反馈中学习，并平衡样本质量与集合多样性。

Result: 在多个基准测试中表现出显著的跨任务可迁移性，证明了SynLearner能够有效利用历史任务经验提升未来任务的生成性能。

Conclusion: 合成数据生成应被视为一个经验驱动的过程，通过任务流学习可以实现跨任务的经验迁移，从而提升后续任务的效果。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have been widely adopted for synthetic data generation, significantly reducing annotation costs. However, most existing studies treat synthesis as a set of isolated tasks and overlook a more fundamental question: whether a model can learn to synthesize by accumulating experience from past tasks and transferring it to future ones. In this work, we introduce StreamSynth, a new setting in which synthesis tasks arrive sequentially and experience from historical tasks provides informative signals for future synthesis. To address this setting, we propose SynLearner, a general framework that enables synthesis models to acquire reusable synthesis experience over a task stream. Instead of generating data independently for each task, SynLearner encourages the model to explore diverse synthesis patterns, learn from feedback, and balance sample quality with set-level diversity as tasks evolve. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks show that SynLearner effectively leverages experience from earlier tasks to improve synthesis performance on later ones, exhibiting consistent cross-task transferability. These findings provide evidence for the feasibility of StreamSynth and highlight synthetic data generation as an experience-driven process that can benefit from task streams.

</details>


### [210] [MuPHI: Learning Implicit Multimodal Harm Reasoning via Semantically Grounded Reward Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29951)
*Anisha Saha,Varsha Suresh,Teodora Kamova,Sophia Wiedmann,Timothy Hospedales,Vera Demberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对视觉语言模型难以识别隐晦多模态有害信息的问题，提出了MuPHI数据集与MuPHIRM训练框架，通过强化推理导向的奖励机制，显著提升了模型检测有害语义及进行深度推理的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉语言模型（VLM）在处理表面线索方面表现良好，但在理解依赖隐含语境、具有复合性质的有害意图方面存在显著缺陷，缺乏跨模态的深度推理能力。

Method: 1. 构建了MuPHI数据集，涵盖需深层语境理解的隐晦多模态有害内容； 2. 提出了MuPHIRM训练框架，通过引入多视角奖励（multi-perspective rewards）机制，强化模型的有害语义推理与检测能力。

Result: 实验表明，MuPHIRM显著提升了模型对隐晦有害内容的识别准确率和生成的推理链质量，在分布外（OOD）数据上相比基准方法表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出基于跨模态推理的奖励优化策略（MuPHIRM）能有效提升视觉语言模型在隐晦有害内容检测与推理上的性能，并增强其泛化能力与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Understanding how harm emerges from interaction between otherwise benign image-text pairs requires intent-aware cross-modal reasoning beyond surface-level features. Existing vision-language models (VLMs) excel at literal reasoning over perceptual cues but often fail to derive harmful semantics that rely on implicit, context-dependent reasoning. To evaluate VLMs on compositional harm detection and reasoning, we introduce Multimodal Pragmatic Harm Interpretation (MuPHI), a dataset containing image-text pairs where harm is encoded in subtle multimodal cues. MuPHI spans diverse harm categories and includes annotated harm rationales for assessing VLM reasoning chains. To improve both detection and reasoning in VLMs, we propose MuPHIRM, a reasoning-augmented training framework which learns joint semantics by optimizing multi-perspective rewards. MuPHIRM improves both harm detection and reasoning quality of VLMs while demonstrating superior out-of-distribution robustness compared to both trained and inference-time baselines. Our findings suggest that reasoning-oriented reward optimization offers a promising direction towards building multimodal systems that generalize beyond benchmark-specific shortcuts.

</details>


### [211] [Formalizing Mathematics at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29955)
*Ahmad Rammal,Niket Patel,Fabian Gloeckle,Amaury Hayat,Julia Kempe,Remi Munos,Charles Arnal,Vivien Cabannes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 AutoformBot 系统，通过多智能体协作及自动化技术，成功将 26 本数学教科书大规模地自动形式化为可验证的 Lean 4 代码库（Atlas）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了通过大规模自动形式化（Autoformalization）将教科书内容转化为机器可验证的定义和证明，建立一个可扩展的数学知识库。

Method: 开发了 AutoformBot 多智能体系统，利用大量大语言模型智能体、形式化验证工具、依赖感知任务调度和协作版本控制系统，将非形式化教科书内容翻译为 Lean 4 代码。

Result: 构建了 Atlas 代码库，包含 26 本开源数学教材，涵盖分析、代数、拓扑等领域，总计超过 45,000 个 Lean 4 声明和 50 万行代码。

Conclusion: 自动形式化研究生水平的核心数学内容在经济和技术上已可行，为研究级别数学的自动化验证奠定了基础。

Abstract: We present AutoformBot, a multi-agent system for building an Autoformalized Textbook Library At Scale (Atlas) in Lean 4. AutoformBot orchestrates thousands of LLM agents, equipped with formal verification tools, dependency-aware task scheduling, and collaborative version control, to translate informal textbook prose into machine-checked definitions and proofs. We apply our methods to a corpus of 26 open-access textbooks spanning analysis, algebra, topology, combinatorics, and probability, producing Atlas: a verified library of over 45,000 Lean 4 declarations and 500 thousand lines of code. We release two artifacts: (i) AutoformBot, the open-source multi-agent framework; and (ii) Atlas, the resulting formal library. Our results suggest that autoformalizing the core content of graduate-level mathematics at scale is now economically and technically feasible. This opens the door to the automated verification of both human- and machine-generated mathematics at a research level.

</details>


### [212] [Meta-Programming for Linear-time Temporal Answer Set Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29965)
*Susana Hahn,Amade Nems,Javier Romero,Torsten Schaub*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对现有 ASP 系统扩展灵活度不足的问题，本文通过改进 clingo 的元编程机制，构建了一个名为 metasp 的统一框架，实现了多种时序逻辑（TEL、DEL、MEL）的高效建模与推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 ASP 系统在实现非单调线性时序（TEL）、动态（DEL）和度量（MEL）逻辑时往往因为系统架构的刚性，导致难以快速探索和验证不同的时序逻辑设计。

Method: 提出了一种灵活的 ASP 元编程框架：通过扩展 clingo 的理论语法（添加形式类型规范和嵌套功能），并引入特定的转换流水线（Transformation Pipeline）来保护嵌套模态，防止在 grounding 阶段被误简化，从而在统一的框架下实现多种时序逻辑的语义。

Result: 成功实现了包括 TEL、MEL 和 DEL 在内的元编码，并开发了 metasp 工具，能够处理 MEL 中的区间约束和 DEL 中的 Fischer-Ladner 闭包等复杂逻辑结构。

Conclusion: 本文提出并实现了 metasp 系统，证明了通过元编程架构能够灵活、统一地实现和扩展多种时序逻辑（TEL, MEL, DEL），为非单调时序推理提供了一个高效的实验与部署平台。

Abstract: The development of temporal extensions of Answer Set Programming (ASP) has led to the emergence of non-monotonic linear-time (TEL), dynamic (DEL), and metric (MEL) temporal equilibrium logics. However, the inherent rigidity of highly optimized ASP systems often hinders the rapid exploration and implementation of alternative logical designs. In this work, we propose a flexible meta-programming framework that operationalizes the semantics of varied temporal logics through a unified, declarative framework. Our approach extends standard ASP meta-programming by augmenting clingo's theory grammar with formal type specifications and nesting capabilities. To ensure semantic correctness, we introduce a transformation pipeline that protects nested modalities from stable-model-based simplifications during grounding. We demonstrate the extensibility of our framework by implementing meta-encodings for TEL, MEL, and DEL. We provide a comprehensive account of TEL and highlight the key features for managing the interval constraints of MEL and the Fischer-Ladner closure in DEL. Finally, we introduce the metasp system, a versatile tool that encapsulates this workflow.

</details>


### [213] [Compass: Navigating Global Marine Lead Data Integration through Expert-Guided LLM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29966)
*Yiming Liu,Bin Lu,Meng Jin,Ziyuan Sang,Shuo Jiang,Lei Zhou,Xinbing Wang,Chenghu Zhou,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对海洋科学文献中数据难以利用的问题，开发了 Compass 框架，利用专家知识树指导 LLM 进行跨文献数据提取，成功构建了大规模、高精度的海洋铅同位素数据库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋铅（Pb）同位素数据对于研究海洋循环与污染至关重要，但分散在海量学术文献中的非结构化数据难以利用，且手动提取效率低下、现有通用大模型直接应用易产生幻觉，缺乏领域专业性。

Method: 提出了 Compass 框架，结合“知识树（Knowledge Tree）”与专家引导的人机协同机制，将复杂的科学数据提取任务分解为可验证的推理步骤，无需微调即可实现高精度信息提取与多层级验证。

Result: 通过对 23 万篇论文进行自动化挖掘，提取出 3,751 条新增 Pb 记录，构建了目前最大的整合型海洋 Pb 数据库；经专家验证，提取准确率高达 92%，弥补了东海和南大洋等区域的数据空白。

Conclusion: 专家引导的智能体（Expert-guided agents）能有效桥接通用大模型与专业科学领域的鸿沟，为地球科学领域提供了一种可扩展、高可靠性的非结构化数据挖掘范式。

Abstract: Marine lead (Pb) and its isotopes are critical tracers for ocean circulation and anthropogenic pollution, yet in-situ observations remain costly and sparse. While vast historical records exist, they lie buried within the unstructured content of academic papers, creating "data silos" inaccessible to comprehensive analysis. Manual extraction is unscalable, while general-purpose Large Language Models (LLMs) lack the necessary domain-specific knowledge, leading to hallucinations and scientifically invalid outputs. To address this, we introduce an expert-guided adaptation approach that enables LLMs to perform rigorous scientific data extraction without fine-tuning. We operationalize this approach through Compass, an LLM agent framework enhanced by a Knowledge Tree co-designed with marine scientists, which decomposes complex tasks into verifiable steps, guiding the agent's reasoning to ensure scientific validity. Deploying Compass across a corpus of over 230,000 relevant open-access papers, we successfully extract 3,751 previously unincorporated Pb records. This effort establishes the largest integrated marine Pb database to date. Beyond standard metrics, Compass demonstrates superior reliability through multi-layered validation, achieving 92% accuracy as confirmed through expert manual verification. The newly integrated data expand coverage in previously under-sampled regions such as the East China Sea and the Southern Ocean, providing an enriched data foundation for future scientific discoveries. We release an interactive visualization platform to facilitate open scientific access. Our work demonstrates that expert-guided agents can effectively bridge the gap between general-purpose LLMs and high-stakes scientific domains, enabling scalable data discovery in geosciences.

</details>


### [214] [Accelerating Constrained Decoding with Token Space Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29986)
*Michael Sullivan,Alexander Koller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CFGzip 是一种通过离线压缩 token 搜索空间来优化 LLM 结构化输出的技术，有效解决了复杂 CFG 约束解码过程中计算开销过大的问题，实现了显著的性能加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 CFG 约束解码引擎在处理复杂语法时，由于在每一解码步骤都需要在整个词表（vocabulary）空间内进行巨大的计算搜索，导致了不可接受的高延迟，限制了其在复杂任务中的应用。

Method: 提出了一种名为“CFGzip”的离线压缩技术，通过压缩大语言模型（LLM）的标记（token）搜索空间，从而减轻 CFG 解码引擎在处理每一步生成时的计算压力。

Result: 实验显示，CFGzip 在与最先进（SoTA）的语法引擎配合使用时，将延迟降低了两个数量级，总约束生成时间提升了高达 7.5 倍。

Conclusion: CFGzip 为复杂语法提供了一种高效的离线压缩方案，极大地降低了结构化生成任务的计算开销，使得在大规模场景下使用复杂 CFG 进行约束解码变得可行。

Abstract: To guarantee that an LLM's outputs conform to a specified structure, context-free grammar (CFG) decoding engines force the selection of next tokens that produce strings that conform to a given CFG. While current CFG-constrained decoding engines are highly optimized, the inherent costs arising from the massive per-step search space -- i.e. the entire token vocabulary -- result in intractably high overhead for more complex CFGs: precisely the situation where CFG engines are most useful. In this paper, we introduce CFGzip, an offline technique for compressing the token search space, which massively reduces CFG engine overhead. In experiments, we report latency reduction of up to two orders of magnitude when CFGzip is used with a SoTA grammar engine, yielding an up to 7.5x speedup in total constrained generation time: with CFGzip, constrained decoding is now feasible at scale for complex CFGs.

</details>


### [215] [Cookie-Bench: Continuous On-screen Key Interaction Evaluation for Web Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30000)
*Haoyue Yang,Zhangxiao Shen,Fan Ding,Hangting Lou,Yifeng Kou,Haoqing Yu,Jingyao Li,Zhengfan Wu,Siqi Bao,Jing Liu,Hua Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个无需参考答案、由智能体驱动的端到端评估框架 Meta-Agent，配合 Cookie 基准测试集，填补了自动化评估无法模拟人类专家对交互式前端代码进行深度、全面的定性分析的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的前端网页自动化评估主要依赖参考实现、测试用例或固定检查表，缺乏人类评审员在观察实时交互过程中所体现的全面推理与判断能力，导致评估缺乏扩展性且不够精准。

Method: 构建了名为 WebDev 的基准测试集 Cookie（包含11个领域、1,000个查询），并提出名为 Meta-Agent 的评估框架，仿照元认知监控，分为“静态感知”、“智能体驱动交互”和“动态评分（基于证据链的全面判决）”三个阶段。

Result: 实验表明，Meta-Agent 框架在处理复杂网页生成任务时与人类专家评估高度一致，且通过该框架对 13 个主流 LLM 的评测发现，模型在交互式网页生成方面仍有巨大提升空间。

Conclusion: Meta-Agent 评估框架不仅与人类专家评分具有高度相关性，还能有效揭示前沿模型在交互式网页生成任务中存在的显著性能上限。

Abstract: Front-end web code has become a core product surface for every frontier LLM release, yet evaluating these interactive applications at development speed remains costly because human-judged leaderboards like Arena do not scale. Existing automated proxies typically lean on reference implementations, test suites, or rigid checklists, and tend to miss the reasoned synthesis a human reviewer performs over a live session. We articulate a new evaluation regime that is simultaneously reference-free, autonomously driven, and holistically reasoned, and instantiate it through two artifacts. \textbf{\dataname} is an 11-domain, 54-leaf, 1,000-query WebDev benchmark spanning both static-presentation and interactive-application tasks, balanced across three difficulty tiers and three target-language groups, with briefs rewritten to resist recall from circulated prompts. \textbf{\framename}, grounded in Flavell's metacognitive monitoring, separates evidence accumulation from judgment across three stages: Static Perception forms a first impression from passive observation; Agent-Driven Interaction explores the application autonomously while capturing continuous screen video, audio, and per-step screenshots; Dynamic Scoring issues holistic functionality and aesthetics verdicts with structured failure attribution only after the evidence chain is complete. On \dataname, \framename aligns closely with expert human ratings while surfacing substantial headroom across 13 frontier LLMs on interactive web generation. \noindenthttps://anonymous.4open.science/r/Cookie-3CE/

</details>


### [216] [From GPS Points to Travel Patterns: Flexible and Semantic Trajectory Generation with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30014)
*Silin Zhou,Chenhao Wang,Yuntao Wen,Shuo Shang,Lisi Chen,Panos Kalnis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HTP 是一种利用 LLM 进行轨迹生成的新框架，通过 RQ-VAE 将轨迹分层建模为旅行模式 Token 再生成 GPS 点，克服了传统方法灵活性差和模式捕捉不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的轨迹生成方法难以显式捕捉复杂的旅行模式，且通常被限制为单条件、固定长度的生成，同时需解决城市轨迹在智能城市应用中的隐私保护需求。

Method: 提出 HTP 模型：1. 设计轨迹专用 RQ-VAE，将 GPS 序列量化为紧凑的宏观旅行模式 Token；2. 扩展 LLM 词表并利用监督微调（SFT）进行训练，实现多条件下的旅行模式序列生成。

Result: 在两个真实世界数据集上，HTP 的生成质量比最强基线模型平均提升了 29.78%。

Conclusion: HTP 通过将轨迹生成从直接生成坐标转变为分层建模（先模式后坐标），有效地解决了现有方法在捕捉旅行模式和灵活性方面的局限性，在生成质量上显著超越了现有基准。

Abstract: Urban trajectories play a crucial role in modeling urban dynamics and supporting various smart city applications. However, privacy concerns restrict access to large-scale and high-quality trajectory datasets. Trajectory generation provides a promising alternative by synthesizing realistic data to mitigate privacy risks. However, existing methods fail to explicitly capture travel patterns and can only generate fixed-length trajectories under a single condition. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{HTP}, which \textbf{H}ierarchically generates \textbf{T}ravel patterns first and then generates GPS \textbf{P}oints by using large language models (LLMs), rather than directly generating GPS points. We first design a trajectory-specific residual quantization variational autoencoder (RQ-VAE) that quantizes micro-level GPS trajectories into compact, macro-level travel pattern tokens in a coarse-to-fine manner. These tokens capture rich segment spatial irregularities, such as point density variations caused by traffic conditions. Then, we extend the LLM vocabulary with travel pattern tokens to align trajectory representations with the LLM input, and apply supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to align the LLM with the trajectory generation task, enabling generation of travel pattern sequences under various conditions. Extensive experiments on two real-world datasets show that HTP outperforms the strongest baseline by an average of 29.78\% in terms of generation quality. Our code is available at https://github.com/slzhou-xy/HTP.

</details>


### [217] [RAISE: RAG Design as an Architecture Search Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30029)
*Zhen Chen,Yibing Liu,Weihao Xie,Yu Liang,Peilin Chen,Shiqi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了RAISE（RAG智能搜索引擎），这是一个用于RAG超参数优化和架构搜索的框架与基准，旨在解决当前RAG设计过于依赖启发式方法、缺乏系统评估的问题。研究发现，并没有一种通用的优化策略适用于所有场景，强调了领域特定评估的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RAG系统设计选择通常依赖启发式方法（heuristics），缺乏系统性的评估和可复现性，因此作者提出将其转化为“RAG架构搜索”问题。

Method: 构建了RAG智能搜索引擎（RAISE）框架，涵盖了检索增强生成流程中的查询重写、分块、检索深度、重排序及上下文压缩等关键设计选择。实现了13种搜索算法，并在7个公共文本及多模态数据集上进行了标准化实验。

Result: 实验表明，检索增强生成管道的优化效果随数据集和任务的不同而产生显著差异，单一指标的排名不应被视为通用策略优越性的证据。

Conclusion: RAG系统的超参数优化高度依赖于具体任务，不存在一种在所有数据集上都始终最优的通用策略。RAISE为该领域的研究提供了一个公平、可复现和系统化的实验基准。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems expose numerous design choices spanning query rewriting, chunking, retrieval depth, reranking, and context compression. In practice, these choices are often configured through heuristics, hindering systematic evaluation and reproducibility across settings. We argue that this challenge is best formulated as RAG architecture search. To support controlled and reproducible study of this problem, we introduce the RAG Intelligence Search Engine (RAISE), a comprehensive framework and benchmark for RAG hyperparameter optimization, which evaluates optimization methods for RAG pipelines under standardized search spaces and budgets. RAISE implements 13 search algorithms and evaluates them across seven public text and multimodal datasets using three random seeds. Our experiments show that optimization performance is highly task-dependent: methods that perform strongly on one dataset may not generalize consistently across others, cautioning against interpreting aggregate rankings as evidence of universally superior strategies. RAISE provides a common experimental substrate for fair, reproducible, and systematic research on RAG hyperparameter optimization.

</details>


### [218] [Teaching Values to Machines: Simulating Human-Like Behavior in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30036)
*Asaf Yehudai,Naama Rozen,Ariel Gera*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过大规模心理学实验证明，大语言模型可以通过价值引导展现出与真实人类一致的价值结构与行为模式，从而成为有效的社会科学模拟工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前尚不清楚大语言模型（LLM）是否能表现出符合连贯、类人价值体系的行为，旨在探索LLM的价值一致性及其模拟人类行为的效度。

Method: 借鉴心理学价值理论，通过提示工程（Prompting）诱导LLM构建类人价值结构；利用大规模验证过的心理学问卷（超过500万个问题）对主要LLM进行测试，并与人类评估数据进行对比分析。

Result: 研究发现，经过价值诱导的LLM在价值结构和价值主导的行为逻辑上与人类表现出高度一致性；通过引入人类价值分布，显著提升了LLM在人口统计学层面的模拟质量。

Conclusion: 基于价值引导的大语言模型展现了与人类高度一致的价值结构和行为模式，证明其作为具备心理学基础的社会模拟工具的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate a remarkable capacity to adopt different personas and roles; however, it remains unclear whether they can manifest behavior that adheres to a coherent, human-like value structure. In this work, we draw on established psychological value theory to induce human-like values in LLMs and assess their alignment with patterns observed in human studies. Using validated psychological questionnaires, we conduct large-scale experiments -- over 5 million questions -- to evaluate value structures and value-behavior relationships in leading LLMs and compare them to humans. Our findings reveal strong agreement between value-prompted LLMs and humans across both dimensions. Moreover, incorporating human value distributions enhances population-level simulations with value-induced LLMs. These findings highlight the potential of value-induced LLMs as effective, psychologically grounded tools for simulating human behavior.

</details>


### [219] [Domain-Specific Data Synthesis for LLMs via Minimal Sufficient Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30039)
*Tong Ye,Hang Yu,Tengfei Ma,Xuhong Zhang,Jianguo Li,Peng Di,Peiyu Liu,Jianwei Yin,Wenhai Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DOMINO 提出了一种无需手动描述的归纳式数据合成框架，通过从示例中自动提取领域特征来指导高质量、高多样性的数据生成，显著提升了大模型的领域适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的领域数据合成多依赖演绎式范式（即手动撰写领域描述或提示词），在难以显式描述的领域面临瓶颈；旨在解决如何仅通过少数参考示例实现高效、可扩展的领域特异性数据合成。

Method: 提出 DOMINO 框架：采用归纳式范式，利用提示微调（Prompt Tuning）结合对比解耦（Contrastive Disentanglement）目标函数，从少量参考示例中学习简约的领域表征，过滤样本噪声并引导高质量数据合成。

Result: 在复杂的编码基准测试中，使用 DOMINO 合成数据微调的模型，相比于现有的指令微调基线模型，Pass@1 准确率最高提升了 4.63%，证明了其提升模型领域适应能力的有效性。

Conclusion: DOMINO 证明了通过归纳学习从少量参考样本中自动提取领域特征进行数据合成的可行性，为领域自适应提供了一种无需显式描述的自动化范式。

Abstract: Large Language Models have demonstrated remarkable progress in general-purpose capabilities and can achieve strong performance in specific domains through fine-tuning on domain-specific data. However, acquiring high-quality data for target domains remains a significant challenge. Existing data synthesis approaches follow a deductive paradigm, heavily relying on explicit domain descriptions expressed in natural language and careful prompt engineering, limiting their applicability in real-world scenarios where domains are difficult to describe or formally articulate. In this work, we tackle the underexplored problem of domain-specific data synthesis through an inductive paradigm, where the target domain is defined only through a set of reference examples, particularly when domain characteristics are difficult to articulate in natural language. We propose a novel framework, DOMINO, that learns a minimal sufficient domain representation from reference samples and leverages it to guide the generation of domain-aligned synthetic data. DOMINO integrates prompt tuning with a contrastive disentanglement objective to separate domain-level patterns from sample-specific noise, mitigating overfitting while preserving core domain characteristics. Theoretically, we prove that DOMINO expands the support of the synthetic data distribution, ensuring greater diversity. Empirically, on challenging coding benchmarks where domain definitions are implicit, fine-tuning on data synthesized by DOMINO improves Pass@1 accuracy by up to 4.63\% over strong, instruction-tuned backbones, demonstrating its effectiveness and robustness. This work establishes a new paradigm for domain-specific data synthesis, enabling practical and scalable domain adaptation without manual prompt design or natural language domain specifications.

</details>


### [220] [Learning to Choose: An Empowerment-Guided Multi-Agent System with semantic communication for Adaptive Method Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30042)
*Geremy Loachamín-Suntaxi,Robert Lazar,Dimitrios G. Giovanis,Ioannis G. Kevrekidis,Eleni D. Koronaki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 为解决多智能体科学计算中的“语义漂移”问题，本研究引入了带有“语义检查点”的框架，确保了代码执行与原始策略的一致性，从而提高了系统在复杂科学任务中的可靠性与适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化科学计算工作流（特别是多智能体流程）中存在“语义漂移（semantic drift）”问题，即智能体的意图与实际执行的策略不一致，导致下游评估失效。需要一种机制来保证决策与执行的保真度。

Method: 提出了一种结合上下文老虎机（contextual bandits）、结构化多智能体通信以及关键的“语义检查点（semantic checkpoints）”的多智能体框架。系统集成了专门的LLM智能体、基于证据的代码生成以及自愈执行循环。

Result: 通过敏感性分析和不确定性量化工作流验证，表明该框架能有效抑制语义漂移，显著提升策略学习的收敛性、稳健性以及对新问题的适应能力。

Conclusion: 在科学多智能体系统中，自适应决策必须与确保语义一致性和可靠信息流的显式机制相结合，这是构建鲁棒性系统的重要设计原则。

Abstract: Automating scientific computing workflows requires more than generating executable code: autonomous systems must also select appropriate computational strategies, implement them faithfully, and ensure that the resulting outcomes remain causally attributable to the decisions that produced them.
