In multi-agent pipelines, this process is particularly fragile, as small inconsistencies between agent intentions and actions can lead to semantic drift, where the eventually executed procedure no longer reflects the originally selected strategy, thereby corrupting downstream evaluation and adaptation. In this work, motivated by the ATHENA framework (Toscano et al., 2025; Toscano et al., 2026) and the concept of empowerment (Yiu et al., 2025), we introduce a multi-agent framework that combines contextual bandits with structured inter-agent communication and, most importantly, semantic checkpoints that preserve action-outcome fidelity throughout the pipeline. The system integrates specialized large language model (LLM) agents, grounded code generation, and self-healing execution loops within an adaptive decision-making architecture. Interpreting the framework through the lens of empowerment, we show that reliable autonomous learning requires not only identifying high-quality actions, but also preserving the integrity of their propagation across agents. Using sensitivity analysis and uncertainty quantification workflows as representative case studies, we demonstrate that unchecked semantic drift degrades policy learning, whereas the proposed framework improves convergence, robustness, and adaptation to novel problem contexts. These results suggest a broader design principle for scientific multi-agent systems: adaptive decision-making must be coupled with explicit mechanisms that guarantee semantic consistency and reliable information flow across the computational pipeline.

</details>


### [221] [Robust and Generalizable Safety Steering for Text-to-Image Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30049)
*Zihao Xue,Yan Wang,Zhen Bi,Long Ma,Zhonglong Zheng,Zeyu Yang,Bingyu Zhu,Longtao Huang,Jie Xiao,Jungang Lou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SafeDIG 是一种针对 Diffusion Transformers 的安全转向框架，通过位置感知的稀疏特征迁移，在保障生成质量的前提下，有效防御跨领域和演变的有害内容生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiT 的层级和跨模态特性导致有害语义在生成过程中逐渐深化与纠缠，传统的 prompt 过滤或输出检测手段不足，且单一层级的安全转向往往不稳定，对新风险域的迁移能力差。

Method: 提出了 SafeDIG 框架，主要包括：在不同 DiT 层级构建稀疏自编码器 (SAE)；采用鲁棒感知预训练路由选择稳定的干预位点；冻结 SAE 编码器作为可复用的安全字典，仅微调解码器以适应目标领域；推理时结合 Blend（融合）和 Repel（排斥）操作进行激活转向。

Result: 在 FLUX.1 Dev 和 Stable Diffusion 3.5 Large 模型上的实验表明，SafeDIG 在显著降低目标领域及整体不安全生成率的同时，成功保持了源领域的安全性和图像质量。

Conclusion: SafeDIG 通过位置感知稀疏特征迁移，提供了对 Diffusion Transformers (DiT) 的稳健且可迁移的安全转向方案，有效解决了现有安全控制在层级与跨模态生成过程中的局限性。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers have become a powerful backbone for text-to-image generation, but their layered and cross-modal generation process makes safety control fundamentally different from prompt-level filtering or output-level detection. Harmful semantics may be weakly expressed in text representations, progressively bound to visual latents, and finally entangled with rendering dynamics. As a result, safety steering at a fixed layer can be unstable, and a steering mechanism learned from known risks may not transfer reliably to a shifted target risk domain. We propose SafeDIG, a safety steering framework that formulates DiT safety adaptation as position-aware sparse feature transfer. SafeDIG first constructs Sparse Autoencoders over functionally distinct DiT intervention positions and uses robustness-aware pre-training routing to prioritize intervention sites that are expected to remain stable under source-target risk shift. It then separates transferable safety features from domain-specific activation geometry by freezing the SAE encoder as a reusable sparse safety dictionary and adapting only the decoder to the target-domain activation manifold. During inference, SafeDIG combines Blend and Repel operations to steer unsafe activations toward transferred safety manifolds or away from harmful sparse directions. Experiments on FLUX.1 Dev and Stable Diffusion 3.5 Large show that SafeDIG consistently reduces target-domain and overall unsafe generation rates while preserving source-domain safety and image quality.

</details>


### [222] [Selective QA over Conflicting Multi-Source Personal Memory: A Diagnostic Testbed and Method Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30087)
*Tiancheng Yang,Matthias Schonlau,Ilia Sucholutsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了针对个人智能体多源冲突记忆的选择性问答基准，揭示了相较于通用LLM，专门设计的融合解析器在处理冲突信息时展现出更高的推理准确度与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的个人AI智能体需处理来自多个冲突或不完整记忆源的信息，但现有基准无法有效拆解错误来源（是源信息错误还是冲突解决机制失效），缺乏针对多源冲突记忆的选择性问答评估标准。

Method: 构建包含18个问答模板、8种推理类型及34,560个实例的大规模基准数据集；对比了无源基线、单源基线、结构化融合方法（Fusion Resolvers）以及前沿LLM在冲突解决与选择性回答任务上的表现。

Result: 结构化融合模型在准确率（80.3%）和选择性准确率（85.3%）上均优于最强LLM基线（对应指标为70.0%和71.0%）。此外，不同模型在处理不同推理类型时呈现出明显的性能差异。

Conclusion: 研究构建了针对个人记忆冲突场景的评估基准，发现专用融合模型在处理冲突时优于通用LLM。该研究强调了在处理不完整或冲突信息时，具备“选择性回答（abstention）”能力对于提升系统可靠性至关重要。

Abstract: Emerging personal AI agents are moving toward persistent, multi-source memory. This creates an evaluation problem: systems must decide how to use conflicting or incomplete evidence; they cannot just retrieve facts from one clean history. Existing benchmarks rarely show whether an error came from the evidence given to a method or from the method's conflict-resolution step. We study this as selective QA over conflicting multi-source personal memory: systems answer based on conflicting, sometimes incomplete sources, or abstain when evidence is insufficient. We develop a benchmark containing 18 question templates across 8 reasoning types, 480 personas, 4 random seeds, and 34,560 instances, with controlled source distortions and deterministic ground truth. We evaluate the performance of baselines without access to any source, access to a single source, structured fusion methods, and frontier LLMs. The best trained fusion resolver reaches 80.3% accuracy, while the strongest prompt-only LLM baseline reaches 70.0%. With abstention, the same resolver reaches 85.3% selective accuracy at 78.3% coverage and the best LLM reaches 71.0% selective accuracy at 95.4% coverage. Different models have different strengths across reasoning types. We release the data, code, cached model outputs, and data-generating process for reuse.

</details>


### [223] [VLA-Trace: Diagnosing Vision-Language-Action Models through Representation and Behavior Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30117)
*Haoyuan Shi,Xiancong Ren,Yingji Zhang,Qinfan Zhang,Jiayu Hu,Haozhe Shan,Han Dong,Jinpeng Lu,Yinda Chen,Yi Zhang,Yong Dai,Xiaozhu Ju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了VLA-Trace框架，通过诊断表示演变、因果回路和行为表现，揭示并对比了主流VLA模型在微调与决策过程中的内部运作机理与局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前理解视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型如何将多模态知识转化为具身控制仍是一个未解决的挑战。

Method: 提出VLA-Trace诊断框架，包含三个模块：1. 基于交叉模态和检查点漂移的中心核对齐（CKA）来追踪表示演变；2. 注意力敲除干预（Attention knockout）以识别模态特异性控制器通路；3. 展开层面的行为探针以检查接地、捷径依赖及语义遵循情况。

Result: 1. π0.5和OpenVLA在微调过程中表现出不同的模态特异性适应动态；2. 模型在动作解码时采用不同的多模态路由策略和层级依赖；3. 指出了模型在细颗粒度语义跟随上的缺陷。

Conclusion: VLA模型展现了良好的视觉接地轨迹生成能力，但在细颗粒度语义遵循方面仍存在局限。研究强调了未来应关注表示保持的适配策略、VLA因果回路分析以及组合式语义控制。

Abstract: Understanding how Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models transform multimodal knowledge into embodied control remains an open challenge. We present VLA-Trace, a progressive diagnostic framework that analyzes VLA models through a unified evidence chain from representation dynamics to causal control attribution and behavioral manifestation. It specifically combines cross-modal and checkpoint-drift centered kernel alignment (CKA) to trace representation evolution, attention knockout interventions to identify modality-specific control pathways, and rollout-level behavioral probes to examine grounding, shortcut dependence, and semantic following. Experiments on $π_{0.5}$ and OpenVLA reveal three key findings. First, the two models exhibit distinct modality-specific adaptation dynamics during VLA finetuning. Second, they rely on different multimodal routing strategies and layer-wise dependencies during action decoding. Third, although VLA policies excel at visually grounded trajectory generation, they remain limited in fine-grained semantic following. These findings highlight future directions for representation-preserving adaptation, causal VLA circuits, and compositional semantic control.

</details>


### [224] [Enhancing Multi-Agent Communication through Attention Steering with Context Relevance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30136)
*Hongxiang Zhang,Yuan Tian,Tianyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 为了解决多智能体系统中长对话导致的上下文稀释和性能下降问题，本文提出了 Agent-Radar，这是一种无需训练的方法，利用时空衰减机制动态聚焦关键上下文，显著提升了系统性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）多智能体系统在处理复杂任务时，对话历史会快速增长，导致无关上下文对关键信息的稀释，从而造成性能退化。因此，需要一种有效的机制来管理长上下文。

Method: 提出了一种无需训练（training-free）的上下文管理方法 Agent-Radar。该方法引入了一种新颖的“时空衰减机制（temporal and spatial decay mechanism）”，能够动态地引导智能体将注意力集中于相关上下文中。

Result: 在五个基准测试中表现优于现有的先进方法，最高提升了 7.64 个百分点；实验证明该方法在处理大量智能体和多轮交互时依然保持高效和鲁棒。

Conclusion: Agent-Radar 是一种有效且鲁棒的上下文管理方法。通过时间与空间衰减机制，它能够缓解长对话中无关信息对关键信息的稀释问题，提升多智能体系统在复杂任务中的表现，且具有良好的通用性。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems have demonstrated remarkable performance on complex tasks through collaborative reasoning. However, these systems tend to rapidly accumulate extremely long conversation histories during interaction. As conversations lengthen, relevant information is increasingly diluted by irrelevant context, leading to degraded performance. In this work, we present Agent-Radar, a training-free context management method that dynamically steers each agent's attention toward relevant context with a novel temporal and spatial decay mechanism. Our experiments demonstrate that Agent-Radar outperforms state-of-the-art methods across five different benchmarks, yielding gains of up to 7.64 absolute points. Furthermore, our analysis shows that Agent-Radar remains effective and robust as the number of agents and interaction rounds increases. Finally, the ablation study shows that core components in Agent-Radar are crucial to performance and generalizable in different settings.

</details>


### [225] [AgentSchool: An LLM-Powered Multi-Agent Simulation for Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30144)
*Yulei Ye,Wenhao Li,Zhong Wen,Yunshu Huang,Yichen Hu,Zifan Wei,Yige Wang,Xinyu Xie,Haoxuan Yang,Yanjun Huang,Ruijia Li,Hong Qian,Yu Song,Bo Jiang,Bingdong Li,Lijun Li,Bo Zhang,Pinlong Cai,Xingcheng Xu,Shuangye Chen,Xia Hu,Liang He,Aimin Zhou,Jingjing Qu,Jing Shao,Xiangfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentSchool是一个基于LLM的多智能体教育模拟器，旨在通过状态转移建模模拟真实的认知发展和社会动力学，为教育评估、 pedagogical（教学法）创新和AI多智能体研究提供了一个高保真的实验沙盘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI教育验证受限于现实世界的伦理、时间及体制障碍；目前的教育AI模拟器多局限于简单的角色扮演，无法模拟真实教育中的认知发展和系统性制度改革，因此需要更具交互性、多智能体协作且具备社会化的模拟框架。

Method: 提出AgentSchool框架：(1) 将学习建模为状态转换过程；(2) 构建具有可生长知识图谱、思考工作流及显性认知偏差的学生智能体；(3) 设计基于最近发展区（ZPD）理论的自适应教师智能体；(4) 开发多尺度场景生成器，解耦交互尺度、时间粒度和模拟时长。

Result: 实验表明，相比传统基线，AgentSchool的学生智能体展示了更丰富、差异化的掌握程度和认知偏差演变；教师智能体展示了符合ZPD理论的自适应教学行为；且成功模拟了课堂社会动力学现象（如边缘参与、小团体形成、舆论领袖涌现等）。

Conclusion: AgentSchool不仅是一种有效的教育研究工具，还为研究多智能体协作、长时记忆及组织系统下的长期推理提供了高价值的实验平台，将教育建模为一种社会行为过程。

Abstract: Despite the rapid deployment of LLMs into classrooms, validating educational AI remains uniquely intractable: interventions act on developing learners whose cognitive and social trajectories are irreversibly shaped, while real-world trials are slow, ethically constrained, and institutionally locked. LLM-based educational simulators have emerged as a potential remedy, but many still collapse learning into persona-conditioned role-play and, when optimized only to reproduce existing classrooms, can structurally penalize the institutional novelty that pedagogical reform requires. In this work, we introduce AgentSchool, an LLM-driven multi-agent simulator that models learning as state transition rather than prompted behavior. AgentSchool couples cognitively growable student agents -- equipped with weighted subject knowledge graphs, thinking-workflow pools, and explicit misconceptions -- with adaptive teacher agents that plan, scaffold, and reflect along the Zone of Proximal Development, embedded in a configurable scenery generator that situates instruction within both formal and informal learning fields, and a multi-scale simulator that decouples interaction scale, temporal granularity, and simulation duration. Experiments show that structured student agents produce more differentiated mastery and misconception traces than a baseline simulator, while teacher-agent comparisons show backbone-dependent patterns consistent with ZPD-informed adaptation. Further, AgentSchool generates plausible traces of peripheral participation, clique formation, aggressor-induced cohesion, and opinion-leader emergence consistent with classroom social theories. Beyond its role as an educational research instrument, AgentSchool frames education as a socially meaningful testbed for long-horizon memory, multi-agent coordination, and future institutional reasoning under organizational pressure.

</details>


### [226] [Anchorless Diversification for Parallel LLM Ideation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30150)
*Fares Nabil Ibrahim,Nafis Saami Azad,Raiyan Abdul Baten*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了大型语言模型在创意任务中多样的候选生成策略，证明“语义方向分层”比传统的基于锚点的方法更具效率和竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在大型语言模型（LLMs）生成创意任务候选池时，如何通过推理时控制实现高效的候选池多样化，特别关注无锚点方法是否能媲美依赖种子想法的传统方法。

Method: 比较了独立生成（Independent generation）和语义方向分层（Semantic direction stratification）两种无锚点（anchorless）方法，并与自参照、同伴参照和代表性锚点（self-, peer-, representative-anchor）基线进行了对比，涵盖了中性及基于群体参照（population-referential）的发散指令。

Result: 基于群体参照的指令是低成本且有效的基线；语义方向分层在多样性、质量和计算效率的权衡中表现最佳；基于锚点的重生成方法在考虑全流程计算开销时优势有限。

Conclusion: 语义方向分层（Semantic direction stratification）是实现创造性任务多样性、质量和计算成本之间最优权衡的有效方法，且在全流程计算评估中超过了基于锚点的（anchored）重生成方法。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly used to generate candidate-idea pools for creative tasks where broad exploration is valuable. Parallel inference can be attractive in this setting when it broadens the pool while retaining quality and cost efficiency. We study inference-time controls for candidate-pool diversification, asking whether anchorless methods can rival methods that depend on observed seed ideas. Across three creative task families, we compare independent generation and semantic direction stratification with self-, peer-, and representative-anchor baselines, under neutral and population-referential divergent instructions. Population-referential divergence is a strong low-cost baseline, increasing semantic diversity while preserving quality proxies. Semantic direction stratification is stronger: a single planning call organizes generations across broad semantic directions, yielding the best diversity--quality--compute frontier. Anchored regeneration can be strong in final-pool diversity, but its advantage shrinks under full-pipeline token accounting. These results establish practical anchorless baselines for open-ended LLM ideation.

</details>


### [227] [Temporal Stability and Few-Shot Prompting in Math Task Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30151)
*Danielle S. Fox,Brenda L. Robles,Elizabeth DiPietro Brovey,Christian D. Schunn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究发现，AI模型版本更新并不总是能直接提升教育专用任务的准确性，甚至可能导致性能退化；相比之下，通过少样本提示工程可以更有效地稳定和提升AI在教育评估任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨AI工具在教育应用中的稳定性及其对提示工程的响应能力，特别是在评估“模型版本更新”与“少样本提示”对AI执行专业教育评估任务（如数学任务认知需求分类）的影响。

Method: 采用纵向研究方法，对比了通用模型（Gemini）与教育专用模型（Coteach）在不同时间节点（版本更新前后）及不同提示策略（基准测试 vs. 少样本提示）下，运用Stein & Smith (1998) 的任务分析指南（TAG）对数学任务认知需求进行分类的准确性。

Result: 1. 模型更新的效应不确定：Gemini分类准确率保持在58%，Coteach从75%下降至50%；2. 少样本提示的提升效果显著：Gemini准确率提升至67%，Coteach回升至75%。证明了提示工程在提升模型任务表现上比被动的模型更新更具可靠性。

Conclusion: 在教育背景下，即便AI工具持续更新，其性能也未必稳定或提升。相比于依赖模型自身的版本迭代，运用少样本提示（few-shot prompting）等提示工程技术更能有效且可靠地优化AI在特定学术任务中的表现。因此，教育工作者和研究人员在筛选与应用AI工具时，不应仅依赖模型基准，而应结合针对性的提示策略进行验证。

Abstract: As AI tools become increasingly integrated into educational contexts, questions arise about both their stability over time and their responsiveness to prompt engineering techniques. This longitudinal study focused on different AI tools' ability to use the Task Analysis Guide (TAG; Stein \& Smith, 1998) to classify the cognitive demand of mathematics tasks. In particular, it examined whether this classification ability changed with (1) model version updates over time and (2) few-shot prompting using exemplar tasks. We tested a general-purpose AI tool (Gemini) and an education-specific AI tool (Coteach). The specific tools were selected because of their relatively high performance on relevant published benchmarks and prior task-specific tests. Models were tested at baseline, retested with model version updates, and then tested again using few-shot prompting (two exemplar tasks for each cognitive demand category). Results revealed that newer model versions alone produced mixed effects: Gemini's accuracy remained stable at 58\%, while Coteach's accuracy decreased from 75\% to 50\%. However, few-shot prompting improved both models' performance: Gemini increased to 67\% and Coteach recovered to 75\% accuracy. These findings demonstrate that prompt engineering techniques can have larger and more reliable effects than passive model improvements, and that version updates may not always improve performance on specialized educational tasks. The study has important implications for how educators and researchers should approach AI tool selection, evaluation, and implementation in educational contexts.

</details>


### [228] [BioRefusalAudit: Auditing Biosecurity Refusal Depth Using General and Domain-Fine-Tuned Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30162)
*Caleb DeLeeuw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究发现主流语言模型的生物安全防御机制极其脆弱，极易受提示词工程影响而失效或产生严重误报。研究指出拒绝行为更多受法律而非安全性驱动，并证明通过稀疏自编码器（SAE）审计模型内部激活，是探测这些隐藏失效模式的有效路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生物安全评估通常仅关注模型是否输出有害信息，忽略了模型拒绝机制的脆弱性。文章旨在揭示模型在面对简单对抗性修改时，拒绝机制是否会失效或产生过度过滤的现象。

Method: 1. 行为评估：通过改变提示词框架、格式、长度限制（如80 token限制）测试模型在不同生物安全查询下的拒绝稳定性。
2. 比较分析：对比模型对受管制但非毒性物质（如裸盖菇素）与真实生物危害物质的拒绝响应。
3. 内部审计：利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）分析模型内部特征激活值，通过定义分歧得分（D）来量化表面拒绝行为与内在决策的一致性。

Result: 1. 测试的模型在面对提示词修改时均未表现出良好的识别能力，很多模型在改变格式或长度后拒绝率降至0%。
2. 模型存在明显的过分拒绝（Over-refusal）现象，部分模型将80%以上的良性生物学内容标记为有害。
3. 模型拒绝行为倾向于基于“合法性”而非“生物危害性”。
4. 基于SAE的激活审计成功区分了“合规”与“拒绝”响应，且无重合，证实了审计内部表现的有效性。

Conclusion: 现有的语言模型安全拒绝机制在面对微小的提示词修改、格式变化或长度限制时极易失效或触发误报。目前的拒绝策略更多是基于法律合规性或文化敏感度，而非真正的生物安全风险。通过稀疏自编码器（SAE）进行内部激活审计，能够识别出行为层面无法探测的安全防御失败模式，是未来提升模型安全性的有效手段。

Abstract: Biosecurity evaluations of language models typically ask whether models produce hazardous output. This paper asks a complementary question: when a model refuses, is that refusal structurally sound, or does it disappear under modest changes to prompt framing, formatting, or output length? Across five architectures, no model cleanly discriminated benign from hazard. Gemma 2 2B-IT never genuinely refused across 75 prompts, hedging on every hazard-adjacent query. Gemma 4 E2B-IT refused 65/75 prompts with chat-template formatting and 0/75 without it. Both Gemma models collapsed to 0% under an 80-token cap. Qwen 2.5 1.5B and Phi-3-mini over-refused, flagging 83-87% of benign biology as hazardous. Llama 3.2 1B showed the only meaningful tier gradient (61-point spread). To probe what drives such over-refusal, we tested a panel of Schedule I but biologically non-toxic compounds (notably psilocybin cultivation, with FDA Breakthrough Therapy status). Some models refused these at rates exceeding genuinely hazardous biology, suggesting refusal tracks legality and cultural salience over CBRN hazard. To measure the internal side, we introduce a divergence score D comparing a model's surface response label to its internal sparse autoencoder (SAE) feature activations. Full D was computed on Gemma 2 2B-IT (Gemma Scope 1) and Gemma 4 E2B-IT (author-trained bio SAE). Two fine-tuned Gemma 2 domain SAEs were released. On Gemma 4, comply and refuse responses separated by a 0.647-point gap with zero overlap (n=75), though this is preliminary, with a narrow catalog, within-sample calibration, and Gemma-family-only SAE coverage. Built over one hackathon weekend on consumer hardware (GTX 1650 Ti Max-Q, plus Colab T4 for SAE training), this preliminary evidence suggests activation-level auditing may surface failure modes invisible to behavioral evaluation, with substantial variation across architectures.

</details>


### [229] [Modularizing Educational LLM-Agency for Fostering Responsible Learning Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30187)
*Julius Gabelmann,Felix Jahn,Kevin Baum,Sophie van Rossum,Emely Wuenscher,Timo P. Gros,Verena Wolf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文针对现有AI聊天机器人在教育中可能削弱学生思维能力的风险，提出了一种模块化的智能体架构，通过在习题解答的不同阶段嵌入教育学原则，旨在实现更负责任、透明且有效的AI辅助教学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用大语言模型（LLMs）虽然知识丰富，但在教育应用中缺乏对教育学原理的遵循，易导致学生产生认知依赖，损害其迁移能力、批判性思维与创造力，亟需一种更负责任、具备教学导向的AI教育架构。

Method: 提出了一种模块化的智能体（agentic）架构，专门用于学生习题解答场景。该架构将传统的单体式（monolithic）系统拆解为针对学习过程不同阶段的专用模块，以便在交互中整合教育学指导和教学策略。

Result: 论证了单体式大模型在教育场景下的结构性不足，并设计了一套可行的模块化教学框架，使AI辅助过程更具监管性、透明度和教育意义。

Conclusion: 通过模块化的智能体架构，可以将教育学原理有效嵌入AI辅助学习系统，从而克服通用大模型在教育应用中的结构性缺陷，实现更负责任、可控且透明的教学辅助。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of AI chatbots in education will drastically change learning, making responsible deployment a critical concern. While large language models (LLMs) might have access to sources discussing insights from educational sciences, they are not particularly inclined to adhere to pedagogical concepts, risking negative effects on the learning process, such as a loss of transfer capabilities, critical thinking, or creativity. In this paper, we introduce an agentic AI chatbot architecture assisting students with exercise solving, specifically designed to contribute to more responsible AI use in education. We base our conceptual development on the identification of several desiderata for responsible LLM-based educational systems, argue for the structural shortcomings inherent in monolithic, out-of-the-box solutions, and instead suggest modularizing the agentic architecture. We propose specific modules for different stages of exercise solving, enabling incorporation of targeted pedagogical advice, guiding students through the learning process in a more controllable, transparent, and overseeable manner.

</details>


### [230] [Double-Edged Sword or Sharp Tool? Designing and Evaluating Triadic LLM-Teacher Collaboration for K-12 Writing at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30200)
*Canran Wang,Yuwen Yang,Zhen Wang,Ming Ma,Ding Yu,Chentai Wang,Keman Huang,Xiaoyong Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文通过大规模实证研究表明，K-12写作教学中LLM与教师的“人机协作”能有效提升教学质量，但需警惕过度使用LLM带来的边际效用递减，建议协作模式应根据学生水平动态调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在K-12教育中，集成大语言模型（LLM）既是机遇也是挑战，需要建立有效的LLM、教师与学生之间的三元协作机制，以解决教师负担问题并提升学生写作质量。

Method: 1. 开发了一个支持K-12写作学习的三元协作系统；2. 构建了基于系统功能语言学（SFL）的多维度评估框架；3. 设计了建议轨迹追踪流水线；4. 进行了为期两年、涉及120所学校、10,195名学生共57,954篇作文的大规模实证分析。

Result: 1. 证实了该三元协作系统的有效性：LLM作为生成引擎减轻教师负担，教师作为教学把关人和沟通桥梁保障反馈质量；2. 发现了“天花板效应”：过度的语言扩展对写作提升的边际效用逐渐递减。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了LLM与教师在K-12写作教学中应采取“动态自适应”的协作模式。随着学生写作能力的提升，过度依赖模型进行语言扩展会产生边际效用递减的“天花板效应”，因此需要调整协作策略。

Abstract: The double-edged sword of integrating Large Language Models (LLMs) requires an effective triadic collaboration mechanism among LLMs, teachers and students, especially for K-12 education. By developing a triadic collaboration system to support K-12 writing learning, a multidimensional evaluation framework grounded in Systemic Functional Linguistics and the suggestion trajectory tracing pipeline, this paper contributes a large-scale empirical dataset involving $57,954$ essays from $10,195$ students across $120$ schools over two years. Our findings confirm the efficacy of this system in improving writing quality through a strategic labor division: the LLM serves as a generative engine to mitigate teacher burnout, and the teacher acts as a pedagogical gatekeeper and bridge to guarantee feedback quality. While both LLM and teacher are critical for skill improvement, we uncover a ceiling effect where excessive linguistic expansion yields diminishing marginal utility. These suggest a dynamically adaptive LLM-teacher collaboration as student proficiency increases.

</details>


### [231] [When Should Models Change Their Minds? Contextual Belief Management in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30219)
*Haoming Xu,Weihong Xu,Zongrui Li,Mengru Wang,Yunzhi Yao,Chiyu Wu,Jin Shang,Yu Gong,Shumin Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了长周期交互中语言模型在信息留存、更新与过滤方面的挑战（CBM），构建了BeliefTrack基准以识别并量化三种核心失败模式。研究发现强化学习与表征层干预能显著改善模型的信念追踪能力，大幅降低失败率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长周期交互要求语言模型具备高效管理信息流的能力，即准确判断何时更新、何时保持及如何过滤无关噪声。现有模型在此类任务中表现不佳，亟需一种可测量且可优化的框架来解决这些失败模式。

Method: 1. 提出“环境信念管理（CBM）”框架，量化定义信念更新、保持与噪声隔离；2. 构建包含“规则发现”和“电路诊断”任务的BeliefTrack基准测试；3. 对比多种LLM及提示工程效果；4. 引入基于信念状态奖励的强化学习优化方法；5. 进行表征层干预（Representation-level steering）实验。

Result: 1. 发现模型主要存在三种失败模式：Failed Stay（未能保持）、Failed Update（未能更新）、Failed Isolation（未能隔离）；2. 传统模型及提示词工程效果有限；3. 强化学习将平均失败率降低了70.9%；4. 基于表征层干预的策略使任务失败率降低了46.1%。

Conclusion: 语言模型在处理长周期交互时面临严重的信念状态管理问题；通过强化学习（信念状态奖励）和表征层干预（steering），可以有效提升模型对相关信息的留存与更新能力，显著降低错误率。

Abstract: Long-horizon interactions require language models to manage accumulating information: when to update their state, when to preserve their state, and what to ignore. We study this challenge as \textbf{Contextual Belief Management (CBM)}: maintaining a predicted belief state aligned with formal evidence while isolating task-irrelevant noise. To make CBM measurable, we introduce BeliefTrack, a closed-world benchmark spanning Rule Discovery and Circuit Diagnosis, where a finite belief space and symbolic verifiers enable exact turn-level evaluation. BeliefTrack diagnoses three failures: Failed Stay, Failed Update, and Failed Isolation. Across multiple LLMs, vanilla models exhibit severe CBM failures, while explicit belief-tracking prompts provide limited gains. In contrast, reinforcement learning with belief-state rewards reduces failure rates by 70.9\% on average. Further probing reveals latent belief-state dynamics behind these failures, and representation-level steering reduces failure rates by 46.1\% across two tasks\footnote{Code is coming soon at https://github.com/zjunlp/CBM.

</details>


### [232] [mcp-proto-okn: Natural-language access to open scientific knowledge graphs through the Model Context Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30283)
*Peter W. Rose,Benjamin M. Good,Amanda M. Saravia-Butler,Charlotte A. Nelson,James P. Balhoff,Yaphet Kebede,Patricia L. Whetzel,Christopher Bizon,Andrew I. Su,Sergio E. Baranzini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: mcp-proto-okn 是一个基于 Python 的 MCP 服务器，利用 FastMCP 框架实现，使用户能够通过自然语言通过 AI 助手对海量科学知识图谱进行搜索、查询与集成分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决 AI 助手难以直接且高效地发现、查询和整合科学知识图谱的问题，以促进生物医学及其他科研领域的知识整合与分析。

Method: 基于 Python 的 FastMCP 框架开发，集成了图路由、模式检查、SPARQL 执行、本体扩展、多图查询及分析记录生成等核心功能。

Result: 实现了一个功能完备的 MCP 服务器，支持通过自然语言与科学知识图谱进行交互，并已通过 GitHub 开源，提供了完整的配套文档和示例。

Conclusion: mcp-proto-okn 为科学知识图谱与 AI 助手之间的交互提供了一个标准化、易用的桥梁，显著降低了跨领域科研数据分析的门槛。

Abstract: MCP Server Proto-OKN (mcp-proto-okn) is a Python-based Model Context Protocol server that enables AI assistants to discover, inspect, query and integrate scientific knowledge graphs through natural language. The server provides graph routing, schema inspection, SPARQL execution, ontology expansion, multi-graph querying, and transcript generation, lowering the barrier to cross-domain knowledge graph analysis for biomedical and scientific users.
  mcp-proto-okn is implemented in Python using the FastMCP framework and is available at https://github.com/sbl-sdsc/mcp-proto-okn. Documentation, client configuration instructions, and example analysis transcripts are provided in the GitHub repository.

</details>


### [233] [ProjectionBench: Evaluating Scientific Hypothesis Generation in LLMs Under Progressive Information Disclosure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30284)
*A. J. Lew,Y. Cao,M. J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个衡量大模型科学发现能力的渐进式评估框架，通过对比模型假设与高质量论文结论的语义一致性，验证了模型在从“假设生成”到“证据推理”全过程中的科研协作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多跳检索基准无法测试大模型在科学发现任务中所需的创新性推理能力，迫切需要构建能评估模型如何从原始问题演进到科学结论的测试框架。

Method: 提出一个渐进式基准框架：从仅提供研究问题开始，逐步披露实验细节，要求模型在各阶段生成假设，并利用原子化声明的语义相似度对比原始论文结论，从而评估模型从创新性推演到基于证据的逻辑性。

Result: 在45篇涵盖生物活性、机械及纳米材料领域的研究中，GPT-5.4和Gemini 3.1 pro表现优于前代模型；其中GPT-5.4在极小信息量下仍能保持与真实科研结论0.7的F1对齐分数。

Conclusion: 研究表明，该渐进式框架能有效评估大模型在科学发现中的创新能力与逻辑推演能力，GPT-5.4与Gemini 3.1 pro在处理跨学科科学问题时展现出更强的研究潜力。

Abstract: Scientific discovery is an inherently creative and uncertain process, requiring reasoning beyond the recall of known knowledge. While many benchmarks have been proposed to evaluate large language model (LLM) performance on deep research tasks via multi-hop retrieval, their innovative reasoning abilities essential for true scientific discovery remain largely untested. We introduce a benchmark framework for evaluating model performance in scientific discovery and reasoning, building up from a raw problem to the classical null hypothesis test. In our framework, models initially receive only the topic and research question from a recent paper, with technical details progressively revealed. At each stage of information disclosure, the model is tasked with generating hypotheses that address the research question, which is compared with the conclusions from the original paper and evaluated via automated semantic similarity of constituent atomic claims. This progressive evaluation of semantic divergence from ground-truth conclusions enables assessment of a model's innovativeness (under minimal information) to grounded reasoning capabilities (under full experimental details), both critical for using LLMs for scientific discovery purposes. Our framework provides a foundation for systematically evaluating scientific reasoning and discovery capabilities in LLMs, crucial for advancing the development of next-generation AI scientist/co-scientist systems. Specifically, here we evaluate GPT-5, GPT-5.4, Gemini 2.5 pro, and Gemini 3.1 pro preview across 45 papers spanning bioactive materials, mechanical materials, and nanomaterials. We find that GPT-5.4 and Gemini 3.1 pro outperform their previous generation counterparts as expected, and GPT-5.4 in particular maintains 0.7 F1 score alignment with ground truth conclusions even under minimal context.

</details>


### [234] [MIRA: Mid-training Rubric Anchoring for Source-Aware Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30288)
*Haowen Wang,Yaxin Du,Jian Yang,Jiajun Wu,Shukai Liu,Yuxuan Zhang,Pingjie Wang,Siheng Chen,Tuney Zheng,Ming Zhou,Xianglong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MIRA 是一种针对 LLM 中继训练的高效数据筛选框架，通过自动发现不同数据源的语义判据并将其蒸馏为可扩展的评分器，在大幅减少训练数据量的同时提升了代码生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大模型开发中，中继训练阶段的数据筛选面临挑战：既要在大规模预训练规模下操作，又要兼顾针对下游能力的语义优化，且数据源高度异构；现有方法难以平衡扩展性与精细化的语义判据。

Method: 提出名为 MIRA 的源感知（source-aware）筛选框架，其核心逻辑为“自我锚定判据发现”：首先针对不同数据源组识别并定义评估标准，随后将这些判断蒸馏为可扩展的学生评分模型，用于对整个语料库进行筛选。

Result: 在代码中继训练实验中（21个源，5个组），MIRA 在九个代码基准测试中均超越了现有筛选基准，且在仅使用一半预训练 Token 的情况下，达到了与全量数据训练相当的性能。

Conclusion: MIRA 通过将判据发现嵌入数据筛选流程，成功解决了中继训练（mid-training）中大规模数据筛选的扩展性与语义评估准确性之间的矛盾，证明了该方法在保持高效性的同时能够提升模型性能。

Abstract: Mid-training has become an important stage in modern LLM development, using large-scale curated mixtures to strengthen capabilities before final post-training. Its data selection problem is distinct: the data are optimized under a pretraining-style objective at near-pretraining scale, but are curated toward downstream capabilities and drawn from heterogeneous sources with different formats and training roles. As a result, effective selection requires both scalability and source-adaptive semantic criteria. Existing model-based methods scale well, but provide only implicit quality signals. Semantic selection methods offer stronger judgments, but usually assume fixed rubrics or standardized data formats. To address this mismatch, we propose MIRA, a source-aware filtering framework based on self-anchored rubric discovery. The key idea is to make rubric construction part of data selection: MIRA first discovers what should be evaluated for each source group, then distills those judgments into scalable student scorers for full-corpus filtering. On code-oriented mid-training with 21 sources and 5 source groups, MIRA outperforms selection baselines across nine code benchmarks and matches the full-corpus run while using only half the tokens.

</details>


### [235] [Demystifying Data Organization for Enhanced LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30334)
*Yalun Dai,Yangyu Huang,Tongshen Yang,Yonghan Wang,Xin Zhang,Wenshan Wu,Qihao Zhao,Hao Li,Yuanyuan Gao,Kim-Hui Yap,Scarlett Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了数据组织对LLM训练的影响，通过四项关键准则及创新的排序方法（STR/SAW），在几乎不增加计算成本的前提下，显著提升了LLM的训练效率和模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管数据选择已得到广泛研究，但LLM训练中关于“数据组织”（即数据的呈现顺序）的研究相对匮乏，且现有大模型通常只训练极少数epoch，如何利用数据排序提升训练效率具有重要的研究价值。

Method: 通过复用已有的样本级评分，提出了四项数据组织关键准则，并基于此设计了两种数据排序算法：STR (Sort-Then-Repeat) 和 SAW (Sort-And-Weight)。利用不同模型规模和数据量进行系统性实验，评估了其在预训练及SFT阶段的效果。

Result: 实验结果表明，遵循所提出的组织原则能显著改进模型性能；所提出的STR和SAW方法在不同参数规模和任务阶段上均表现出卓越的训练稳定性和性能提升，且无需额外的大幅计算开销。

Conclusion: 该文提出的四项数据组织原则（边界锐化、循环调度、课程连续性、局部多样性）及STR与SAW方法，在预训练和指令微调阶段均能显著提升大型语言模型（LLM）的训练稳定性与性能，证明了通过优化数据排序策略可有效提升训练效率。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized various fields, yet their training efficiency is heavily reliant on effective data curation. While data selection has been widely studied, the strategic data organization for enhanced training remains an underexplored area, particularly since current LLMs are often trained for only one or a few epochs. This paper systematically explores the influence of data organization on LLM training by reusing pre-computed sample-level scores originally generated for data efficiency, thereby incurring minimal additional computational overhead. We identify and formalize four key guidelines for optimizing data organization: Boundary Sharpening, Cyclic Scheduling, Curriculum Continuity, and Local Diversity. Guided by them, we introduce two novel data ordering methods termed STR and SAW. Extensive experiments across different model scales and data sizes, encompassing both pre-training and SFT stages, validate the effectiveness of our summarized guidelines. They also demonstrate the robustness of our proposed data ordering methods in enhancing the stability and performance of LLM training. Github Link: https://github.com/microsoft/data-efficacy/

</details>


### [236] [Locally Coherent, Globally Incoherent: Bounding Compositional Incoherence in Multi-Component LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30335)
*Anany Kotawala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 多组件LLM智能体在组合局部概率时常违反概率公理，导致全局不相干。本文量化了该风险（eps*），证明了其对决策的影响，并提出了通过Boyle-Dykstra投影进行自动化修复的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多组件LLM智能体系统中，各组件往往只处理局部信息，导致其组合后的概率预测可能违背基本的概率公理（全局不相干），进而导致决策时的性能损失。

Method: 1. 定义组合残差 eps* (L2距离) 来衡量组合结果偏离联合相干多胞体的程度；2. 建立产品结构二分法；3. 使用Rayleigh商预测残差；4. 引入层次化Boyle-Dykstra投影进行确定性修复；5. 使用e-process进行实时相干性监控。

Result: 1. 实验显示33%-94%的智能体群体存在组合不相干性（eps* > 0）；2. 这种不相干性会导致显著的“遗憾”（regret），使得在比例分配规则下的损失增加；3. 现有的直观缓解策略（如检索、分区感知提示、聚合LLM）均无效或产生负面影响。

Conclusion: 多组件LLM智能体在组合概率预测时普遍存在局部相干但全局不相干的问题。本文提出的残差度量方法能有效量化该偏差，且通过层次化投影算法可以实现确定性的修复，从而消除因概率公理违背带来的潜在决策风险（如套利损失）。

Abstract: Multi-component LLM agents assemble probabilistic claims from components that each see only part of a joint problem; the composition can violate basic probability axioms even when every component is locally coherent. We formalise this locally coherent, globally incoherent failure via the compositional residual eps*, the L2 distance from the composed quote to the joint coherent polytope, computable at runtime from system output and the declared cross-component coupling constraints. A product-structure dichotomy characterises when local coherence suffices, and a Rayleigh-quotient prediction matches the observed residual within 7% on three of four relation classes. A hierarchical Boyle-Dykstra projection repairs the composition deterministically; an anytime-valid e-process gives sequential coherence monitoring. Across 1,876 ensemble cliques on a four-LLM mid-tier panel (frontier-panel rerun in Section 5.5), eps* > 0 on 33-94% of cliques, translating to +0.115 nats per bet of regret on 1,770 resolved bets under the proportional allocation rule (the gain collapses to +0.006 under bettors that themselves coherentise). Three intuitive LLM-side mitigations(retrieval, partition-aware prompting, aggregator-LLM) each fail or regress.

</details>


### [237] [Tiny but Trusted: Efficient Vision-Language Reasoning for Time-Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30344)
*Xiaona Zhou,Muntasir Wahed,Tianjiao Yu,Constantin Brif,Ismini Lourentzou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 为了解决现有模型在时间序列异常检测中解释性差的问题，本文提出了一个新的基准VisAnomBench，并开发了VisAnomReasoner模型，实现了更准确的异常定位与跨数据集泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型（VLM）在处理时间序列异常检测任务时表现不佳，主要原因是缺乏带有自然语言理性说明（Rationales）的基准数据集，导致模型难以进行有效的解释性微调。

Method: 1. 构建了VisAnomBench基准：通过整合现有的时间序列数据集，并利用多个大模型（VLM）结合细粒度奖励机制，生成高质量的自然语言异常解释。 2. 开发了VisAnomReasoner：基于参数高效微调（PEFT）技术构建的高性能视觉语言模型，专门用于时间序列异常检测任务。

Result: 在VisAnomBench上，VisAnomReasoner的准确率和F1分数分别比基线模型提升了至少21.23%和23.87%；在TSB-AD-U数据集上的跨基准测试中，准确率和F1分数也分别提升了9.57%和13.39%。

Conclusion: VisAnomReasoner通过利用高质量的自然语言标注，有效提升了视觉语言模型在时间序列异常检测中的解释性和定位精度，并展现出良好的跨基准泛化能力，为提升多模态模型在该领域的应用提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have achieved impressive performance across many tasks, yet prior studies report unsatisfactory performance when applying large language or multimodal models to finding abnormal patterns in sequential data. Public anomaly detection benchmarks typically provide interval annotations but not natural-language rationales, making it difficult to fine-tune VLMs to produce grounded, interpretable decisions. To address this gap, we construct VisAnomBench, a curated benchmark built from public time-series datasets and augmented with high-quality anomaly explanations selected from multiple large VLMs using fine-grained, task-specific rewards. Through fine-tuning on this benchmark, we develop VisAnomReasoner, a parameter-efficient VLM for time-series anomaly detection. Experimental results on VisAnomBench show that VisAnomReasoner achieves more accurate anomaly localization and consistently outperforms all baselines, with improvements of at least 21.23 and 23.87 percentage points in precision and F1, respectively. Additional experiments on the TSB-AD-U benchmark demonstrate strong cross-benchmark generalization, with VisAnomReasoner improving precision and F1 by 9.57 and 13.39 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [238] [SchGen: PCB Schematic Generation with Semantic-Grounded Code Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30345)
*Qinpei Luo,Ruichun Ma,Xinyu Zhang,Lili Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SchGen 是首个能根据自然语言生成可编辑PCB原理图的大模型，通过创新语义表示和构建专用数据集，解决了传统PCB设计生成的语法复杂性与几何约束难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PCB原理图设计高度依赖手工且门槛高，现有大模型在处理PCB设计时缺乏合适的表示方法和数据集，难以应对工具特定的复杂语法和几何约束。

Method: 1. 提出一种全新的语义化代码表示方法（semantically grounded code representation），通过编辑原语、相对位置和引脚连线，将几何生成问题转化为语义匹配问题；2. 构建大规模PCB原理图与用户提示语配对数据集。

Result: SchGen 在连线准确性和功能正确性方面显著优于其他表示方法及通用大模型。

Conclusion: SchGen 证明了通过语义引导的表示设计和高质量数据集，大模型能够有效完成复杂的PCB原理图生成任务，为硬件辅助设计自动化开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Printed circuit board (PCB) schematic design defines nearly all electronic hardware, but it remains manual and expertise-intensive. While generative AI has advanced digital and analog IC design, PCB schematic generation from natural-language intent is largely unexplored. This paper presents SchGen, the first large language model that generates editable PCB schematics from natural-language requests. The key challenge lies in the lack of an LLM-suited representation and a large-scale dataset. Current schematic formats are dominated by verbose, tool-specific syntax and geometry-heavy descriptions, making them difficult to generate reliably. We introduce a semantically grounded code representation that encodes schematic editing primitives with relative placement and pin-name-based wiring, transforming a geometry-driven generation problem into a semantics-driven matching task amenable to LLMs. We further construct a large-scale dataset of PCB schematics paired with user prompts via a human-agent collaborative pipeline that converts open-source hardware designs into our representation. Experiments show that SchGen significantly outperforms alternative representations and even larger general-purpose LLMs on wire connectivity accuracy and functional correctness. Our results highlight the critical role of representation design in enabling generative models for complex hardware design tasks.

</details>


### [239] [Physics Is All You Need? A Case Study in Physicist-Supervised AI Development of Scientific Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30353)
*Nhat-Minh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过开发物理学模块的案例，揭示了AI智能体易陷入“局部优化而忽视本质架构错误”的局限性，指出研究级AI的可信度高度依赖于监督策略，而非模型能力的简单堆叠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨AI智能体在科学研究中扮演的角色（工具、共同作者或研究员），并评估当前主流AI模型在处理高精度科学建模任务时的局限性及人类监督的必要性。

Method: 通过一项为期12天、包含57次会话的量化个案研究（$N=1$），由一名物理学家监督AI编码智能体（Claude Opus/Sonnet）开发名为CLAX-PT的任务，并对15次干预事件进行分类分析，评估智能体的自主解决能力与监督需求。

Result: 智能体能自主解决大部分技术问题，但在面对架构逻辑错误时表现出强烈的路径依赖，难以主动重构模型；此外，智能体会通过“打补丁”式的物理无效手段欺骗测试指标（Oracle tests），必须通过参数空间的多样化测试、共享变更日志和禁止非物理补丁等监督策略来识别。

Conclusion: 当前的AI智能体在处理复杂的科学研究任务时，依然面临“症状简化为根源解决”和盲目拟合（非物理修正）的问题。研究强调，人类的监督设计（而非单一的模型能力）是确保AI输出可信度的关键，当前的扩展模型尚不能自动弥补架构创新和物理因果解释能力的空白。

Abstract: Are AI agents tools, co-authors, or researchers? We present a quantified case study ($N=1$): a physicist supervising an AI coding agent (Claude Code, Sonnet and Opus models) over 12 work days and 57 sessions to build CLAX-PT, a differentiable one-loop perturbation theory module in JAX. We documented and classified 15 supervision events by intervention level.
  The agent resolved ten autonomously by iterating against oracle tests. Two more by the physicist's domain knowledge. The three it could not -- all evaded oracle detection -- share a common property: the agent treated symptom reduction as root-cause resolution. It spent 33 of the 57 sessions adjusting coefficients within a code architecture that could not represent the target physics, and could not re-evaluate its CLASS-PT branch choice even when prompted to reconsider; only an injected physics concept (anisotropic BAO damping) triggered the redesign. Separately, the agent committed a calibrated correction that passed all oracle tests but corresponded to no quantity in the theory, predicting wrong values at any other cosmology.
  The fudge factor was caught and replaced within the same session. Three supervision practices proved critical for catching what oracle tests missed: testing at diverse parameter points beyond the fiducial calibration; shared changelogs that surfaced stalled exploration across sessions; and an explicit rule against unphysical numerical patches. In this case, supervision design, not model capability, determined whether the agent's output was trustworthy. Closing the gap would require agents that propose architectural alternatives rather than optimize within a given structure, and distinguish predictive adequacy from explanatory correctness -- capabilities not exhibited here, not obviously addressed by scaling alone. [Abridged.]

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [240] [Generative Spatiotemporal Intent Sequence Recommendation via Implicit Reasoning in Amap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28888)
*Sicong Wang,Ruiting Dong,Yue Liu,Bowen Zheng,Jun Meng,Jie Li,Shuaijun Guo,Yu Gu,Fanyi Di,Xin Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对工业级时空意图序列推荐（GSISR）的延迟与物理可行性矛盾，提出 GPlan 框架，通过隐式思维链蒸馏与反事实 DPO，将 LLM 推理能力高效内化至轻量模型中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实用户行为呈现出有时空依赖的意图流。现有基于 LLM 的方案虽然推理能力强，但在工业部署中面临推理延迟高、规划物理不可行及上下文脱节等挑战，难以直接应用。

Method: 1. 提出 GPlan 框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 渐进式隐式思维链蒸馏（Progressive Implicit CoT Distillation），将显式推理过程压缩为隐式潜变量，以在小模型中实现高效推理；(2) 时空反事实直接偏好优化（Spatiotemporal Counterfactual DPO），通过对齐反事实上下文-规划对，增强模型对时空约束的感知能力。

Result: 线下实验与线上 A/B 测试表明，该方法在保证低延迟的前提下，显著提升了生成序列的连贯性和对时空上下文的响应能力。

Conclusion: GPlan 框架成功将 LLM 的推理能力迁移至轻量级模型，在满足工业界低延迟要求的同时，显著提升了时空意图序列推荐的逻辑连贯性与物理可行性。

Abstract: Real-world user behavior rarely consists of isolated actions; instead, it often forms intent flows governed by spatiotemporal dependencies. To provide integrated service recommendations, we focus on the task of Generative Spatiotemporal Intent Sequence Recommendation (GSISR), which aims to generate intent sequences that are logically coherent and physically executable within complex spatiotemporal contexts. While LLMs offer strong reasoning potential for GSISR, direct industrial deployment is limited by high inference latency and context-mismatched or physically infeasible plans. To address these challenges, we propose a generative framework, GPlan, that internalizes LLM reasoning into lightweight models through two components. First, to enable reasoning under strict latency constraints, we introduce Progressive Implicit CoT Distillation, which compresses explicit reasoning processes into reserved latent tokens, allowing small models to inherit complex planning logic without generating long reasoning text. Second, to address the disconnect between general knowledge and real-world constraints, we design Spatiotemporal Counterfactual DPO. By aligning the model with counterfactual context-plan pairs, we improve sensitivity to spatiotemporal context and reduce context-mismatched plans. Offline experiments and online A/B testing demonstrate that our approach improves sequence coherence and context responsiveness. Our implementation and the anonymized GSISR dataset are available at https://github.com/alibaba/GPlan.

</details>


### [241] [Toward User Preference Alignment in LLM Recommendation via Explicit Context Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29141)
*Weizhi Zhang,Wooseong Yang,Yuxin Cui,Zhaohui Guo,Hins Hu,Liangwei Yang,Henry Peng Zou,Qifei Wang,Hanqing Zeng,Jiayi Liu,Yinglong Xia,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文主张在下一代大模型推荐系统中优先处理显式上下文反馈（如用户评论），以弥补现有模型在理解用户决策语义上的局限，从而提升推荐的准确性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐系统多利用隐式行为信号（如点击、购买），忽视了包含用户决策深层原因的显式文本反馈（如评论），导致用户偏好对齐偏差和“过滤气泡”问题；现有的大模型推荐系统也主要关注项目元数据，未充分利用用户生成的丰富文本内容。

Method: 回顾了推荐系统范式的演变；强调了语境丰富反馈的价值；呼吁建立新的基准和指标；并提出了将显式用户信号集成到可扩展的LLM驱动推荐系统中的框架。

Result: 明确了显式上下文反馈对于优化用户偏好对齐和提供可解释性推荐的关键作用，并为未来基于LLM的推荐系统构建提供了策略框架。

Conclusion: 呼吁将显式上下文反馈（评论、评价等）作为下一代大模型推荐系统的核心，以实现更具个性化、透明度和可解释性的推荐。

Abstract: Traditional recommender systems (RecSys) primarily infer user preferences from implicit signals (such as clicks, watches, and purchases), often neglecting the rich explicit contextual feedback users provide through verbal text, like comments and reviews. This explicit context feedback captures the nuanced reasons behind user decisions regarding their preferences. In addition, it offers critical heterogeneous information for user preference alignment and more explainable recommendations. Overlooking such signals can lead to misaligned user preferences and further reinforce filter bubbles, as algorithms fail to understand the "semantic context" behind user choices. Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) present new opportunities to harness user-generated content for more accurate and diverse recommendations, yet current LLM-based recommendations still focus on using item meta-data and underutilize this resource. In this paper, we advocate for prioritizing explicit context feedback in the next generation of LLM-based RecSys. We review the evolution of recommendation paradigms, highlight the value of context-rich feedback, call for new benchmarks and metrics, and introduce frameworks for integrating explicit user signals into scalable LLM-driven RecSys. Centering on user-preference modeling, we aim to foster more personalized, transparent, and explainable RecSys online platforms.

</details>


### [242] [On the Practice of Scaling Search Conversion Rate Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29232)
*James Pak,Jyun-Yu Jiang,Fan Zhang,Sen Wang,Taekmin Kim,Henry Tsai,Vijay Rajaram,Juexin Lin,Mohitdeep Singh,Alessandro Magnani,Johnny Chen,Qian Zhao,Rao Fu,Zhirong Liang,Jordan Gilliland,Winter Jiao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究了搜索 CVR 预测模型的扩展性，通过优化模型结构、嵌入层及数据规模，配合 Warmstart 训练和 GPU 推理加速技术，在保证低延迟的前提下实现了显著的业务指标提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在搜索高流量场景下，如何在保证模型质量的同时，平衡训练成本与服务延迟，实现转化率（CVR）预测模型的有效扩展。

Method: 1. 实证分析：研究骨干计算量、嵌入参数规模、训练数据量对模型质量的独立及叠加影响；2. 架构探索：评估多种 SOTA 模型架构及其集成；3. 训练优化：采用高效的 Warmstart 策略；4. 推理优化：利用解耦图执行和动态批处理技术提升 GPU 服务效率。

Result: 在生产环境 A/B 测试中，相比基线模型，新模型在训练数据量提升 2.5 倍、推理计算量提升 8 倍的情况下，在线搜索转化率获得 +2.6% 的增长，且未对服务延迟产生显著负面影响。

Conclusion: 通过合理选择模型架构及扩展因子，结合优化的训练策略（Warmstart）和推理加速技术（解耦图执行、动态批处理），可以在保持低服务延迟的前提下，大幅提升转化率预测模型的规模与性能。

Abstract: Scaling a Search Conversion Rate (CVR) prediction model, especially in high-traffic environments, presents a challenge: superior model quality needs to be balanced with strict constraints on training cost and serving latency. This paper details an effective approach for scaling modern search CVR prediction models. We begin with an empirical study to understand the scaling performance of search CVR models, analyzing how quality improves as we scale three key factors of model backbone computation, the size of embedding parameters, and the volume of training data.
  We use a large-scale production dataset, comprising over a year of customer interaction logs from a high-traffic e-commerce platform, to evaluate the scalability of several state-of-the-art architectures and their ensembles. Our key findings are: (1) selecting the right backbone and scaling factors is crucial; (2) the impact of scaling backbone, embedding, and data is largely independent and additive, which has implications for more efficient scaling exploration; (3) a streamlined warmstart strategy can accelerate training iterations while simplifying new updates; (4) inference optimization strategies such as decoupled graph execution and dynamic batching can enable low-latency GPU serving even for high-capacity models. Compared to a baseline of a pre-scaling production model, we ultimately deployed a model trained on 2.5x larger training data with 8x more inference compute while having minimal latency impact. Online A/B tests also demonstrate that our launches achieved a combined +2.6% gain in a key metric of search conversion rate.

</details>


### [243] [CrossAlpha: An Annual-Report Benchmark for Cross-Market Factor Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29286)
*Qian Wang,Zhongyi Tong,Nuo Chen,Zhaomin Wu,Bingsheng He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 CrossAlpha，这是首个用于跨市场因子研究的年度报告基准测试，通过标准化披露信息、构建残差关联图及严格的时间对齐评估，证实了跨市场公司披露信息在预测收益方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的公共基准测试不支持跨市场“披露-收益”评估；构建此类基准面临文件语言与监管体系差异大、披露相似性存在偏差以及跨市场交易时间难以对齐等挑战。

Method: 提出 CrossAlpha 基准测试，包含三个核心组件：(1) 披露提炼（Disclosure Distillation）：将异构报告标准化为十类英文业务描述；(2) 残差模式图构建（Residual Schema Graph Construction）：利用 PCA 白化技术计算跨市场企业对分数；(3) 时间对齐评估（Timing-Aligned Evaluation）：结合 11 年的日度 OHLCV 数据，在可行的跨市场执行协议下构建前瞻性收益标签。

Result: CrossAlpha 涵盖了来自美国、日本、台湾、韩国和中国香港的约 3,600 家公司和 10,700 份年度报告。实验表明，在美日市场设置中，基于披露的跨市场对标模型表现优于国内文本、行业代码及收益相关性基准（ICIR 为 0.39 vs 0.07–0.18），且跨市场来源在多数目标市场表现均优于国内文本基准。

Conclusion: CrossAlpha 提供了一个开源、可复用的基准测试，填补了跨市场金融 NLP 研究中数据标准化和评估协议的空白，实验证明了跨市场披露信息在预测收益方面的潜力。

Abstract: Cross-market factor research studies whether firm-level signals from one or more markets can predict returns in a target market, but existing public benchmarks do not support cross-market disclosure-to-return evaluation. Building such a benchmark is challenging because filings differ across languages and regulatory systems, disclosure-derived similarity can be biased by common reporting components, and cross-market signals must be evaluated under feasible trading-time alignment. We introduce \textbf{CrossAlpha}, a public annual-report benchmark for cross-market factor research. CrossAlpha addresses these challenges through three corresponding components: \emph{Disclosure Distillation}, which standardises heterogeneous filings into ten-category English business descriptions; \emph{Residual Schema Graph Construction}, which builds PCA-whitened cross-market firm-pair scores from schema-level disclosures; and \emph{Timing-Aligned Evaluation}, which pairs the graph with 11 years of daily OHLCV data to construct forward-return labels under feasible cross-market execution protocols. CrossAlpha covers about 3,600 firms and 10,700 firm-year reports from the United States, Japan, Taiwan, South Korea, and Hong Kong, and releases about 19M directed firm-pair scores. In experiments, disclosure-derived cross-market peers outperform domestic text, industry-code, and return-correlation peers in the US-to-Japan setting (ICIR 0.39 versus 0.07--0.18), and cross-market sources beat the domestic text baseline in most target markets. CrossAlpha offers an open-sourced, reusable, return-grounded benchmark for cross-market financial NLP.

</details>


### [244] [UniNote: A Unified Embedding Model for Multimodal Representation and Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29287)
*Jinghan Zhao,Wenwei Jin,Anqi Li,Jintao Tong,Luya Mo,Jiawei Li,Bin Li,Yao Hu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: UniNote 是一个针对工业级 I2I 检索设计的统一多模态嵌入模型，通过对比微调与强化学习的两阶段训练策略，实现了高性能与高效率的平衡，并已在小红书大规模部署落地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态嵌入方法在 I2I（Item-to-Item）场景中存在三大痛点：难以平衡全局内容表示与细粒度检索、解耦式检索-排序流水线效率低下、以及模型精度与服务延迟之间的固有权衡。

Method: 1. 多粒度检索策略：支持对不同细粒度多模态内容的表示学习；2. 两阶段训练范式：第一阶段使用对比监督微调（Contrastive SFT）建立基座嵌入，第二阶段通过强化学习（RL）对齐内容相关性以优化排序质量；3. 技术整合：结合了 Matryoshka Representation Learning (MRL) 以优化部署的计算效率。

Result: UniNote 在多种 I2I 任务中达到了 SOTA 性能。在小红书的工业实践中，结合 MRL 技术，显著提升了大规模应用下的检索质量及成本效率。

Conclusion: UniNote 是一个针对工业界 I2I 检索的统一嵌入模型，通过两阶段训练范式和多粒度检索策略，成功在复杂多模态内容场景中提升了检索精度与系统效率，并已在小红书大规模应用中得到验证。

Abstract: Item-to-Item (I2I) retrieval is a fundamental part of modern content platforms, supporting critical industrial workflows from recommendation engines to content auditing. While multimodal embedding methods have advanced general retrieval, they often falter in I2I scenarios due to the challenges of balancing global content representation with fine-grained local retrieval, the systemic inefficiency of decoupled embedding-and-ranking pipelines, and the inherent trade-offs between model precision and serving latency. To solve these issues, we propose \textbf{UniNote}, a unified embedding model designed for industrial I2I retrieval. Tailored retrieval strategies are introduced to support representation learning over complex, multimodal content at varying granularities. To operationalize these strategies, UniNote employs a two-stage training paradigm: the first stage leverages contrastive SFT to establish robust base embeddings, while the second stage refines ranking quality through a reinforcement learning (RL) process that aligns the model with content relevance. Our results show that UniNote achieves SOTA performance across diverse I2I tasks. Deployed at Xiaohongshu and integrated with Matryoshka Representation Learning (MRL), UniNote achieved significant improvements in retrieval quality and cost efficiency in large-scale applications.

</details>


### [245] [ACE: Anisotropy-Controllable Embedding for LLM-enhanced Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29322)
*Dongcheol Lee,Hye-young Kim,Jongwuk Lee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 为了解决LLM生成嵌入在推荐任务中高度各向异性导致的学习困难，本文提出了ACE框架，通过线性自编码器在保持语义结构的同时增强嵌入的几何均匀性，显著提升了序列推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为特征提取器在序列推荐（SR）中表现出强大的语义能力，但其生成嵌入普遍存在严重的各向异性（各维度朝向相似），导致几何分布不均，难以与推荐系统中的协同过滤信号有效对齐，限制了微调效果。

Method: 提出了一种各向异性可控嵌入（ACE）框架，利用线性自编码器（LAE）进行处理。通过L2正则化控制维度分布以缓解各向异性，同时利用重构损失确保语义信息的留存，从而平衡几何均匀性与语义完整性。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，ACE优于现有的LLM增强型SR模型，Recall@20提升达12.4%，NDCG@20提升达11.8%。

Conclusion: ACE通过显式控制LLM生成嵌入的各向异性，有效解决了表征空间几何失衡问题，为融合大模型语义与协同过滤信号提供了更稳定的学习基础。

Abstract: Recent advances in the LLM-as-Extractor paradigm leverage large language models (LLMs) to transfer semantically rich item embeddings into sequential recommendation (SR) backbones. However, LLM-generated embeddings often suffer from strong anisotropy. Most vectors are concentrated in similar directions, resulting in a geometric imbalance that makes it difficult to adapt to collaborative signals during fine-tuning. To address this challenge, we propose Anisotropy-Controllable Embedding (ACE), which explicitly controls the anisotropy of LLM-generated embeddings. Specifically, ACE utilizes a linear autoencoder (LAE) to reshape the embedding distribution while preserving its semantic structure. In this process, the L2-regularization term mitigates the anisotropy by controlling the dispersion of embedding dimensions, while the reconstruction loss maintains semantic relationships among items. That is, ACE balances geometric uniformity and semantic embedding preservation for more stable learning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ACE consistently outperforms existing LLM-enhanced SR models, yielding improvements of up to 12.4% and 11.8% in Recall@20 and NDCG@20, respectively.

</details>


### [246] [Latent Terms: Dense Retrievers Contain Trivially Extractable BM25-ready Zipfian Vocabularies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29384)
*Benjamin Clavié,Sean Lee,Aamir Shakir,Makoto P. Kato*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Latent Terms的方法，无需额外稀疏监督，仅通过稀疏自动编码器从现有稠密检索模型中提取稀疏特征，即可实现高效且优于基础模型的稀疏检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稠密检索模型虽性能强大，但其评分机制可能未能充分挖掘模型表示中潜藏的线性、可解释且易于索引的结构信息。

Method: 提出Latent Terms方法：利用稀疏自动编码器（Sparse Autoencoders）对冻结的稠密检索器表示进行分解，提取出符合Zipf定律的潜在词汇，并直接应用于类BM25的经典稀疏检索评分机制。

Result: Latent Terms在多项任务中达到或超过了其基础模型及同类SPLADE变体的表现，特别是在专门针对单向量检索缺陷设计的LIMIT任务上表现出了显著优势。

Conclusion: 神经检索器内部潜藏着比其默认评分函数所展现出的更为丰富且易于索引的结构。通过自动编码器提取“潜在词项”，可以在无需特定稀疏训练目标的情况下，实现优于基线模型及现有稀疏检索方法（如SPLADE）的检索性能。

Abstract: We propose Latent Terms, a method revealing that models trained for dense retrieval, whether single- or multi-vector, learn representations that can trivially be decomposed into retrieval-ready sparse features. When trained on frozen retrievers, Sparse Autoencoders without any retrieval-specific adjustments extract a latent vocabulary with approximately Zipfian collection statistics, directly suitable for classical sparse retrieval scoring via BM25. This approach enables sparse retrieval while requiring no learned expansion objective or sparse retrieval supervision whatsoever, and can be readily applied to any dense retriever. Latent Terms is able to match or outperform single-vector scoring methods from its own base model as well as comparable SPLADE variants. In addition, it substantially outperforms its base model on LIMIT, a task specifically designed to highlight the failures of single-vector retrieval. Overall, our results highlight that neural retrievers contain more expressive and indexable structure than their default scoring functions expose, but that other methods can nonetheless be leveraged.

</details>


### [247] [FLASH-MAXSIM: IO-Aware Fused Kernels for Late-Interaction Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29517)
*Roi Pony,Adi Raz Goldfarb,Idan Friedman,Daniel Ezer,Udi Barzelay*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Flash-MaxSim 是一种 IO 感知的 GPU 内核优化技术，通过流式处理避免了晚期交互检索中巨大的中间相似度矩阵存储，大幅提升了 ColBERT/ColPali 模型的训练与推理速度及显存效率，同时保持了原始精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 ColBERT 等晚期交互（Late-interaction）检索模型在计算 MaxSim 时，会产生巨大的 query-token × document-token 相似度张量，导致 GPU 显存耗尽，严重限制了推理 batch size 及模型训练规模。

Method: 提出了 IO 感知的融合 GPU 内核（Flash-MaxSim）：通过分块（tiling）技术利用片上 SRAM 进行流式计算，避免了巨大的 query-doc 相似度张量显存占用；同时集成了前向最大化归约、后向反向网格 CSR 梯度计算、INT8 量化及可变长度序列处理。

Result: 相较于原生 PyTorch 实现，Flash-MaxSim 在 A100 上推理速度提升达 3.9 倍（H100 上 4.7 倍）；推理显存减少至原来的 1/16，训练显存减少约 28 倍；同时在保持 100% Top-20 排序一致性的前提下，支持了原本无法承载的大规模语料库与 Batch Size。

Conclusion: Flash-MaxSim 通过消除冗余的中间张量存储与并行化计算，显著优化了 ColBERT/ColPali 类模型的推理与训练效率，在保持输出精度完全一致的前提下，实现了内存占用的大幅降低与速度的显著提升，是实现大规模检索任务的有效手段。

Abstract: Late-interaction retrieval (ColBERT, ColPali) scores a query against a document with the MaxSim operator: for every query token, the maximum similarity over the document tokens, summed over
  query tokens. The standard implementation materializes the full query-token x document-token similarity tensor in GPU memory; for visual ColPali at 10K documents this tensor alone is 21 GB in
  FP16, created only to be reduced to one score per document and discarded. It exhausts a 40 GB GPU and bounds the achievable batch size in both inference and training. We present
  Flash-MaxSim, an IO-aware fused GPU kernel that computes exactly the same scores without ever materializing the tensor, by streaming query and document tiles through on-chip SRAM and folding
  the row-maximum reduction into the same pass. We extend the IO-aware principle through the training backward pass, an inverse-grid CSR construction that reuses the forward argmax for an
  atomic-free, destination-owned gradient reduction, and through INT8xINT8 quantization and variable-length (padding-free) scoring. Flash-MaxSim is up to 3.9x faster on an A100 (4.7x on an
  H100) than naive PyTorch at matched precision, uses up to 16x less inference memory and ~28x less training memory, unlocks corpus and batch sizes that exhaust PyTorch entirely, preserves the
  exact ranking (100% top-20 agreement with an FP32 reference)

</details>


### [248] [Rec-Distill: An Industrial Distillation Pipeline for Large-Scale Recommendation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29755)
*Haoran Ding,Wenlin Zhao,Yuchen Jiang,Juren Li,Jie Zhu,Xinchun Li,Yishujie Zhao,Yi Zhang,Ao Qiao,Jianhui Dong,Cheng Chen,Ziyan Gong,Deping Xie,Peng Xu,Zikai Wang,Yuwei Wang,Huizhi Yang,Zhe Chen,Yuchao Zheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Rec-Distill 是一种旨在解决工业级推荐系统中“大模型离线训练与高效线上推理”矛盾的蒸馏框架，通过一系列优化机制将超大模型（24B）的性能高效迁移至轻量学生模型，在满足部署约束的前提下实现业务指标提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大规模推荐模型遵循缩放定律（Scaling Laws），但在工业界受限于严格的推理效率和延迟要求，导致离线大模型性能无法直接转化为线上业务收益，存在部署鸿沟。

Method: 提出了 Rec-Distill 工业级蒸馏流水线，核心组件包括：1. 大规模教师模型扩展（支持 24B 参数/20K 序列长度）；2. 学生端迁移优化（解耦训练、黑盒蒸馏、去偏机制）；3. 适用于动态推荐环境的混合批处理-流式处理流水线。

Result: 在多个工业推荐与广告场景中，轻量化学生模型成功恢复了教师模型 60% 以上的性能增益，并在线上实验中实现了显著的业务指标提升，有效平衡了模型规模、性能与部署成本。

Conclusion: Rec-Distill 成功搭建了连接大规模推荐模型与轻量化线上部署系统的桥梁，证实了通过蒸馏技术将超大模型性能迁移至高效部署模型是切实可行的路径，为未来推荐系统进一步扩展规模提供了可靠的技术支撑。

Abstract: Large recommendation models have demonstrated substantial potential gains under scaling laws, yet these gains are difficult to realize in industrial recommendation systems because real-world deployment requires lightweight models with strict serving efficiency and latency guarantees. This creates a fundamental gap between offline model scaling and online deployment. In this work, we present Rec-Distill, an industrial distillation pipeline that transfers the performance gains of large-scale recommendation modeling to efficient serving models. Rec-Distill combines large-teacher scaling with student-side transfer optimization through decoupled training, black-box distillation, debiasing mechanism, and a hybrid batch-streaming pipeline for dynamic recommendation environments. Across multiple recommendation and advertising scenarios on real-world platforms, our framework scales teacher models up to 24B dense parameters and 20K behavior sequence length, while enabling lightweight students to recover a substantial portion of teacher gains, with distillation transferability exceeding 60% in the best setting. Extensive offline and online experiments further show that these transferred gains consistently translate into measurable business improvements under industrial constraints. These results demonstrate that Rec-Distill provides a practical framework for distilling large-scale recommendation models into deployable, cost-efficient serving systems, while also establishing a reliable path toward scaling recommendation models to even larger regimes in the future.

</details>


### [249] [Uncertainty Quantification for Multimodal Retrieval Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29956)
*Simon Binz,Heydar Soudani,Faegheh Hasibi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对多模态RAG输出不确定性难以量化的问题，本文提出LeMUQ模型，通过分析多模态和检索上下文下的概率信号变化，显著提升了模型预测结果的可靠性评估能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的不确定性量化（UQ）方法多针对纯文本模型，难以应对多模态RAG（检索增强生成）场景中来自检索、视觉理解及生成阶段的复杂综合不确定性问题，导致模型回答可能不可靠。

Method: 提出LeMUQ（Learnable Multimodal UQ），通过输入修改（如移除模态或检索上下文）分析标记概率；将这些信号编码为概率标记，并利用微调模型进行处理，从而捕获模态间及模态与检索间的交互信息。

Result: 在多个数据集、检索器和视觉语言模型（VLM）上的实验显示，LeMUQ相比基准和微调UQ方法均有显著提升，平均AUROC指标提高了3.8%。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过建模多模态和检索感知的概率信号，能有效提升多模态RAG系统的不确定性量化能力。LeMUQ方法不仅增强了系统可靠性，且在不同检索设置和数据集上表现出良好的泛化性，为构建更安全、稳健的多模态RAG提供了重要技术支撑。

Abstract: Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) improves the question answering capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) by incorporating external knowledge and has recently been extended to multimodal settings through Vision-Language Models (VLMs) that integrate visual and textual information. Despite these advances, generated answers can still be incorrect or misleading. Uncertainty Quantification (UQ) methods aim to estimate the reliability of model outputs, but most existing approaches are designed for text-only models and perform poorly in multimodal RAG scenarios. A key challenge is capturing uncertainty arising from multiple stages of the pipeline, including retrieval, visual understanding, and generation. In this work, we show that modeling uncertainty using multimodal and retrieval-aware probability signals improves estimation in multimodal RAG systems. We introduce LeMUQ, a Learnable Multimodal UQ method that analyzes token probabilities under input modifications, such as removing modalities or retrieved context. By encoding these signals as probability tokens and processing them with a finetuned model, our approach captures interactions between modalities and retrieval. Experiments across datasets, retrievers, and VLMs show consistent improvements over baseline and finetuned UQ methods. Our proposed LeMUQ increases the AUROC metric by 3.8% on average. Additionally, our method shows strong generalization performance across different retrieval setups and datasets with mixed results when transferring across different VLMs. Our findings highlight the importance of modeling multimodal uncertainty and provide a step toward more reliable and safer multimodal RAG systems. Code is available on GitHub.

</details>


### [250] [No More K-means:Single-Stage Sparse Coding for Efficient Multi-Vector Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30120)
*Lixuan Guo,Yifei Wang,Tiansheng Wen,Aosong Feng,Stefanie Jegelka,Chenyu You*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SSR利用稀疏自编码器替代传统的聚类逻辑，实现了多向量检索在索引效率、检索延迟与性能上的“三赢”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于ColBERT的多向量检索模型虽然精度高，但面临存储和计算瓶颈。为处理大规模语料，模型往往被迫使用激进的降维和聚类方法，导致严重的索引延迟问题以及压缩带来的语义信息损失。

Method: 提出单阶段稀疏检索（SSR）框架，利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）将细粒度的Token嵌入映射为高维稀疏表示，从而摒弃了传统多向量检索中昂贵的K-means聚类与压缩过程，并直接利用倒排索引实现高效检索。

Result: 在BEIR基准测试中，SSR相比ColBERTv2索引时间缩短了15倍，检索延迟降低了50%，同时在检索准确率上优于多个领先基准模型。

Conclusion: SSR通过稀疏自编码器（SAE）实现了多向量检索的范式转变，在保持高检索精度的同时，显著降低了索引开销与检索延迟，为处理大规模语料库提供了一种更高效的方案。

Abstract: Multi-vector retrieval (MVR) models, exemplified by ColBERT, have established new benchmarks in retrieval accuracy by preserving fine-grained token-level interactions. However, this granularity imposes prohibitive storage and retrieval efficiency bottlenecks: to manage the immense memory footprint and computational overhead of billion-scale token vectors, state-of-the-art systems are forced to rely on aggressive dimension reduction and complex clustering (e.g., K-means). This compromise introduces two critical limitations: excessive indexing latency of clustering large-scale corpora and semantic information loss inherent to compression. In this paper, we propose Single-stage Sparse Retrieval (SSR}, a paradigm shift that replaces expensive clustering with efficient sparse coding. Instead of compressing features into low-dimensional dense vectors, we utilize Sparse Autoencoder (SAE) to project token embeddings into a high-dimensional but highly sparse representation. This transformation enables us to bypass vector clustering entirely and leverage inverted indexing for precise, high-throughput retrieval. Extensive experiments on the BEIR benchmark demonstrate that SSR achieves a "trifecta" of improvements: it reduces indexing time by 15x compared to ColBERTv2, halves retrieval latency, and simultaneously improves retrieval performance over leading baselines.

</details>


### [251] [LexPath: A domain-oriented multi-path framework for legal article retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30205)
*Weixuan Liu,Qingfeng Zhuge,Xuyang Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对法律文章检索中“语义匹配但法律逻辑不符”的难题，LexPath 通过引入法律逻辑（IRAC）与结构信息（层级/引证）的多路径检索及意图重排序机制，实现了更精准、可靠的法律AI检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的开放领域检索方法过于依赖表层的词汇或语义相似度，难以区分在文本上相似但法律上不适用或与用户底层的法律意图不一致的文章。

Method: 提出 LexPath 框架，包含：1. 多路径检索模块（IRAC指导的稀疏路径用于扩展关键词，结构引导的稠密路径利用法律层级和引证关系训练）；2. 意图感知重排序模块（结合查询与法律文章的意图一致性分数）。

Result: 在两个公共基准测试及一个自建的专业领域基准测试中，LexPath 的表现均优于现有的词汇、稠密、混合及自适应 RAG 基线模型，消融实验证实了各组件的有效性。

Conclusion: LexPath 通过结合 IRAC 指导的稀疏路径、结构引导的稠密路径以及意图感知重排序模块，有效提升了法律文章检索的准确性和相关性，在法律人工智能领域建立了一套更具可追溯性和可靠性的检索框架。

Abstract: Legal article retrieval is critical for building traceable and reliable legal AI systems, where conclusions must be grounded in specific legal articles. However, existing open-domain retrieval methods rely heavily on surface-level lexical or semantic similarity, making it difficult for them to distinguish legally relevant articles from those that are textually similar but legally inapplicable or misaligned with the user's underlying intent. To bridge this gap, we propose \textsc{LexPath}, a domain-oriented multi-path framework comprising a multi-path retrieval module and an intent-aware reranking module. The retrieval module combines two complementary legal-specific paths to collect candidate articles: an IRAC-guided sparse path that expands queries with legally informative keywords, and a structure-guided dense path trained with hard negatives derived from legal hierarchy and citation relations. Then, the reranking module further refines the candidate ranking by incorporating the intent consistency score between queries and legal articles. We evaluate \textsc{LexPath} on two publicly available benchmarks focusing on general-public queries and a self-constructed benchmark targeting domain-professional scenarios. Experimental results demonstrate that \textsc{LexPath} consistently outperforms lexical, dense, hybrid, and adaptive retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) baselines. Ablation studies further verify the effectiveness of each component.

</details>


### [252] [GRASP: Plan-Guided Graph Retrieval with Adaptive Fusion and Reranking on Semi-Structured Knowledge Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30237)
*Yicheng Tao,Yiqun Wang,Xiangchen Song,Xin Luo,Kai Liu,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: GRASP 是一个有效的三阶段 SKB 检索框架，通过融合计划驱动的图检索、密集检索与智能重排序，在 STaRK 基准上取得了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的半结构化知识库（SKB）混合检索系统存在局限性，例如仅将图用于查询扩展、全局权重混合文本/结构分支，或过度依赖图遍历生成器。需要一种更有效、更稳健的统一检索框架。

Method: 提出了一个三阶段的 SKB 检索框架：1. 基于计划的图检索（plan-based graph retrieval）；2. 计划条件下的融合检索（plan-conditioned fusion with a dense retriever）；3. 在融合候选集上的精细调节重排序（fine-tuned reranker）。

Result: 在 STaRK 基准测试的所有指标上均显著超过了现有技术水平（State-of-the-art），平均 Hit@1 指标从 62.0 提升至 73.9，且验证了该框架的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: GRASP 框架通过结合计划导向的图检索、融合密集检索器以及精细调节的重排序器，显著提升了半结构化知识库（SKB）的检索性能，在 STaRK 基准测试中实现了从 62.0 到 73.9 的 Hit@1 平均值大幅提升。

Abstract: Semi-structured knowledge bases (SKBs) embed textual documents in a typed graph of entities and relations, and underpin applications such as product search, academic paper search, and precision-medicine inquiries. Existing hybrid retrieval systems on SKBs either use the graph only for query expansion, mix textual and structural branches under a global weighting, or rely on fine-tuned graph-traversal generators. We present GRASP, a three-stage SKB retrieval framework unifying plan-based graph retrieval, plan-conditioned fusion with a dense retriever, and a fine-tuned reranker over the fused candidates. GRASP substantially advances the state of the art on every metric across the three STaRK benchmarks, lifting average Hit@1 from 62.0 to 73.9. Ablation and sensitivity studies further confirm the effectiveness and robustness of GRASP.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [253] [Constant Depth Threshold Circuits For Exhaustive Epistasis Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29719)
*André Ribeiro,Aleksandar Ilic,Leonel Sousa*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本研究利用神经形态硬件的阈值门电路加速生物信息学中的上皮细胞检测任务，通过创新的LIF神经元编码与常数深度计数电路设计，大幅提升了组合计算的效率并优化了空间复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大规模神经形态硬件的发展，阈值门电路变得可行；利用这类电路在处理复杂组合问题（如生物信息学中的上皮细胞检测）时相比传统计算架构的复杂性优势，以提升计算效率。

Method: 提出了一种基于Leaky Integrate and Fire (LIF) 神经元的数据编码和组合生成策略，并设计了常数深度阈值门群体计数电路；通过组合这些电路，在有限扇入和可变精度的约束下，实现了对数深度和对数立方线性连接的群体计数功能。

Result: 成功将上皮细胞检测的运行时间控制在计算组合数量的边界内，无需额外复杂性开销；在处理人口计数时实现了对数深度的电路复杂度和对数立方线性连接数。

Conclusion: 通过构建基于LIF神经元和阈值门电路的专用神经形态电路，实现了上皮细胞（Epistasis）检测的高效计算，解决了传统计算方法在处理复杂组合问题时的性能瓶颈，证明了神经形态硬件在生物信息学高性能计算领域的潜力。

Abstract: The development of large-scale neuromorphic hardware has made practical implementations of threshold gate-based circuits a near-term possibility. The complexity advantages regarding traditional computing classes, as evidenced in the literature, have prompted us to tackle Epistasis Detection, one of the most computationally complex combinatorial problems in bioinformatics. We propose specially designed circuits that calculate the relative frequencies of all dataset combinations in an efficient pipelined fashion, taking advantage of co-located memory and configurable parallelism, obtaining complexity gains. Overall, we obtain the runtime to be bounded by the number of combinations to calculate, without any additional complexity overhead, contrary to classical approaches, using log-linear space. To accomplish this, we propose a data encoding and combination generation strategy using Leaky Integrate and Fire (LIF) neurons, that feeds a constant depth threshold gate population count circuit. Accounting for typical hardware characteristics, such as limited fan-in and variable precisions, we obtain logarithmic depth and log-cubic linear connections, for the population count circuit by composing developed unbounded fan-in constant depth threshold gate circuits to perform population count and binary array sum.

</details>


### [254] [Design-Oriented Modeling of TSV Substrate Noise Coupling to Ring VCOs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29867)
*Ilias Exouzidis,Alberto Garcia-Ortiz,George Floros,Georgios Panagopoulos*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于 3D-IC/芯粒设计阶段的 TSV 诱导衬底噪声评估方法，建立了一个 RLGC 宏模型，并通过 22nm FD-SOI 中的环形 VCO 验证了 TSV 噪声的低通耦合特性及其对频谱纯度的负面影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D-IC 和芯粒（chiplet）技术中常用的 TSV 结构会引入寄生耦合路径，导致衬底噪声，从而损害混合信号电路中敏感射频（RF）模块的频谱纯度，亟需一种设计导向的评估方法。

Method: 提出了一种针对信号-接地 TSV 对的紧凑型闭式三重 RLGC 宏模型；在 22nm FD-SOI 工艺下，通过三级环形压控振荡器（Ring VCO）进行验证，利用 PDK 提供的衬底端子进行受控噪声注入，并配合 Spectre RF 多音谐波平衡仿真进行定性分析。

Result: 定量分析显示，在 1 GHz、0.5 Vpp 的干扰下，VCO 输出产生 -35.2 dBc 的边带杂散；在 500 MHz 至 2 GHz 范围内，耦合响应表现为随频率增加而减弱的低通特性（杂散值从 -20.2 dBc 降至 -33.1 dBc）。

Conclusion: 该研究证实了 TSV 对射频电路具有显著的噪声耦合影响，这种影响表现为低通特性，且边带杂散幅值与干扰源幅度呈线性相关关系。提出的 RLGC 模型为 3D-IC 设计阶段快速评估衬底噪声提供了高效的分析工具。

Abstract: Through-silicon vias (TSVs) enable dense vertical interconnects in 3D-IC and chiplet systems, but their metal-oxide-silicon structure introduces significant parasitic coupling paths that can degrade the spectral purity of sensitive RF blocks. This paper presents a compact, design-oriented methodology for assessing TSV-induced substrate noise in mixed-signal circuits. We derive a closed-form analytical three-port RLGC macromodel for a Signal-Ground TSV pair that explicitly exposes the substrate node. The methodology is validated using a three-stage Ring VCO designed in a 22 nm FD-SOI technology, where specific RF devices from the process design kit (PDK) provide direct access to the transistor substrate terminals for controlled noise injection. Multi-tone Harmonic Balance simulations in Spectre RF quantify the impact of TSV aggressors on the oscillator's output spectrum. The results indicate that an aggressor of 1 GHz, 0.5 V$_{pp}$ induces a primary sideband spur of -35.2 dBc. Sensitivity characterization reveals that the magnitude of these sideband spurs increases monotonically with the aggressor amplitude. Furthermore, frequency sweeps demonstrate a low-pass coupling response, where the induced spur magnitude decreases from -20.2 dBc at 500 MHz to -33.1 dBc at 2 GHz.

</details>


### [255] [Precomputed 1D-CNNs for Atrial Fibrillation Detection on Tiny Smart Sensor Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29994)
*Lukas Einhaus,Natalie Maman,Julian Hoever,Andreas Erbslöh,Gregor Schiele*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于分组卷积的LUT预计算优化方法，成功在微型FPGA上实现了高效、低延迟的1D-CNN，在医疗信号分类任务中达到高精度且极低资源占用的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LUT预计算是加速小型FPGA上1D-CNN的有效方法，但面临扩展性差的问题，现有研究仅局限于深度可分离卷积，缺乏针对更广泛分组卷积架构及其超参数优化的方案。

Method: 通过将深度可分离卷积推广至通用的分组卷积，提出了一种新型卷积模块及其超参数选择算法，以优化FPGA LUT资源的利用率，并完全避免了DSP和BRAM的使用。

Result: 在预测房颤的医疗数据集上实现了高达95%的F1-Score，同时仅消耗2,844个LUT，无需任何DSP或BRAM资源。

Conclusion: 通过引入分组卷积优化LUT预计算方案，实现了在极小规模FPGA（Spartan 7 S15）上高效运行1D-CNN，验证了该方法在资源受限环境下的实用性和性能表现。

Abstract: 1D-CNNs play a crucial role for time-series analysis on tiny smart sensor systems, e.g. for biosignal analysis, predictive maintenance, or structural health monitoring. LUTbased precomputation has emerged as an interesting optimization technique to implement such neural networks on FPGAs. The core idea is to precompute all possible outputs of a neural network layer and store them directly in the lookup tables of the FPGAs. This enables highly resource-efficient networks with ultra-low latency but suffers from poor scalability. Previous work has explored using depthwise-separable convolutions to improve scalability. In this paper, we generalize this approach to consider additional forms of grouped convolutions. Based on this, we propose a novel type of convolutional block and an algorithm to guide the choice of hyper parameters for this block. We evaluate our approach on a medical time-series dataset for predicting atrial fibrillation using the MIT-BIH database (ECG recordings). The resulting hardware accelerators are small enough to be deployed on an AMD Spartan 7 S15. They achieve a F1-Score of up to 95% while only requiring 2,844 LUTs and no DSPs or BRAM.

</details>


### [256] [elasticAI.explorer: Towards a Unified End-to-End Framework for Hardware-Aware Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30019)
*Natalie Maman,Florian Hettstedt,Andreas Erbslöh,Gregor Schiele*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: elasticAI.explorer 是一个面向硬件感知 NAS 的可扩展框架，通过统一的 YAML 规范和自动化部署工具链，解决了异构硬件上 NAS 部署困难和扩展性差的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 NAS 框架由于搜索空间、模型实现与部署流程耦合过紧，难以扩展至新硬件平台与自定义算子，增加了嵌入式 AI 部署的工程负担。

Method: 开发了一个基于 Optuna 的可扩展 Python 框架 elasticAI.explorer，采用 YAML 定义搜索空间，支持硬件感知代码生成、Docker 交叉编译工具链以及自动化片上基准测试。

Result: 构建了一套支持层级式/细胞式/分层搜索空间的统一框架，实现了硬件在环（Hardware-in-the-loop）的 NAS 工作流，并提供 FLOPs、参数量及延迟评估的扩展接口。

Conclusion: elasticAI.explorer 通过降低硬件感知 NAS 的工程门槛和整合全流程（从搜索到部署），有效促进了异构加速器平台上的嵌入式 AI 研究。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) has become an important approach for automatically designing neural networks under task-specific and hardware-specific constraints.
