However, many existing NAS frameworks tightly couple search space definitions, model implementations, and deployment pipelines, making extension to new hardware platforms and custom operators difficult. In this paper, we present the elasticAI.explorer, an extensible Python framework for hardware-aware NAS built on top of Optuna. The framework introduces a YAML-based search space specification that dynamically translates into executable neural network models during sampling. The approach supports layer-wise, cell-based, and hierarchical search spaces while maintaining a unified interface for optimization and deployment. Beyond architecture generation, the framework integrates hardware-specific code generation, Docker-based cross-compilation toolchains, and automated creation of on-device benchmarking binaries, enabling hardware-in-the-loop NAS workflows. The system further provides extensible evaluators for FLOPs, parameter count, and latency estimation. The elasticAI.explorer aims to reduce the engineering overhead of embedded AI deployment and accelerate research on hardware-aware NAS for heterogeneous accelerator platforms

</details>


<div id='cs.PF'></div>

# cs.PF [[Back]](#toc)

### [257] [From Roofline to Ruggedness: Decomposing and Smoothing the GEMM Performance Landscape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29752)
*Aditya Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 本文研究了GPU GEMM计算中被Roofline模型忽略的性能剧烈波动现象（Performance Ruggedness），提出了一种分析框架并开发了双阶段软件优化策略，成功实现性能波动降低70%及平稳化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Roofline模型无法解释GPU在相邻GEMM算子间观察到的高达30%的性能波动（即所谓的“性能崎岖性”），这种现象在非峰值负载中非常普遍，阻碍了深度学习负载的性能最大化。

Method: 提出了一种性能崎岖性分析（Performance Ruggedness Analysis）框架；在Intel Battlemage GPU上进行了32,768组GEMM配置的超大规模扫描；应用了“动态平铺选择”和“基于动态规划的填充与拆分优化”两阶段软件优化策略。

Result: 该方法将初步的性能波动（16.8 TFLOPs/128步）降低了70%，并将平均吞吐量提升了30%；通过实验明确了残余性能波动源于硬件架构特性（如核开销、波阵面量化、DPAS几何结构及内存通道哈希冲突）。

Conclusion: 研究发现，GPU性能呈现出显著的“崎岖性（Ruggedness）”，即微小的维度变化会导致巨大的吞吐量波动。通过所提出的框架，研究人员能够有效分解性能波动的来源（软件可优化的 vs 硬件固有的），并利用动态平铺和填充策略显著降低性能波动并提升平均吞吐量。

Abstract: Adjacent GEMM problems that differ by a single 128-element step in N can show 30% different throughput on the same GPU. This pervasive performance ruggedness - invisible to roofline analysis and peak-FLOPs intuition, yet dominant for every non-peak workload - is the subject of this paper.
  We propose performance ruggedness analysis as an analytical framework complementary to roofline: rather than summarizing GPU performance with a scalar bound, treat the full multidimensional performance surface as the object of study, decompose its texture into mechanism-attributable components and separate software-removable contributions from hardware-bound ones. The framing is directly analogous to deep-learning loss landscapes - a continuous quantity (the idealized time 2MNK / compute_throughput_peak) made rugged by interaction with discrete hardware substrates (tiles, sub-groups, cache lines, DRAM channels).
  We apply the framework to BF16 NN (no transpose) GEMM on Intel Battlemage (Arc B580, sycl-tla) via a 32,768-configuration sweep (M, N, K) belongs to {128, ..., 4096}^3. The peak is 110.8 TFLOPs at the non-square shape M=3840, N=2048, K=4096 with the default tile size; the initial landscape roughness is 16.8 TFLOPs per 128-step against an ideal of 2.0. A two-stage software stack - (i) best-of-six dynamic tile selection and (ii) a novel dynamic-programming based padding-and-splitting optimizer with O(1) runtime lookup - reduces roughness by 70% and raises mean throughput by 30%. Cross-tile experiments establish that the residual sawtooth period scales exactly with software tile size, ruling out cache set conflicts and attributing the remaining variance to four hardware-bound sources (per-kernel base overhead, wave quantization, DPAS atom geometry and GDDR6 channel-hash interactions).

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [258] [Dynamics of Stochastic Momentum with Sparse Updates in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28961)
*Katie Everett,Elliot Paquette*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了稀疏更新场景下动量法的动力学行为，指出动量优化的稳定性取决于“动量保持”与“学习速度”的平衡，并揭示了稀疏性导致的动量参数选取中的频谱冲突问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动量理论假设梯度以恒定速率到达，但在现实中，重尾数据分布和现代架构导致的稀疏更新违背了此假设，需重新评估稀疏更新下动量的动力学行为。

Method: 通过分析最小二乘法（具有稀疏输入）和逻辑回归（具有稀疏类别）这两个易于处理的动力学模型，推导二阶矩动力学的精确闭式解，利用高维极限分析 sparsity、batch size 和动量衰减这三个因素。

Result: 揭示了相变结构由动量保持时间与学习时间的比例决定：若学习慢于保持则趋同于 SGD，快于保持则系统不稳定，两者相等时对应经典重球动力学；此外，不同稀疏度的 token 导致最佳动量值冲突。

Conclusion: 动量优化器的性能取决于“动量保持时间”与“学习时间”的平衡，且在不同频率（稀疏度）的参数间存在“频谱冲突”，说明全局动量设置在处理异构数据时存在局限性。

Abstract: Existing theory of momentum assumes that gradients arrive at every parameter at a roughly constant rate, an assumption violated in practice by heavy-tailed data distributions and modern architectures. We theoretically analyze the dynamics of two tractable models of momentum under sparse updates: a least squares model with sparse inputs and a logistic regression model with a rare class. Both admit exact closed-form second-moment dynamics whose high-dimensional limits we characterize across three scaling exponents for sparsity, batch size, and momentum decay. The phase structure on both problems is governed by the ratio of two intrinsic timescales: a momentum retention timescale (how many active updates the buffer survives) and a learning timescale (how many active updates it takes to reduce the squared error). When learning is much slower than retention, the limit matches SGD; when learning is faster, the system is unstable; where the timescales coincide, we recover classical heavy-ball dynamics. The oscillatory dynamics occur at different momentum values for different token sparsity, creating a spectral conflict for global momentum across token frequencies.

</details>


### [259] [Anytime-Valid Federated Conformal RAG for LLM Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29139)
*Prasanjit Dubey,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: Anytime-FC-RAG 将联邦 RAG 的覆盖保证从固定时域扩展至任意停止时间，通过引入可加的校准偏差预算和非负超鞅机制，在支持自适应带宽调整和模型刷新的同时，维持了统计的严格有效性，显著降低了通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 FC-RAG 仅限于固定时域，无法处理需要任意停止时间的覆盖保证，且无法在自适应调整（如根据需要动态调整带宽）时保持统计有效性。

Method: 提出 Anytime-FC-RAG，核心在于采用可加的每步校准偏差预算将边际覆盖界转化为条件边界，并构建截断的投注 e-process（非负超鞅）以实现时间一致的有效性保证。同时引入训练端的联邦探测逻辑蒸馏（FPLD）误差传播管理。

Result: 在 MMLU、DBpedia 和 AG News 等数据集上的实验表明，该方法实现了预测的告警率和覆盖包络，并在保持告警准确性的同时，实现了 14%-57% 的通信带宽节省。

Conclusion: Anytime-FC-RAG 成功实现了在任何停止时间（anytime-valid）下的序列覆盖保证，且在存在预测性自适应控制机制（重校准、带宽升级、蒸馏刷新）的情况下保持有效性。实验证明该方法在保证告警准确性的前提下，显著降低了联邦RAG系统的通信带宽成本。

Abstract: Federated Conformal RAG (FC-RAG) provides distribution-free coverage for a bandwidth-limited swarm of weak language models, but only at a fixed horizon. We extend it to anytime-valid sequential coverage: validity at every stopping time, preserved under predictable adaptive control (recalibration, per-node bandwidth escalation, distilled-student refresh), at no extra cost in assumptions over fixed-horizon FC-RAG. Naive composition fails because FC-RAG's marginal coverage bound makes the betting e-process a non-supermartingale on adverse calibration draws, and Ville's inequality cannot be invoked. We give Anytime-FC-RAG, a sequential extension built on a summable per-step calibration-deviation budget that converts the marginal bound into a strict conditional bound on a calibration-good event, paired with a truncated betting e-process that is a nonnegative supermartingale on the entire probability space. From these two ingredients, we obtain four guarantees: time-uniform alarm validity $\mathbb{P}(\sup_t E_t \ge 1/δ_e) \le δ_e + δ_{\mathrm{cal}}$, a Hoeffding-stitched cumulative-miscoverage envelope at the same total budget, safety under any predictable controller (recalibration, bandwidth escalation, student refresh), and training-side error propagation across an unbounded sequence of Federated Probe-Logit Distillation (FPLD) refreshes via a summable training budget. As a practical consequence, an adaptive controller that escalates retrieval bandwidth only when the e-process crosses a warning threshold matches the alarm rate of a fixed-high-bandwidth schedule at substantially lower communication cost. Experiments on a GPT-2-small + MiniLM swarm across MMLU, DBpedia, and AG News verify the predicted alarm rate, detection delay, envelope coverage, and $14$-$57\%$ bandwidth savings; the alarm fires when and only when coverage genuinely breaks.

</details>


### [260] [Matching Rates and Optimal Allocation for Federated Probe-Logit Distillation under Heterogeneous Bandwidth Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29642)
*Prasanjit Dubey,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过理论分析和实验验证，确定了联邦学习中逻辑蒸馏在特定带宽限制下的最优误差速率，并提出了一种针对异构带宽节点的高效通信预算分配策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在联邦语言模型训练中，各节点受限于通信带宽，现有研究对通信开销与误差界限的紧密性理解不足，且缺乏对异构节点带宽分配的理论指导。该文旨在补全FPLD在带宽项上的速率分析缺口，并解决异构预算下的最优分配问题。

Method: 1. 理论下界证明：采用抖动（dithered）FPLD构造，证明了单轮通信的极小极大下界；2. 多轮优化：引入嵌套/缩放残差量化器实现顺序细化；3. 异构资源分配：推导了基于“对数倾斜注水”算法的最优带宽分配规则，并设计了自适应插件变体。

Result: 1. 确立了带宽项的紧度为 $\Theta(K^{-1} \cdot 2^{-2B/V})$；2. 证明了多轮细化可将速率改进为 $O(K^{-1} \cdot 2^{-2TB/V})$；3. 给出了异构预算 $B_i$ 下的最优分配公式 $B_i^*$。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了联邦探针逻辑蒸馏（FPLD）在带宽限制下的极小极大速率下界，证明了多轮顺序细化方案的优化性，并提出了针对异构带宽节点的“对数倾斜注水”优化分配策略。

Abstract: In federated language modeling, $K$ nodes each hold $n$ samples but cannot pool data or exchange full-precision gradients or weights. We study the minimax rate at which a conditional distribution over $V$ tokens can be estimated when each node may upload at most $B$ bits per query in a public probe set. In federated probe-logit distillation (FPLD), each node transmits a scalar-quantized logit vector on the probe set, and an aggregator distills a global parametric student. Prior work (Dubey and Huo, 2026) establishes a high-probability KL rate $O(d/(Kn) + ρ\sqrt{V \log V / m} + K^{-1} \cdot 2^{-2B/V})$ plus optimization slack, with the bandwidth term in its trace-sharpened form. Whether this bandwidth-term rate is tight, and how the upper bound generalizes to heterogeneous per-node bandwidths, are left open.
  We close both gaps. First, the dithered FPLD construction has a matching single-round lower bound $Ω(K^{-1} \cdot 2^{-2B/V})$ under non-degeneracy, pinning the bandwidth-axis rate at $Θ(K^{-1} \cdot 2^{-2B/V})$. $T$-round sequential refinement with nested/scaled residual quantizers achieves $O(K^{-1} \cdot 2^{-2TB/V})$; vanilla FPLD's $T$-independent bandwidth term is suboptimal for every $T > 1$. Second, we establish a heterogeneous-bandwidth upper bound for per-node budgets $B_i$, paired with a closed-form optimal allocation $B_i^* = B_{\mathrm{tot}}/K + (V/2) \log_2(w_i / \bar{w}_g)$, a log-tilted water-filling rule that is the per-node analogue of reverse water-filling for distortion-rate optimization. A plug-in adaptive variant estimates the weights from a short warm-up phase and attains $1 + O(\sqrt{\log(K/δ)/(m T_0)})$ relative suboptimality. Synthetic n-gram simulations confirm that empirical KL is bracketed by the upper and lower bounds and that the optimal allocation strictly dominates uniform and inverse-weighted baselines under heterogeneous clipping.

</details>


### [261] [Instance-dependent Stochastic Lipschitz bandit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29748)
*Marius Potfer,Vianney Perchet*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对 Lipschitz 带宽问题，提出了一种基于次优性差距积分的后悔分析新框架，实现了超越传统 Zooming 算法的自适应后悔界，在最优解集合维度大于零时表现尤为优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 Lipschitz 带宽问题算法（如 zooming 算法）主要依赖于水平集的渐近增长，无法捕捉函数的精细结构，导致在某些情形下的后悔界不够紧致。

Method: 提出了一种基于函数次优性差距对水平集进行积分的分析方法，利用函数局部增长特性而非仅仅是水平集的渐近增长特性来衡量后悔。

Result: 不仅在随机 Bandit 设置下获得了更优的后悔界 $\tilde{\mathcal{O}} ( T^{\frac{d_z+1}{\max(d_z,d^\star)+2}})$，还证明了该分析框架在全信息（Lipschitz experts）设置下的有效性，并放宽了正则性假设。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于Lipschitz函数次优性差距积分的后悔（regret）分析框架，证明了该方法在 maximizer 集合具有正维数时能显著改进渐近 Zooming 算法的后悔界，并将其扩展到了全信息设置。

Abstract: We study the Lipschitz bandit problem, where a learner sequentially maximizes an unknown Lipschitz function $f$ over a domain $\mathcal{X} \subset [0,1]^d$ using noisy pointwise evaluations. Existing regret bounds are either worst-case, scaling as $\tildeΘ \left ( T^{d+1/d+2}\right )$, or adaptive via the zooming dimension $d_z$, yielding $\tildeΘ \left ( T^{d_z+1/d_z+2}\right )$. However, such zooming-based guarantees are only partially instance-dependent, as they depend solely on the asymptotic growth of near-optimal level sets and fail to capture finer structural properties of $f$. We provide an analysis and an algorithm that characterizes the regret through integrals of the suboptimality gap of $f$ over its level sets. This yields regret bounds that adapt to the local growth of level sets, rather than only their asymptotic behavior. As a corollary, when the set of maximizers has dimension $d^\star>0$, we obtain improved adaptive rates of order $\tilde{\mathcal{O}} \left ( T^{d_z+1 / \max(d_z,d^\star)+2}\right )$ strictly improving over classical zooming bounds in this regime. Finally, we extend our analysis to the full-information setting (Lipschitz experts) and show how some of the regularity assumptions can be relaxed.

</details>


### [262] [Joint Model and Data Sparsification via the Marginal Likelihood](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29908)
*Alexander Timans,Thomas Möllenhoff,Christian A. Naesseth,Mohammad Emtiyaz Khan,Eric Nalisnick*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对传统稀疏贝叶斯学习对异常值敏感的问题，本文提出了一种同时对特征和样本进行稀疏化的贝叶斯建模方法，实现了鲁棒且高效的稀疏回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统稀疏贝叶斯学习（SBL）基于异方差噪声假设，对离群点和噪声误设定极其敏感，导致模型鲁棒性差。

Method: 提出了一种联合学习特征权重与样本相关性（Sample Relevancy）的方法，将其融入单一贝叶斯目标函数中，实现联合稀疏化（对称剪枝），并保持了共轭性与高效的闭式更新。

Result: 在多种回归任务下的实验表明，相较于传统方法，该模型能有效识别噪声样本，并在保持模型稀疏性的同时显著提升预测鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过联合学习特征和样本权重，实现了一种兼具鲁棒性和稀疏性的贝叶斯回归框架。该方法不仅扩展了传统稀疏贝叶斯学习（SBL），还提升了处理异常值的能力，且保留了闭式解的计算优势。

Abstract: Sparse recovery in linear systems underpins applications from signal processing to high-dimensional regression. Sparse Bayesian Learning, grounded in the principle of automatic relevance determination (ARD), offers a practical Bayesian mechanism for feature sparsity via marginal likelihood optimization. Yet, its reliance on a homoscedastic noise model renders it sensitive to data contaminations such as outliers or misspecified noise, harming model fit and predictions. Instead, we propose jointly learning individual feature and sample relevancies, enabling simultaneous model and data sparsification via a single Bayesian objective. This symmetric pruning of model and data offers a natural extension that preserves conjugacy, admits closed-form updates for standard optimization procedures, and aligns with perspectives from robust regression and influence functions. Empirical results across diverse regression tasks affirm that a joint ARD approach consistently yields both sparse and robust prediction models.

</details>


### [263] [Improved Guarantees for Heterogeneous Treatment-Effect Estimation via Matrix Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30319)
*Anay Mehrotra,Phuc Tran,Van H. Vu,Manolis Zampetakis*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对面板数据中异质性处理效应估计难题，本文提出一种基于低秩矩阵补全的高效算法，并证明了其在逐行估计误差上的最优性，完善了低秩矩阵的逐行扰动理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的矩阵补全理论主要针对全局（平均）误差界，无法满足因果推断中对每个个体（逐行）的异质性处理效应进行精确估计的需求。

Method: 将异质性处理效应估计问题转化为矩阵补全问题，基于低秩假设，设计了一种在未知倾向得分（propensity scores）下亦能运行的简单且计算高效的估计器，并运用新的逐行 $\ell_2$ 扰动理论进行分析。

Result: 该估计器在无需预知倾向得分且满足正则性假设的前提下，证明了达到了 $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{1/n + n/m^2})$ 的逐行 $\ell_2$ 误差界，解决了因果推断中异质性效应估计的理论瓶颈。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种新的低秩矩阵补全估计器，能够实现对异质性处理效应的精确逐行估计，其误差界为 $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{1/n + n/m^2})$，并提供了首个严谨的低秩逼近逐行 $\ell_2$ 扰动界。

Abstract: A central goal of modern causal inference is estimating heterogeneous treatment effects to answer questions like "how does an intervention affect each unit," rather than only on average. We study this problem with panel-data where we observe $n$ units across $m$ times under unknown, non-uniform treatment assignments. The data in this setting is naturally represented as a matrix of all unit--time treatment effects. Estimating heterogeneous treatment effects can then be expressed as obtaining a good estimation of each row's average in this matrix. This allows us to formulate the problem as matrix completion, which can be solved under natural low-rankness assumptions. However, existing matrix-completion guarantees are not powerful enough to get meaningful bounds for the per-row guarantee required for estimating the heterogeneous treatment effect; roughly speaking, they are only useful for estimating average treatment effect bounds, as also illustrated in a recent line of work. We give a simple, computationally efficient estimator that, without knowledge of the propensities and under standard low-rankness and regularity assumptions, achieves a row-wise $\ell_2$ error of $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{\frac{1}{n} + \frac{n}{m^2}})$. Technically, our analysis establishes the first sharp row-wise $\ell_2$-perturbation bound for low-rank approximation, complementing existing spectral-, Frobenius-, and entrywise perturbation theory.

</details>


### [264] [Diffusion Models Are Statistically Optimal for Learning Low-Dimensional Multi-Modal Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30153)
*Jingda Wu,Changxiao Cai*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文证明了扩散模型能够通过学习数据的内在低维结构实现高效的统计学习，无需强假设即可在多模态数据上达到最优的收敛速率，解释了其在高维任务中的成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对扩散模型的理论分析往往依赖于较强的正则性假设（如全局平滑或均匀有界密度），无法解释其在处理高维、多模态及具有低维结构数据时的优异性能。

Method: 通过理论分析扩散模型样本复杂度的上限，针对数据分布在低维子空间并集上的情况，推导出其在1-Wasserstein距离下的收敛界限。

Result: 在子空间数据为亚高斯分布的假设下，扩散模型达到 $\varepsilon$ 误差所需的样本复杂度至多为 $\widetilde{O}(\varepsilon^{-k \vee 2})$（$k$ 为本征维度），该结果摆脱了对环境维度的依赖。

Conclusion: 扩散模型在学习低维流形数据时具有统计适应性，能够克服维度灾难，在无需强正则化假设下实现近乎最优的收敛速率。

Abstract: Score-based diffusion models have demonstrated remarkable empirical success in learning high-dimensional distributions, particularly those exhibiting low-dimensional and multi-modal structures. However, theoretical understanding of their statistical efficiency remains limited. Existing theories typically rely on strong regularity assumptions, such as uniformly bounded densities or globally smooth score functions, which fail to capture such intrinsic structures. In this work, we study the sample complexity of diffusion models for learning distributions supported on a union of low-dimensional subspaces. Assuming that the data distribution within each subspace is subgaussian, we show that diffusion models require at most $\widetilde{O}(\varepsilon^{-k \vee 2})$ samples to achieve $\varepsilon$ error in 1-Wasserstein distance, where $k$ is the intrinsic dimension. This near-optimal convergence rate depends only on the intrinsic dimension and significantly improves upon prior theoretical guarantees that suffer from the curse of dimensionality. Notably, our analysis applies to a broad collection of distributions without imposing smoothness, bounded-density, or log-concavity assumptions. Overall, our results show that diffusion models can statistically adapt to intrinsic low-dimensional structure while naturally accommodating multi-modal data, offering a rigorous theoretical justification for their success in complex high-dimensional learning tasks.

</details>


### [265] [Wasserstein Contraction of Coordinate Ascent Variational Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30253)
*Rocco Caprio,Adrien Corenflos,Sam Power*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过传输-信息不等式证明了坐标上升变分推断（CAVI）算法在多种模型和空间下的Wasserstein距离收缩性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立坐标上升变分推断（CAVI）在Wasserstein度量下的全局或局部收缩性理论基础，以填补算法收敛性分析的理论空白。

Method: 利用传输-信息不等式（transport-information inequality）分析算法在其不动点周围的收缩行为，并结合函数平滑度条件在一般平滑流形及部分非平滑空间中进行理论推导。

Result: 证明了CAVI算法在满足特定正则性条件下的收缩特性，验证了其在贝叶斯高斯混合模型、高维Probit回归及基于Pólya-Gamma变量的逻辑回归中的有效性。

Conclusion: 在特定的传输-信息不等式和函数平滑度条件下，坐标上升变分推断（CAVI）算法表现出Wasserstein距离意义下的收缩性质；该结果通过了高斯混合模型和贝叶斯回归等实证应用验证。

Abstract: We study the contraction in Wasserstein distance of the coordinate ascent variational inference algorithm. This is shown to hold under a transport-information inequality at the fixed points and a functional smoothness condition. The results are general and sharp, allow for local convergence guarantees, hold for general smooth manifolds, and also in some non-smooth spaces. We consider applications to Bayesian Gaussian Mixture Models, and high-dimensional Bayesian Probit Regression, and Logistic Regression with Pólya-Gamma random variables (i.e. Jaakkola-Jordan's algorithm).

</details>


### [266] [Leave a Window Out: Modifying the Jackknife for Predictive Inference in Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30292)
*Hanyang Jiang,Rina Foygel Barber,Ashwin Pananjady,Yao Xie*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对时间序列预测中的覆盖失效问题，本文提出了“留窗法”（LWO），在无需数据分割的前提下，通过针对时间依赖性调整的重采样机制，实现了比现有方法更窄且更稳健的预测区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统共形预测依赖可交换性假设，在存在时间依赖性的时间序列数据中失效；而基于数据分裂的共形预测虽然稳健但牺牲了预测精度。因此，研究人员希望寻求无需数据分割且适用于时间序列的可靠预测推断方法。

Method: 提出了“留窗法”（Leave-a-Window-Out, LWO），通过量化数据对“循环可交换性”（Cyclic Exchangeability）的偏离程度，并引入新的系数来度量这种偏离，从而实现有效的覆盖。

Result: 实验表明，在传统Jackknife方法由于时间依赖失效的情况下，LWO方法能够保持有效的覆盖率，同时产生的预测区间显著优于分裂共形预测。

Conclusion: LWO方法在时间序列设置下能够弥补传统留一法（Jackknife）的覆盖失效问题，且在保持有效覆盖的前提下，相比分裂共形预测（Split Conformal Prediction）产生了更窄的预测区间。

Abstract: Conformal prediction methods enjoy strong theoretical and empirical predictive inference performance, provided the data is exchangeable, and predictors are trained in a memoryless fashion. However, these assumptions and constraints are impractical in many real-data settings, such as time series (where temporal dependence violates exchangeability, and where memoryless predictors will inevitably have poor predictive accuracy). Recent work shows that the split conformal prediction method is robust to these issues of memory-based predictors and deviations from exchangeability that are common features of time-series data. However, since using sample splitting can lead to lower accuracy, this motivates asking whether other predictive inference methods (that do not rely on data splitting) could also be reliably used in the time series setting.
  In this work, we show that the vanilla leave-one-out jackknife can suffer an arbitrary loss of coverage even in canonical time series models with mild temporal dependence. As a remedy, we propose a careful modification tailored to such settings, which we term the \emph{leave-a-window-out} (LWO) method, and show that it can achieve valid coverage provided that the model-fitting procedure satisfies mild stability properties. Our proofs are based on quantifying the degree to which the data departs from \emph{cyclic exchangeability}, and we introduce new coefficients to measure the extent of this departure. Experiments on time series data demonstrate that our LWO method often enjoys valid coverage when the vanilla jackknife fails to cover, while producing much narrower intervals than split conformal prediction.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [267] [Understanding and Reducing Metadata-Driven Host Overheads in Sampling-Based GNN Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29346)
*Yidong Gong,Saima Afrin,Yuchen Ma,Guannan Wang,Bin Ren,Pradeep Kumar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ZEROGNN 通过将元数据处理和控制逻辑完全移至 GPU 内部，去除了主机端对动态 GNN 训练的调度干预，解决了传统方法难以应对执行结构动态变化的难题，大幅提升了训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习（特别是基于采样的 GNN 训练）表现出动态、依赖元数据的执行特征，导致 CPU 频繁参与主机-设备调度与同步，成为性能瓶颈，而传统技术（如 CUDA Graphs）难以处理这种动态变化。

Method: 1. 将运行时元数据保留在 GPU 设备端；2. 在固定的启动结构（fixed launch structure）内协调动态执行；3. 采用保守但紧凑的执行包络（execution envelope）策略，恢复了 CUDA Graph 的可重放性。

Result: 在基于采样的 GNN 工作负载中，实现了最高 5.28 倍的端到端加速，GPU 执行占比接近 100%，内存效率与理想情况相当，并具备优秀的跨多 GPU 扩展能力。

Conclusion: ZEROGNN 成功将基于元数据的控制循环从 CPU 卸载到 GPU，实现了完全驻留 GPU 的执行，从而消除了主机端瓶颈，显著提升了 GNN 训练的端到端性能和扩展性。

Abstract: Modern deep learning workloads increasingly exhibit dynamic, metadata-driven execution, where runtime-generated information determines memory provisioning and kernel launch decisions. In sampling-based graph neural network (GNN) training, this behavior places the CPU on the critical path, introducing persistent host-device orchestration overhead and frequent GPU-CPU synchronization, which dominate end-to-end runtime when GPU computation is small. Existing approaches, including CUDA Graphs and GPU dynamic parallelism, fail to address this problem because the metadata-driven control loop remains host-mediated, and execution structure varies across iterations. We present ZEROGNN, a system that removes the host from the metadata-driven control loop and enables fully GPU-resident execution under dynamic behavior. ZEROGNN keeps runtime metadata on-device, mediates dynamic execution within a fixed launch structure, and provisions a conservative yet tight execution envelope to restore CUDA Graph replayability. Experiments on sampling-based GNN workloads show that ZEROGNN achieves up to 5.28 x end-to-end speedup, near 100% GPU execution fraction, and memory efficiency comparable to ideal metadata-informed allocation, while enabling strong multi-GPU scaling by eliminating host-side bottlenecks.

</details>


### [268] [Silent Data Corruption Protection through Efficient Task Replication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29506)
*Mia Reitz,Claudia Fohry*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对超级计算机中静默数据损坏（SDC）问题，本论文提出了一种针对嵌套分治程序的任务树记录与差异化重执行方案，有效解决了AMT运行时环境下任务副本识别与高效恢复的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着超级计算机集群规模的扩大，静默数据损坏（SDC）风险日益增加。传统的副本检测机制在异步多任务（AMT）运行时环境中，因任务动态生成和工作窃取机制，难以有效识别和追踪副本任务。

Method: 引入了一种基于任务树记录的复制方案：在执行过程中记录任务树结构；当检测到最终结果不匹配时，通过自顶向下遍历任务树来定位受损任务，并仅对这些任务进行重执行（re-computation），同时复用未受损子任务的执行结果。

Result: 在Itoyori集群AMT运行时系统上的实验表明，该机制在识别和处理受损任务方面的时间开销几乎可以忽略不计。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种适用于嵌套分治（fork-join）程序的SDC检测与恢复机制，经验证其在识别和处理受损任务上的开销极低，并具备扩展至支持基于Future协作任务的潜力。

Abstract: The trend of increasing cluster sizes of supercomputers leads to a growing susceptibility to Silent Data Corruption (SDC) that can invalidate program results. A common strategy for SDC protection is replication, where the computation is repeated, and the correct result is determined as the one that is the same in at least two different computations. Applying replication to Asynchronous Many-Task (AMT) runtimes on clusters is challenging due to dynamic task spawning and work stealing, which complicate the identification of replicated tasks.
  To address the challenge, this paper introduces a novel replication scheme that detects and corrects SDCs for nested fork-join programs. Briefly stated, our approach replicates the computation and records the task tree. Upon a mismatch in the final result, it traverses the tree top-down to identify all corrupted tasks that could have impacted the final result. Recovery is then performed by recomputing these tasks, while the results of correct child tasks are reused.
  We demonstrate our implementation within a variant of the Itoyori cluster AMT runtime. Our experimental results suggest that the time to identify and reprocess the affected tasks is negligible. The paper concludes by discussing the adaptability of our scheme to tasks that cooperate through futures.

</details>


### [269] [Design and Implementation of a Serverless MapReduce Framework for Scalable Data Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29573)
*Angelos Dorotheos Chatzopoulos,Babis Andreou,Kakia Panagidi,Stathes Hadjiefthymiades*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种部署在云原生环境（Kubernetes/Knative）下的实时事件驱动MapReduce框架，通过集成FaaS架构与高性能消息中间件，有效解决了物流大数据处理的实时性与可扩展性需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物流系统产生持续的数据流，迫切需要能实时分析这些海量数据以进行高效运营监控和智能决策，但传统方案在实时性和弹性上存在不足。

Method: 基于Kubernetes和Knative构建，利用Apache Kafka作为通信骨干，采用五种松耦合服务实现MapReduce编程模型；使用Redis存储工作流元数据，AWS S3进行持久化存储；结合FaaS原则实现基于负载的自动缩放。

Result: 实验表明该系统能随输入数据量有效地进行横向扩展，并成功实现了“规模缩减至零”（scale-to-zero）的按需处理能力。

Conclusion: 提出并验证了一个可扩展、基于事件驱动的Serverless MapReduce框架，能够高效处理物流领域的实时流数据，并支持按需弹性伸缩与自动缩容至零。

Abstract: Modern logistics systems tend to generate continuous streams of data from sources such as GPS, IoT sensors, and logistics management systems. The aggregation, processing, and analysis of data have become vital for monitoring operations, optimizing efficiency, and responding quickly to decision making tasks. In this paper, an event-driven MapReduce framework for real-time data processing in logistics environments is presented. This system runs on Kubernetes with Knative and utilizes Apache Kafka as the backbone for communication between the components. This platform is composed of five loosely coupled services that receive, process, and aggregate the incoming data in real-time. Redis is used to preserve workflow metadata, while an AWS S3 service provides persistent storage for the framework. The design is inspired by the MapReduce programming model. It integrates Function-as-a-Service (FaaS) principles with distributed processing techniques that allow configurable scaling based on the workload demands and the underlying hardware. Experimental evaluation shows that the system can scale effectively as the input data volume increases while supporting scale-to-zero, on-demand processing.

</details>


### [270] [TC-MIS: Maximal Independent Set on Tensor-cores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29604)
*Prajjwal Nijhara,Dip Sankar Banerjee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了 TC-MIS，首次成功利用 Tensor Core 加速极大规模图的 Maximal Independent Set (MIS) 计算，通过将图计算转化为高效的矩阵分块运算，在保持解质量的同时大幅提升了计算性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 GPU 图算法受限于不规则的内存访问和负载不均衡，且现代 GPU 的 Tensor Core（比 CUDA Core 快 8-16 倍）在图算法领域尚未被充分挖掘和利用。

Method: 将 MIS 计算中的关键阶段重构成稀疏矩阵向量乘法（SpMV）；通过对图邻接矩阵进行分块（Tiling），利用 Warp Matrix Multiply-Accumulate (WMMA) 指令集将不规则的图遍历转化为规则的、大规模并行的矩阵计算。

Result: 在多代 NVIDIA GPU（Ampere, Ada, Hopper, Blackwell）架构上实现了显著加速。平均加速比在 2.84x 至 18.80x 之间，最大加速比在 H200 上达到了 44.38x，同时保证了 MIS 解的质量。

Conclusion: TC-MIS 通过将图算法重构为矩阵运算，成功利用 Tensor Core 的高算力突破了 GPU 处理图任务时的性能瓶颈，证明了张量核心在非矩阵类图计算任务中的巨大应用潜力。

Abstract: Maximal Independent Set (MIS) in a graph is a fundamental problem with applications in resource allocation, scheduling, and network optimization. Although graphs are inherently un-structured and challenging for GPU parallelism due to irregular memory access and workload imbalance, specialized GPU algorithms have achieved good performance, processing million-vertex graphs in milliseconds. Modern GPUs are equipped with Tensor Cores (TCs), specialized units for matrix operations with 8-16x higher throughput than CUDA Cores (CCs), which are extensively used for ML, DL, and inference tasks but remain largely unexplored for graph algorithms. In this paper, we present TC-MIS, a TC-accelerated algorithm that reformulates key phases of MIS computation as sparse matrix-vector multiplication (SpMV). TC-MIS tiles the graph adjacency matrix and employs Warp Matrix Multiply-Accumulate (WMMA) operations to transform irregular graph traversal into regular, massively parallel computation. Our evaluation across TC-enabled microarchitectures (Ampere, Ada Lovelace, Hopper, Blackwell) demonstrates that TC-MIS achieves an average speedup of 2.84x on RTX A5000, 4.84x on L40S, 18.80x on H200 GPUs, and 5.20x on RTX 5080 with a maximum speedup of 44.38x on H200 GPU over state-of-the-art methods, while maintaining solution quality comparable to that obtained by established heuristics that produce near-maximum independent sets.

</details>


### [271] [AMDP: Asynchronous Multi-Directional Pipeline Parallelism for Large-Scale Models Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29664)
*Ling Chen,Houming Wu,Wenjie Yu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: AMDP 通过限制参数更新间隔与多流水线并发策略，解决了异步流水线并行训练中的模型收敛难题，实现了训练效率与精度的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的异步流水线并行方法在高负载训练时易因前向和反向传播之间的参数不匹配（Parameter Mismatch）导致模型收敛性下降。

Method: 提出了一种异步多向流水线并行（AMDP）方案：(1) 限制前向与反向传播间的参数更新次数（最多两次）；(2) 引入并发流水线机制以填充流水线气泡；(3) 采用梯度累积策略，确保参数失配被严格限制在单个优化步内。

Result: 在GPT和BERT风格的模型实验中，AMDP在保持模型收敛性的前提下，显著提升了训练速度。

Conclusion: AMDP 通过严格限制参数更新频率与引入并发流水线机制，在实现高效异步训练的同时，成功解决了传统异步流水线并行中的收敛性劣化问题。

Abstract: Pipeline parallelism is essential for large-scale model training, but existing asynchronous approaches often degrade convergence due to parameter mismatch between forward and backward passes. We propose Asynchronous Multi-Directional Pipeline parallelism (AMDP) to mitigate this issue while sustaining high utilization. AMDP limits the first stage of each pipeline to process at most two minibatches before backpropagation, bounding the number of parameter updates between forward and backward passes. To alleviate the resulting pipeline bubbles, AMDP launches multiple concurrent pipelines and adapts their number according to pipeline depth. In addition, AMDP accumulates gradients across minibatches and applies them in a single update, ensuring that only a bounded number of minibatches experience parameter mismatch, limited to within one optimization step. Experiments on GPT- and BERT-style models demonstrate that AMDP significantly accelerates training while preserving convergence.

</details>


### [272] [CARM Tool: Cache-Aware Roofline Model Automatic Benchmarking and Application Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29740)
*José Morgado,Leonel Sousa,Aleksandar Ilic*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种跨平台自动化工具，通过定制化汇编微基准测试和性能插桩，实现了在x86、ARM和RISC-V架构上高精度构建Cache-aware Roofline Model (CARM)，解决了异构架构下性能分析工具缺失的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的HPC系统日益复杂，虽然Cache-aware Roofline Model (CARM) 是性能优化的有效工具，但现有的CARM分析工具在不同架构间的支持极不平衡，且缺乏对AMD、ARM和RISC-V等架构的有效支持。

Method: 开发了针对不同CPU架构及指令集扩展（从标量到向量化）的定制化汇编微基准测试（microbenchmarks），覆盖了计算单元及所有内存层级；并集成了基于性能计数器（performance counters）和动态二进制插桩的应用分析方法。

Result: 实验结果表明，该自动化框架构建出的CARM性能“屋脊线”（roofs）在各种体系结构下的偏差小于1%。

Conclusion: 该研究成功开发了一种跨平台的自动化CARM构建框架，能够为x86、ARM和RISC-V等主流架构提供高精度的性能建模，有效填补了目前异构CPU架构下性能分析工具的空白。

Abstract: In recent years, HPC systems and CPU architectures as their central components, have become increasingly complex, making application development and optimization quite challenging. In this respect, intuitive performance models like the Cache-aware Roofline Model (CARM) offer effective guidance by providing insights into bottlenecks that limit the application's ability to reach the system's maximum performance. To fully exploit the benefits of CARM optimization guidance for application development, automatic tools for cross-architecture model construction and in-depth application characterization are absolutely essential. Given a plethora of existing CPU architectures, the current landscape of CARM-enabled tools covers either vendor-specific (Intel Advisor), not sufficiently developed (ARM) or simply non-existing (AMD, RISC-V) tools. This is a particular gap that this work intends to close by bringing automatic CARM support to all major CPU architectures and ISAs, i.e., x86 (Intel, AMD), ARM, and RISC-V, by developing assembly microbenchmarks specifically tailored to cover a full performance spectrum of modern CPUs (from scalar to all supported vector ISA extensions) for both computational units and all memory hierarchy levels. Additionally, this work integrates application analysis within the CARM framework using performance counters and dynamic binary instrumentation. Experimental results show that the CARM roofs constructed with the proposed automated framework provide less than a 1% deviation across various tested architectural maximums.

</details>


### [273] [Effective MPI: User-defined Datatypes and Cartesian Communicators for Zero-copy All-to-all Communication in Multidimensional Tori](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29970)
*Jesper Larsson Träff*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对 $d$ 维环面拓扑的零拷贝全对全（All-to-all）通信优化算法，通过 MPI 派生数据类型和通信器缓存技术，消除了显式内存重排，实现了高性能通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 $d$ 维环面拓扑结构中，现有的 MPI 全对全（All-to-all）通信实现往往效率不足，且通常需要显式的内存拷贝操作，本研究旨在优化此类算法的性能与资源消耗。

Method: 1. 使用笛卡尔通信器将 $p$ 个进程分割为 $d$ 个维度上的子通信器并进行缓存；2. 将 All-to-all 操作分解为 $d$ 次维内通信；3. 利用 MPI 派生数据类型替代显式的内存重排（实现 zero-copy）；4. 采用双缓冲技术（double-buffering）处理临时缓冲需求。

Result: 实验表明该算法性能与原生 MPI_Alltoall 实现具有竞争力，并能有效规避部分原生实现在特定场景下的潜在问题，具有良好的可调优性和适应性。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种高效的、无需显式内存拷贝（zero-copy）的 $d$ 维环面（torus）全对全通信算法，该算法通过合理利用 MPI 派生数据类型和缓存通信器实现了高性能，并在特定场景下优于原生 MPI 实现。

Abstract: We present and show how to implement a non-trivial all-to-all communication algorithm for arbitrary $d$-dimensional tori effectively in MPI. Given a factorization of the number of processes $p$ into $d$ factors that can be mapped onto a $d$-dimensional torus, we first utilize a Cartesian communicator to split a given $p$-process MPI communicator into, for each MPI process, $d$ smaller communicators spanning each of the dimensions of the torus to which the process belongs, and cache these communicators in order to avoid expensive splitting at each all-to-all operation. The all-to-all operation itself is decomposed into a sequence of $d$ MPI_Alltoall operations on the dimension-wise communicators. The non-trivial data rearrangement before and after each MPI_Alltoall call is implicit only and effected by MPI derived datatypes. This makes the implementation of the algorithm formally \emph{zero-copy}, meaning that no explicit process-local reordering of data blocks ever has to be performed. In order to achieve this, the algorithm employs a double-buffering scheme with modest temporary buffer requirements. By choosing the factorization of $p$ and selecting appropriate implementations for the component MPI_Alltoall operations, the presented implementation gives ample opportunities for algorithm tuning and adaptation to the particular high-performance system. A few, select experimental results show competitive performance with native MPI_Alltoall implementations and illustrate problems that common MPI_Alltoall implementations may have.

</details>


### [274] [RAFI -- A Ray/Work Forwarding Infrastructure for Data Parallel Multi-Node/Multi-GPU Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30294)
*Ingo Wald,Serkan Demirci,Alper Sahistan,Stefan Zellmann,Andrea Paris,Patrick Moran,Milan Jaros,Tatiana von Landesberger,Ugur Gudukbay,Valerio Pascucci*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: RaFI 是一个基于 CUDA 和 MPI 的框架，旨在简化跨多 GPU 的数据并行任务（如光线轨迹）迁移实现，隐藏了底层复杂的通信与编排细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开发 GPU 加速的数据并行软件时，跨 GPU 的工作项（如光线追踪中的 rays）迁移与同步逻辑实现复杂，现有的底层通信手段开发难度大。

Method: 基于 CUDA 和 MPI 构建的软件框架，通过提供简单的接口，在底层自动处理跨 GPU 工作项（如光线）迁移涉及的 CUDA 通信与 MPI 协调机制。

Result: 成功构建了 RaFI 框架，并展示了其在多个示例应用中的实际潜力，能够有效降低多 GPU 开发门槛。

Conclusion: RaFI 提供了一种高效的抽象层，简化了多GPU环境下工作项迁移的设计复杂度，且在多种数据并行应用场景中展现了良好的可行性。

Abstract: We present RaFI, a CUDA and MPI based software framework that simplifies the task of building GPU-enabled data-parallel software where rays or similar work items need to migrate between different GPUs. RaFI provides a simple interface for CUDA kernels to forward such work items to other GPUs, while under the hood managing all the CUDA and MPI related work required to make this happen. We describe RaFI's motivation and implementation, and show its potential in several example applications.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [275] [When LLM Reward Design Fails: Diagnostic-Driven Refinement for Sparse Structured RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28918)
*Youting Wang,Yuan Tang,Bowen Liu,Xuan Liu,Dingyan Shang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM生成奖励函数视为一个调试过程，通过构建错误分类法进行诊断引导的迭代优化，大幅提升了稀疏强化学习任务的智能体训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）在为稀疏且结构化的强化学习任务生成奖励函数时，一次性生成往往导致失败。研究旨在探讨如何通过迭代调试机制来解决这些失败模式，并提高智能体在复杂环境下的表现。

Method: 提出了一种“基于诊断的迭代优化（diagnostic-driven iterative refinement）”框架。通过构建奖励函数错误分类法（failure-mode taxonomy），利用训练产生的指标诊断失败模式（如奖励溢出、API误解等），指导LLM进行针对性的奖励函数修正。并通过对比实验、消融实验及敏感性分析验证机制。

Result: 通过迭代优化，DoorKey-8x8任务成功率从2.3%提升至97.6%，KeyCorridor提升至86.7%。实验证明这种提升源于分类法引导的反馈，而非简单的重复尝试或额外训练。同时明确了该方法在连续控制任务中存在由诊断误报引起的局限性。

Conclusion: LLM生成的奖励塑形应被重新定义为“调试（debugging）”过程而非“一次性生成（one-shot generation）”。基于诊断的迭代优化方法能够显著提升 sparse 强化学习任务的性能。但在密集奖励环境（如连续控制）或受限场景下，该方法的有效性存在边界。

Abstract: For sparse, structured reinforcement-learning tasks with semantic reward-function interfaces, LLM-generated reward shaping is better framed as debugging than one-shot generation. We study PPO-trained agents using MiniGrid as core evaluation and MuJoCo as boundary stress test. Our audit finds two dominant one-shot failure modes -- reward flooding and semantic/API misunderstanding -- plus a rarer weak-shaping case. We propose diagnostic-driven iterative refinement, where training diagnostics and a failure-mode taxonomy guide targeted reward-function revision. Refinement improves DoorKey-8x8 from 2.3% to 97.6% and KeyCorridor from 31.2% to 86.7% with high seed-to-seed variance. Controls show these gains are not from retrying or extra training: metrics-only re-prompting yields large drops, while a static-vocabulary control recovers much of the gap (87.6%; 70.7%), showing the taxonomy prompt is a major mechanism and dynamic labels provide only partially isolated incremental evidence. Budget-matched and Best-of-3 comparisons separate refinement from selection and training-time effects. Component-removal tests, sensitivity analyses, and an audit against author labels provide converging evidence for the debugging interpretation while revealing calibration limits. Continuous-control results show the boundary: success-based diagnostics can misfire in dense-reward locomotion, and return-trend feedback removes one false-positive mechanism without robust gains. The low-call protocol is a cost contrast with population-based reward search, not a benchmark comparison. In four crossed-variance-design environments, point estimates suggest larger gains when LLM reward-function variance dominates but bootstrap intervals are wide. The method is bounded to sparse structured tasks with reliable interfaces under PPO; fields like event_text may help, hurt, or be neutral.

</details>


### [276] [Conf-Gen: Conformal Uncertainty Quantification for Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28920)
*Gabriel Loaiza-Ganem,Kevin Zhang,Wei Cui,Marc T. Law,Kin Kwan Leung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了“保形生成”（Conf-Gen）框架，将保形风险控制（CRC）扩展至无监督生成模型，为LLM和图像生成等AI系统的输出提供了严格的统计保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的保形预测（CP）和保形风险控制（CRC）主要针对有监督学习，难以直接应用于当前主流的无监督生成模型（如LLM和图像生成器）。为了解决这些模型在实际应用中的不确定性定量化问题，亟需一种通用的生成式保形方法。

Method: 提出了“保形生成”（Conf-Gen）框架。该方法通过放宽传统的CRC理论假设，使其能够适应无需显式监督标签的生成模型，并利用保形预测原理对生成输出的质量进行统计控制。

Result: Conf-Gen在多个前沿任务中表现出色，包括确保图像生成器不产生“记忆”（训练集重现）图像、确保对话AI在生成响应前通过恰当的澄清性提问来减少歧义，以及保证智能体（AI Agents）输出的正确性。

Conclusion: Conf-Gen成功将保形风险控制（CRC）扩展至无监督生成模型领域，提供了一种灵活且通用的框架。它证明了在LLM和图像生成等复杂生成任务中，通过保形方法实现严格的统计质量保证是可行且有效的。

Abstract: Conformal prediction (CP) and its extension, conformal risk control (CRC), are established frameworks for quantifying uncertainty in supervised machine learning through formal guarantees. However, recent breakthroughs in artificial intelligence (AI) have been driven by unsupervised generative models, such as large language models (LLMs) and image generators, which are not directly compatible with CP or CRC. In this work we introduce conformal generation (Conf-Gen), a general framework adapting CRC to generative tasks while relaxing its theoretical assumptions. Conf-Gen unifies and generalizes previous attempts to apply CP to LLMs, and extends conformal methodology to entirely new domains. We demonstrate the flexibility of Conf-Gen through some novel applications, including obtaining conformal guarantees on: image generators producing non-memorized images, conversational AI systems having asked enough clarifying questions, and the output of AI agents being correct.

</details>


### [277] [Q-ANCHOR: Federated Quantum Learning with ZNE-guided Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30075)
*Hoang M. Ngo,Quan Nguyen,Wanli Xing,My T. Thai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为解决量子联邦学习在真实硬件上因数据不均衡和量子噪声引起的“双重漂移”问题，本文提出了 Q-ANCHOR 架构，通过零噪声外推和状态化纠偏技术，显著提升了模型训练的准确性与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子联邦学习（QFL）主要依赖 FedAvg，但在实际量子硬件中面临“双重漂移”问题（非 IID 数据导致的客户端漂移 + 量子噪声导致的硬件偏差），导致模型难以收敛且存在明显的误差底界。

Method: 提出 Q-ANCHOR 架构，结合零噪声外推（Zero-Noise Extrapolation）来固定服务器更新，并引入状态化客户端修正（Stateful Client Correction）技术。

Result: 理论证明表明 Q-ANCHOR 可减轻客户端漂移并降低硬件偏差带来的误差底界；实验结果显示，该方法相比传统联邦学习基线具有更高的训练稳定性。

Conclusion: Q-ANCHOR 有效解决了非独立同分布（non-IID）数据导致的客户端漂移以及量子噪声带来的硬件偏差，证明了在真实量子硬件环境下实现鲁棒联邦学习的可行性。

Abstract: Quantum Federated Learning (QFL) offers a promising framework to train quantum models across distributed clients while keeping data strictly local. Due to its simplicity and low communication overhead, Federated Averaging (FedAvg) is the standard aggregation choice in QFL literature. However, deploying QFL on practical hardware exposes a severe double-drift phenomenon: the global model is simultaneously derailed by client drift from non-IID data and hardware bias from noisy quantum gradient estimates. In this work, we first analyze the convergence of FedAvg under these realistic conditions, mathematically demonstrating that quantum hardware bias creates a persistent error floor that standard averaging cannot correct. To overcome this limitation, we propose Q-ANCHOR, a quantum-aware federated aggregation architecture that anchors server updates with zero-noise extrapolation while applying stateful client correction to suppress both client drift and hardware-induced bias. Our convergence theory proves that Q-ANCHOR successfully mitigates classical client drift while actively reducing the hardware-bias floor. Experimental results demonstrate that Q-ANCHOR achieves significantly more stable training than conventional FL baselines.

</details>


### [278] [Theoretical Foundations and Effective Algorithms for Policy-Aware Simulator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29032)
*Christoph Dann,Yishay Mansour,Mehryar Mohri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对MBRL中的模型剥削问题，提出了一种基于极大极小博弈的战略鲁棒性学习框架，通过Error-MDP对偶性实现了高效的数据选择和更强的现实应用性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型基强化学习（MBRL）通过最小化预测损失学习世界模型，导致模型微小的预测误差被RL优化器过度利用（即“模拟器剥削”），造成策略在仿真中表现优异但在现实环境中失效（Reality Gap）。

Method: 1. 将模型学习建模为仿真器（模型玩家）与策略（对抗性玩家）之间的零和极大极小博弈（Minimax Game）；2. 引入基于Critic的简化方法以界定策略价值差距；3. 利用Error-MDP对偶性，将寻找最坏情况策略转化为以单步Critic误差为奖励的标准RL问题，并据此设计了主动数据选择算法。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在连续控制任务中，将关键战略区域的预测误差降低了1.5至2.2倍，使得纯仿真训练的策略能够达到接近最优的真实世界性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于博弈论的仿真器训练方法，能够有效解决模型基强化学习中的“模拟器剥削”问题，证明了通过优化战略鲁棒性而非单纯的预测精度，可以显著缩小仿真与现实之间的性能鸿沟（Reality Gap）。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) agents typically learn world models by minimizing predictive loss. However, powerful RL optimizers inevitably exploit minor model inaccuracies, leading to simulator exploitation and a reality gap where policies succeed in simulation but fail in the real world. We propose that the objective for learning simulators should be strategic robustness rather than predictive accuracy, and formulate this as a zero-sum minimax game between a model player and an adversarial policy player. We provide a comprehensive theoretical analysis: (1) an online learning guarantee showing the game is learnable with sublinear regret bounds; (2) a tractable critic-based simplification bounding the global policy-value gap by the local critic's loss; and (3) an Error-MDP duality, proving that finding the worst-case policy is formally dual to a standard RL problem where the reward is the one-step critic error. This duality yields a provably convergent active data selection algorithm. Experiments on continuous control tasks demonstrate that our approach reduces prediction error in strategically important regions by $1.5$-$2.2\times$ and enables policies trained purely in simulation to match near-optimal real-world performance.

</details>


### [279] [PROTOCOL: Late Interaction Retrieval for Protein Homolog Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29158)
*Gabrielle Cohn,Rohan Gumaste,Minh Hoang,Vihan Lakshman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProtoCol 通过保留残基级嵌入并使用后期交互机制，解决了蛋白质语言模型在远程同源搜索中因特征池化而丢失局部细微特征的问题，显著提高了在低相似度区间的检索精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前蛋白质语言模型（PLM）检索管道多采用“单向量池化”表示，这会丢失关键的局部模体（motifs）、结构域及保守残基信息，导致在“昏暗区”（twilight zone，即全局序列相似度极低时）难以精确检索同源蛋白。

Method: 提出模型“ProtoCol”，将蛋白质表示为残基嵌入集合，利用 ColBERT 风格的后期交互机制（MaxSim 操作）进行候选蛋白评分，无需在检索时重新编码，支持候选集预计算。

Result: 在 SCOPe 超家族和 Pfam 家族基准测试中，ProtoCol 的性能显著优于序列组成法、传统比对方法、池化 PLM 方法以及其他单向量训练基线。

Conclusion: “后期交互”（late interaction）机制是提升远程同源搜索（remote homology search）能力的有效Retrieval层设计，优于传统的池化嵌入及序列比对方法。

Abstract: Protein homology search underlies function annotation, structure prediction, and evolutionary analysis, but remains challenging in the "twilight zone," where global sequence similarity is weak and classical alignment methods lose sensitivity. Protein language models provide context-aware representations that could improve alignment sensitivity in this regime. However, prior protein embedding-based retrieval pipelines often pool these representations into a single vector, potentially obscuring local motifs, domains, or conserved residues that reveal remote homology. We introduce ProtoCol, a model which represents proteins as sets of residue embeddings and uses ColBERT-style late interaction to test whether residue-level comparison improves homolog retrieval. ProtoCol encodes proteins independently, keeps candidate representations pre-computable, and scores candidates with MaxSim over residue embeddings. On SCOPe superfamily and Pfam clan benchmarks, ProtoCol outperforms sequence-composition, alignment-based, pooled PLM, and trained single-vector baselines, supporting late interaction as an effective retrieval layer for remote homology search.

</details>


### [280] [Optimal Gap-Dependent Regret for Private Stochastic Decision-Theoretic Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29148)
*Tommaso Cesari,Roberto Colomboni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究解决了在线学习在纯差分隐私下的最优遗憾界问题，提出了一种无需预设视界的算法，实现了O(log K / Δmin + log K / ε)的遗憾界，匹配了理论下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决计算学习理论（COLT）中关于随机决策在线学习在纯事件级差分隐私（pure event-level DP）条件下最优遗憾界（gap-dependent regret）的公开问题。

Method: 该研究设计了一种将时间划分为指数级增长块的算法：在每个块内仅执行单一动作；通过在每个块的前一部分应用指数机制（Exponential Mechanism）来选择下一块的动作，并利用“数据无关的随机前缀”技术将块遗憾转化为Softmax选择误差之和；结合熵势（Entropy-potential）分析方法来控制由隐私需求引起的大间隙动作的遗憾。

Result: 对于任意时间视界 T，证明了遗憾界满足：Reg_T ≤ 1000 * (log K / Δmin + log K / ε)。该结果在遗憾的间隙依赖项和隐私项之间达到了最优的理论权衡。

Conclusion: 该研究通过提出一种无视界（horizon-free）的纯差分隐私算法，证明了随机决策在线学习在纯事件级差分隐私约束下，达到最优的依赖间隙（gap-dependent）的遗憾界，彻底解决了Hu和Mehta提出的公开问题，确认了遗憾界的下界为O(log K / Δmin + log K / ε)。

Abstract: We study stochastic decision-theoretic online learning with full information and event-level pure differential privacy. A COLT open problem of Hu and Mehta asks to determine the optimal gap-dependent regret rate for stochastic decision-theoretic online learning under pure event-level differential privacy. For $K$ actions, losses in $[0,1]$, and a unique best action separated from the second-best action by gap $Δ_{\min}$, the known lower bound is of order $
  \frac{\log K}{\min\{Δ_{\min},\varepsilon\}}, $
or equivalently, up to universal constants, of order \[
  \frac{\log K}{Δ_{\min}}+\frac{\log K}{\varepsilon}. \] We give a horizon-free pure-DP algorithm and prove the explicit regret bound \[
  \operatorname{Reg}_T
  \le
  1000 \cdot \left(\frac{\log K}{Δ_{\min}}+\frac{\log K}{\varepsilon}\right) \] for every horizon $T$. The numerical constant is not optimized. The algorithm partitions time into blocks of exponentially increasing size, plays a single action throughout each block, and chooses the next action by an exponential mechanism applied to a data-independent random prefix of the previous block. The random prefix converts block regret into a sum, over all prefix lengths, of softmax selection errors. A single entropy-potential argument controls all privacy-dominated large-gap actions at cost $\log K/\varepsilon$.

</details>


### [281] [Attention as In-Context Empirical Bayes: A Two-Stage View via Particle Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29351)
*Matthew Smart,Soumya Ganguly,Nilava Metya,Alexandre V. Morozov,Anirvan M. Sengupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Transformer 是一种隐式的“二阶段经验贝叶斯推断机”：层深度负责细化分布（粒子演化），注意力机制则执行后验均值估计（语境推理），从而解释了 Transformer 在无显式密度建模下实现深度去噪和推理的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于注意力机制的解释往往缺乏统计学视角，作者旨在解释为何深度 Transformer 可以表现出类似于扩散过程的行为，以及注意力残差和不同深度的架构在处理输入信号时具有何种明确的统计学功能。

Method: 利用粒子动力学模型对 minimal attention-only transformers 进行二阶段经验贝叶斯（Empirical Bayes）解释：阶段 1 通过层深度（depth）细化分布，阶段 2 通过长程跳跃连接（skip-connection）进行后验推断。结合逆扩散限制（reverse-diffusion limits）与核密度估计理论进行分析。

Result: 证明了在特定条件下，注意力机制能收敛于贝叶斯最优预测器；揭示了固定核带宽（kernel bandwidth）下，深层模型能自动形成有效的能量景观并实现去噪，无需显式的噪声调度（noise schedule）。

Conclusion: 该研究通过将注意力机制建模为基于样本的后验估计，揭示了 Transformer 的深度与残差连接在统计推断中的独特作用。注意力机制不仅是信息路由，更是一种在不进行显式密度建模的情况下，通过上下文进行“语境推理”（in-context inference）的机制。

Abstract: We study minimal attention-only transformers under all-token corruption and show they admit a two-stage empirical Bayes interpretation. A single attention step computes a kernel-weighted posterior mean with respect to the empirical distribution defined by the context. Depth refines this distribution through particle dynamics (Stage 1), while a long-range skip-connection carries the noisy input as a query for posterior inference (Stage 2), revealing distinct statistical roles for depth and attention residuals. The framework isolates a minimal setting in which the context itself induces a depth-dependent energy landscape governing in-context inference. We show that effective denoising can emerge without an explicit noise schedule: a fixed kernel bandwidth and finite integration horizon suffice, yielding a principled depth-noise relationship. We further establish a posterior-mean recovery guarantee for a class of well-behaved priors, where the empirical estimator converges to the Bayes-optimal predictor under asymptotic conditions. Connecting these dynamics to reverse-diffusion limits, our results provide a statistical interpretation of attention as in-context inference via sample-based posterior estimation, without explicit density modeling.

</details>


### [282] [On the Optimizer Dependence of Neural Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29387)
*Vansh Ramani,Shourya Vir Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了神经缩放定律中的缩放指数α不仅取决于模型和数据，还受优化器类型的显著影响。研究表明，利用更高效的优化器（如自然梯度）可以显著提升缩放指数，从而加速模型性能随参数规模增长的提升速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经缩放定律往往将缩放指数α视为固定常数，忽略了优化器本身对模型训练效率及缩放斜率的影响。

Method: 在随机特征回归（Random-feature regression）框架下，对比了五种不同优化器变体在六种频谱条件（spectral conditions）下的缩放行为。

Result: 预处理优化器（如自然梯度）能显著增大缩放指数α。例如在s≈1.0的频谱条件下，自然梯度的α值约为梯度下降法的2.6倍，表明优化器选择对模型缩放效率具有显著影响。

Conclusion: 神经缩放定律中的缩放指数α并非由架构和数据决定的固定常数，而是高度依赖于优化器。研究通过频谱诊断工具为预测先进优化器的性能提升提供了量化依据。

Abstract: The scaling exponent $α$ in neural scaling laws $L(N) \propto N^{-α}$ is commonly treated as a fixed constant set by architecture and data. We present evidence that $α$ depends systematically on the optimizer. In controlled random-feature regression experiments -- the canonical theoretical framework for neural scaling -- we measure $α$ across five optimizer variants and six spectral conditions. Preconditioned optimizers consistently yield steeper scaling (larger $α$), with the $α$-shift increasing across most of the tested spectral range, peaking near $s = 1.5$, and remaining large at $s = 2.0$. At $s \approx 1.0$ (characteristic of natural language), the full natural gradient achieves $α\approx 0.31$ versus $α\approx 0.12$ for gradient descent -- a $2.6\times$ larger fitted exponent that, within the random-feature model, compounds with each model-size doubling. Whether and how this exponent shift transfers to large-scale LLM training -- where recent evidence suggests the advantage may attenuate with scale -- remains an important open question. Our results imply that scaling-law forecasts should account for optimizer choice, and we provide a spectral diagnostic predicting when advanced optimizers will pay off.

</details>


### [283] [LoopFM: Learning frOm HistOrical RePresentations of Foundation Model for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29280)
*Shali Jiang,Hua Zheng,Boyang Liu,Laming Chen,Kenny Lov,Chuanqi Xu,Lisang Ding,Qinghai Zhou,Can Cui,Xiaolong Liu,Xiaoyi Liu,Yasmine Badr,Xin Xu,Jiyan Yang,Ellie Dingqiao Wen,Gerard Jonathan Mugisha Akkerhuis,Chenxiao Guan,Rong Jin,Ruichao Qiu,Xian Chen,Shifu Xu,Zhehui Zhou,Ping Chen,Rui Yang,Haicheng Chen,Xiangge Meng,Song Zhou,Dharak Kharod,Shuyu Xu,Qiang Jin,Qiao Yang,Wankun Zhu,Qin Huang,Yuzhen Huang,Darren Liu,Parish Aggarwal,Hui Zhou,Erzhuo Wang,Shuo Chang,Xiaorui Gan,Wenlin Chen,Santanu Kolay,Huayu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoopFM 是一种新型知识迁移框架，通过将 FM 的中间表示作为 VM 的输入特征，突破了传统蒸馏方法的性能瓶颈，显著提升了大规模工业推荐系统的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的知识蒸馏（KD）仅利用单一标量预测进行知识传递，难以捕捉基础模型（FM）丰富的中间知识，导致“转移比（transfer ratio）”随着模型规模扩大而递减。

Method: 提出 LoopFM 框架，将基础模型（FM）的中间嵌入（intermediate embeddings）结构化为纵向模型（VM）的输入特征（如用户历史序列）。该方法解耦了 FM 和 VM 的架构，且无需在推理阶段实时调用 FM。此外，构建了增益分解与转移比分析的理论框架。

Result: 在三个公开基准测试上大幅提升了 AUC（如 TaobaoAd 提升 6%+）；在工业级超大规模系统中，LoopFM 在 KD 基础上将知识转移比提升了约一倍，在不同阶段实现了 +0.5% 至 +1.22% 的业务转换率提升。

Conclusion: LoopFM 通过将基础模型（FM）的历史中间表示转化为纵向模型（VM）的输入特征，有效解决了传统知识蒸馏中“转移比递减”的瓶颈，在学术及工业级大规模推荐系统中展现了显著的性能增益，并与传统KD具有互补性。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) transfers a single scalar prediction from a large foundation model (FM) to compact vertical models (VMs), suffering from diminishing transfer ratio -- the fraction of FM improvement captured by the VM -- as a single scalar cannot convey the rich intermediate knowledge that larger FMs learn. To address this bottleneck, we propose LoopFM (Learning frOm HistOrical ReP*resentations of FM), a framework that opens a high-bandwidth transfer channel by structuring FM intermediate embeddings as input features (e.g., user history sequence) for downstream VMs, without requiring real-time FM inference at serving and architectural coupling between FM and VM. We provide a theoretical framework for LoopFM with a gain decomposition and transfer-ratio analysis. On three public benchmarks, LoopFM demonstrates strong AUC improvements (e.g., 6\%+ on TaobaoAd) and complementary knowledge transfer capability with KD. On industrial-scale systems (billions of examples, trillion-parameter FMs), LoopFM approximately doubles the knowledge transfer ratio on top of KD, delivering a +0.5\% conversion improvement in Y1H1, and a +1.03\% and +1.22\% conversion improvement from two individual launches respectively in Y1H2.

</details>


### [284] [The Good, the Bad, and the Ugly of Markov Boundary for Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29411)
*Shu Wan,Abhinav Gorantla,Huan Liu,K. Selçuk Candan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了马尔可夫边界在 tabular 预测中的理论价值与工程实现之间的鸿沟，指出目前的因果发现技术在预测任务上表现欠佳，需开发更具预测导向的特征选择方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨理论上的“马尔可夫边界”能否在实际的表格数据预测任务中提升模型性能，并解释为什么现代机器学习模型通常倾向于使用全特征集而非边界特征。

Method: 在SCM3K基准测试（包含3,450个合成结构因果模型任务，40-1000个特征）上，对比了使用“oracle边界”与“通过因果发现算法恢复的边界”进行回归预测的效果，并分析了性能差距的根本原因。

Result: 研究发现：1. 使用oracle边界确实能显著提升预测性能，尤其是在高维稀疏数据上；2. 现有的因果发现算法无法达到提升性能所需的恢复精度，且计算开销巨大；3. 结构学习与预测任务目标不一致，且特征选择中假阳性/假阴性的代价非对称。

Conclusion: 尽管马尔可夫边界在理论上是特征选择的理想目标，但现有的因果发现算法在预测任务中表现不佳。未来的路径应转向“预测对齐（prediction-aligned）”的特征选择方法，而非单纯追求结构恢复的准确性。

Abstract: Under standard graphical assumptions, the Markov boundary of a target variable is the smallest set of features that renders every other feature redundant. Once the boundary is observed, the target is conditionally independent of the rest of the table. This is a tempting object for tabular prediction, since it names exactly the columns a model should need. Yet modern regressors are still trained on the full feature set. We ask whether the Markov boundary is genuinely useful for prediction on SCM3K, a 3,450-task synthetic SCM benchmark with feature counts from 40 to 1000 and six SCM families, evaluated with six regressors. The answer is more nuanced than the theory suggests. Restricting a regressor to the oracle boundary often improves prediction substantially, and the improvement grows as the feature space becomes larger and sparser. But the natural pipeline of recovering the boundary with causal discovery and training on the recovered mask does not deliver. Existing estimators exhaust the compute budget before reaching the regime where the boundary helps most, and even where they run they rarely beat the full feature set. We trace this to three causes. Discovery optimizes structural recovery rather than prediction. False negatives and false positives carry sharply asymmetric predictive cost. The exact boundary is only one of many feature sets that beat all features. We then develop what these facts imply for prediction-aligned feature selection and for tabular models that learn to use causal structure.

</details>


### [285] [On the Construction and Implications of Low-Loss Valleys in LoRA-based Bayesian Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29580)
*Daniel Dold,Emanuel Sommer,Julius Kobialka,Oliver Dürr,David Rügamer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 LoRA 模型不确定性估计难的问题，本文提出了 LoRA-Curve，通过在 LoRA 参数空间构建并优化分段贝塞尔曲线，连接多个低损失最优解，实现了更高效的贝叶斯模型平均。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的参数高效微调（如 LoRA）在量化认知不确定性方面表现不佳。尽管在深度学习中集成独立最优解通常能提升泛化性，但在 LoRA 空间中，简单的线性插值常遇到损失障碍，导致离散集成方法效果有限。研究旨在验证 LoRA 空间中是否也存在连接模型最优解的低损失路径，以实现更优的贝叶斯模型平均。

Method: 提出 LoRA-Curve 方法，即在 LoRA 参数空间中使用分段贝塞尔曲线进行参数化。该方法包含两种变体：1）联合优化所有控制点的自由配置；2）连接独立微调 LoRA 最优解的锚定配置。同时辅以平坦极小值扰动（flat-minima perturbations）和 Jensen-Shannon 散度正则化来增强功能多样性。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5 7B 模型上的实验表明，简单的线性插值存在损失障碍，而 LoRA-Curve 的锚定多段曲线成功连接了独立的最优解。该方法显著提高了预测分布的互信息（Mutual Information），提升了不确定性估计的质量，同时保持了模型在推理与分类任务上的性能。

Conclusion: LoRA-Curve 通过在 LoRA 参数空间构建分段贝塞尔曲线，成功连接了独立的模型最优解，通过平滑的低损失洼地实现了贝叶斯模型平均（BMA），从而在不损失性能的前提下有效提升了模型的认知不确定性估计能力。

Abstract: While parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods like low-rank adaptation (LoRA) are standard for large language models, principled estimation of epistemic uncertainty remains challenging. Recent results in the LoRA regime suggest that discrete multi-mode approaches such as deep ensembles offer little benefit over single-mode methods. This contradicts broader observations in deep learning, where ensembling independent optima typically improves generalization, and linking these modes through continuous low-loss valleys further enhances Bayesian model averaging (BMA). Whether such structure exists in the LoRA space and whether it yields functional diversity missed by local or discrete methods has not been studied. We introduce LoRA-Curve, a segmented Bézier curve parameterization in the LoRA space, with two variants: a free configuration that jointly optimizes all control points, and an anchored configuration that connects independently fine-tuned LoRA optima. We prove pathwise continuity and Lipschitz regularity of the loss along the curve and empirically show, across reasoning and classification benchmarks with Qwen2.5 7B, that linear interpolation encounters loss barriers, while our anchored multi-segment curves connect independent optima through continuous low-loss valleys. Combined with flat-minima perturbations and a Jensen-Shannon divergence regularizer, LoRA-Curve yields measurably higher mutual information of the predictive distribution without sacrificing performance, and links continuous parameter-space traversal to functional diversity.

</details>


### [286] [The Sample Complexity of Multiclass and Sparse Contextual Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29645)
*Liad Erez,Fan Chen,Alon Cohen,Tomer Koren,Yishay Mansour,Shay Moran,Alexander Rakhlin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了稀疏上下文老虎机问题的样本复杂度问题，通过DEC理论分析和低方差探索算法，在s-稀疏设置下达到了理论最优采样指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决零一奖励下的上下文多类分类任务中，针对s-稀疏奖励设置（即在每个上下文中，奖励向量的L1范数远小于动作空间大小）的样本效率问题，以降低对动作空间大小|A|的依赖。

Method: 1. 决策估计系数（DEC）的信息论分析方法：证明了s-稀疏奖励诱导的模型类具有紧的DEC界；2. 低方差探索技术（Low-variance exploration）：设计了计算上更易实现且可推广至组合半老虎机问题的显式算法。

Result: 在置信度下，算法实现了Õ((s/ε² + |A|/ε)log|Π|/δ)的近似最优样本复杂度，相对于已知工作去除了多项式级别的|A|^9依赖。

Conclusion: 提出了针对s-稀疏上下文老虎机问题的最优样本复杂度算法，关闭了先验工作（Erez et al.）中存在的较大理论缺口，并在广义Natarajan类上得到了匹配的下界。

Abstract: We study contextual bandits in the stochastic i.i.d.\ setting, where a learner observes contexts drawn from an unknown distribution, selects actions from a finite set $A$, and aims to identify an approximately optimal policy from a given class based on bandit feedback. Motivated by bandit multiclass classification with zero-one rewards, we focus on the \emph{$s$-sparse} setting in which, for every context, the reward vector has $L_1$-norm at most $s \ll |A|$. Our main result is the design of algorithms that, with high probability, output an $ε$-optimal policy compared to policy class $Π$ using $\tilde{O} ((s/ε^2 + |A|/ε)\log |Π|/δ)$ samples. We extend this bound to general Natarajan classes and complement it with a matching lower bound (up to logarithmic factors), thereby closing a substantial gap left by prior work (Erez et al., 2024, 2025), which incurred an additional $Θ(|A|^9)$ dependence. We obtain these results via two complementary approaches. First, we analyze contextual bandits through the lens of contextual decision making with structured observations, designing an exploration-by-optimization algorithm whose sample complexity is governed by the \emph{decision-estimation coefficient} (DEC; Foster et al., 2021, 2022). We show that, with $s$-sparse rewards, the induced model class admits a sharp DEC bound that scales with $s$ and directly yields the optimal rate. Since this approach is largely information-theoretic and involves solving complex min-max optimization problems, we also develop a second, more specialized algorithmic method based on a low-variance exploration technique. This approach leads to concrete, tractable algorithms and naturally extends to contextual combinatorial semi-bandits, leading to improved sample complexity guarantees for bandit multiclass list classification.

</details>


### [287] [SCOPE: A Lightweight-training LLM Framework for Air Traffic Control Readback Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29543)
*Qihan Deng,Minghua Zhang,Yang Yang,Zhenyu Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SCOPE框架，通过结合冻结LLM、即插即用分类器和上下文学习，解决了航空管制语音复诵监控中传统方法泛化差、LLM计算成本高等痛点，实现了高准确率、低延迟且可解释的异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空管制中复诵异常是导致飞行事故的主要原因；传统规则方法难以泛化，已有LLM方案存在部署及计算负载过高的问题，亟需一种高效且准确的自动化监控手段。

Method: 提出SCOPE（Semantic reasoning for Communication via Open-set Plug-in with Examples）框架：由一个冻结的大语言模型（LLM）作为核心，叠加一个即插即用的开放集分类器，并结合仔细设计的上下文学习（In-context Learning）机制。

Result: 在半合成数据集上，SCOPE在少样本设置下达到91.05%的开放集检测准确率和96.63%的复诵异常纠错率，性能显著优于现有基准模型，且具备低延迟和可解释性。

Conclusion: SCOPE框架通过冻结LLM并结合即插即用分类器与上下文学习（ICL），证明了在实际航空交通管制场景中实现高效、高准确率且可解释的复诵监控的可行性。

Abstract: Pilot readback of Air Traffic Control (ATC) voice instructions is a primary safeguard against miscommunication in air transportation. However, readback anomalies remain implicated in approximately 80% of aviation incidents. This vulnerability is further exacerbated by rising traffic volume and elevated cognitive workload, thereby motivating automated readback monitoring by machine. Traditional rule-based and machine learning approaches struggle to generalize across the highly variable and evolving phraseology of air traffic controller-pilot communications. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have opened a new avenue through their strong reasoning and generalization capabilities, existing approaches still face deployment and computational barriers in practice. In this work, we propose Semantic reasoning for Communication via Open-set Plug-in with Examples (SCOPE), a novel lightweight-training LLM framework that advances both the efficiency and accuracy of machine-based ATC readback monitoring. The core idea is to couple a plug-in open-set classifier with a carefully designed in-context learning mechanism on top of a frozen LLM. Extensive experiments on the semi-synthetic communication dataset show that SCOPE attains superior accuracy while delivering the low-latency response required for operational environments. Under a few-shot setting, SCOPE achieves 91.05% accuracy in open-set detection and corrects 96.63% of anomalous readbacks, thereby outperforming the strongest available baselines while providing explanations for its decisions. These findings demonstrate the potential of our framework as a practical pathway toward interpretable and controllable ATC readback monitoring.

</details>


### [288] [CB-SLICE: Concept-Based Interpretable Error Slice Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29836)
*Yael Konforti,Mateo Espinosa Zarlenga,Elaf Almahmoud,Mateja Jamnik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CB-SLICE 是一种新型错误切片发现方法，它通过分析概念瓶颈模型（CBM）中的概念预测失败，能够更准确地定位深度学习模型的系统性错误，并提供具备高度可解释性的错误归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的错误切片发现方法（SDMs）生成的解释与模型的推理过程脱节，导致模型错误根源的定位不够准确。为此，研究者希望利用概念瓶颈模型（CBMs）中与预测直接相关的概念表示来提高错误归因的准确性和可解释性。

Method: 提出了一种名为 CB-SLICE 的方法。它利用 CBMs 内部的人类可理解概念表示，通过聚类分析具有相似概念预测失败的情况来识别模型错误切片（error slices），并确定导致这些错误的关键概念。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，CB-SLICE 在揭示模型偏差方面优于现有的顶尖方法，不仅能更准确地识别错误切片，还提供了更丰富、更忠实于模型推理逻辑的解释。

Conclusion: CB-SLICE 通过利用概念瓶颈模型（CBMs）的可解释性，成功实现了更精确、更具解释性的错误切片发现，优于现有的非概念驱动方法。

Abstract: Despite strong average-case performance, deep learning models often exhibit systematic errors on specific population groups, known as error slices. Identifying these groups and the root causes of their failures is critical for model debugging and bias mitigation. However, existing error Slice Discovery Methods (SDMs) typically generate explanations disconnected from the model's inference process, thus only approximating the underlying error source and may be inaccurate.
  We address this limitation by leveraging Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs), whose predictions are directly dependent on human-understandable semantic concepts. Since downstream task failures in CBMs commonly arise from concept mispredictions, concept representations provide a strong candidate for error slice identification, offering fine-grained explanations directly linked to the error source. Building on this insight, we introduce CB-SLICE, a concept-based SDM that groups samples with shared concept prediction failures and identifies the keyword concepts most responsible for each slice's failure mode. Across multiple benchmarks, we show that CB-SLICE outperforms state-of-the-art methods in uncovering well-known biases while providing richer and more faithful explanations of model errors.

</details>


### [289] [Open Problem: Separating Geometric and Algorithmic Compression via Cayley-Table Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29885)
*Dongsung Huh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出深度学习在离散代数规则外推上的不足，提出以凯莱表补全作为测试平台，并建议通过开发平坦度先验来学习离散算法结构，而非依赖传统的连续容量控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习理论在连续域表现优异，但在处理精确算法或离散代数规则的外推时表现不佳，研究者认为这源于模型缺乏对算法复杂度最小化的归纳偏置。

Method: 提出将“凯莱表补全（Cayley-table completion）”作为评估离散算法归纳偏置的核心测试平台，并利用算子值张量分解结合平坦度先验来模拟算法偏置。

Result: 研究表明，通过算子值张量分解与平坦度先验的结合，模型能够表现出对精确离散结合律的隐式算法偏置，类比于矩阵分解中的低秩偏置。

Conclusion: 现有的深度学习缺乏对算法复杂性最小化的归纳偏置，无法有效外推离散代数规则。研究者呼吁发展能够自动发现离散算法公理的泛化平坦度先验（Flatness Priors），以超越传统的正则化方法。

Abstract: Modern statistical learning theory and deep learning characterize generalization primarily in terms of continuous capacity control (e.g., norm-based regularization, margin maximization, low-rank bias). While highly successful in continuous domains, deep learning consistently fails to extrapolate exact algorithmic or discrete algebraic rules, reflecting a missing inductive bias toward algorithmic complexity minimization. We propose the Cayley-table completion as the canonical testbed for this missing bias, serving as the discrete algebraic counterpart to matrix completion. Just as matrix factorization combined with weight decay yields an implicit geometric bias toward low linear rank, recent results demonstrate that operator-valued tensor factorizations paired with a flatness prior yield an implicit algorithmic bias toward exact discrete associativity. We pose the open problem of establishing formal exact recovery bounds for Cayley-table completion, and challenge the community to generalize continuous flatness priors to autonomously discover broader discrete algorithmic axioms without combinatorial search.

</details>


### [290] [Ridge Regression from Poisson Resetting: A Renewal Perspective on Spectral Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30059)
*Petar Jolakoski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将统计物理中的“随机重置”过程与机器学习中的“岭回归”联系起来，证明了通过特定的重置策略（如指数采样）可以自然诱导出岭回归的正则化效应，并拓展了非指数重置在设计新型谱滤波器中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索统计物理中的随机重置动力学与机器学习中正则化方法之间的深层数学联系，试图通过非平衡动力学的视角解释岭回归的收敛行为和谱特性。

Method: 1. 将非平衡统计物理中的随机重置（stochastic resetting）与统计学习中的线性梯度流（gradient flow）结合；2. 利用拉普拉斯变换建立了梯度流指数加权平均与岭估计之间的恒等关系；3. 将重置时间分布推广至一般的更新过程（renewal processes）；4. 在存在Ornstein-Uhlenbeck噪声的情境下（模拟SGD），分析了重置过程的统计矩与平稳分布。

Result: 1. 证明了在重置率为r的泊松过程中，线性梯度流的平稳平均值即为正则化参数为λ=r的岭估计；2. 证明了指数重置律是唯一能让所有特征方向均产生等价岭回归谱滤波器的更新律；3. 指出在带噪声的情境下，随机重置导致的平稳分布中心与岭估计一致，但在随机波动（协方差）上存在本质区别。

Conclusion: 研究确立了随机重置过程与岭回归（ridge regularization）在数学结构上的等价性：通过选择特定的重置概率分布（尤其是指数分布），可以构造出与岭回归相同的谱滤波器。此外，非指数重置律可以生成通用的新型谱滤波器，为统计学习中的正则化提供了新的动力学视角。

Abstract: We connect stochastic resetting from non-equilibrium statistical physics with ridge regularization in statistical learning. For linear gradient flow, resetting to the origin at rate $r$ produces stationary mean $(X^\top X+rI)^{-1}X^\top y$, exactly the ridge estimator with penalty $λ=r$. This uses the known Laplace-transform relationship between ridge regression and exponential-time averaging of gradient flow, with the exponential time now interpreted as the stationary age associated with Poisson resetting. We then extend this identity to general renewal reset laws: the exponential reset time distribution is the unique renewal law whose stationary mean reproduces scalar ridge in every eigendirection as an exact filter identity for every positive curvature, while non-exponential renewal laws generate alternative spectral filters. At the fluctuation level, we study a separate additive Ornstein-Uhlenbeck extension with constant diffusion, interpreted as a stylized SGD approximation. In this setting, the equality holds only at the level of the mean, since the reset process has a nonzero stationary covariance from accumulated OU noise and reset-timing variance, whereas deterministic ridge is a fixed estimator with the same center. Stylized experiments compare the deterministic renewal-induced filters directly and illustrate when filters induced by non-exponential reset-time laws can differ predictively from ridge. The results for the stationary mean and the induced spectral filters are established for continuous-time gradient flow with isotropic resetting on quadratic objectives; the covariance and risk formulas additionally assume additive noise with state-independent covariance.

</details>


### [291] [Learning to Extrapolate to New Tasks: A Relational Approach to Task Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30132)
*Adam Ousherovitch,Yixin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对模型难以处理分布外任务的局限性，论文提出了一种基于“任务间变换关系”的 RTE 算法，通过将目标任务解析为锚点任务与关系变换的组合，实现了卓越的任务外推泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代学习系统在训练分布内的插值任务表现优异，但在处理训练分布之外的未知任务（如超出参数范围、更长的序列长度或新颖的组合）时泛化能力不足。现有模型即便在基础模型范式下，也难以实现系统性的任务外推。

Method: 提出“关系任务外推器”（Relational Task Extrapolator, RTE）算法。核心在于将目标任务分解为“已知锚点任务”与“连接锚点和目标任务的变换”。通过学习一个关系算子（relational operator），将锚点-变换对映射到目标任务的预测结果，从而赋予模型跨任务推理和外推的能力。

Result: 在函数预测任务（参数、长度、组合外推）和序列预测任务（针对基础模型的微调）中进行了验证，结果表明 RTE 在面对各种分布外任务时均能实现显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过学习任务间的变换关系，RTE 能够在参数、长度和组成结构等方面实现系统性的任务外推。实验证明，该方法在处理训练分布之外的未知任务时，性能显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Modern learning systems excel at interpolation but struggle to generalize to unseen tasks outside the training distribution's support. This failure occurs even in simple settings, such as handling task parameters beyond the training range, and persists despite advances in foundation models. To this end, we develop the Relational Task Extrapolator (RTE), an algorithm designed to enable systematic extrapolation to novel tasks. The key observation is that extrapolation is inherently relational: extrapolating to unseen tasks requires learning how tasks transform into one another. If a model learns the transformation between tasks A and B during training, it can apply that same transformation to relate known tasks to unseen ones at test time. RTE operationalizes this idea by decomposing each target task into a known anchor task and a transformation linking the anchor and target. It then learns a relational operator, mapping an anchor-transformation pair to predictions for the target task. We instantiate RTE across multiple task extrapolation regimes in function prediction, e.g. where target tasks use out-of-range parameters (parameter extrapolation), have greater compositional depth (length extrapolation), and/or recombine function primitives in unseen ways (compositional extrapolation). We further extend RTE to sequence prediction, integrating it into fine-tuning algorithms for foundation models. Across empirical studies, we find that RTE substantially outperforms existing approaches on extrapolation to novel, unseen tasks.

</details>


### [292] [CalArena: A Large-Scale Post-Hoc Calibration Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30188)
*Eugène Berta,David Holzmüller,Francis Bach,Michael I. Jordan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个包含近2000次实验的大规模后验校准基准测试框架，利用“后验校准改进（PHI）”指标评估各类校准方法，旨在解决现有校准评估不一致的问题，并为不同分类任务提供最佳实践参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习分类器普遍存在校准不良问题。虽然目前存在多种后验校准（Post-hoc calibration）方法，但由于缺乏统一的评估标准和实验规模较小，导致研究人员难以辨别哪些方法在实际应用中真正有效。

Method: 构建了一个涵盖表格数据和计算机视觉任务、包含近2000次实验的大规模标准化基准；引入了基于适当评分规则的“后验校准改进”（Post-Hoc Improvement, PHI）作为评估指标，并提供了一套统一的、可重现的开源实现框架。

Result: 通过该基准测试，研究揭示了不同领域中的通用校准规律，填补了现有校准评估方法中对校准质量与预测性能权衡的评估空白，为未来校准研究提供了参考基准。

Conclusion: 平滑型校准函数优于基于分箱的方法；在多类别高维场景中，专用的校准方法至关重要；通用机器学习模型若不经过特定的校准设计，其校准效果并不具有竞争力。PH I（后验校准改进）被证明是评估校准方法可靠且具有原则性的指标。

Abstract: Reliable probability estimates are critical in many machine learning applications, yet modern classifiers are often poorly calibrated. Post-hoc calibration provides a simple and widely used solution, but the large number of proposed methods, combined with small-scale and inconsistent evaluations, makes it difficult to determine which approaches are truly effective in practice. We introduce a large-scale, standardized benchmark for post-hoc calibration, covering nearly 2000 experiments across tabular and computer vision tasks, including binary, multiclass, and large-scale classification settings. Our benchmark aggregates predictions from a diverse set of classical models, modern deep learning architectures, and foundation models, and provides unified, reproducible implementations of dozens of calibration methods within a common evaluation framework. We argue that Post-Hoc Improvement (PHI) in proper scoring rules offers a principled alternative to traditional calibration error estimators for comparing post-hoc methods, capturing both calibration quality and potential degradation to the model's predictive performance. Using this framework, we conduct the most comprehensive empirical study of post-hoc calibration to date. Our results reveal consistent patterns across domains: smooth calibration functions outperform binning-based approaches, dedicated multiclass methods are essential in high-dimensional settings, and generic machine learning models are not competitive without calibration-specific design. To facilitate future research, we release all data, code, and evaluation tools, providing a plug-and-play benchmark for developing and comparing calibration methods.

</details>


### [293] [Statistical Embeddings for Similarity, Retrieval, and Interpretable Alignment of Numeric Tabular Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30289)
*M. Ross Kunz,John Merickel,Keith Wilson*
