We provide a rigorous convergence analysis using differential inclusions, proving that S-Adam converges almost surely to ($δ$,$ε$)-Clarke stationary points at the optimal O(1/$\sqrt(T)$) rate. Empirical evaluations on Quantization-Aware Training (QAT) and high-noise small-batch learning demonstrate that S-Adam consistently outperforms AdamW and Prox-SGD, achieving accuracy gains of up to 6 percent on CIFAR-100 and 3 percent on TinyImageNet while effectively mitigating gradient oscillations.

</details>


### [330] [The Little Book of Generative AI Foundations: An Intuitive Mathematical Primer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29713)
*Tianhua Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本书是一本专注于生成式AI数学基础的导论教材，通过严谨的数学推导，串联了从传统PCA到前沿扩散模型与GAN等各类生成模型的底层逻辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对目前生成式人工智能领域纷繁复杂的架构，旨在降低学习门槛，帮助研究人员和开发者在不牺牲数学严谨性的前提下，理解和掌握生成式建模背后的统一数学逻辑。

Method: 采用数学推导导向的方法，系统性地梳理了从经典方法（PCA、概率PCA）到现代架构（VAE、扩散模型、标准化流、GAN、能量模型等）的演进脉络，重点剖析核心数学原理而非单纯罗列工程实现细节。

Result: 呈现了一套系统性的生成式建模理论体系，成功将不同类别的生成模型抽象为相互关联的数学结构，为后续深入研究奠定了基础。

Conclusion: 通过提供严谨的数学推导，构建起现代生成式模型之间内在联系的逻辑框架，为读者提供坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: This book provides a compact, derivation-oriented introduction to the mathematical foundations of modern generative artificial intelligence. Rather than surveying every recent architecture or implementation detail, it develops a coherent route through the ideas connecting major families of generative models, from PCA, probabilistic PCA, variational autoencoders, and diffusion models to normalising flows, autoregressive factorisations, GANs, Wasserstein GANs, and energy-based models. The aim is to make the structure of generative modelling more accessible without removing the mathematical substance needed to understand how these models are derived and related. The book is intended as a foundation-building primer for mathematically curious researchers, practitioners, and students.

</details>


### [331] [HARP: Hadamard-Preconditioned Adaptive Rotation Processor for Extreme LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29843)
*Artur Zagitov,Gleb Molodtsov,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 LLM 极低比特量化面临的精度损失问题，HARP 提出了一种可学习的自适应正交变换架构，替代了传统的固定 Hadamard 变换，在提升量化鲁棒性的同时保持了高效的推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于 RHT 的 PTQ 方法虽然能缓解激活离群点和权重各向异性导致的问题，但其变换矩阵是固定的，无法针对不同层、校准分布或量化器进行自适应优化。

Method: 提出 HARP（Hadamard-preconditioned Adaptive Rotation Processor），这是一种可学习的结构化两面正交处理器。其核心特征包括：使用一系列稀疏蝴蝶状块正交阶段代替固定 Hadamard 变换、支持通过混合基（Mixed-Radix）调度处理非 2 的幂次维度、以及通过在校准数据上进行训练来根据各层的特性自适应调整量化基。

Result: 在 1B 到 70B 参数模型及 2-4 比特量化设置下，HARP 在困惑度（Perplexity）和零样本（Zero-shot）准确率上均优于固定 RHT 方法，并保持了极高的推理效率（高达 128 tok/s）。

Conclusion: HARP 提出了一种可学习的结构化正交变换方案，证明了通过针对特定层和校准数据学习旋转基，可以显著优于传统的固定 Hadamard 变换（RHT），为极端低比特量化提供了更高效、更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is essential for deploying LLMs under memory and bandwidth constraints. However, extreme low-bit quantization remains highly sensitive to activation outliers and anisotropic weight curvature. Existing incoherence-based PTQ methods mitigate this issue with fixed randomized Hadamard transforms (RHTs), which improve quantization robustness but cannot adapt the rotated basis to the layer, calibration distribution, or quantizer. We introduce HARP (Hadamard-preconditioned Adaptive Rotation Processor), a learnable structured two-sided orthogonal processor that replaces fixed Hadamard mixing while preserving exact full-precision equivalence. HARP represents each rotation as a product of sparse butterfly-like block-orthogonal stages, supports non-power-of-two dimensions via Mixed-Radix schedules, and initializes to the RHT processor up to a fixed permutation. Fitted only on calibration data, HARP adapts the quantization basis to each layer and backend. Across 2-4 bit settings on models ranging from 1B to 70B parameters, HARP improves perplexity and zero-shot accuracy over fixed RHT. Importantly, HARP preserves deployment efficiency, reaching 128 tok/s versus 61 tok/s for FP16.

</details>


### [332] [LaRA: Layer-wise Representation Analysis for Detecting Data Contamination in RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29888)
*Minju Gwak,Minseo Kwak,Dongseok Lee,Guijin Son,Alan Ritter,Jaehyung Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对RL训练后LLM的数据污染问题，本文提出了LaRA框架，通过分析模型深层表征的几何特性进行有效检测，克服了困惑度等传统指标在RL训练模型上的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的污染检测方法依赖于模型输出（如困惑度），这在经过强化学习（RL）微调的LLM中失效。研究旨在解决RL后训练中数据污染导致的泛化性和评估可靠性问题。

Method: 提出了LaRA（层级表征分析）框架，引入了三种补充指标（扰动敏感性、方向坍塌、局部表征刚性），通过测量模型在控制扰动下的多层表征变化来检测污染。

Result: 研究发现，模型在受到污染时呈现出跨层的几何偏差，包括扰动敏感性增强、更强的方向坍塌和更高的局部刚性。基于这些特征，构建的检测协议显著优于现有的输出级检测基线。

Conclusion: LaRA通过分析层级表征偏差，在RL训练后的LLM污染检测中优于基于输出信号的传统基准，证明了表征级分析是解决复杂模型污染问题的关键。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training has shown to improve reasoning in large language models (LLMs). However, there has been little exploration on the problem of data contamination in RL post-training, potentially undermining generalization and evaluation reliability of the training process itself. Existing detection methods primarily rely on output-level signals such as likelihood or entropy, which become unreliable for RL-trained models since RL shapes behavior through trajectory-level rewards rather than token likelihoods. We propose LaRA, a layer-wise representation analysis framework for detecting contamination in RL post-trained LLMs. LaRA introduces three complementary metrics, measuring perturbation sensitivity, directional collapse, and local representation rigidity under controlled perturbations. We find that contamination produces progressive geometric deviations across layers, including amplified perturbation sensitivity, stronger directional collapse, and enhanced local rigidity. Based on our findings, we also develop a contamination detection protocol that aggregates representation-level deviations across layers and metrics. Experiments on RL-trained reasoning models show that our protocol outperforms existing output-level baselines for contamination detection.

</details>


### [333] [Test Time Training for Supervised Causal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30015)
*Zizhen Deng,Jiaru Zhang,Rui Ding,Huang Bojun,Jinzhuo Wang,Qiang Fu,Shi Han,Dongmei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对监督因果学习（SCL）在真实世界应用中泛化能力差的问题，提出了 TTT-SCL 框架，通过在测试时动态生成对齐的训练集，显著提升了模型的泛化性和因果发现精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监督因果学习（SCL）方法在处理分布外（OOD）数据时面临严峻挑战，具体表现为合成基准与真实数据间的巨大性能差距、对分布偏移的脆弱性以及缺乏组合泛化能力。

Method: 提出了 Test-Time Training for Supervised Causal Learning (TTT-SCL) 框架。该框架利用经典评分函数构建了一个高效模块，能够为每一个特定的测试实例动态生成分布对齐的训练集，从而在推理阶段进行自适应学习。

Result: 在合成、伪真实及真实世界数据集上的实验结果表明，TTT-SCL 在因果发现性能上显著优于现有的 SCL 方法和传统因果发现算法。

Conclusion: TTT-SCL 通过在测试时针对特定实例动态生成训练集，有效克服了监督因果学习在分布外泛化和真实世界应用中的局限性，实现了比传统方法及现有SCL方法更优的性能。

Abstract: Supervised Causal Learning (SCL) has shown promise in causal discovery by framing it as a supervised learning problem. However, it suffers from significant out-of-distribution generalization challenges. We reveal three limitations of previous SCL practices: a significant performance gap between synthetic benchmarks and real-world data, fragility to distribution shifts, and failure in compositional generalization, collectively questioning its real-world applicability. To address this, we propose Test-Time Training for Supervised Causal Learning (TTT-SCL), a novel framework that dynamically generates training sets explicitly aligned with any specific test instance. We demonstrate the correlation between TTT-SCL and score-based methods, and design an efficient module for generating training sets based on the classic scoring function. Experiments on synthetic benchmarks, pseudo-real and real-world datasets demonstrate that TTT-SCL significantly outperforms existing SCL and traditional causal discovery methods.

</details>


### [334] [Masked Diffusion Modeling for Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30046)
*Lixing Zhang,Yuchen Liang,Liyan Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 MaskDiff-AD，这是一种针对分类、混合型和离散数据设计的掩码扩散异常检测方法，通过重构掩码值的难度来计算异常分数，无需反向采样，并在多项基准测试中展现了领先的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的异常检测方法在处理分类、混合型和离散序列数据时面临挑战，且目前缺乏针对此类数据的有效扩散模型应用。作者旨在利用掩码扩散机制通过恢复掩码值的方式，构建一种直接在离散状态空间下运作的异常检测框架。

Method: 提出一种基于掩码扩散模型（Masked Diffusion Model）的正向异常检测方法。通过在仅包含正常数据的集合上训练模型，利用重构掩码坐标的难度衡量异常分数；同时开发了非参数变体，并推导了固定检测阈值下的 I 类和 II 类错误界限。

Result: 在 ADBench、UADAD 和 NLP-ADBench 的 18 个数据集上进行了实验，结果显示 MaskDiff-AD 优于多种经典及最新的基线方法，在 12 个表格数据集的评估中取得了整体平均排名第一的成绩。

Conclusion: MaskDiff-AD 提供了一种无需反向采样、适用于非连续数据的有效异常检测方案，在处理分类、混合型表格数据和文本数据方面表现出色，并在理论上提供了检错保障。

Abstract: Anomaly detection aims to identify samples that deviate from the nominal data distribution and is central to many safety-critical applications. However, developing effective anomaly detection methods for categorical, mixed-type, and discrete sequence data remains challenging and relatively underexplored. Masked diffusion models provide a natural way to model such data by learning to recover masked values from the remaining visible context. In this paper, we propose Masked Diffusion for Anomaly Detection (MaskDiff-AD), a forward-only method based on masked diffusion models trained only on nominal data. Given a test sample, MaskDiff-AD constructs anomaly scores from the difficulty of reconstructing randomly masked coordinates, yielding a content-sensitive score that operates directly on discrete state spaces while avoiding reverse-time sampling. We also develop a non-parametric variant of MaskDiff-AD and provide theoretical guarantees by characterizing Type-I and Type-II errors under a fixed detection threshold. Experiments on fourteen categorical and mixed-type tabular datasets from ADBench and UADAD, as well as four text anomaly detection datasets from NLP-ADBench, show that MaskDiff-AD achieves competitive performance against classical, diffusion-based, and recent tabular/text anomaly detection baselines. Notably, MaskDiff-AD achieves the best overall average rank, outperforming all twelve tabular baseline methods.

</details>


### [335] [A Predictive Law for On-Policy Self-Distillation From World Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30070)
*Tommy He,Jerome Sieber,Matteo Saponati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）的后训练效果可以通过初始性能差距进行线性预测，这种可预测性不随模型规模变化，为RL后训练提供了一种高效的可调优框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL后训练需要从简单的标量奖励转向更丰富的世界反馈（World Feedback），而OPSD方法的可靠性尚不明确，缺乏对训练效果的预测机制。

Method: 通过对模型性能差距与最终提升效果进行定量分析，揭示了OPSD中的线性预测规律，并验证了该规律在不同语境及模型规模下的稳定性。

Result: 发现并验证了“初始差值-最终提升”之间的线性相关性；证明了该预测定律随模型规模增加依然成立。

Conclusion: 在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）的性能与初始模型性能差距存在线性相关性，该规律具有跨模型规模的可预测性，为在大模型后训练中高效利用外部反馈提供了原则性指导。

Abstract: Moving beyond simple scalar rewards toward richer world feedback is a natural path to more scalable RL post-training. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) is a promising recent approach that uses arbitrary feedback as learning signal, yet its reliability compared to established methods, such as GRPO, remains unclear. We identify a strikingly consistent linear correlation between the initial student-self-teacher performance gap and the final performance improvement in OPSD. This relationship holds across context types and model families, providing a powerful predictive law for anticipating the outcome of an OPSD configuration without running the full training procedure. Interestingly, we show that this linear predictability holds with model scale, suggesting a potential basis for new empirical scaling laws on larger models with stronger in-context learning capabilities. In essence, our findings show that OPSD performance can be predicted and tuned before training, offering a principled way to incorporate world feedback as a first-class component of the post-training pipeline.

</details>


### [336] [Evolving Features vs Evolving Entire Trees with GP for Interpretable Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30119)
*Thalea Schlender,Peter A. N. Bosman,Tanja Alderliesten*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合遗传编程的进化方法，用于自动构建高性能、易解释的浅层生存树，通过同时优化特征组合与树结构，有效解决了传统生存树在准确性与可解释性之间的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统生存树在追求高预测精度时往往需要大幅增加模型深度，导致可解释性下降；同时，贪婪生长策略可能错失全局最优解。本文旨在通过进化计算在保持可解释性的同时提升浅层生存树的精度。

Method: 利用遗传编程（Genetic Programming）进行多目标进化，构建可检查特征集，并引入一种联合优化生存树结构与非线性分裂逻辑的进化策略。

Result: 基于进化特征构建的方法在不同树诱导策略和不同深度下均提升了预测性能，且全联合进化方法表现最优。

Conclusion: 联合进化方法（协同优化生存树结构与非线性分裂逻辑）能够生成多棵高性能且具有内在可解释性的浅层生存树。

Abstract: Survival analysis concerns the task of predicting the time until an event occurs. Often used in the medical field, survival analysis deals with incomplete (i.e., censored) data, for instance, from patients who did not experience the event during the duration of the study. For practical use, both accuracy and interpretability are important.
  Survival trees are easy-to-follow survival models that split the patient cohort recursively into discrete patient groups. Whilst survival trees can capture complex relationships, they typically need to grow large, threatening interpretability. Moreover, survival trees are often built using greedy approaches that may overlook globally optimal split combinations, limiting predictive performance.
  Shallow survival trees require expressive, higher-order feature combinations to achieve competitive accuracy. We therefore use genetic programming to multi-objectively evolve inherently inspectable feature sets and study how they interact with different tree induction strategies. We further introduce an evolutionary approach that jointly optimises the survival tree structure and the non-linear split logic.
  Our findings demonstrate that evolutionary feature construction improves predictive performance across different tree induction strategies on two real-world datasets and two different survival tree depths. Full joint evolution has the overall highest potential to propose multiple inherently inspectable shallow survival trees of good performance.

</details>


### [337] [Beyond MSE: Improving Precipitation Nowcasting with Multi-Quantile Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30122)
*Gijs van Nieuwkoop,Siamak Mehrkanoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究表明，通过使用多分位数损失函数（Pinball loss）替代传统的 MSE/MAE，可显著提升深度学习降水预报模型的精度，并实现更稳健的强降水风险评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习降水临近预报模型常使用 MSE 或 MAE 等逐点损失函数，导致预测结果过于平滑，且难以准确模拟强降水事件。

Method: 以 SmaAt-UNet 为基础架构，对比了均方误差（MSE）、平均绝对误差（MAE）以及多分位数“分位数损失函数”（Pinball loss）三种训练策略在荷兰雷达降水临近预报中的表现。

Result: 与使用 MSE 训练的模型相比，引入多分位数训练后，中央确定性预测的测试集 MSE 降低了 8.6%，同时生成的上分位数输出能有效应用于强降水风险预警。

Conclusion: 使用多分位数回归（Multi-quantile regression）作为损失函数是现有降水临近预报模型的一种有效改进方案，能在不改变模型架构的前提下，兼顾确定性预报精度与极端降水风险预测能力。

Abstract: Deep-learning precipitation nowcasting models are often optimized using pointwise losses such as mean squared error or mean absolute error, which can lead to overly smooth forecasts and poor representation of heavy rainfall. This study investigates whether the predictive performance of an established deterministic nowcasting architecture can be improved by reformulating training as a multi-quantile regression problem. Using SmaAt-UNet as a core model, we compare MSE, MAE, and multi-quantile pinball-loss training on radar precipitation nowcasting over the Netherlands. The results show that multi-quantile training improves the central deterministic forecast, decreasing test-set MSE by 8.6\% compared to a model trained using MSE, while also producing upper-quantile outputs that are useful for risk-sensitive prediction of heavy precipitation. These findings suggest that quantile regression provides a simple alternative to standard pointwise losses without requiring a new architecture or generative sampling procedure. The implementation of our models and training setup is available on \href{https://github.com/gijsvn/Multi-Quantile-Precipitation-Nowcasting}{GitHub}.

</details>


### [338] [Overcoming Forgetting in LLM Fine-Tuning with Evolution Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30148)
*Kajetan Schweighofer,Conor F. Hayes,Roberto Dailey,Risto Miikkulainen,Xin Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了ES微调大模型时的“遗忘”现象，指出其本质为性能漂移而非永久遗忘，并提出AWD（锚定权重衰减）技术有效解决了该问题，验证了ES在持续学习中的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有研究认为ES微调大模型会导致严重灾难性遗忘的观点，探究其产生机制及是否可通过技术手段克服，从而提升ES在持续学习中的应用价值。

Method: 1. 实验验证：对比分析ES与RL方法在任务微调中的表现；2. 理论分析：研究演化策略优化动态（尤其是弱约束方向的随机游走行为）；3. 提出算法：引入“锚定权重衰减”（Anchored Weight Decay, AWD），通过将权重约束回初始参数来稳定性能。

Result: 1. 发现遗忘本质上是“性能漂移”而非不可逆遗忘，且RL方法同样存在该问题；2. 揭示了遗忘源于训练过程中的随机游走行为；3. 实验表明AWD在显著降低计算成本的同时，能有效稳定旧任务性能并保持目标任务性能。

Conclusion: 在ES微调大模型时，遗忘并非不可逆，通过引入AWD正则化技术可以有效缓解性能漂移，证明了ES在持续学习场景下的潜力和实用性。

Abstract: Evolution Strategies (ES) has recently emerged as a competitive alternative to reinforcement learning (RL) for large language model (LLM) fine-tuning, offering advantages through simplicity, scalability, and inference-only training. However, recent work suggests that ES fine-tuning on new tasks may induce forgetting of prior tasks. First, this paper shows that prior task forgetting (1) is better characterized as performance drift rather than irreversible forgetting, with prior-task performance often recovering during ES training; and (2) is not a specific failure mode of ES, but can also arise for fine-tuning with RL methods. Second, it analyzes when and why such drift arises, highlighting its dependence on ES training dynamics, particularly random walk behavior in weakly constrained directions of the weight space. Third, based on these insights, it introduces Anchored Weight Decay (AWD) as a parameter-space regularization technique that constrains optimization toward the initial model parameters. AWD effectively stabilizes prior-task performance while preserving target-task performance, achieving benefits comparable to large ES population sizes at much lower computational cost. Thus, contrary to previous beliefs, the paper shows that prior-task forgetting under ES is largely avoidable, positioning ES as a promising approach for continual learning in LLMs.

</details>


### [339] [On Distributional Reinforcement Learning in Chaotic Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30160)
*James Rudd-Jones,Mirco Musolesi,María Pérez-Ortiz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了混沌系统导致传统强化学习不稳定的根源，并论证了为何分布强化学习通过处理回报分布而非期望值，能有效缓解混沌动力系统带来的梯度和算子不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混沌动力系统对初始条件具有指数级敏感性，导致传统强化学习方法中基于标量值函数的引导目标（bootstrap targets）出现高方差，且梯度更新条件恶劣。

Method: 研究了在受控混沌动力系统下回报分布的演化规律，提出在 1-Wasserstein 度量下，回报分布比个体轨迹表现出更平滑的特性，并利用分布强化学习（Distributional RL）对齐优化目标。

Result: 证明了在温和的统计稳定性假设下，混沌系统中的回报分布具有比单纯追踪个体轨迹更好的性质（即更平滑的分布贝尔曼目标），验证了分布强化学习在应对混沌系统时的鲁棒性优势。

Conclusion: 分布强化学习通过关注回报分布而非仅关注期望值，能够稳定地表征混沌动力系统，从而在具有高度敏感性和发散性的环境中实现更稳健、更高效的策略学习。

Abstract: Chaotic dynamical systems pose a fundamental challenge for Reinforcement Learning (RL): exponential sensitivity to initial conditions induces high-variance bootstrap targets and poorly conditioned gradient updates. Chaotic dynamics arise across scientific and engineering domains, from fluid flows and climate systems to multi-agent systems, where reliable learning is highly desirable. Standard RL methods optimise expected returns through scalar value functions, implicitly averaging over diverging trajectories and entangling trajectory level instability with the learning objective. We show that under mild statistical stability assumptions, the return distribution evolves more regularly than individual trajectories when measured under the $1$-Wasserstein metric, yielding a smoother distributional Bellman objective. By aligning optimisation with this measure level structure, distributional RL provides better conditioned learning. We offer a principled explanation for the advantages of distributional methods in chaotic systems and the geometries of RL objectives under chaos.

</details>


### [340] [iLoRA: Bayesian Low-Rank Adaptation with Latent Interaction Graphs for Microbiome Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30179)
*Yang Song,Yixuan Zhang,Lingfa Meng,Tongyuan Hu,Haizhou Shi,Hao Wang,Samir Bhatt,Hengguan Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: iLoRA 是一种创新的图条件 LoRA 方法，通过动态生成输入感知的低秩更新来捕捉复杂科学任务中的潜在交互结构，提升了 LLM 在科学领域的预测能力和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 LoRA 使用静态低秩更新，无法捕捉驱动科学预测（如微生物互作）的潜在交互关系，且交互分析通常只能在预测后进行，缺乏对潜在逻辑的建模。

Method: 引入 iLoRA 框架，该框架从输入数据中推断潜在交互图，并利用该图动态生成输入条件下的 LoRA 更新矩阵，从而实现预测任务与潜在结构学习的协同优化。

Result: 在交互式问答和溃疡性结肠炎（IBD）诊断任务中，iLoRA 优于标准 LoRA 及其他贝叶斯微调基线；能够成功恢复与人类标注及群组关联度一致的图结构，并具备良好的预测不确定性校准能力。

Conclusion: iLoRA 是首个贝叶斯图条件 LoRA 框架，通过联合学习预测和潜在交互结构，显著提升了参数高效微调在复杂科学领域（如微生物组诊断）的表现，并提供了可靠的预测不确定性估计。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient adaptation has made LLMs practical for domain prediction, but standard LoRA still relies on a static low-rank update and does not expose the latent interactions that often drive scientific labels. We introduce iLoRA. To our knowledge, it is the first Bayesian graph-conditioned LoRA framework. It infers a latent interaction graph from the input and uses it to generate input-conditioned LoRA updates. As a result, iLoRA learns prediction and latent interaction structure jointly, rather than training a predictor and applying interaction analysis only post hoc. We instantiate this idea for microbiome diagnosis, where disease state can depend on both species-level abundance and microbe-microbe cross-talk, and evaluate it in two complementary settings: interactive QA with human-annotated graphs, which tests latent structure recovery, and multi-cohort IBD diagnosis, which tests biomedical utility. Across both settings, iLoRA improves over strong LoRA and Bayesian adaptation baselines, recovers graphs aligned with human annotations and cohort-level microbiome associations, and provides calibrated uncertainty with moderate graph-branch overhead.

</details>


### [341] [HPO: Hysteretic Policy Optimization for Stable and Efficient Training under Sparse-Reward Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30201)
*Mohamed Sana,Nicola Piovesan,Antonio De Domenico,Fadhel Ayed,Haozhe Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 GRPO 在稀疏奖励下的训练不稳定问题，本文提出了自适应滞后策略优化（A-HPO），通过动态调整负优势样本的权重及优化长度归一化方法，有效提升了训练初期的学习效率和最终任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO 在稀疏奖励场景下存在失效模式：训练前期负优势样本较多，且原有的响应级长度归一化导致更新幅度与输出长度强耦合，阻碍了模型的学习稳定性。

Method: 提出了 Hysteretic Policy Optimization (HPO)，通过降低负优势样本的权重并将响应级长度归一化改为均值长度归一化；进一步提出了自适应版本 A-HPO，基于批处理统计信息自动调整滞后权重（hysteretic weight），无需手动调参。

Result: 在 TeleLogs 和 Countdown 任务中，A-HPO 显著优于 GRPO（TeleLogs 上提升 15%）、SAPO 和 GSPO，特别是在训练初期及高难度配置下表现更为稳健。

Conclusion: 通过调节负优势更新的权重并改进长度归一化机制，A-HPO 能够更有效地处理强化学习训练初期的稀疏奖励问题，从而显著提升模型在推理和计算任务中的表现。

Abstract: We investigate a narrow but common failure mode of GRPO-style reinforcement learning in the context of sparse verifiable rewards: early updates contain more responses with negative advantages than those with positive advantages, while response-level length normalization ties the magnitude of the update to the length of the output. We propose Hysteretic Policy Optimization (HPO), a minimal modification of GRPO that reduces the weight of negative-advantage updates and replaces per-response length normalization with mean-length normalization. We further introduce Adaptive HPO (A-HPO), which sets the hysteretic weight based on batch-level advantage-sign statistics, thereby removing the need for tuning a fixed hysteretic weight. In our TeleLogs and Countdown experiments, A-HPO improves the reward per update compared to GRPO, with the largest gains in early sparse reward regimes. On TeleLogs, A-HPO achieves a final reward of 0.84, outperforming SAPO by 5%, GSPO by 11%, and GRPO by 15%, while maintaining a comparable response-length. On Countdown, A-HPO achieves the largest gains in initial and most difficult configurations across 1.5B-7B models. Ablation studies on the hysteretic weight show that the gains of A-HPO come from better balancing the contributions of positive and negative advantages compared to positive-only or fully symmetric updates.

</details>


### [342] [In-Context Reward Adaptation for Robust Preference Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30323)
*Zhenyu Sun,Zheng Xu,Ermin Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种In-Context Reward Adaptation框架，通过利用上下文学习并结合人类响应时间，解决了大模型在处理动态、未知人类偏好时的适应性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的RLHF依赖静态奖励模型，难以应对人类偏好的多样性与异构性，且现有多奖励框架无法在无需重新训练的前提下适应未见过的偏好分布。

Method: 提出了一种基于Transformer的上下文奖励适应（In-Context Reward Adaptation）框架。该方法利用上下文学习能力，通过少量的偏好演示样本推断底层奖励结构，并创新性地将“响应时间”作为辅助输入信号以纠正模型评估偏差。

Result: 研究发现标准Transformer架构存在趋向地面真值的渐近偏差，而引入响应时间作为辅助信息后，模型能够有效适应来自未见领域的人类偏好，显著增强了偏好建模的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过引入响应时间作为辅助信号，作者成功解决了单一奖励模型在面对多样化和未知偏好分布时的泛化难题，为实现更灵活、鲁棒的AI对齐提供了可扩展的方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) typically relies on static reward models to align Large Language Models with human preferences. However, human values are inherently diverse and heterogeneous, and a single reward model often lacks the robustness required to generalize to unseen preference domains. While existing multi-reward frameworks attempt to address this, they are often restricted to a fixed set of known domains and fail to adapt to unseen human distributions without costly retraining. In this work, we propose In-Context Reward Adaptation, a transformer-based framework designed to model diverse and unseen human preferences on the fly. By leveraging the in-context learning capabilities of transformers, our approach adaptively infers the underlying reward structure from a small set of preference demonstrations. We demonstrate that while a standard transformer architecture is insufficient for this task by characterizing an asymptotic bias to the ground-truth, incorporating human response time as an auxiliary input signal enables the model to successfully adapt to preferences from previously unseen domains. Our findings show that this approach provides a more robust foundation for preference modeling, allowing for the representation of heterogeneous rewards and preference distribution shift, and offering a scalable path toward more flexible human-AI alignment.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [343] [CA-AC-MPC: CUDA-Accelerated Actor-Critic Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29155)
*Antoonio Buo,Vittorio Cammarota,Michele Avagnale,Pierluigi Arpenti,Vincenzo Lippiello,Fabio Ruggiero*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过CUDA加速技术优化了AC-MPC的微分MPC层，解决了其计算延迟瓶颈，在实现高性能无人机控制的同时大幅提升了运算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的AC-MPC方法中，微分MPC层在正向和反向传播过程中需要重复进行计算密集型的优化求解，导致训练和推理存在严重的延迟瓶颈。

Method: 提出了一种CUDA加速的AC-MPC（Actor-Critic Model Predictive Control）变体，通过对微分MPC层进行并行化计算加速。

Result: 在敏捷无人机竞速任务中达到了SOTA（当前最佳）的圈速表现，并展现了近乎极限的动态控制能力，同时显著缩短了训练和推理时间。

Conclusion: 通过CUDA硬件加速能够有效解决AC-MPC中因重复求解优化问题导致的计算瓶颈，在保持控制性能的同时大幅提升训练与推理效率。

Abstract: In the literature, actor-critic model predictive control (AC-MPC) integrates MPC with reinforcement learning to enable high-performance control of complex dynamical systems. However, its differentiable MPC layer requires repeatedly solving an optimization problem in both the forward and backward passes, leading to substantial training and inference latency. This paper tackles this bottleneck introducing a CUDA-accelerated variant that significantly reduces end-to-end execution time while preserving the control performance of the baseline formulation. Simulation results on an agile drone racing task show that our approach achieves state-of-the-art lap times and near-limit dynamic behaviour with markedly reduced training and inference time.

</details>


### [344] [Extreme dynamic symmetry enables omnidirectional and multifunctional robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29254)
*Jiaxun Liu,Boxi Xia,Boyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究引入并量化了“动态对称性”概念，证明通过优化机器人重心加速度的均匀性（动态各向同性），能显著提升机器人在复杂环境下的敏捷性、鲁棒性和环境适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人设计对对称性的应用多局限于几何层面，但自然系统往往依赖更深层的对称性来协调动作。研究旨在探索利用“动力学执行能力的对称性”作为一种提升机器人性能的统一设计策略。

Method: 1. 提出了“动态对称性”（dynamic symmetry）和“动态各向同性”（dynamic isotropy）的概念，用于量化机器人重心加速度的一致性；2. 在1000多种模拟形态上进行了实证研究；3. 设计并开发了Argus系列球形机器人，通过径向线性执行器直接控制重心动力学，其中20腿版本实现了近乎极限的动态各向同性。

Result: 1. 模拟实验显示，动态各向同性越高，机器人在轨迹跟踪、任务成功率、鲁棒性、弹性和能效方面表现越好；2. 20腿Argus版本成功展示了方向不变性移动、复杂地形穿越、快速自稳定以及对执行器故障的弹性，并实现了全向感知与交互。

Conclusion: 在机器人设计中，除了传统的几何对称性，引入动态对称性是提升机器人敏捷性、鲁棒性和多功能性的有效且通用的策略，特别是在复杂及不确定环境下。

Abstract: Symmetry is a central organizing principle in natural systems, yet its use as a unifying design strategy in robotics has largely remained limited to geometric form. We show that symmetry can instead be leveraged at the level of dynamic actuation capability. We introduce dynamic symmetry, the uniformity of a robot's attainable center-of-mass accelerations, and formalize it through a measure coined as dynamic isotropy. Across more than 1000 simulated morphologies, we found that higher dynamic symmetry consistently improved trajectory tracking, task success, robustness, resiliency, and energy efficiency, with the benefits becoming most pronounced as dynamic isotropy approached its theoretical limit. To study this regime systematically, we developed Argus, a family of spherical robots designed to explore the effects of increasing dynamic symmetry. Members of the Argus family vary in their actuation geometry and dynamic symmetry level while sharing a common architectural principle: radially oriented linear actuators that directly shape the robot's center-of-mass dynamics. Among them, we built a physical 20-leg Argus variant that achieved near-extreme dynamic isotropy and demonstrated orientation-invariant locomotion, agile traversal of cluttered and deformable terrain, rapid self-stabilization, and resilience to partial actuator failures. Its distributed sensing further enabled omnidirectional perception and object interaction during continuous motion. These results show that designing robots for symmetry not only in morphology but also in their attainable dynamics provides a powerful and general pathway toward agility, robustness, and multifunctionality in uncertain terrestrial and extraterrestrial environments.

</details>


### [345] [MonoDuo: Using One Robot Arm to Learn Bimanual Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29298)
*Sandeep Bajamahal,Lawrence Yunliang Chen,Toru Lin,Zehan Ma,Jitendra Malik,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MonoDuo 是一种允许利用单臂机器人数据通过人机协作合成双臂演示，从而训练双臂机器人操控策略的新型框架，有效降低了对双臂机器人数据的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂机器人硬件和数据集稀缺，限制了双臂操作任务的学习；而单臂机器人资源丰富，因此探索如何利用单臂演示训练双臂策略具有重要意义。

Method: 提出 MonoDuo 框架：1. 数据采集：利用单臂机器人与人类协作完成任务（互换角色）；2. 数据增强：通过手部姿态估计、图像/点云分割与补全技术，将单臂数据合成为双臂机器人演示；3. 策略训练：利用生成的合成演示训练双臂策略，并进行小样本微调。

Result: 在5项复杂任务（装箱、打包、折叠布料、拉拉链、递盘子）中验证，零样本部署成功率达70%；结合25次目标机器人演示进行小样本微调，比从头开始训练的成功率提升了65-70%。

Conclusion: MonoDuo 有效解决了双臂机器人训练数据稀缺的问题，通过单臂机器人演示与人类协作，能够高效地将知识迁移至双臂机器人策略，实现了零样本部署和高效的小样本微调。

Abstract: Bimanual coordination is essential for many real-world manipulation tasks, yet learning bimanual robot policies is limited by the scarcity of bimanual robots and datasets. Single-arm robots, however, are widely available in research labs. Can we leverage them to train bimanual robot policies? We present MonoDuo, a framework for learning bimanual manipulation policies using single-arm robot demonstrations paired with human collaboration. MonoDuo collects data by teleoperating a single-arm robot to perform one side of a bimanual task while a human performs the other, then swapping roles to cover both sides. RGB-D observations from a wrist-mounted and fixed camera are augmented into synthetic demonstrations for target bimanual robots using state-of-the-art hand pose estimation, image and point cloud segmentation, and inpainting. These synthetic demonstrations, grounded in real robot kinematics, are used to train bimanual policies. We evaluate MonoDuo on five tasks: box lifting, backpack packing, cloth folding, jacket zipping, and plate handover. Compared to approaches relying solely on human bimanual videos, MonoDuo enables zero-shot deployment on unseen bimanual robot configurations, achieving success rates up to 70%. With only 25 target robot demonstrations, few-shot finetuning further boosts success rates by 65-70% over training from scratch, demonstrating MonoDuo's effectiveness in efficiently transferring knowledge from single-arm robot data to bimanual robot policies.

</details>


### [346] [The Open Motion Planning Library 2.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29301)
*Weihang Guo,Theodoros Tyrovouzis,Emiliano Flores,Clayton W. Ramsey,Zachary K. Kingston,Ioan A. Şucan,Mark Moll,Lydia E. Kavraki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OMPL 2.0 升级发布，重点提升了硬件加速以支持实时运动规划，并强化了与现代 AI 研究工作流的深度集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决机器人领域对实时运动规划的需求，并适应当前与 AI 技术深度融合的研究趋势，需要对已有近 20 年历史的 OMPL 库进行重大升级。

Method: 在保持原有采样算法库的基础上，引入了针对硬件加速的优化技术，并重构了集成架构以适配现代 AI 研究工作流。

Result: 发布了 OMPL 2.0，实现了对实时运动规划的支持，增强了硬件加速能力，并提升了与现代 AI 环境的兼容性和集成度。

Conclusion: OMPL 2.0 代表了该库的一次重大演进，旨在通过硬件加速支持实时运动规划，并更好地与现代人工智能研究工作流集成，继续保持其在运动规划领域的基石地位。

Abstract: The Open Motion Planning Library (OMPL), first released in 2008, has become a cornerstone of the motion planning community, providing implementations of a wide range of state-of-the-art sampling-based algorithms. Over almost two decades of continuous development, we have steadily expanded the library with new planners, state spaces, and problem formulations. These additions range from asymptotically optimal and lazy planners to constrained motion planning and planning with temporal-logic goals. Building on this foundation, we introduce OMPL 2.0, a major evolution of the library that targets real-time motion planning through hardware acceleration and integrates seamlessly with modern AI research workflows. We also reflect on how OMPL and the field of motion planning have grown together over the years, and discuss the library's broader impact on the research community.

</details>


### [347] [Phase-Conditioned Imitation Learning with Autonomous Failure Recovery for Robust Deformable Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29407)
*Dayuan Chen,Kai Tang,Yukuan Zhang,Kazuhiro Kosuge,Yasuhisa Hirata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种具备力感知和相位调制功能的Hierarchical ACT架构（PHASER），通过融合多模态反馈解决可变形物体操作中的状态混淆问题，并显著提升了自主操作与错误恢复的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模仿学习策略（如ACT）在推理时往往默认马尔可夫性，导致在视觉相似但操作需求不同的场景下出现“状态混淆”（State aliasing），且在执行失败时缺乏自主恢复能力。

Method: 提出了一个闭环的分层架构，核心组件包括：1. 基于FiLM（Feature-wise Linear Modulation）调制的ACT编码器，实现相位特定的行为决策；2. 多模态（视觉、力觉、位姿）实时相位预测器，用于检测接触失败并触发恢复；3. 混合阻抗控制器，用于实现柔顺执行；4. 配套的触觉远程操作接口，用于收集力感知数据。

Result: 在T恤悬挂与移除任务中，通过相位调制和自主错误恢复，成功率从56%提升至87%；t-SNE分析证实FiLM诱导了良好的相位特征分离。

Conclusion: 通过引入相位条件（Phase-conditioned）和力感知机制，该框架成功解决了可变形物体操作中的状态混淆问题。实验证明，该方法能有效提升任务执行的成功率，并具备强大的自主错误恢复能力，为复杂操纵任务提供了鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a phase-conditioned, force-aware framework for robust deformable object manipulation. Standard imitation learning policies such as Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT) rely on a Markovian assumption at inference, causing state aliasing when visually similar observations require contradictory actions and preventing autonomous recovery from execution failures. We address this with a closed-loop hierarchical architecture. A FiLM-conditioned ACT encoder modulates feature extraction based on the current task phase, enabling a single unified policy to produce phase-specific behaviors while sharing action dynamics across phases. A multi-modal phase predictor fusing visual, force, and pose feedback estimates the phase in real time, detecting contact failures that are invisible to vision alone and autonomously triggering recovery trajectories. The system is completed by a hybrid impedance controller for compliant execution and a haptic teleoperation interface for force-aware data collection. Ablation studies show that FiLM-based modulation significantly outperforms both unconditioned and token-level conditioned baselines, and t-SNE analysis confirms that FiLM induces well-separated, phase-specific feature representations. Validated on hanging and removing a T-shirt with dual arms, the closed-loop system improves the hanging success rate from 56\% to 87\% through autonomous error recovery. Code and videos: https://leledeyuan00.github.io/phaser/

</details>


### [348] [A Progress-Aware Leader-Follower Midair Docking System for Dual-Drone Aerial Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29410)
*Yifan Cai,Jan Ming Kevin Tan,Xiangqi Li,Chenzhe Jin,Narsimlu Kemsaram,Valerio Modugno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于被动磁吸附的轻量化双无人机空中对接平台及任务管理框架，并构建了完整的软硬件评估基准，有效解决了受限环境下的高精度自主对接难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型无人机在进行模块化空中协作和操作时，面临严苛的推力和载荷限制，且需要实现精确的相对姿态控制与可重复的对接平台，现有的方案缺乏系统化的评估基准。

Method: 提出了一种双无人机对接平台，采用领航-跟随者（leader-follower）编队，利用轻量级模块化框架和被动磁吸附机构；开发了一套“任务感知”监督程序（Mission Supervisor）来管理对接过程的四个阶段（接近、对齐、捕获、稳定）；基于ROS 2构建了软硬件栈，并在仿真及真实环境中进行了全面评估。

Result: 实现了低功耗、高可靠性的飞行器对接；通过定量指标（如编队误差、成功率、对接时间等）验证了系统的有效性，并提供了详细的失败模式统计信息。

Conclusion: 该平台为模块化空中协作和重复性中空作业提供了一个实用的测试和基准评估环境，验证了所提出的双无人机对接方法的可靠性。

Abstract: Reliable midair docking between small unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) is essential for modular aerial cooperation and manipulation, but it requires precise relative-pose control and repeatable platform under tight thrust and payload constraints. We present a dual-drone docking platform where two quadrotors operate in a leader-follower formation and dock using a lightweight modular frame with passive magnetic latching. A progress-aware mission supervisor manages phase transitions: approach, alignment, capture, and settle. This platform integrates a complete hardware-software stack (ROS 2 with Crazyflie/PX4 interfaces) and synchronized logging for benchmark evaluation. We evaluate the platform in simulation and real-world experiments using quantitative metrics such as formation error, baseline and yaw consistency, docking success rate, time-to-dock, and failure-mode statistics. The platform enables statistically grounded comparison of docking supervision and synchronization strategies and provides a practical testbed for modular aerial cooperation and repeatable midair aerial manipulation.

</details>


### [349] [3DVLA: Enhancing Vision-Language-Action Models via 3D Spatial and Instance Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29416)
*Zhongyu Xia,Yousen Tang,Bingqing Wei,Yongtao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对 VLA 模型缺乏 3D 理解的缺陷，3DVLA 通过无需额外标注的注入式设计，利用多视图一致性编码、实例感知模块和掩码预测分支，大幅提升了机器人操纵能力及对遮挡的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作 (VLA) 模型在机器人操作中缺乏 3D 场景理解能力，表现为 3D 空间定位弱、实例理解不足以及在遮挡下推理脆弱。同时，现有的 3D 感知方法难以直接集成到 VLA 中，且过分依赖昂贵的标注数据。

Method: 提出 3DVLA 框架，包含三个核心模块：1. 空间条件几何聚合（SCGA）以实现多视图一致的空间特征编码；2. 具有高层实例 Token 的 3D 实例评估模块；3. 基于掩码自监督学习的 3D 编码分支，增强遮挡环境下的鲁棒性。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Plus 和 RoboTwin 2.0 基准测试中，集成 3DVLA 的 VLA 模型性能取得了显著的提升，证明了该方法的有效性和“即插即用”的兼容性。

Conclusion: 3DVLA 作为一个即插即用的框架，有效弥补了现有 VLA 模型在 3D 空间理解上的不足，显著提升了机器人操作性能，并兼顾了与现有预训练模型的兼容性，无需昂贵的实例级标注。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models have achieved remarkable progress in robotic manipulation, yet they suffer from a critical limitation: a lack of 3D scene understanding. This deficiency manifests as three intertwined challenges: weak extraction of 3D spatial positions without enforcing multi-view consistency, inadequate 3D instance understanding, and fragile reasoning under occlusion. Although mature 3D perception methods exist, their direct integration into VLA pipelines is hindered by architectural incompatibility and by heavy reliance on costly instance-level annotations. To address the above challenges, we propose 3DVLA, a plug-and-play framework that injects robust 3D reasoning into pretrained VLAs without requiring extra manual labels or discarding VLM priors. Specifically, 3DVLA tackles the three challenges through: (1) pervasive 3D feature encoding with explicit multi-view consistency constraints across all modalities and a Spatially-Conditioned Geometry Aggregation method, (2) an instance estimation module with high-level instance tokens for 3D instance awareness, and (3) a masked self-supervised 3D encoding branch that retains its predictor for visual token completion to handle occlusions. We integrate 3DVLA with multiple VLA baselines and evaluate on LIBERO-Plus and RoboTwin 2.0. Results show consistent and significant gains in manipulation performance, validating both the effectiveness and plug-and-play compatibility of our approach.

</details>


### [350] [ElegantVLA: Learning When to Think for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29438)
*Ye Li,Huanan Liu,Kangye Ji,Yuan Meng,Jiajun Fan,Yuansong Wang,Shiyu Qin,Chenglei Wu,Shu-Tao Xia,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ElegantVLA 是一个插件式推理优化框架，通过模仿人类运动控制的资源分配逻辑，动态调度 VLA 模型的计算负载，在无需微调模型的前提下实现了显著的实时控制性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型计算成本高、控制频率低，且大多采用固定计算策略，忽略了具身控制中不同阶段对计算需求的不一致性。

Method: 提出 ElegantVLA 插件式阶段自适应推理框架，包含一个轻量级调度器，根据时间表征相似度、机器人运动线索及任务进展，对视觉编码器、大语言模型和动作头进行动态计算分配；采用五级视觉-语言计算模式（从全重计算到多步复用）和三级动作生成去噪模式。

Result: 在 GR00T 和 CogACT 上实现了最高 3.77 倍的加速，在六项真实世界任务中将控制频率从 13.8 Hz 提升至 26.3 Hz，同时计算量减少了 2.18 倍。

Conclusion: ElegantVLA 是一种通用的 VLA 加速框架，通过动态计算调度显著提升了推理效率和控制频率，且无需对基础模型进行修改或重新训练。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are a powerful paradigm for generalist robotic control. However, their high computational cost and limited control frequency hinder real-time robotic manipulation, especially when large vision-language backbones and iterative action heads run at every control step. Existing VLA acceleration methods often optimize individual components or rely on fixed acceleration rules, treating different control steps with largely fixed computation and overlooking the non-uniform reasoning demands of sequential embodied control. Inspired by human motor control, where cognitive and feedback resources concentrate on goal-sensitive stages, we argue that VLA models should learn when to invest full computation and when to reuse prior computation. We propose ElegantVLA, a plug-in phase-adaptive inference framework that accelerates VLA models through intra-model dynamic compute scheduling. ElegantVLA introduces a lightweight scheduler that observes temporal representation similarity, robot-motion cues, and episode progress to jointly allocate computation across the vision encoder, LLM, and action head. For perception-language reasoning, the scheduler selects a five-level Vision-LLM compute mode, from full recomputation to multi-step temporal reuse, based on visual-language representation stability. For action generation, it selects a three-level denoising mode, reusing intermediate denoising states during stable motion while preserving full refinement for goal-sensitive stages. By coordinating these decisions, ElegantVLA offers a general acceleration framework for modern VLA pipelines with explicit action-generation modules, without modifying or retraining the base model. Experiments on GR00T and CogACT achieve up to 2.55x and 3.77x speedup, and on six real-world GR00T tasks ElegantVLA cuts computation by 2.18x while raising control frequency from 13.8 Hz to 26.3 Hz.

</details>


### [351] [VLA-Pro: Cross-Task Procedural Memory Transfer for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29562)
*Shengyu Si,Yuanzhuo Lu,Ruimeng Yang,Ziyi Ye,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA-Pro 是一种增强 VLA 模型跨任务泛化能力的插件式框架，通过在训练时存储、推理时检索并动态融合 LoRA 适配器（作为程序性记忆），大幅提升了机器人面对未知任务的处理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在面对未见过的任务时，难以有效迁移不同物体、场景和动作模式中的经验，导致泛化能力受限。

Method: 提出 VLA-Pro 框架：在训练阶段将特定任务的 LoRA 适配器存储为参数化的“程序性记忆”；在推理阶段，根据当前多模态上下文检索最相关的记忆，并动态融合这些记忆以生成动作序列。

Result: 在 RoboTwin、RLBench 及真实机器人操作任务中表现优异：模拟环境中相对提升高达 207%，真实世界操作任务成功率从 5.8% 提升至 65.0%。

Conclusion: 通过程序性记忆的检索与动态自适应，VLA-Pro为跨任务泛化提供了一种有效的机制，能够在保持模型模块化和执行稳定性的同时，显著提升机器人对未知任务的处理能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action~(VLA) models have shown strong potential for general-purpose robotic manipulation, yet they still struggle to generalize to unseen tasks that necessitate transferring relevant experience across objects, scenes, and action patterns. This paper proposes VLA-Pro, a plug-and-play framework designed to enhance cross-task generalization by storing task-relevant procedural memories at training time and transferring these memories during inference. Specifically, VLA-Pro stores task-specific LoRA adapters as parameterized procedural memories during training. At inference time, VLA-Pro retrieves relevant procedural memories based on the current multi-modal context and dynamically fuses these memories for generating the current action chunk. Experiments on RoboTwin, RLBench, and real-world manipulation tasks show that VLA-Pro consistently improves cross-task generalization across multiple backbones, achieving up to a 207% relative improvement in simulation and increasing real-world success rate from 5.8% to 65.0%. These results suggest that procedural memory retrieval and adaptation provide an effective mechanism for transferring manipulation experience to novel tasks while preserving modularity and execution stability.

</details>


### [352] [VE2VF: Vision-Enabled to Vision-Free Distillation via Real-world Reinforcement Learning for Robust Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29564)
*Victor Kowalski,Chengxi Li,Dongheui Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从视觉引导到本体感觉（视觉缺失）的蒸馏强化学习框架，在无需模拟器训练或域随机化的前提下，实现了真实世界中高效、鲁棒的机器人操作学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉强化学习策略在接触丰富的机器人操作任务中容易过拟合训练环境，导致鲁棒性和迁移能力不足；且传统方法通常依赖大规模域随机化。

Method: 提出了一种人机协同（Human-in-the-loop）的强化学习框架，采用“教师-学生”蒸馏（Teacher-Student Distillation）策略；训练阶段使用视觉信息引导，测试/执行阶段转化为仅依赖位姿、扭转和力反馈（Proprioception）的视觉缺失策略（Vision-free student）。

Result: 在NIST装配基准测试中，50分钟训练后达到95%的整体成功率，并能鲁棒泛化至8个未见过的任务变种；优于现有的对比基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过教师-学生蒸馏方法（将视觉信息转化为仅依赖本体感觉的执行能力），能够实现在无需域随机化或数据增强的情况下，在真实世界环境中进行高效、鲁棒且具备强泛化能力的机器人操作学习。

Abstract: When using reinforcement learning (RL) for contact-rich robotic manipulation, vision can provide task-relevant information that accelerates learning beyond what proprioception alone can achieve. However, vision-enabled policies tend to overfit to the visual conditions seen during training, limiting their robustness and transferability. We present a human-in-the-loop RL framework that employs teacher-student distillation to achieve robust performance across multiple task variants, trained entirely in the real world without requiring domain randomization or data augmentation. A vision-enabled teacher distills its knowledge into a vision-free student that relies solely on pose, twist, and wrench sensing, combining fast training with strong task generalization. On the real-world NIST assembly benchmark board, our approach achieves 95\% overall success after approximately 50 minutes of training on 3 representative tasks, including robust generalization to 8 unseen task variants. Fine-tuning with distillation achieves full success on the most challenging task. We demonstrate that the resulting policies outperform baselines in both robustness and adaptability.

</details>


### [353] [Learning to Feel Materials from Multisensory Tactile Data via Interpretable Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29572)
*Li Zou,Yasemin Vardar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个多模态触觉计算框架，证明了结合压力、摩擦与热觉信息能显著提升材料感知准确度，并指出当前机器人触觉系统在热觉与顺应性传感方面存在严重欠缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类触觉感知机制尚不明确，制约了数字环境触觉集成及具备类人感知能力的机器人开发。研究旨在解析低级触觉信号与感知表征之间的关系。

Method: 提出了一个包含三个相互关联模型的计算框架：模型1将手指与表面的交互特征映射为心理物理感觉属性；模型2基于感知表征进行材料分类；模型3直接从触觉特征进行材料分类。框架整合了按压、静态接触和滑动交互的多模态触觉数据。

Result: 结合按压、静态接触和滑动交互信息显著提升了预测准确率；热觉信号在感知建模与材料分类中均具有极高的信息价值。

Conclusion: 热觉与压力感在触觉感知中至关重要，目前机器人感知系统亟需强化这些维度的能力，以实现更接近人类的触觉识别水平。

Abstract: Human tactile perception of materials relies on complex multisensory touch cues, yet the relationship between low-level tactile signals and perceptual representations remains poorly understood. This knowledge gap hinders the integration of touch in digital environments and the development of robots capable of human-like tactile perception. Here, we present an interpretable computational framework for modeling human material perception and recognition using multisensory touch data. Our framework comprises three interconnected models: Model 1 maps finger-surface interaction features to psychophysical sensory attributes, Model 2 classifies materials based on these perceptual representations, and Model 3 directly classifies materials from tactile features. The results showed that combining information from pressing, static contact, and sliding interactions improves prediction accuracy, and that thermal cues are particularly informative for both perceptual modeling and material classification. These findings highlight the importance of thermal and compliance cues, which remain underrepresented in current robotic fingers and haptic displays. Incorporating such cues may enhance artificial systems' ability to approximate human material perception and guide the design of more perceptually grounded haptic interfaces.

</details>


### [354] [How to Relieve Distribution Shifts in Semantic Segmentation for Off-Road Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29599)
*Ji-Hoon Hwang,Daeyoung Kim,Hyung-Suk Yoon,Dong-Wook Kim,Seung-Woo Seo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对越野环境语义分割面临的分布偏移难题，提出ST-Seg框架，通过风格扩展增加域多样性并结合纹理正则化保持特征稳定性，提升了复杂越野场景下的导航精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越野场景中存在的源-目标域差异及复杂地形导致的传感器噪声（分布偏移），会导致现有语义分割模型在导航任务中出现预测错误及失效。

Method: 提出了一种名为ST-Seg的框架，包含两个核心模块：1. 风格扩展（SE）：通过生成多样化的真实风格来扩大源域的分布范围；2. 纹理正则化（TR）：利用深层纹理流形来稳定受风格增强影响的局部纹理表示。

Result: 在多种分布偏移的目标域实验中，ST-Seg的表现显著优于现有方法，证明了其在增强语义分割对于越野导航实际应用鲁棒性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: ST-Seg通过风格扩展（SE）和纹理正则化（TR）有效解决了越野场景下的域偏移问题，显著提升了语义分割模型在复杂且多变环境中的鲁棒性与导航可靠性。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation is crucial for autonomous navigation in off-road environments, enabling precise classification of surroundings to identify traversable regions. However, distinctive factors inherent to off-road conditions, such as source-target domain discrepancies and sensor corruption from rough terrain, can result in distribution shifts that alter the data differently from the trained conditions. This often leads to inaccurate semantic label predictions and subsequent failures in navigation tasks. To address this, we propose ST-Seg, a novel framework that expands the source distribution through style expansion (SE) and texture regularization (TR). Unlike prior methods that implicitly apply generalization within a fixed source distribution, ST-Seg offers an intuitive approach for distribution shift. Specifically, SE broadens domain coverage by generating diverse realistic styles, augmenting the limited style information of the source domain. TR stabilizes local texture representation affected by style-augmented learning through a deep texture manifold. Experiments across various distribution-shifted target domains demonstrate the effectiveness of ST-Seg, with substantial improvements over existing methods. These results highlight the robustness of ST-Seg, enhancing the real-world applicability of semantic segmentation for off-road navigation.

</details>


### [355] [VLAConf: Calibrated Task-Success Confidence for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29605)
*Dehao Huang,Aoxiang Gu,Chengjie Zhang,Bolin Zou,Wenlong Dong,Zilang Cen,Yue Wang,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLAConf 是一种高效的机器人执行任务信心评估框架，它通过轻量化模型在单次前向传播中识别潜在的动作失败（异常），解决了现有方法计算成本高且不适用于连续动作空间的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 VLA 模型信心评估方法依赖于资源消耗大的集成采样或仅限于离散动作空间，导致推理效率低下且难以推广至连续动作空间，无法满足机器人开放世界任务中实时高效的风险规避需求。

Method: 引入了一种单类判别式信心评估框架，利用冻结的预训练 VLA 内部表示，通过轻量级信心头在单次前向传递中直接估计步骤级异常分数，并结合步骤条件建模对滚动相位信息进行编码。

Result: 在 LIBERO 基准测试中，VLAConf 在置信度信号的质量上显著优于现有基准，且在推理效率上有巨大提升，并在真实机器人实验中验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: VLAConf 提出了一个高效、通用的单类判别式信心评估框架，通过利用冻结的 VLA 内部表示和轻量级信心头，实现了在无需重复采样的情况下进行准确的步骤级异常评分，提升了机器人操作中的风险感知能力。

Abstract: Confidence estimation for Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models is essential for robots to perform manipulation tasks in the open world, providing crucial signals for risk-sensitive decision-making and failure anticipation. Existing confidence estimation methods typically rely on ensemble-based paradigms or action-token probabilities to predict the likelihood of task success. However, they still encounter challenges in computational efficiency and cross-architecture generalizability. These methods usually require repeated sampling, leading to inference inefficiency, and are restricted to VLA models with discrete action outputs, making them difficult to apply to continuous action spaces. To address this issue, we propose VLAConf, a one-class discriminative confidence framework. By leveraging frozen pretrained VLA internal representations, VLAConf directly estimates step-wise anomaly scores in a single forward pass using a lightweight confidence head, thereby eliminating the overhead of exhaustive resampling. We additionally use step-conditioned modeling to encode rollout-phase information along the manipulation trajectory. Experiments on the LIBERO benchmark demonstrate that VLAConf significantly improves the quality of the confidence signal constructed for post-hoc calibration, outperforming existing baselines by a large margin in inference efficiency. The effectiveness of VLAConf is further validated in real-robot experiments. To access the source code and supplementary videos, visit https://sites.google.com/view/vlaconf.

</details>


### [356] [EXACT-MPPI: Exact Signed-Distance Navigation for Arbitrary-Footprint Robots from Point Clouds via Path Integral Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29663)
*Chen Peng,Zhikang Ge,Wenwu Lu,Haiming Gao,Stavros Vougioukas,Peng Wei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EXACT-MPPI 是一种无需训练的局部导航框架，通过在 GPU 上直接对非凸机器人足迹进行精确距离评估，赋予了机器人感知真实物理几何形状的能力，从而在复杂动态环境中实现了更优的避障与路径规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的地面机器人局部规划器常将复杂的机器人足迹简化为凸包或膨胀圆，或依赖占用栅格地图，导致在狭窄空间中丢失许多可行解，且无法处理异形附件带来的几何复杂性。

Method: 提出一种名为 EXACT-MPPI 的框架，弃用传统的中间地图表示（如占用网格），将点云数据直接映射到运动指令。核心在于嵌入一种解析型的、精确的符号距离评估器，并利用 JAX 实现 GPU 并行计算。机器人足迹被建模为多边形（支持非凸），避免了凸分解或膨胀处理。

Result: 实验表明，EXACT-MPPI 在计算速度上优于基于学习的基线方法，在凸包规划器失效的场景中保持了运动可行性，且能够鲁棒地应对静态及动态障碍物，并可在不同运动学平台（差速、阿克曼、全向等）间直接部署，无需重新训练。

Conclusion: EXACT-MPPI 提供了一种无需训练的本地导航通用方案，通过将精确的物理轮廓几何计算与 MPPI 控制相结合，有效解决了复杂形状机器人（非凸/异形）在复杂环境中导航受限的问题，具备设备泛化能力和实时性。

Abstract: Ground robots often carry payloads, implements, or other attachments that turn their effective footprint into complex, non-convex shapes. Navigating safely through clutter then requires reasoning about this true geometry, yet most local planners simplify it with convex or inflated proxies and rasterize sensor data into occupancy grids or distance fields. Both choices eliminate feasible motions when clearance is comparable to the footprint geometry. We present EXACT-MPPI, a training-free local navigation framework that maps local point-cloud observations and sparse guidance directly to motion commands, without any intermediate map representation. The framework embeds an analytic, exact signed-distance evaluator into a Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) controller. The footprint is represented as a simple polygon for general convex or concave planar shapes, with a rectangle-cover specialization for faster evaluation of rectilinear footprints, enabling footprint-aware collision costs without convex decomposition, inflation, or learned encoders. During each MPPI rollout, observed obstacle points are transformed into the predicted body frame and evaluated against the footprint. All operations are batched in JAX, leveraging GPU parallelism for real-time receding-horizon control. Experiments show that EXACT-MPPI accelerates batched distance evaluation over a learned point-to-robot baseline, preserves feasible motion where convex-footprint planners fail, and remains robust under dense static and moving obstacles. The same framework deploys on differential-drive, Ackermann, omnidirectional, and hybrid-mode platforms by changing only the footprint description and motion model without per-platform training. Pairing exact footprint geometry with sampling-based predictive control thus offers a practical, training-free path to footprint-aware local navigation across diverse robots.

</details>


### [357] [PhAIL: A Real-Robot VLA Benchmark and Distributional Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29710)
*Sergey Arkhangelskiy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了PhAIL基准，通过引入基于时间分布的统计评估方法（而非简单的成功率），解决了机器人模型评估中统计效力不足和对比不明确的问题，并揭示了当前VLA模型与人类操作效率的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前真实世界机器人（VLA）模型评估方法普遍存在二元成功率统计效力不足、缺乏置信区间的问题，使得在小样本（N≤25）评价下难以区分不同算法的优劣。

Method: 引入PhAIL（实体AI排行榜）基准测试，采用基于“成功时间累积分布函数（CDF）”的评估范式；使用“人类相对吞吐量（HRT）”作为标准化评分指标；通过Kolmogorov-Smirnov (KS) 参数检验进行统计意义上的显著性对比，并提供Bootstrap置信区间。

Result: 在4个公开VLA模型上进行测试，证明了新的统计评估方法能成功区分二元评价法无法分辨的性能差异（如GR00T与ACT的对比）；测评显示现有最强VLA模型的执行效率仅为人类参考水平的1/7左右。

Conclusion: 传统的二元成功率评估在机器人学习中存在样本量不足、无置信区间等问题，导致模型间的细微性能差异难以分辨。通过采用基于时间分布的评估方法（CDF）及HRT指标，能显著提高评估的统计效力。目前的顶尖VLA模型在执行效率上仍大幅落后于人类。

Abstract: Real-world evaluation of vision-language-action (VLA) policies still rests on binary success rate at a fixed timeout with $N \le 25$ rollouts per condition, almost always without confidence intervals or paired statistical comparison; these cohort sizes struggle to resolve close comparisons reliably. We introduce PhAIL (Physical AI Leaderboard, https://phail.ai), an open real-robot benchmark on a Franka FR3 (dataset, per-rollout artifacts, and end-to-end reference implementation) of a distributional evaluation methodology: the time-to-success cumulative distribution function (CDF) as the evaluation primitive, with two separated jobs. The first is scoring via Human-Relative Throughput (HRT), a dimensionless scalar with bootstrap confidence intervals, anchored to same-fixture human teleoperation. The second is a significance test (Kolmogorov-Smirnov, computed per-object and macro-averaged across objects). On four publicly-available VLAs, the macro-averaged KS test resolves two close comparisons (GR00T vs. ACT, OpenPI vs. ACT) at $N \le 30$ rollouts per (model, object) cell where binary-threshold metrics do not; the closest pair (OpenPI vs. GR00T) remains unresolved within our budget. The best evaluated VLA is $\sim 7\times$ slower per operation (RMST ratio) than the human reference.

</details>


### [358] [MARS Policy: Multimodality Only When It Matters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29766)
*Jindou Jia,Tuo An,Yuxuan Hu,Gen Li,Jingliang Li,Bohan Hou,Xiangyu Chen,Jiaqi Bai,Bofan Lyu,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对生成式机器人策略在大规模任务中推理慢、训练难的问题，MARS通过自适应控制噪声引入时机，在保持多模态能力的同时大幅提升了推理效率和模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式机器人策略严重依赖随机噪声和复杂的去噪过程，导致训练成本高且推理效率低下，且未考虑到并非所有任务阶段都需要行为多样性。

Method: 提出“模态自适应机器人采样”（MARS）策略，该方法根据任务需求动态调整随机性，仅在需要多模态表达时引入噪声，在单模态阶段使用确定性学习。

Result: 在8个仿真和4个真实机器人任务中表现出色，真实世界测试中成功率提升了16.67%，推理延迟降低了83.20%。在近确定性任务中，其训练效率甚至优于传统确定性策略。

Conclusion: MARS策略通过在必要时动态激活多模态生成，在多模态生成能力与训练/推理效率之间取得了平衡，证明了选择性随机性在机器人操作任务中的优越性。

Abstract: Imitation learning has become a cornerstone for solving complex robotic manipulation tasks. In particular, multimodality, which enables robots to capture diverse yet valid behavioral patterns, has driven the rapid emergence of generative policies as a dominant paradigm in robot learning. However, achieving such multimodality typically relies on stochastic noise initialization and iterative denoising procedures, resulting in substantial training complexity and low inference efficiency. Meanwhile, not all phases of a robotic task inherently require behavioral diversity. Motivated by this insight, we propose the Modality-Adaptive Robot Sampling (MARS) policy, which adaptively invokes tailored stochasticity only when it is truly beneficial, while reverting to an efficient deterministic learning during single-modal phases. In other words, the proper amount of noise is injected only at the proper time. By selectively activating multimodal generation, MARS policy bridges the gap between the multimodal capability of generative policies and the superior training and inference efficiency of deterministic models. Empirical studies across 8 simulated and 4 real-world tasks demonstrate that MARS exhibits robust multimodal expressivity and high efficiency, with a 16.67% success rate improvement and an 83.20% inference latency reduction in real-world tests. Counterintuitively, MARS also outpaces deterministic policies in training efficiency on near-deterministic tasks by more effectively modeling nuanced action diversity.

</details>


### [359] [Joint Angle Estimation with Customized Wristband Based on Online Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29771)
*Shuo Wang,Xiaobin Chen,Xiaoming Tao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对软性可穿戴传感器在多场景下易受数据漂移影响的问题，本文提出了一种基于在线增量学习的腕关节角度估计系统，通过双阶段训练与自适应算法，实现了在不同佩戴条件下约15度误差的准确估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的软性可穿戴传感器研究方案往往过于简单，难以适应不同的佩戴配置（如左右手互换、位置偏移）及个体差异，导致模型泛化能力差、数据漂移严重。

Method: 提出了一种基于在线增量学习的腕关节角度估计系统，采用两阶段法：第一阶段利用IMU实时数据作为基准（Ground Truth）进行模型在线自适应更新；第二阶段闭环使用更新后的模型，仅依赖腕带传感器数据进行角度推断。

Result: 实验结果表明，该系统在多种场景下适应性良好，在应对复杂佩戴变化时，腕关节轨迹估计的平均误差约为15度。

Conclusion: 该研究通过在线增量学习方法成功解决了软性可穿戴传感器在不同使用场景下的鲁棒性问题，实现了即便在佩戴位置偏移、左右手差异或个体差异下仍能保持高精度的腕关节角度估计。

Abstract: Intelligent wearable technology plays an increasingly important role in human-computer interaction, motion, and health monitoring. To ensure comfort and practicality of use, one common form for motion monitoring is to utilize soft wearable sensors. However, many research applications regarding wearable sensors are simplistic and difficult to adapt to different situations. This study proposes a system for estimating the angle of the wrist joint using a customized wristband based on an online incremental learning approach. It is a two-stage estimation method: the first stage updates the model based on the wearer's wrist movement characteristics using online learning, integrating real-time data from an IMU as ground truth. The second stage utilizes the updated model for estimation of wrist joint angle solely with the wristband. In other words, model training is completed during data acquisition, allowing the trained model to be used for subsequent angle estimation. This method offers advantages in adapting to data drift caused by variations in different testing configurations, such as the left and right wrists of the same subject, deviations in the wearing position on the same wrist, and even differences among various subjects. The results indicate that the sensors exhibit good performance under strain variations, and the wrist joint trajectory estimation of the proposed system has an approximate error of 15 degree in different scenarios.

</details>


### [360] [LLM-Guided Future Hypotheses for Horizon-Aware Exploration in Multi-Step Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29864)
*Mohammad Khoshnazar,Andrew Melnik,Michael Beetz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FEC框架，通过生成短时任务一致性视频作为先验条件，显著提升了机器人多步操作任务下的策略学习与适应效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步机器人操作中环境演变具有不确定性，导致探索与策略适应困难，旨在探索短时程且任务一致的未来视频能否作为提升机器人控制能力的有效先验。

Method: 提出“未来经验条件化（FEC）”框架，通过三阶段生成未来视频：1) 利用LLM基于任务本体论推理；2) 生成数字孪生物体运动轨迹；3) 通过无遮罩视频扩散模型合成机器人一致性未来视频。该视频作为潜在条件输入闭环策略模型（BC/BC+RL）。

Result: 在RoboCasa和CALVIN基准测试中，基于生成的未来视频（GenFuture）不仅优于无未来信息（NoFuture），且能显著加速BC+RL的收敛并提高最终性能；验证了未来信息的质量与性能提升呈正相关性。

Conclusion: 短时程的未来场景视频可作为一种结构化先验，有效地引导机器人进行策略探索与适应；即使未来预测存在一定偏差，该方法仍能提升控制性能，且结合BC+RL能达到最优效果。

Abstract: Multi-step robot manipulation requires acting under uncertainty about how the scene will evolve, making exploration and policy adaptation challenging. We study whether short-horizon, task-consistent future videos can provide useful structured priors for control and reinforcement-learning fine-tuning. We formalize this idea through Future-Experience Conditioning (FEC), a simple interface that conditions closed-loop policies on a latent representation of a short future video. In our simulation setup, future clips are generated in three stages, an LLM reasoner operating over a task ontology initialized from the current scene state, a robot-free digital-twin rollout of the intended object motion, and a mask-free video diffusion model that synthesizes a robot-consistent future clip without requiring segmentation at inference. We instantiate this future-conditioning interface primarily with BC and BC+RL, and compare against a future-conditioned Streaming Flow Policy (SFP) baseline on RoboCasa and CALVIN under NoFuture, GTFuture, GenFuture, and WrongFuture. Generated futures improve performance over no-future conditioning, while mismatched futures degrade it, and our BC+RL instantiation achieves the strongest overall results. An average BC+RL learning-curve analysis across 8 CALVIN tasks further shows that GTFuture improves fastest, GenFuture improves earlier and to a higher level than NoFuture, and WrongFuture remains at zero throughout training. These results suggest that short-horizon future videos can serve as useful structured priors for exploration and policy adaptation under imperfect future predictions. https://enact2026.github.io/

</details>


### [361] [Fisher-Preserving Guidance: Training-Free Manifold Constraints for Safe Diffusion Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29937)
*Hao Ren,Zetong Bi,Yiming Zeng,Le Zheng,Zhi Li,Zhaoliang Wan,Lu Qi,Hui Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种无需训练的扩散模型推理方法（Fisher Preserving Guidance），通过低秩雅可比分析防止路径规划偏离训练分布，实现了更鲁棒、实时的导航操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在视觉导航路径规划中虽然有效，但标准的采样和推理时引导容易导致执行动作偏离训练流形（Fisher drift），从而产生不可靠或低效的轨迹。

Method: 1. 提出 Fisher Preserving Guidance with Outer Product Span Projection：通过低秩雅可比矩阵分解，在优化任务目标的同时限制偏离训练数据流形；2. 引入 Truncated Fisher Denoising Sensitivity 作为不确定性度量；3. 多样本动作融合机制。

Result: 在 Maze2D、PushT 及仿真与真实机器人视觉导航任务中，该方法在不增加训练成本的前提下，显著优于现有的扩散策略（Diffusion Policy）基线模型。

Conclusion: Fisher Preserving Guidance 是一种无需额外训练的推理优化方法，能够有效解决扩散模型在导航任务中偏离分布的问题，显著提升动作生成的稳定性和效率。

Abstract: Diffusion models are effective for waypoint prediction in visual navigation, but standard sampling and test time guidance can produce unreliable or inefficient trajectories when updates drift off the training manifold. We propose Fisher Preserving Guidance with Outer Product Span Projection, a training-free inference method that avoids large Fisher drift associated with off-distribution actions while optimizing a task objective. Our method computes the Fisher-preserving update via a low-rank Jacobian factorization, requiring only a single backward pass per step and enabling real-time use. We further introduce Truncated Fisher Denoising Sensitivity as an uncertainty signal and use it for robust multi-sample action blending. Experiments on toy and realistic navigation benchmarks, including Maze2D with TSDF-based guidance, PushT with official Diffusion Policy weights, and visual navigation in simulation and on real robots, demonstrate consistent improvements in performance over strong diffusion-policy baselines without additional training.

</details>


### [362] [Replicable Simulation-Based Robot Validation through Provenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29973)
*Argentina Ortega,Samuel Wiest,Frederik Pasch,Nico Hochgeschwender*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出通过在机器人仿真测试流程中集成数据源追踪（Provenance）和FAIR数据原则，解决实验结果可复现性差的问题，并给出了具体的实施框架与建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的机器人行为仿真测试缺乏透明度，导致测试结果难以复现。解决这一问题的关键在于完善数据源追踪（Provenance）并遵循FAIR原则（可发现、可访问、可互操作、可重用），确保从测试设计到产出数据的端到端证据可追溯。

Method: 作者通过扩展一个现有的机器人仿真测试框架，引入了数据源追踪记录机制和元数据采集模块；利用该改进后的框架生成了一份具有结构化数据源和符合FAIR标准元数据的移动机器人导航数据集，并通过该实践过程总结了实施过程中的挑战与建议。

Result: 成功地为移动机器人导航数据集构建了结构化的数据源追踪和FAIR元数据；识别了实施过程中的实际障碍（如词汇对齐、属性选择、领域标准采用），并总结出一套可操作的实施建议。

Conclusion: 在机器人仿真测试中集成数据源追踪（Data Provenance）和FAIR原则是实现测试可复现性的关键。作者强调应将元数据采集内置于测试工作流中，而非事后补充，并为在复杂的机器人验证流程中实施这些机制提供了实践指南和建议。

Abstract: Robot behavior is often validated through simulation-based testing, yet the replicability of such campaigns depends critically on transparent documentation of how tests are configured, executed, and post-processed. We argue that data provenance, coupled with the FAIR principles (findability, accessibility, interoperability, and reusability), addresses this gap by explicitly tracking links between artifacts and by attaching machine-readable metadata about file origins and key design decisions. Moreover, provenance and metadata cannot be treated as an afterthought confined to final datasets; they must be integrated into the testing processes that generate those datasets so that evidence can be reconstructed end-to-end. We demonstrate this by augmenting an existing simulation-based testing framework with provenance tracking and metadata collection mechanisms, and by using these extensions to enrich a mobile robot navigation dataset with structured provenance and FAIR-aligned metadata. Finally, we discuss obstacles encountered in this integration -- such as vocabulary alignment, attribute selection, and adoption of domain standards -- and provide actionable recommendations for implementing provenance-centric, FAIR metadata in robotics validation workflows.

</details>


### [363] [BORA: Bridging Offline Reinforcement Learning and Online Residual Adaptation for Real-World Dexterous VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30226)
*Zhongxi Chen,Yifan Han,Yanming Shao,Huanming Liu,Congsheng Xu,Xiaoyu Chen,Yao Mu,Wenzhao Lian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BORA 是一种针对 VLA 灵巧操作的离线转在线强化学习框架，通过动作条件价值评估与轻量级人机回路残差适配，显著提升了机器人真实环境下的操作成功率与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型在处理机器人高维灵巧操作时存在控制难度大、执行误差累积的问题；传统的强化学习（RL）微调则面临样本效率低、探索风险高及时间不一致性等挑战。

Method: 1. 离线阶段：构建引入 VLM 认知 Token 与动作块的 Critic，实现动作条件的价值评估；2. 在线阶段：冻结 VLA 基座，采用轻量级、人机回路（HiL）的块级残差适配机制，利用介入驱动奖励进行策略矫正与适应。

Result: 在五项复杂的真实世界灵巧操作任务中，较纯模仿学习与传统解耦 RL 基线，平均成功率提升了 33%，在未见物体泛化能力上提升了 43%。

Conclusion: BORA 通过结合离线价值引导与在线残差校准，有效解决了 VLA 模型在机器人复杂灵巧操作中面临的样本效率低、执行误差累积及硬件风险问题，为 real-world 机器人控制提供了一种稳定且高效的微调范式。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising paradigm for grounding visual-language understanding into real-world robotic manipulation. However, dexterous manipulation remains challenging for VLA policies due to high-dimensional hand control and compounding execution errors, which makes real-world RL post-training essential for bridging the gap between visually grounded action generation and physically reliable dexterous execution. However, high-dimensional dexterous exploration often triggers temporal inconsistency, sample inefficiency and hardware risks in the real world. To address these challenges, we propose BORA, an offline-to-online RL post-training framework designed for real-world dexterous VLA models. In the offline phase, BORA constructs a critic that takes both the VLM's cognition tokens and action chunks as inputs. This design enables action-conditioned value guidance, allowing the critic to evaluate dexterous hand motions beyond visual context alone. During the subsequent online phase, BORA freezes the VLA base and introduces a lightweight, Human-in-the-Loop (HiL) chunk-wise residual adaptation mechanism to mitigate real-world execution errors and further correct the offline-learned intents within the actual physical environment. By inheriting the offline critic and employing intervention-driven rewards, BORA effectively corrects execution discrepancies and adapts to real-world physical variances while preserving the pretrained policy as a stable prior. Extensive evaluations across five complex real-world dexterous tasks demonstrate that BORA significantly outperforms pure imitation learning and traditional decoupled RL baselines, achieving a 33% absolute increase in average success rate under standard settings and up to a 43% improvement in unseen object generalization.

</details>


### [364] [Qwen-VLA: Unifying Vision-Language-Action Modeling across Tasks, Environments, and Robot Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30280)
*Qiuyue Wang,Mingsheng Li,Jian Guan,Jinhui Ye,Sicheng Xie,Yitao Liu,Junhao Chen,Zhixuan Liang,Jie Zhang,Xintong Hu,Xuhong Huang,Pei Lin,Junyang Lin,Dayiheng Liu,Shuai Bai,Jingren Zhou,Jiazhao Zhang,Haoqi Yuan,Gengze Zhou,Hang Yin,Ye Wang,Yiyang Huang,Zixing Lei,Wujian Peng,Delin Chen,Yingming Zheng,Jingyang Fan,Xianwei Zhuang,Xin Zhou,Haoyang Li,Anzhe Chen,Tong Zhang,Xuejing Liu,Yuchong Sun,Ruizhe Chen,Zhaohai Li,Chenxu Lü,Zhibo Yang,Tao Yu,Xionghui Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Qwen-VLA是一个统一的具身基础模型，通过视觉-语言-动作建模和多源协同预训练，实现了跨任务与跨硬件平台的通用具身决策与控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当前具身智能领域任务单一、模型碎片化、跨平台泛化能力差的问题，旨在通过单一视觉-语言-动作模型实现多种复杂具身决策任务的统一。

Method: 1. 架构：基于Qwen视觉-语言模型架构，引入基于DiT（Diffusion Transformer）的动作解码器；2. 数据：采用大规模联合预训练，涵盖机械臂操作、人类视觉演示、仿真数据及导航数据；3. 机制：设计了“具身感知提示调节”（Embodiment-aware Prompt Conditioning），通过文本描述适配不同机器人硬件与控制约定。

Result: Qwen-VLA在LIBERO、Simpler-WidowX、RoboTwin、R2R/RxR导航以及真实环境ALOHA机械臂实验中均取得了优异表现，特别是在面对未知环境配置、光照及机器人形态变化时表现出良好的零样本与泛化性能。

Conclusion: Qwen-VLA证明了通过单一视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型统一多种具身任务（操作、导航、轨迹预测）的可行性，展现了跨平台、跨任务的强泛化能力，为具身智能通用模型的开发提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Embodied intelligence is often studied through specialized models for individual tasks such as manipulation or navigation, resulting in fragmented capabilities and limited generalization across tasks, environments, and robot embodiments. In this work, we study whether heterogeneous embodied decision-making problems can be unified within a single vision-language-action model. We present Qwen-VLA, a unified embodied foundation model that extends Qwen's vision-language modeling stack from perception, understanding, and reasoning to continuous action and trajectory generation through a DiT-based action decoder. Qwen-VLA is trained with a large-scale joint pretraining recipe over diverse data sources, including robotics manipulation trajectories, human egocentric demonstrations, synthetic simulation data, vision-and-language navigation data, trajectory-centric supervision, and auxiliary vision-language data. To support multiple robot platforms, we introduce embodiment-aware prompt conditioning, where robot-specific textual descriptions specify the current embodiment and control convention. We further cast manipulation, navigation, and trajectory prediction into a unified action-and-trajectory prediction framework, enabling transferable visual grounding, spatial reasoning, and continuous action generation across robot morphologies, task families, and environments. Experiments on manipulation, navigation, and trajectory-centric benchmarks show consistent multi-task performance and out-of-distribution generalization under variations in scene layout, background, lighting, object configuration, and robot embodiment. Qwen-VLA-Instruct achieves 97.9% on LIBERO, 73.7% on Simpler-WidowX, 86.1%/87.2% on RoboTwin-Easy/Hard, 69.0% OSR on R2R, 59.6% SR on RxR, 76.9% average OOD success in real-world ALOHA experiments, and 26.6% zero-shot success on DOMINO dynamic manipulation.

</details>


### [365] [A Heterogeneous Architecture for Robot RL Beyond GPU-Dominant Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30313)
*Yufei Jia,Zhanxiang Cao,Mingrui Yu,Heng Zhang,Shenyu Chen,Dixuan Jiang,Meng Li,Xiaofan Li,Yiyang Liu,Junzhe Wu,Zheng Li,XiLin Fang,Tingyu Cui,Shengcheng Fu,Haoyang Li,Anqi Wang,Zifan Wang,Dongjie Zhu,Chenyu Cao,Zhenbiao Huang,Ziang Zheng,Jie Lu,Xin Ma,Zhengyang Wei,Xiang Zhao,Tianyue Zhan,Ye He,Yuxiang Chen,Yizhou Jiang,Yue Li,Haizhou Ge,Yuhang Dong,Fan Jia,Ziheng Zhang,Meng Zhang,Xiwa Deng,Zhixing Chen,Hanyang Shao,Chenxin Dong,Yixuan Li,Yizhi Chen,Bokui Chen,Kaifeng Zhang,Hanqing Cui,Yusen Qin,Ruqi Huang,Lei Han,Tiancai Wang,Xiang Li,Yue Gao,Guyue Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UniLab提出了一种新型异构机器人强化学习框架，证明了通过优化CPU仿真与GPU学习之间的数据同步，不仅能实现显著的性能提升，还打破了对特定GPU仿真平台的依赖，实现了跨硬件平台的训练部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人强化学习主要依赖“GPU驻留式仿真”范式，但这一假设并非总是最优。作者旨在探索是否可以通过优化仿真吞吐量、策略学习和运行时同步的端到端循环，在非GPU仿真路径上实现同等甚至更卓越的训练效率。

Method: 提出了UniLab，一种异构计算架构。它解耦了在CPU上进行的批量物理仿真（利用MuJoCoUni和MotrixSim）与在GPU上进行的策略学习，并通过统一的运行时（Runtime）处理数据移动、缓冲和同步，以构建高效的端到端训练循环。

Result: 在相同的硬件配置下，UniLab将端到端训练效率提升了3至10倍，同时降低了对NVIDIA CUDA栈的依赖，并实现了对Apple macOS、AMD ROCm及Intel XPU等跨平台硬件的支持。

Conclusion: GPU物理仿真并非实现高效强化学习训练的唯一路径；异构CPU-GPU体系结构通过优化的数据流和同步机制，可以在机器人强化学习中实现比全GPU架构更高的训练效率，同时具备更好的硬件兼容性。

Abstract: Simulation-based RL for contemporary robot control is increasingly organized around GPU-resident simulation: physics, rollout collection, and learning are placed on a single GPU-centric execution path. This paradigm has greatly improved training speed, but it has also encouraged a default assumption that efficient training requires physics to reside on the GPU. We revisit this assumption. Our view is that, in simulation-dominated robot control, the essential question is not which processor runs physics, but whether simulation throughput, policy learning, and runtime synchronization form an efficient end-to-end loop. We present UniLab, a heterogeneous CPU-simulation / GPU-learning architecture that decouples CPU-parallel simulation from GPU policy updates through a unified runtime for data movement, buffering, and synchronization. UniLab is implemented as a complete and extensible training system using MuJoCoUni and MotrixSim CPU-batched physics backends, supporting PPO, SAC, FlashSAC, TD3, and APPO. On representative simulation-based robot control tasks, UniLab improves end-to-end training efficiency by 3--10$\times$ under the same hardware configuration, while reducing dependence on the NVIDIA CUDA-based software stack and supporting cross-platform execution on the Apple macOS platform and the AMD ROCm and Intel XPU accelerator backends. These results show that GPU simulation is an effective path to efficient training, but not a necessary one, broadening the practical system choices available for robot RL training. Project page: https://github.com/unilabsim/UniLab.

</details>


### [366] [RoboWits: Unexpected Challenges for Robotic Creative Problem Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30326)
*Chunru Lin,Hongxin Zhang,Fenghao Yu,Zhehuan Chen,Thomas L. Griffiths,Yejin Choi,David Held,Chuang Gan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboWits是一个新型双臂机器人认知推理基准，利用多智能体自动化流水线生成多样化突变任务，旨在揭示当前模型在应对复杂、未知真实世界挑战时的逻辑推理与适应性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基准测试过度关注技能执行，缺乏对复杂认知推理、创造性工具使用及应对突发状况能力的评估，限制了在真实动态环境下的机器人应用。

Method: 开发了RoboWits双臂机器人基准测试集，并引入了一个多智能体合作框架的自动化任务生成流水线，包含任务生成与验证、指标设计、场景布置及任务突变（Task Mutation）模块。

Result: 构建了包含30个种子任务和208个涵盖几何、材料和装配推理的突变任务；测试显示，主流VLA在种子任务上表现尚可，但在突变任务中性能显著下降，暴露了模型在推理和环境适应性上的缺陷。

Conclusion: 虽然视觉语言动作模型（VLA）在基础任务上有所表现，但面对未知的环境变化和多步逻辑推理任务时表现脆弱，现有模型缺乏真正的认知推理与策略适应能力，RoboWits为评估并提升机器人的通用具身智能水平提供了关键基准。

Abstract: The ability to reason, adapt, and creatively solve problems under unexpected challenges is essential for robots operating in real-world environments. However, current robotic benchmarks primarily emphasize skill-level execution and provide limited insight into such cognitive reasoning capabilities. We introduce RoboWits, a bi-manual robotic benchmark designed to systematically evaluate cognitive reasoning, creative tool use, and robustness to unexpected conditions. To enable scalable construction of high-quality reasoning-centric unexpected scenarios, we propose an automated task generation pipeline formulated as a multi-agent cooperative framework, comprising agents for seed task generation and verification, metric generation, scene generation, and task mutation. Using the pipeline, we curated 30 diverse seed tasks and 208 tasks with mutations and graded difficulty across geometry, material, and assembly-based reasoning. We benchmark popular robot policies, pre-trained VLAs, and oracle-state planners. Our results reveal a significant performance gap: while pre-trained VLAs exhibit preliminary success on seed tasks after single-task fine-tuning, they struggle to perform on mutated tasks, implying their brittleness in manipulation tasks requiring reasoning, strategy adaptation, and robustness to deceptive or constrained environments. Project page is available at https://umass-embodied-agi.github.io/RoboWits.

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

### [367] [RTP-LLM: High-Performance Alibaba LLM Inference Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29639)
*Boyu Tan,Jiarui Guo,Zongwei Lv,Hanbo Sun,Tong Yang,Kan Liu,Xinfei Shi,Zetao Hu,Yaxin Yu,Chi Zhang,Jianning Zhang,Xi Yang,Wei Zhang,Bo Cai,Silu Zhou,Xiyu Wang,Na He,Yinghao Yu,Wending Bao,Guiyang Huang,Yuxing Yuan,Juncheng Yin,Nan Wang,Lin Yang,Zechao Zhang,Lu Chen,Guoding Li,Tao Lan,Lin Qu*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: RTP-LLM 是阿里巴巴研发的一款高性能、生产级 LLM 推理引擎，通过架构解耦、高效缓存和并行策略等集成优化，解决了大规模部署中的瓶颈，性能大幅超越现有通用框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模语言模型（LLM）在工业级部署中面临严重的性能挑战，如模型加载缓慢、推理延迟高及资源利用率不足，难以支撑亿级用户的并发访问需求。

Method: 采用集成化设计架构，包括：1. 基于文件顺序驱动的 I/O 及并行 I/O 通信重叠技术；2. Prefill-Decode Disaggregation 架构（计算密集型预填充与内存密集型解码解耦）；3. 分层多级 KV Cache 管理；4. 支持多种算法的模块化推测解码；5. 自适应 KV Cache 量化与多模态解耦处理。

Result: 相比 vLLM 和 SGLang，RTP-LLM 在生产环境中实现了 4.7x-6.3x 的模型加载加速，TTFT P95 延迟降低 35-37%，KV Cache 复用率提升 215%，并在推测解码、多模态推理及量化推理方面均有显著的吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: RTP-LLM 作为一种经过生产环境验证的高性能推理引擎，通过集成多种优化技术，显著提升了工业级大规模 LLM 部署的效率、延迟表现及吞吐量，为大规模应用提供了成熟的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized AI applications, but deploying them at scale presents significant challenges. We present RTP-LLM, a high-performance inference engine for industrial-scale LLM deployment, successfully deployed across Alibaba Group serving over 100 million users. RTP-LLM addresses fundamental bottlenecks through integrated design. It optimizes model loading via file-order-driven I/O and parallel I/O-communication overlapping. The Prefill-Decode Disaggregation architecture decouples compute-intensive prefill from memory-bound decode phases, combined with hierarchical multi-tiered KV cache management enabling efficient cache reuse. In addition, RTP-LLM incorporates modular speculative decoding supporting multiple algorithms, adaptive KV cache quantization, and decoupled multimodal processing, with support for multi-level parallelism.
  Comprehensive evaluations across diverse model architectures (8B-235B parameters) have been conducted, where both controlled benchmarks and real production workloads are used. The results demonstrate RTP-LLM's superior performance against vLLM and SGLang: 4.7x-6.3x model loading speedup, 35-37% TTFT P95 latency reduction with 215% cache reuse improvement in production traffic scheduling, 1.12x-2.48x and 1.86x-2.52x throughput improvements in speculative decoding and multimodal inference, respectively, and 35-40% batch latency reduction with 1.9x-3.0x TTFT improvement in quantized inference. RTP-LLM's production-proven architecture and open-source availability make it a comprehensive solution for industrial LLM deployment.

</details>
